### [1] [Base change and the Mackey formula in Deligne--Lusztig theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22331)
*Sean Cotner*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用Digne的思想与Gaitsgory–Rozenblyum–Varshavsky的工作，证明Lusztig诱导与Glauberman对应（的一个微变体）在大素数阶域扩张下可交换，从而将Deligne–Lusztig理论中的Mackey公式化归到大基域情形（该情形已由Bonnafé证明）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deligne–Lusztig理论中的Mackey公式是刻画Lusztig诱导函子性质的核心工具，但在一般基域上尚未完全解决。已有结果（Bonnafé）仅覆盖"大"基域情形，因此需要一种归约手段，把一般情形化归到该已知情形，以完善理论的适用范围。

Method: 采用Digne的思路，并借助Gaitsgory–Rozenblyum–Varshavsky的近期工作，证明Lusztig诱导与Glauberman对应（的一个微小变体）在大素数阶域扩张下彼此交换；由此建立从一般基域到"大"基域的归约。

Result: 证明了Lusztig诱导与（微变体）Glauberman对应在大素数阶域扩张下的交换性；作为推论，Mackey公式可从一般情形归约到"大"基域情形，后者已由Bonnafé给出证明。

Conclusion: 该交换性结果给出了Deligne–Lusztig理论中Mackey公式的一个有效归约路径，将问题简化为大基域情形，从而在Bonnafé已有结果的基础上推进了对Mackey公式的完整理解。

Abstract: Using ideas of Digne and recent work of Gaitsgory--Rozenblyum--Varshavsky, we show that Lusztig induction commutes with (a minor variant of) the Glauberman correspondence for large prime degree field extensions. This allows one to reduce the Mackey formula in Deligne--Lusztig theory to the case of ``large'' base fields, in which case the result is due to Bonnafé.

</details>


### [2] [From $τ$-tilting to $τ_2$-tilting via splitting tilting triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22989)
*Zhongyuan Wang,Haicheng Zhang,Tiwei Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在分裂倾斜三元组$(A,T,B)$框架下，给出从$A$的$\tau$-倾斜模构造$B$的$\tau_2$-倾斜模的两种方法，并证明二者在非消没条件下一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论中$\tau$-倾斜模的构造多局限于单一代数或倾斜模情形（如Peng的构造），缺乏从$\tau$-倾斜模到高阶$\tau_2$-倾斜模的一般性迁移机制，且缺乏对其导出等价与代数结构的刻画。

Method: 设$(A,T,B)$为分裂倾斜三元组。第一种构造：直接从$A$的$\tau$-倾斜模产生$B$的$\tau_2$-倾斜模。第二种构造：当$A$遗传时，考察某二项淤积复形在导出等价$\mathbf{R}{\rm Hom}_A(T,-):D^b(A)\to D^b(B)$下的像的变异，得到$B$的三项淤积复形$\mathbf{W}$，在两项之间态射锥的非消没条件下分析${\rm H}^0\mathbf{W}$。并计算所构造模的自同态代数为三角矩阵代数。

Result: 在态射锥非消没条件下，${\rm H}^0\mathbf{W}$是基础$2$-倾斜$B$-模，且与第一种构造所得模一致（当其为$2$-倾斜时）。该模的自同态代数为显式三角矩阵代数，与$A$和$B$导出等价，并给出$K^b(\operatorname{proj}A)$的完全半正交分解。

Conclusion: 将Peng关于倾斜模的构造推广到$\tau$-倾斜模；统一了$\tau_2$-倾斜模的两种构造途径，并揭示其自同态代数结构与半正交分解性质。

Abstract: Let $(A,T,B)$ be a splitting tilting triple. We provide a construction of $τ_2$-tilting $B$-modules from $τ$-tilting $A$-modules. On the other hand, if $A$ is hereditary, by considering the mutation of a certain two-term silting complex of $A$ and its image under the derived equivalence $\mathbf{R}{\rm Hom}_A(T,-):D^b(A)\to D^b(B)$, we get a three-term silting complex ${\bf W}$ of $B$. Under an explicit non-vanishing condition on the cones of morphisms between the two summands, we prove that ${\rm H}^0\boldsymbol{W}$ is a basic $2$-tilting $B$-module and that it coincides with the module obtained from the first construction. As an application, we generalize the construction of Peng from tilting modules to $τ$-tilting modules. We also determine the endomorphism algebra of the constructed module as an explicit triangular matrix algebra, which is derived equivalent to $A$ and $B$ whenever the constructed module is $2$-tilting, and give a full semi-orthogonal decomposition of $K^b(\operatorname{proj}A)$.

</details>


### [3] [Maximal proper extremal subset in the crystal basis of a finite-dimensional irreducible module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23078)
*You Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究量子群晶体基中extremal subset的结构，证明有限型有限维不可约模晶体基存在唯一极大真extremal subset，其补集为能生成整个晶体基的元素集合，并在A型下验证了关于该补集由特定Kashiwara降算子迭代作用所得非零元素构成的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: extremal subsets是Demazure晶体的自然推广，由Assaf、Dranowski和González引入。论文旨在刻画晶体基中extremal subset的极大结构，理解哪些元素能够生成整个晶体基，并给出补集的显式描述。这一问题的动机在于深化对晶体基生成结构与extremal subset理论的理解。

Method: 方法上，作者在有限型量子群的有限维不可约模中考察extremal subset的偏序结构，证明存在唯一极大真extremal subset；随后构造一个由晶体基最低元素确定的distinguished element，并通过迭代施加Kashiwara降算子来刻画其补集，最后在A型情形下给出该猜想的完整证明。

Result: 主要结果有三：一是证明有限型有限维不可约模的晶体基存在唯一的极大真extremal subset；二是证明该极大真extremal subset的补集恰好是能够作为extremal subset生成整个晶体基的元素集合；三是提出关于补集由distinguished element经Kashiwara降算子迭代作用所得全部非零元素组成的猜想，并在A型情形下予以证明。

Conclusion: 论文建立了extremal subset在晶体基中的极大性理论，给出了生成整个晶体基的元素集合的刻画，并在A型下验证了补集的显式构造猜想。该工作将extremal subset理论与Kashiwara算子作用联系起来，为后续在一般有限型中证明该猜想提供了框架。

Abstract: The notion of extremal subsets was introduced by Assaf, Dranowski, and González as a natural generalization of Demazure crystals. In this paper, we show that the crystal basis of a finite-dimensional irreducible module over a quantum group of finite type has a unique maximal proper extremal subset, whose complement is the set of elements that generate the whole crystal basis as an extremal subset. We conjecture that this complement consists of all nonzero elements obtained by successively applying the lowering Kashiwara operators to a distinguished element, which is constructed from the lowest element of the crystal basis. We prove this conjecture in type A.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum Satake in Type A: The General and Generic Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23253)
*Ben Elias,Koppara Philip Thomas*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将几何Satake等价（经代数重构后）的q-形变从A_1、A_2型推广至A_{n-1}型（n≥4），在对q非单位根的特征零域上（并添加q的n次根）证明了量子群表示2-范畴与奇异Soergel双模2-范畴之间的等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何Satake等价此前被（非严格地）重释为两个代数定义的2-范畴之间的等价：一个由李代数表示构建，另一个由奇异Soergel双模构建；并说明了在A型中如何q-形变该等价（用量子群替代特殊线性李代数，并采用形变反射表示的奇异Soergel双模）。但此代数重构及其q-形变此前仅在A_1、A_2型中得到证明，亟需推广到更高秩。

Method: 在A_{n-1}型（n≥4）中，工作于特征为零的域上，q不是单位根，并添加q的n次根，进行泛型（generic）证明。过程中将文献中的若干结果——如Soergel-Williamson范畴化定理、球元素的Soergel猜想、各种对称性——推广到形变反射表示的情形。

Result: 在A_{n-1}型（n≥4）的泛型设定下证明了该（q-形变的）2-范畴等价；同时给出了若干已知结果在形变反射表示下的推广。

Conclusion: 该工作将几何Satake等价代数重构的q-形变从低秩（A_1、A_2）扩展至任意n≥4的A_{n-1}型，并顺带推广了Soergel-Williamson范畴化定理、球元素的Soergel猜想及相关对称性等结果。

Abstract: In work of the first author, the geometric Satake equivalence was (non-rigorously) reinterpreted as an equivalence between two algebraically-defined $2$-categories, one built from representations of a Lie algebra, and one built using singular Soergel bimodules. It was then explained how to $q$-deform this equivalence in type $A$, replacing the special linear Lie algebra with its quantum group, and using singular Soergel bimodules for a deformed reflection representation. Both the algebraic reformulation and its $q$-deformation were only proven in types $A_1$ and $A_2$. In this paper, we prove the result in type $A_{n-1}$ for $n \ge 4$, while working generically: more precisely, we work over a field of characteristic zero, where $q$ is not a root of unity, and having adjoined an $n$-th root of $q$. Along the way we generalize certain results in the literature (e.g. the Soergel-Williamson categorification theorem, the Soergel conjecture for spherical elements, various symmetries) to the deformed reflection representation.

</details>


### [5] [Sign components of diagonal superspace coinvariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23297)
*Nicolle González,John Lentfer,Hanna Mularczyk*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明余不变量环R_n^{(2,1)}与R_n^{(2,0)}⊗R_n^{(0,1)}的符号等型分量同构，其三重分次重数为Schröder多项式S_n(q,t,a)/(1+a)，从而解决Zabrocki与F. Bergeron关于符号特征的猜想，并改进Gorsky–Mellit关于(n,n+1)-环面纽结Khovanov–Rozansky同调的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zabrocki (2019) 关于Delta定理所需模的猜想以及F. Bergeron (2020) 关于R_n^{(2,1)}符号特征重数的猜想尚未解决；同时Gorsky–Mellit (2026) 建立了(n,n+1)-环面纽结的Khovanov–Rozansky同调与R_n^{(2,0)}⊗R_n^{(0,1)}的联系，但该纽结的Poincaré级数计算尚待刻画。

Method: 通过构造R_n^{(2,1)}与R_n^{(2,0)}⊗R_n^{(0,1)}符号等型分量之间的同构，并将该符号特征的三重分次重数表示为Schröder多项式S_n(q,t,a)除以(1+a)；随后结合Hogancamp (2017)的结果进行推广。

Result: 两个余不变量环的符号等型分量同构；其符号特征的三重分次重数等于S_n(q,t,a)/(1+a)；据此解决了Zabrocki猜想的符号特征部分并证明了F. Bergeron的猜想；此外证明(n,n+1)-环面纽结的Poincaré级数可由R_n^{(2,1)}的符号分量计算。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了符号特征重数与Schröder多项式之间的联系，明确了余不变量环的符号分量结构，并将纽结同调的计算归结为R_n^{(2,1)}的符号分量，深化了Delta定理与纽结同调之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We prove the sign-isotypic components of the coinvariant rings $R_n^{(2,1)}$ and $R_n^{(2,0)} \otimes R_n^{(0,1)}$ are isomorphic and show that the triply-graded multiplicity of this sign character is the Schröder polynomial $S_n(q,t,a)$, divided by $1+a$. This settles the sign-character component of a conjecture of Zabrocki (2019) on a module for the Delta theorem and proves a conjecture of F. Bergeron (2020) on the multiplicity of the sign character of $R_n^{(2,1)}$. Finally, using a result of Hogancamp (2017), we enhance a recent result of Gorsky--Mellit (2026) which relates the Khovanov--Rozansky homology of the $(n,n+1)$-torus knot to $R_n^{(2,0)} \otimes R_n^{(0,1)}$, by showing that the associated Poincaré series for this knot can be computed from the sign component of $R_n^{(2,1)}$.

</details>


### [6] [Local theta correspondence and generalized Shalika models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23349)
*Chong Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究等秩第二类对在局部Theta对应下广义Shalika模型的行为，推广了Gan关于Shalika模型与线性模型关系的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shalika模型与线性模型在自守表示与局部表示论中具有重要意义，Gan已建立二者在局部Theta对应下的关系，但其结论能否推广至广义Shalika模型尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在等秩第二类对（type II pairs of equal rank）的局部Theta对应框架下，考察广义Shalika模型的行为，通过将Shalika模型纳入广义Shalika模型的统一视角，比较其在Theta对应两端的对应关系。

Result: 建立了广义Shalika模型在等秩第二类对局部Theta对应下的行为描述，并验证其结果在退化情形下可回归到Gan关于Shalika模型与线性模型关系的已知结论。

Conclusion: 该工作将Shalika模型与线性模型的关系推广到广义Shalika模型情形，拓展了局部Theta对应下模型理论的认识。

Abstract: We study the behavior of generalized Shalika models under the local theta correspondence for type II pairs of equal rank. This generalizes a result of Gan on the relation between Shalika models and linear models.

</details>


### [7] [Transferred $L_\infty$-structures and a classification of binary brackets for finite-dimensional skew-gentle algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23527)
*Zixu Li,Liyu Liu,Lingchao Meng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在有向广义反例（skew-gentle）代数的平移Chouhy--Solotar上同调复形上构造了转移$L_\infty$-结构，给出二元运算$l_2$的显式基公式，并证明$A_n$型情形Hochschild dg Lie代数是同伦交换的、因而是$L_\infty$-形式的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限维skew-gentle代数Hochschild理论中的高阶代数结构，厘清形式性与非形式性的边界：已有Wong关于对偶数$\mathbb{K}[\theta]/\langle\theta^2\rangle$非形式性的结果，需要在更广的代数类上建立形式性判定。

Method: 在平移的Chouhy--Solotar上同调复形上构造转移$L_\infty$-结构，并在所有平移次数上给出$l_2$的基级显式公式；对$A_n$型skew-gentle代数，证明其Hochschild dg Lie代数同伦交换（homotopy abelian）。

Result: 得到转移$L_\infty$-结构及$l_2$的显式基公式；证明$A_n$型skew-gentle代数的Hochschild dg Lie代数同伦交换，从而$L_\infty$-形式；将其与对偶数的非形式性结果对照，导出一般的（非）形式性分类问题。

Conclusion: $A_n$型skew-gentle代数的Hochschild dg Lie代数是$L_\infty$-形式的，与对偶数情形形成对比；这引出一个更一般的（非）形式性分类问题。

Abstract: We construct a transferred $L_\infty$-structure on the shifted Chouhy--Solotar cochain complex of a finite-dimensional skew-gentle algebra and give explicit basis-level formulas for its binary operation $l_2$ in all shifted degrees. We further prove that the Hochschild dg Lie algebra of an $A_n$-type skew-gentle algebra is homotopy abelian, and hence $L_\infty$-formal, and contrast this with Wong's non-formality result for the dual numbers $\mathbb{K}[θ]/\langle θ^2 \rangle$, leading to a general formality classification problem.

</details>


### [8] [Affine Harish-Chandra center in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23560)
*Gurbir Dhillon,Ivan Losev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在正特征且特征大于单因子Coxeter数的情形下，该文将非临界水平的Kac--Moody顶点代数与包络代数的Harish--Chandra中心，等同于Langlands对偶群$\check{G}$的联络模空间（oper模空间）上的函数代数，给出显式同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正特征下非临界水平loop群的Harish--Chandra中心。这类中心在正特征非临界水平是全新现象：它非平凡，不同于零特征非临界水平（中心平凡），也不同于有限维约化群或临界水平loop群（中心为特征零中心模p的约化）。动机在于刻画这些新中心的几何本质。

Method: 设$G$为分裂约化群，基域特征大于各单因子的Coxeter数。取$G$的非临界水平$\kappa$，考虑形式圆盘$\mathscr{D}$上的Kac--Moody顶点代数$V_\kappa(\mathfrak{g})$及其弧群不变量中心$V_\kappa(\mathfrak{g})^{\mathscr{J}G}$。利用对偶水平$\check{\kappa}$、其Artin--Schreier像$\check{\kappa}^p-\check{\kappa}$，以及Frobenius扭曲圆盘$\mathscr{D}^{(1)}$上的oper模空间$\operatorname{Op}_{\check{G}}(\mathscr{D}^{(1)})_{\check{\kappa}^p-\check{\kappa}}$，构造谱同构；对水平$\kappa$的滤完备包络代数中心，类似地用穿孔圆盘$\mathscr{D}^\times$处理。

Result: 建立典范同构$\operatorname{Spec} V_\kappa(\mathfrak{g})^{\mathscr{J}G}\simeq \operatorname{Op}_{\check{G}}(\mathscr{D}^{(1)})_{\check{\kappa}^p-\check{\kappa}}$。对滤完备包络代数在水平$\kappa$的Harish--Chandra中心有类似识别，只需将$\mathscr{D}$换成穿孔圆盘$\mathscr{D}^\times$。

Conclusion: 正特征非临界水平loop群的Harish--Chandra中心非平凡，且不是特征零中心模$p$的约化，构成真正的新现象；其几何实现为Langlands对偶群在Frobenius扭曲圆盘上的oper模空间。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a split reductive group defined over a field of characteristic bigger than the Coxeter numbers of its simple factors. We identify the Harish--Chandra centers of the associated Kac--Moody vertex algebras and enveloping algebras at noncritical levels with algebras of functions on moduli spaces of connections for the Langlands dual group $\check{G}$.
  Namely, given a noncritical level $κ$ for $G$, consider the associated Kac--Moody vertex algebra $V_κ(\mathfrak{g})$ defined on a formal disc $\mathscr{D}$, and its Harish--Chandra center of arc group invariants $$V_κ(\mathfrak{g})^{\mathscr{J}G} \subset V_κ(\mathfrak{g}).$$ Write $\checkκ$ for the dual level for $\check{G}$, $\checkκ^p - \checkκ$ for its image under the Artin--Schreier map, and $\operatorname{Op}_{\check{G}}(\mathscr{D}^{(1)})_{\checkκ^p - \checkκ}$ for the moduli space of $(\checkκ^p - \checkκ)$-opers on the Frobenius twisted disc $\mathscr{D}^{(1)}$. We establish a canonical isomorphism $$\operatorname{Spec} V_κ(\mathfrak{g})^{\mathscr{J} G} \simeq \operatorname{Op}_{\check{G}}(\mathscr{D}^{(1)})_{\checkκ^p - \checkκ}.$$ There is a similar identification for the Harish--Chandra center of the filtered complete enveloping algebra at level $κ$, where instead of $\mathscr{D}$ we need to consider the punctured disc $\mathscr{D}^\times$.
  The presence of these Harish--Chandra centers is a genuinely new phenomenon for loop groups at noncritical level in positive characteristic: these centers are nontrivial, unlike for loop groups at noncritical level in characteristic zero, and in particular are not the reductions mod $p$ of the characteristic zero Harish--Chandra centers, unlike for finite dimensional reductive groups or loop groups at critical level.

</details>


### [9] [Lift of the Trivial Representation: The Nonsplit Type $D$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23675)
*Wan-Yu Tsai*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究非分裂实Spin群（D_n型）的非线性双覆盖，确定Kazhdan–Patterson提升Lift_G^{G̃}(C)在ρ/2相容真实中心特征下的不可约成分，并用Langlands参数显式描述相关的真实小表示，揭示非分裂情形下提升未必穷尽Π_{ρ/2}^s(G̃)的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作引入了Π_{ρ/2}^s(G̃)（无穷小特征为ρ/2的真实小表示集合），并在单链分裂情形证明其被平凡表示的Kazhdan–Patterson提升穷尽。本文旨在将研究推广至非分裂的D_n型实Spin群，考察提升是否仍能穷尽该类表示。

Method: 针对D_n型非分裂实Spin群的每个ρ/2相容的真实中心特征，计算Lift_G^{G̃}(C)的不可约成分，并用Langlands参数显式描述相关的真实小表示。

Result: 非分裂情形出现新现象：平凡表示的提升未必穷尽Π_{ρ/2}^s(G̃)。在所考察的族中，仅当G̃=Spin(2p+2,2p)时取等；另外两族中提升是真子集，其中一族甚至为零。

Conclusion: 非分裂D_n型实Spin群的情形与分裂情形本质不同，Kazhdan–Patterson提升不再普遍穷尽真实小表示集合，其是否穷尽依赖于具体族。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个统一的数学框架，揭示感知能力、日照与生态相互作用如何共同塑造睡眠与活动节律，并表明睡眠策略的均衡具有跨物种相互依赖性与多样性。

Abstract: An animal's ability to perceive its surroundings, as well as the ecological interactions it experiences, can be strongly influenced by the sleep patterns of both itself and surrounding species. Animals with limited sensory capabilities may struggle to navigate their habitat at night, while in a predator-prey system, it may be detrimental for prey to be inactive while predators are awake and hunting. Abstract: Genomic epidemiology has several methods for identifying which sampled individuals are linked by close proximity in the transmission chain, whether by grouping them into clusters under a genetic distance threshold or by identifying probable direct transmission pairs. Whether any two sampled individuals prove to be linked depends on the size of the sampling frame and the number of independent lineage introductions into it, and thus the balance of linked and unlinked individuals is itself data about these quantities.

Abstract: A well-known phenomenon in statistical analyses of populations of phylogenetic trees in the Billera-Holmes-Vogtmann space is that the topology of the Fréchet mean tree can contain multifurcations (i.e., internal nodes with more than two children), which raises the practical question of whether this reflects a population-level branching structure (hard polytomy) or merely sampling variability in the data (soft polytomy). Using this representation, we develop a fully nonparametric procedure for estimating the probability that trifurcations in a sample Fréchet mean tree will bifurcate at some future time if more observations are collected. Lastly, we apply our methodology to a problem in phylogenetics where we consider whether an observed trifurcation in the species tree of primates, glires, and tree shrews is genuinely trifurcated at the population level.

Abstract: On a $d$-dimensional, $d\geq 2$, globally hyperbolic spacetime with a timelike boundary $(\mathcal{M},g)$, we investigate the Klein-Gordon equation with Robin boundary conditions. Abstract: Trust develops through learning, while collective behavior can alter the environment in which later decisions are made. Reinforcement-learning models describe adaptation, and eco-evolutionary models describe behavior-environment feedback, but how an endogenous environment changes trust through material incentives and perceived states remains unclear. For the main feedback comparisons, we initialize the fixed-payoff baseline and feedback branches from the same learned state to control for prior learning. We then compare responses when payoff parameters or the observed environmental tier vary. As the payoff multiplication factor increased, successful trust in the fixed-payoff game crossed from a low to a higher finite-horizon level, led by risk-seeking investors. Environmental feedback promoted trust near perception-tier boundaries and reinforced its decline at the high-return operating point. At the high-return operating point, payoff feedback reinforced the learning-driven decline in trust. Stronger drive and longer horizons deepened this negative effect. Compared with a fixed-payoff trust game, environmental feedback carries past collective outcomes into future payoffs and observed states.

Abstract: In environmental feedback systems, self-interested behaviors of rational agents often undermine cooperation and environmental sustainability. Although punitive incentive mechanisms are widely recognized as effective in addressing such social dilemmas, the timing of their implementation under different environmental conditions remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we develop a multi-agent environmental feedback game framework with coupled incentive intensities across resource states. We investigate how system dynamics vary with incentive intensity under both resource-abundant and resource-scarce conditions. Theoretical analysis shows that imposing penalties in resource-abundant states can transform the system from a tragedy of the commons into a bistable regime that supports full cooperation. In contrast, imposing penalties in resource-scarce states leads to complex dynamical behaviors, including interior stability, heteroclinic cycles, and Hopf bifurcations, which hinder the emergence of stable cooperation. These results highlight the importance of timing in incentive regulation. Proactive intervention under favorable environmental conditions is more effective in sustaining cooperation than delayed enforcement after environmental degradation.

One also requires that $(M,d_{0})$ admits a contracting homotopy $K$ that is a differential operator of the same order as $d_{0}$.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体-边对应通常要求体谱存在真正的谱隙，而实际物理体系中常出现体谱并未完全打开、仅存在局域化区间（迁移率隙）的情形。作者希望将体-边对应推广到这类更一般的能量区间，刻画体拓扑指标对边缘谱的强制性影响。

Method: 在适当的局域化假设与边界条件下，对二维格点哈密顿量和磁薛定谔算子进行分析，利用辅助空间切割构造（auxiliary-space cut construction），并结合半空间算子的谱理论进行研究。

Result: 证明了若存在非零的常数体拓扑指标，则整个迁移率隙区间都属于对应半空间算子的绝对连续谱；并且在区间内几乎每一点处，绝对连续谱重数不低于该拓扑指标的绝对值。

Conclusion: 该结果把体-边对应从谱隙情形推广到迁移率隙情形，说明体拓扑指标在体谱仅为局域化的区间内依然强制产生绝对连续边缘态，并给出了边缘谱重数的定量下界。

Abstract: We study the edge spectrum of two-dimensional lattice Hamiltonians and magnetic Schrödinger operators in a mobility gap, an energy interval in which the bulk spectrum is potentially localized rather than necessarily absent. Under suitable localization and boundary assumptions, we prove that a constant nonzero bulk topological index implies that the entire interval belongs to the absolutely continuous spectrum of the corresponding half-space operator. The absolutely continuous spectral multiplicity is bounded below by the absolute value of the index at almost every energy in the interval. The first version of the auxiliary-space cut construction in Section 2.3 was generated using ChatGPT 6.

</details>

Abstract: The transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons are measured as a function of the $W$ boson rapidity, $y_{W}$, in the range $0 \leq y_{W} \leq 5$ using $W\toμν_μ$ decays in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded by the LHCb experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.1$ fb$^{-1}$. The fractions are extracted from a template fit to the muon transverse momentum and pseudorapidity. The results show a strong rapidity dependence and agree with next-to-leading-order Standard Model predictions, providing the first determination of the transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons in the forward region.

</details>


### [36] [Benchmarking Nucleon Production in Hadron Interactions Relevant for GeV-scale Neutrino Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22662)

For popular target nuclei, we find that the number of emitted nucleons for mesonless hadron interactions can be parameterized by simple Gaussian and exponential decay functions. Abstract: Neutrino momentum reconstruction at hadron colliders is intrinsically ambiguous because the longitudinal momentum is not directly observed. These results indicate that mixture density networks are a competitive alternative to normalizing flows for neutrino reconstruction.

The production threshold has a larger effect than the electromagnetic model: Penelope and Livermore agree within about 3% in L/E, whereas Standard gives higher L/E, especially at low thresholds.

Abstract: The mkFit algorithm provides an implementation of the Kalman filter-based track reconstruction algorithm that exploits both thread- and data-level parallelism. It has been adopted by the CMS Collaboration as the primary track building algorithm during the Run 3 of the LHC for both the online and offline track reconstruction sequences. Thanks to mkFit, an average speedup of a factor 3.5 is achieved in track building while retaining or improving physics performance. As a consequence of the speedup provided by mkFit in track building, track fitting has become a comparably significant component of the overall tracking time. Given the increased demands of the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, further speedup can be achieved by extending mkFit to the track fitting task. We present preliminary results for track fitting in the CMS High-Level Trigger using mkFit, covering both the physics and the computational performance, based on realistic Phase-2 simulations.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出range-projected χ²检验统计量，将拟合优度检验限制在携带独立统计信息的子空间，得到自由度为 N_bins - N_null 的χ²，其中 N_null 为独立约束数目。并证明该秩亏是事件共享结构的可预测结果，且 N_null 可分解为：由分bin几何先验确定的结构性贡献 + 依赖相空间占据情况的运动学贡献。

Conclusion: 事件共享引发的协方差秩亏及其自由度损失是可量化、可预测的：结构性约束由binning几何决定，运动学约束由相空间占据决定。range-projected χ²可避免χ²被人为膨胀，为多微分截面联合拟合提供正确的拟合优度检验。

Abstract: When multiple differential cross-section measurements are extracted from a common event sample, the same events contribute simultaneously to multiple distributions. This event-sharing structure imposes exact linear constraints among the bin counts, reducing the effective dimensionality of the measurement below the total number of bins. Since the covariance matrix obeys these inter-distribution constraints, it contains a rank deficiency. While limited numerical precision may inadvertently restore invertibility, the $χ^{2}$ contributions along constrained dimensions will be arbitrary, yielding unphysically inflated $χ^{2}$ values in global goodness-of-fit tests. We present the range-projected $χ^{2}$, a test statistic that restricts the goodness-of-fit test to the subspace carrying independent statistical information, yielding a $χ^{2}$ with $N_\text{bins} - N_\text{null}$ degrees of freedom, where $N_\text{null}$ is the number of independent constraints. We show that this rank deficiency is a predictable consequence of the event-sharing structure, and that $N_\text{null}$ decomposes into a structural contribution determined a priori from the binning geometry and a kinematic contribution that depends on the phase-space occupancy. The method is validated with an analytical toy model and a simulated neutrino--argon cross-section measurement including unfolding and multi-source systematic uncertainties.

</details>

Abstract: A search for a new top-philic vector boson ($Z'$) produced in association with a top-quark or a pair of top quarks, and decaying into a top quark pair is presented. The search uses $pp$ collision data collected by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider at centre-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} =$ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV, corresponding to integrated luminosities of 140 fb$^{-1}$ and 56 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. Events containing exactly two same-sign leptons or at least three leptons (electrons or muons) are selected. A transformer-based classifier parameterised as a function of the $Z'$ mass is used to optimise the signal-to-background discrimination. The results are interpreted in a simplified model with a colour-singlet vector boson coupled to top quarks with coupling strength $c_t$ and chirality angle $θ$. No significant excess is observed above the expected Standard Model background. A $Z'$ boson with mass below 1150 GeV is excluded at 95% confidence level for a benchmark scenario with $c_t = 1$ and $θ= 0$, with an expected limit of 1250 GeV.

### [44] [Equivariant Homotopy Interleaving and Persistent Whitehead Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23440)
*Kushal Halder,Subhankar Sau,Debasis Sen*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文建立了等变持久同伦理论，引入 $G$-稳定与 $G$-同伦不变距离，定义 $G$-同伦交错距离 $d^G_{HI}$，证明其稳定性、同伦不变性与普适性，并给出等变持久Whitehead定理与等变持久神经引理，进而得到带对称性的随机滤流的等变大数定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典持久同伦理论（如Blumberg-Lesnick的同伦交错距离）未考虑有限群对称性。实际问题中的滤流常带有群作用，因此需要发展等变版本的持久同伦理论，并建立相应的稳定性与统计一致性理论。

Method: 在持久 $G$-空间范畴中发展等变持久同伦理论：引入 $G$-稳定、$G$-同伦不变距离的概念，定义等变同伦交错距离 $d^G_{HI}$；证明其 $G$-稳定性、$G$-同伦不变性及普适性（支配所有同类距离）；进一步证明等变持久Whitehead定理（将等变持久同伦群的交错与 $G$-同伦交错相联系）以及等变好覆盖的持久神经引理。

Result: 证明 $d^G_{HI}$ 是 $G$-稳定且 $G$-同伦不变的，并具有普适性，即支配任何其他 $G$-稳定、$G$-同伦不变距离。建立等变持久Whitehead定理，刻画等变持久同伦群交错与 $G$-同伦交错的关系。证明等变持久神经引理，适用于持久 $G$-空间的等变好覆盖。作为推论，得到滤流的等变弱大数定律。

Conclusion: 该工作为带有限群对称性的持久空间提供了系统的等变同伦理论框架，所得等变大数定律给出随机滤流在等变同伦意义下的统计一致性结果。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group. In this article, we develop some results of equivariant persistent homotopy theory for persistent $G$-spaces. We introduce the notions of $G$-stable and $G$-homotopy invariant distances and define the $G$-homotopy interleaving distance, an equivariant analogue $d^G_{HI}$ of the homotopy interleaving distance of Blumberg and Lesnick. We prove that this distance $d^G_{HI}$ is $G$-stable and $G$-homotopy invariant and establish its universality by showing that it dominates any such distance. We further prove a version of equivariant persistent Whitehead theorem relating interleavings of equivariant persistent homotopy groups to $G$-homotopy interleavings of persistent $G$-spaces. We also prove an equivariant persistent nerve lemma for equivariant good covers of persistent $G$-spaces. As a consequence, we obtain an equivariant weak law of large numbers for filtrations, providing a homotopy-theoretic consistency result for random filtrations equipped with finite group of symmetries.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将持续性同调中临界点的概念推广到超图与单纯复形及其映射（态射）层面，通过相对同调群刻画临界点，以建立持续性结构的拓扑不变量框架，并借助参数化构型空间将抽象构造与填充、覆盖等几何/组合问题联系起来。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the homological critical points for persistence hypergraphs and persistence simplicial complexes. With the help of the relative homology groups, we define the homological crtical points for persistence morphisms between persistence hypergraphs and persistence simplicial maps between persistence simplicial complexes. We prove some commutative diagrams of subset relations for the homological critical points of persistence hypergraphs as well as persistence morphisms between them. We also prove some commutative diagrams of subset relations for the homological critical points of persistence simplicial complexes as well as persistence simplicial maps between them. By using the parametric configuration spaces, we give the parametric independence complexes to be the space of packings and give the parametric dominating hypergraphs to be the space of coverings, as some examples.

</details>


### [46] [Local coefficients for genuine equivariant cohomology I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23749)
*Nikolai Konovalov,Artem Prikhodko*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文发展等变局部系统理论，为Atiyah-Segal K理论、Lurie的tempered上同调及Grojnowski-Greenlees-Gepner-Meier等变椭圆上同调等"真等变上同调理论"提供范畴化，类比于普通局部系统范畴对奇异上同调的范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真等变上同调理论（如等变K理论、tempered上同调、等变椭圆上同调）缺乏统一的范畴化框架。普通局部系统可范畴化奇异上同调，作者希望将这一类比推广到等变情形，并统一处理全局空间、非平凡系数以及超出有限群的tempered理论。

Method: 对全局空间X与系数系统A: Orb^op → Pr^L，引入全局等变局部系统范畴LS^glo(X,A)与真等变局部系统范畴LS^gen(X,A)，后者将紧Lie群G的真稳定范畴Sp^G推广到非常数系数。当系数来自局部复周期底上的定向阿贝尔群栈A时，构造tempered局部系统范畴LS^temp(X,A)，将Lurie理论推广至有限群之外，其定义条件由tempered形式的Atiyah-Segal完备化定理所支撑。

Result: 证明全局截面诱导等价LS^temp(BU(1),A) ≃ QCoh(A)；并证明当X为orbispace时，LS^temp(X,A)是LS^gen(X,A)的smashing局部化。

Conclusion: 该理论为多种真等变上同调理论提供了统一的范畴化框架，将Lurie的tempered理论从有限群推广到更一般的群，并通过BU(1)的等价与smashing局部化结果刻画了tempered局部系统的结构。

Abstract: We develop a theory of equivariant local systems which categorifies genuine equivariant cohomology theories -- such as Atiyah--Segal $K$-theory, Lurie's tempered cohomology, and the equivariant elliptic cohomology of Grojnowski, Greenlees, and Gepner--Meier -- analogously to how the category of ordinary local systems categorifies singular cohomology.
  More precisely, we introduce, for a global space $X$ and a coefficient system $\mathscr{A} \colon \mathrm{Orb}^{\mathrm{op}} \to \mathrm{Pr}^{\mathrm{L}}$, the categories $\mathrm{LS}^{\mathrm{glo}}(X,\mathscr{A})$ and $\mathrm{LS}^{\mathrm{gen}}(X,\mathscr{A})$ of globally equivariant and genuine local systems, the latter generalizing the genuine stable category $\mathrm{Sp}^G$ of a compact Lie group $G$ to non-constant coefficients. When the coefficients come from an oriented abelian group stack $A$ over a locally complex periodic base, we also construct the category $\mathrm{LS}^\mathrm{temp}(X,A)$ of tempered local systems, extending Lurie's theory beyond finite groups; the defining condition is justified by a tempered form of the Atiyah--Segal completion theorem. We show that global sections induce an equivalence $\mathrm{LS}^{\mathrm{temp}}(BU(1),A) \simeq \mathrm{QCoh}(A)$ and we prove that $\mathrm{LS}^\mathrm{temp}(X,A)$ is a smashing localization of $\mathrm{LS}^\mathrm{gen}(X,A)$ if $X$ is an orbispace.

</details>


### [47] [Automated proofs of unstable Adams differentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23984)
*Jake Francis Baer*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 提出一种基于计算机的方法，通过系统应用不稳定Leibniz法则及EHP序列的自然性来计算不稳定Adams谱序列中的微分，并给出2-primary不稳定球面同伦群直至第50干（stem）的阶的上、下界表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不稳定Adams谱序列中的微分计算通常依赖大量手工推导，过程繁琐且易出错；作者希望借助计算机系统化地处理这些微分，从而扩展到更高的stem范围并得到更精确的同伦群阶信息。

Method: 基于计算机的方法：系统应用不稳定Leibniz法则，并利用关于EHP序列中映射的自然性来推导微分；将结果整理为表格，记录2-primary不稳定球面同伦群阶的上界与下界（直至不稳定第50 stem）；同时通过若干微分的证明示例，说明如何解读相关的不稳定Adams图表与流程图。

Result: 得到直至不稳定第50 stem的2-primary不稳定球面同伦群阶的上、下界表格，并给出若干微分的证明示例以及解读不稳定Adams图表与流程图的指南。

Conclusion: 该计算机辅助方法能够系统化地计算不稳定Adams谱序列微分，扩展了不稳定球面同伦群的计算范围，并为后续相关计算与图表解读提供了实用工具和参考。

Abstract: We present a computer-based approach to computing differentials in the unstable Adams spectral sequence by systematically applying the unstable Leibniz rule and naturality with respect to maps in the EHP sequence. We record our results in tables of upper and lower bounds on the orders of 2-primary unstable homotopy groups of spheres through the unstable 50-stem. We provide examples of proofs for several differentials and give a guide to interpreting the associated unstable Adams charts and flow chart diagrams for differential proofs.

</details>


### [48] [Equivariant generating hypotheses for finite groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24390)
*Sihao Ma,XiaoLin Danny Shi,Zhouli Xu,Shangjie Zhang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文否定了Bohmann关于任意非平凡有限群G等变生成假设的猜想，构造了具有任意长非零复合幂的ghost映射，并证明同伦模函子非满、存在同构同伦模但不同伦等价的有限G-谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变生成假设是稳定同伦论中生成假设在等变情形的推广，Bohmann曾提出相应猜想。作者要检验该猜想在一般有限群下是否成立，即便在更强的检验条件下（每个子群H处所有RO(H)-分级同伦群都被检验），猜想是否仍然成立。

Method: 利用圆幂映射（circle power maps）以及射影空间乘积的循环置换构造ghost映射，方法受Ma–Xu在motivic情形下的范畴方法与射影空间幂映射的启发。

Result: 对每个非平凡有限群G及整除|G|的素数p，构造出有限G-谱上的ghost，其复合幂可任意长且非零，从而否定Bohmann等变生成假设；同时证明同伦模函子非满，并构造出具有同构完整同伦模但彼此不同伦等价的有限G-谱。

Conclusion: 等变生成假设对任意非平凡有限群均不成立，即使在强化检验条件下；同伦模函子不能完全区分有限G-谱的同伦类型。

Abstract: We disprove Bohmann's equivariant generating hypothesis for every nontrivial finite group $G$, even when all $\RO(H)$-graded homotopy groups at every subgroup $H$ are tested. For each fixed $G$ and prime $p$ dividing $|G|$, we construct ghosts on finite $G$-spectra with arbitrarily long nonzero composition powers. We also prove that the homotopy-module functors are nonfull and construct non-equivalent finite $G$-spectra with isomorphic full homotopy modules.
  Our constructions use circle power maps and cyclic permutations of products of projective spaces, and are motivated by Ma--Xu's categorical method in the motivic setting and the projective-space power maps.

</details>

### [49] [CHOREO: Every Humanoid Skill as a Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22274)
*Ziyi Sun,Jingwen Chen,Yuxi Wang,Xiuze Xia,Long Cheng,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Yujun Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CHOREO框架，将异构人形技能统一表达为可执行运动轨迹SkillMotion，实现免训练的技能组合，在Unitree G1上对130个多动作任务达到95.4%序列成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的技能虽多样，但因表示方式、接口和控制器互不兼容而被隔离，难以组合复用。

Method: 基于"任何技能最终都可表达为可执行运动轨迹"的观察，将每种能力转换为统一表示SkillMotion（融合运动状态、接触、语义与边界条件），并通过直接接续、三次Hermite插值混合或经过验证的过渡动作进行组合，无需重训练源模型或测试时更新模型。

Result: 在MuJoCo中的Unitree G1上，CHOREO组织2,950个来自异构来源的SkillMotion资产，在130个多动作任务上达到95.4%的序列成功率，其中八动作序列成功率为93.8%。

Conclusion: 可执行轨迹为积累和组合预训练人形能力提供了可扩展的接口。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics have produced diverse skills through reinforcement learning, motion imitation, and generative modeling. Yet these capabilities remain siloed because they are built around incompatible representations, interfaces, and controllers. We present CHOREO, a framework for training-free composition of heterogeneous humanoid skills. Our key observation is that, regardless of how a skill is learned, it can ultimately be expressed as an executable motion trajectory. Based on this observation, CHOREO converts each capability into SkillMotion, a unified representation that combines motion states, contacts, semantics, and boundary conditions. Skills are composed through direct continuation, cubic Hermite blending, or validated bridge motions, without retraining source models or updating models at test time. On Unitree G1 in MuJoCo, CHOREO organizes 2,950 admitted SkillMotion assets derived from heterogeneous sources and achieves 95.4\% sequence success across 130 multi-action tasks, including 93.8\% success on eight-action sequences. These results demonstrate that executable trajectories provide a scalable interface for accumulating and composing pretrained humanoid capabilities.

</details>


### [50] [D3DWA: Adaptive Weight and Prediction-Horizon for Dynamic Window Approach via Dueling Double Deep Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22276)
*Zahra Jooyandeh,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D3DWA：基于Dueling Double DQN的自适应DWA框架，在每个控制步联合选择评价权重与预测时域，在仿真与真机实验中均完整到达目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWA广泛用于局部导航，但其参数通常在导航前固定；合适的预测时域随局部自由空间变化——开阔区域宜用长时域提高效率，狭窄或杂乱区域宜用短时域保持可行运动，因此需要自适应调节参数（尤其是评价权重与预测时域）。

Method: 提出D3DWA框架，采用Dueling Double Deep Q-Network（D3QN），在每个控制步从连续导航状态中同时选择DWA评价权重与预测时域；同时保留DWA原有的轨迹生成与碰撞检测机制。

Result: 在八个仿真环境（含未见布局）中，D3DWA均到达全部目标；真机实验中完成全部三种测试配置，包括一个仅调权重变体超时的受限场景。

Conclusion: 结果表明联合自适应评价权重与预测时域具有明显收益；补充材料见 https://mertcookimg.github.io/d3dwa/ 。

Abstract: The Dynamic Window Approach (DWA) is widely used for local navigation, but its performance depends strongly on parameters that are typically fixed before navigation. In particular, the appropriate prediction horizon can vary with local free space: longer horizons support efficient motion in open areas, whereas shorter horizons help preserve feasible motions in narrow or cluttered regions. This paper proposes D3DWA, an adaptive DWA framework based on a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network (D3QN), which jointly selects the DWA evaluation weights and prediction horizon from a continuous navigation state at every control step while retaining DWA's trajectory generation and collision checking. In eight simulated environments, including unseen layouts, D3DWA reached every goal. Real-robot experiments further showed that D3DWA completed all three tested configurations, including a constrained case in which the weights-only variant timed out. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly adapting the evaluation weights and prediction horizon. Additional material is available at https://mertcookimg.github.io/d3dwa/

</details>


### [51] [DeViGrasp: Robust Visual Mobile Grasping for Quadruped Manipulators under Degraded Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22278)
*Liang Zhou,Jiaming Su,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeViGrasp-Bench 基准与 DeViGrasp-Net 师生框架，解决四足机械臂在视觉退化下的移动抓取与目标跟踪问题，在退化、新物体与复杂地形上显著优于 VBC 与 DQ-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机械臂可在复杂环境中移动抓取，但其全身控制策略依赖不可靠的机载视觉感知。现有方法通常在较可靠观测下开发，未系统考察遮挡、分割掩码丢失、深度噪声与目标定位抖动对抓取推理和目标跟踪的影响，因此需要一个受控视觉退化的评测基准与鲁棒控制方法。

Method: 提出 DeViGrasp-Bench：包含受控视觉退化、已见/未见物体、多难度等级与复杂地形的移动抓取基准，评估任务成功率、执行效率与动作平滑度。提出 DeViGrasp-Net：教师-学生框架，融合状态条件抓取推理与可靠性感知的时序目标估计。特权教师基于物体、机器人、末端执行器与任务状态关注离线抓取候选；可部署学生通过 Target Hold Memory 与 Temporal Memory Attention 融合双视角分割深度观测及当前、记忆与恢复三类目标假设。

Result: DeViGrasp-Net 在各退化等级、未见物体与复杂地形上均优于 VBC，并在所有评估退化等级上超过适配的 DQ-Net。Difficult 设置下成功率达 62.3%，分别较 VBC 与 DQ-Net 提升 16.1 与 4.3 个百分点；Hard 设置下对 DQ-Net 的优势扩大至 10.5 个百分点。消融实验验证了抓取感知监督与可靠性感知时序记忆的互补收益。

Conclusion: 通过受控视觉退化基准的系统评测与师生式可靠性感知时序记忆设计，可有效提升四足机械臂移动抓取的鲁棒性与执行质量，抓取感知监督与时序记忆机制具有互补增益。

Abstract: Quadruped manipulators enable mobile grasping in complex environments, yet their whole-body control policies remain vulnerable to unreliable onboard visual perception. Existing methods are typically developed under relatively reliable observations and have not systematically examined how occlusion, segmentation-mask dropout, depth noise, and target-localization jitter affect grasp reasoning and target tracking. To address this gap, we introduce DeViGrasp-Bench, a benchmark for mobile grasping under degraded vision that incorporates controlled visual degradations, seen and unseen objects, multiple difficulty levels, and complex terrains, and evaluates task success, execution efficiency, and action smoothness. We further propose DeViGrasp-Net, a teacher--student framework that combines state-conditioned grasp reasoning with reliability-aware temporal target estimation. The privileged teacher attends to offline grasp candidates conditioned on object, robot, end-effector, and task states, while the deployable student fuses dual-view segmented-depth observations with current, memory, and recovery target hypotheses through Target Hold Memory and Temporal Memory Attention. DeViGrasp-Net outperforms VBC across degradation levels, unseen objects, and complex terrains, and surpasses an adapted DQ-Net across all evaluated degradation levels. Under the Difficult setting, it achieves a success rate of 62.3\%, improving upon VBC and DQ-Net by 16.1 and 4.3 percentage points, respectively; under the Hard setting, its margin over DQ-Net increases to 10.5 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of grasp-aware supervision and reliability-aware temporal memory.

</details>


### [52] [ORDER: A Fictitious-World Benchmark for Domain-Adaptive Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22285)
*Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Anuj Tiwari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ORDER基准，用不可见于预训练的合成虚构物理世界，解决持续预训练中新知识与既有知识混淆的评估问题，并验证小模型可实现真正的世界模型归纳与机器人规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练常因训练语料与模型既有知识重叠，导致性能提升无法归因于"新学习"。在知识密集、任务轻（KHTL）的机器人场景（如药品分发、危险品处理、设施专属规程）中，规则私有且安全攸关，实机测试昂贵甚至危险，因此亟需能干净评估新知识习得的基准。

Method: 构建ORDER（Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning）基准：基于一个虚构世界，用342,069 token的合成语料定义自洽且不可能出现在任何模型预训练数据中的物理规则。包含两部分：500题的ORDER-BENCH知识测试，以及更难的组合任务ORDER-SPATIAL——在熟悉与全新场景下对物体排序以实现安全操作。随后接入机器人流水线，增加技能适配阶段，并在带人机闭环修正的仿真iiwa7机械臂上完成从感知到执行的完整验证。

Result: GPT-4.1零样本在ORDER-SPATIAL上低于随机水平（Kendall's tau=0.441），说明其先验与虚构物理冲突。持续预训练后，小模型在熟悉和全新场景上均大幅提升，表明是真正的世界模型归纳而非记忆。仅答好知识测试的模型常无法产出可执行计划，需技能适配阶段；完成后小规模全离线模型超越获得同一规则检索能力的GPT-4.1（tau=0.848 vs 0.606）。ORDER-SPATIAL表现而非知识测试准确率才能预测真实规划质量。

Conclusion: 以不可见于预训练的虚构世界为基础，可干净地隔离并评估持续预训练带来的新知识习得；小模型经持续预训练与技能适配后，在KHTL机器人任务上可实现优于大模型的规划性能，且ORDER-SPATIAL是比知识问答更可靠的能力指标。

Abstract: Adapting language models to new domains via continual pre-training raises a basic evaluation problem: if the training corpus overlaps with what the model already knows, performance gains cannot be cleanly attributed to new learning rather than pre-existing knowledge. This matters most for knowledge-intensive, task-light (KHTL) robot deployments - pharmaceutical dispensing, hazardous-material handling, facility-specific protocols, where the physical task is simple but the governing rules are proprietary and safety-critical, and where extensive live testing is costly or unsafe. We introduce ORDER (Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning), a benchmark built on a fictitious world: a 342,069-token synthetic corpus defining a self-consistent physics that cannot appear in any model's pre-training data. ORDER pairs a 500-question knowledge test (ORDER-BENCH) with a harder compositional task, ORDER-SPATIAL: ordering objects for safe manipulation across both familiar and entirely novel scenes. GPT-4.1 without adaptation scores below chance on ORDER-SPATIAL (Kendall's tau = 0.441), showing its priors actively conflict with the invented physics. After continual pre-training, small models improve substantially on both familiar and novel scenes alike evidence of genuine world-model induction rather than memorization. We then carry this through to a robot pipeline: models that answer the knowledge test well often cannot produce valid, executable plans without a further skill-adaptation stage, after which small, fully offline models outperform GPT-4.1 even when GPT-4.1 is given retrieval access to the same rules (Kendall's tau = 0.848 vs. 0.606), on a full perception-to-execution loop demonstrated on a simulated iiwa7 arm with human-in-the-loop correction. Throughout, ORDER-SPATIAL performance, not knowledge-test accuracy is what predicts real plan quality.

</details>


### [53] [MR-SPITE: Accelerating Multi-Robot Conflict Scans via Hierarchical Swept-Volume Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23928)
*Marta Markowicz,James Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MR-SPITE是一种基于运动段的保守过滤方法，用于加速多机器人路径的冲突扫描，在不改变底层离散扫描器行为的前提下提升检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步机器人路径的冲突扫描需对每对机器人在每个时间步进行细致碰撞检测，且冲突修复过程中可能反复执行，计算开销大。需要一种能加速这些扫描同时保持原有检测准确性的过滤手段。

Method: MR-SPITE将每条路径划分为时间区间，为每段分配保守边界；通过区间调度器比较时间上重叠运动的边界，对边界不相交的共享窗口直接判定无冲突，对无法解析的窗口交由底层碰撞检测器处理。该方法被集成到ARC中，并与基于VAMP的碰撞检测结合。

Result: 在16个Fetch机器人场景下，集成MR-SPITE的ARC相比使用PRM+VAMP的基线ARC实现，冲突扫描速度成对中位数提升7.18倍，规划时间中位数减少57%。

Conclusion: 运动段边界可补充配置级碰撞加速，在保持底层离散扫描器行为的同时显著提升多机器人冲突扫描与规划效率。

Abstract: Conflict scanning over synchronized robot paths requires detailed collision checking, potentially across every robot pair at every timestep, and may be repeated many times as conflicts are repaired. We present Multi-Robot SPITE (MR-SPITE), a conservative, motion-segment-based filter for accelerating these scans. MR-SPITE partitions each path into temporal intervals and assigns conservative bounds to each segment. An interval scheduler compares bounds for temporally overlapping motions: disjoint bounds certify the shared window as conflict-free, while unresolved windows are passed to the underlying collision checker. We integrate MR-SPITE into ARC and combine it with VAMP-based collision checking. For 16 Fetch robots, ARC with MR-SPITE achieves a paired median conflict scan speedup of 7.18x and reduces median planning time by 57% relative to the baseline ARC implementation with PRM+VAMP. These results demonstrate that motion-segment bounds complement configuration-level collision acceleration while preserving the behavior of the underlying discretized scanner.

</details>


### [54] [OJOx: Specification-Conditioned Demonstrations for Embodied AI in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22289)
*Mohamed Dawod*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"规格条件演示"（specification-conditioned demonstration）这一新数据范式，并给出面向建筑领域的采集接口 OJOx：将外部设计模型中的目标几何同步投射至头显并锚定于物理工作空间，与全身及手部动作同步录制。基于一次含33个构件的砌墙实验验证了记录的物理一致性，并可在仿真中回放至 Unitree G1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自我中心与全身人体演示已成为具身智能的主要数据来源，但其仅记录"感知与动作"，缺失赋予动作以目的的外部规格。在建筑等技能型作业中，这一缺失尤为关键：工作目标是由设计模型定义的、当前环境中尚不存在的项目专属构型；可迁移的能力并非某面墙的几何，而是依据规格实现新几何的能力。

Method: 提出"规格条件演示"范式，即对演示者所感知的物理状态、由外部设计提供的目标状态、以及连接二者的行为进行同步记录。实现为 OJOx 采集接口：将设计几何传输至头显、在物理工作空间中锚定、渲染进演示者的立体透视视图，并与全身运动与手部运动同步录制；录制结果兼容现有人形机器人重定向基础设施。

Result: 完成一次全流程仪器化实验：砌筑一面33个构件的墙体，且规格在作业过程中动态变化；通过一台独立于采集系统配准的外部相机，将记录与物理场景进行一致性核验；录制的会话可在仿真中回放至 Unitree G1。

Conclusion: OJOx 提供了一个数据接口，用于检验具身策略能否不只模仿已演示的动作，而是朝向其训练经验中未曾出现的规格行动，从而支撑规格条件下的可迁移技能学习。

Abstract: Large-scale egocentric and whole-body human demonstrations are becoming a primary source of data for embodied intelligence. They record what people perceive and do, but rarely the external specification that gave an action its purpose. In construction that omission is consequential: skilled work is directed at project-specific configurations defined in a design model - configurations not yet present in the environment being observed. A mason's transferable competence is not the geometry of one wall but the ability to realise a new geometry from a specification. We introduce the specification-conditioned demonstration: a synchronised record of the physical state a demonstrator perceives, the intended state supplied to them by an external design, and the behaviour connecting the two. We present OJOx, a capture interface that realises this for construction - delivering design geometry to a headset, anchoring it in the physical workspace, rendering it into a demonstrator's stereo passthrough view, and recording that view synchronously with whole-body and hand motion. We report one fully instrumented session - a 33-component wall laid against a specification that changes while the work proceeds - and check the record against the physical scene through an external camera registered independently of the capture. Recorded sessions remain compatible with existing humanoid retargeting infrastructure and replay onto a Unitree G1 in simulation. The result is a data interface for testing whether embodied policies can learn not merely to imitate demonstrated actions, but to act toward specifications absent from their training experience.

</details>


### [55] [When Does Test-Time Physical Diagnosis Pay? A Frozen Policy Buys Evidence It Never Reads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22299)
*Zhengshu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出机器人物理诊断应满足的六个有序经验条件链，并在受控与公开环境中检验该链条。受控环境端到端成立；迁移到未见机制后在"证据使用"环节断裂：冻结解码器不改变选择，而仅用轨迹增量的线性模型能恢复正确选择，说明证据有信息却未被使用；失败集中在最丰富的证据层级，该分层被聚合准确率掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人面对陌生的物理条件时，常见做法是收集关于"变了什么"的证据并进行适应。然而，要使这种诊断真正改善行为，需要一系列前提条件成立。论文的动机在于：验证"成功识别物理条件"是否真的能保证证据被使用和有效适应，并揭示评估方式（依赖恢复准确率或聚合性能）可能掩盖的失败环节。

Method: 提出六个有序的经验条件（meaningful reference、物理条件可识别性、所获证据的使用、决策价值、相对固定替代方案的选择价值、安全实现），构成一条诊断链条；在受控环境和公开环境中对该链条逐环节进行检验，并做迁移测试（转移到未见机制）。通过对比冻结解码器的选择变化，以及仅使用轨迹增量的线性模型在未参与拟合机制上的表现，判断证据是否被使用；并按证据层级（丰富度）分解决策结果。

Result: 在受控环境中，链条端到端成立。迁移到未见机制后，链条在"证据使用"环节断裂：在需要完整轨迹的决策上，冻结解码器不改变其选择；而仅使用轨迹增量的线性模型在未参与拟合的机制上仍能恢复正确选择，表明轨迹信息是有用的但未被使用。失败集中在最丰富的证据层级——这些决策退化为随机水平，而用较低成本证据即可解决的决策仍保持正确，这一分化被聚合准确率所掩盖。在公开环境中，同一条链条也可能在其他环节断裂。

Conclusion: 成功的物理识别既不保证证据被使用，也不保证有效适应。评估不应仅依赖恢复准确率或聚合性能，而应识别链条具体在何处断裂，采用逐环节的评估方式。

Abstract: When a robot faces unfamiliar physical conditions, a common approach is to collect evidence about what changed and adapt. For such diagnosis to improve behavior, six ordered empirical conditions must hold: a meaningful reference, identifiability of the physical condition, use of the acquired evidence, decision value, selection value over a fixed alternative, and safe realization. We test this chain in controlled and public environments. It holds end to end in our controlled environments. After transfer to unseen mechanisms, however, it breaks at evidence use. On decisions requiring the full trace, the frozen decoder does not change its choice. A linear model using only trace increments recovers the correct choice on mechanisms excluded from fitting, showing that the trace is informative but unused. The failure is concentrated at the richest evidence level: those decisions fall to chance, while decisions settled with lower-cost evidence remain correct, a split hidden by aggregate accuracy. The same chain can fail at other links in public environments. Successful physical identification therefore guarantees neither evidence use nor useful adaptation; evaluation should identify where the chain breaks rather than rely on recovery accuracy or aggregate performance alone.

</details>


### [56] [EditWM: Event-Decomposed World Modeling with Incremental Correction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22317)
*Junjie Yang,Qingwei Zeng,Youyou Li,Zicheng Ding,Ziyi Shi,Shuqi Shen,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EditWM 世界模型，将未来预测分解为「常规演化 + 事件驱动增量修正」，在紧凑视觉特征空间中以门控修正解码器实现选择性校正，并用于轨迹评分，在 NAVSIM 上取得 91.05 EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶动态的可预测性存在差异，常规演化与事件引发的偏离应被区别对待，后者需要选择性修正；现有世界模型未显式区分二者，因而难以对关键偏离进行针对性建模。

Method: 在紧凑视觉特征空间中，用轨迹条件化的常规预测器给出基础未来特征并冻结；修正解码器对比该预测与观测历史、规划动作，输出有界特征增量，并通过学习门控调节其贡献；修正后的未来特征通过候选特定的交叉注意力参与轨迹评分。推理时仅使用历史与当前观测、自车状态和候选轨迹。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest 全部 12,146 个场景中，以专家轨迹为条件，未来特征 MSE 相比 Normal 基线下降 5.35%，83.54% 的场景有改善；使用官方 EPDMS 评估器获得 91.05 EPDMS（百分制）。

Conclusion: 显式分离常规演化与事件驱动修正，并将修正后的未来表征接入规划，可同时提升未来特征预测质量与轨迹选择性能，验证了选择性修正世界模型在自动驾驶中的有效性。

Abstract: World models support autonomous driving by predicting the scene evolution associated with candidate trajectories. Driving dynamics differ in predictability, motivating a distinction between regular evolution and event-induced deviations that call for selective correction. We propose EditWM, a world model that decomposes future prediction into normal evolution and event-driven incremental correction in compact visual feature space. A trajectory-conditioned normal predictor provides the base forecast and is then frozen for correction learning. A correction decoder compares this forecast with observation history and planned actions, producing a bounded feature update whose contribution is regulated by a learned gate. The corrected future features condition trajectory scoring through candidate-specific cross-attention, linking world modeling to plan selection. At inference, EditWM uses only past and current observations, ego state, and candidate trajectories. Across all 12,146 NAVSIM navtest scenes, expert-trajectory-conditioned evaluation shows a 5.35\% reduction in future-feature MSE over Normal, with improvements in 83.54\% of scenes. The system achieves 91.05 EPDMS on a 100-point scale using the official EPDMS evaluator. These results demonstrate improved future-feature prediction and competitive trajectory selection when corrected future representations are integrated into planning.

</details>


### [57] [Embedding Physics Priors in Robot Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22319)
*Mattia Piccinini,Lucas Schulze,Alice Plebe,Matteo Saveriano,Thomas Beckers,Yuan Gao,Oleg Arenz,Baha Zarrouki,Dingrui Wang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Jan Peters,Johannes Betz,Gastone Pietro Rosati Papini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了"物理嵌入机器人学习"这一领域，提出统一分类法（物理引导的输入/数据/表征、物理编码的模型架构、物理约束的训练损失），涵盖从单层感知机到生成式基础模型的方法及开源生态，并指出未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动方法在视觉与自然语言处理中表现优异，但机器人领域受限于数据稀缺、真实交互复杂以及可靠性要求，难以直接套用。物理先验可借助机器人特有的归纳偏置弥补数据不足，有望提升泛化性、可解释性与样本效率。然而现有文献在术语、方法与应用领域上高度碎片化，难以评估整体进展，故需系统综述。

Method: 采用综述方法：广泛覆盖多种物理先验、机器人应用与机器学习模型（从单层感知机到生成式基础模型）。核心是提出统一分类法，按物理嵌入方式划为三类——物理引导的输入、数据与表征；物理编码的模型架构；物理约束的训练损失函数。并据此对机器人动力学学习、轨迹规划、预测、控制与估计等方法及相关开源软件生态进行归纳。

Result: 建立了统一的分类框架，并据此整合了机器人动力学学习、轨迹规划、预测、控制与估计等任务上的现有方法，同时梳理了相应开源软件生态，识别出关键开放性挑战。

Conclusion: 物理先验是一种尤其契合机器人领域的专用归纳偏置，它补充而非取代数据驱动学习，有助于通向更具泛化性、数据高效且可信赖的机器人系统。文章还给出了若干有前景的未来研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid progress of artificial intelligence is reshaping robotics and accelerating the adoption of learning-based approaches. While purely data-driven methods have achieved remarkable success in computer vision and natural language processing, robotics remains constrained by limited data, complex real-world interactions, and the need for reliable operation. These challenges have motivated the exploration of physics-embedded robot learning, which embeds physics priors into learning algorithms. By encoding the underlying physical laws and constraints, physics priors can complement limited data with robotics-specific inductive biases, potentially improving generalization, interpretability, and sample efficiency. However, the literature on physics-embedded robot learning remains fragmented across terminology, methodologies, and application domains, making it difficult to assess this growing body of work. This survey reviews physics-embedded robot learning across a broad range of physics priors, robotics applications, and machine learning models, from single-layer perceptrons to generative foundation models. We adopt a unified taxonomy that classifies existing approaches according to their physics embedding: physics-guided inputs, data, and representations; physics-encoded model architectures; and physics-informed training loss functions. Building on this taxonomy, we review methods for robot dynamics learning, trajectory planning, prediction, control, and estimation, together with the corresponding open-source software ecosystem. We identify key open challenges, and outline promising future research directions. Overall, we argue that physics priors provide a particularly relevant robotics-specific inductive bias, complementing rather than replacing data-driven learning, and paving the way toward more generalizable, data-efficient, and trustworthy robotic systems.

</details>


### [58] [ReliCAD: From Uncertain LLM Generation to Reliable Parametric CAD Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22325)
*Peng Zheng,Xintong Dong,Chuanyang Li,Jiaxin Jing,Chuqi Han,Hailong Shen,Yanzhi Song,Zhouwang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReliCAD框架，将LLM的概率性生成转化为可靠的参数化CAD建模，实现99.8%有效性率和0.8753 IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自然语言驱动的参数化CAD建模中潜力巨大，但其概率性生成与CAD建模的确定性需求存在根本矛盾，导致可靠性、设计意图保持和几何有效性受限。现有方法依赖大规模标注数据、缺乏对设计意图的显式建模，且未充分利用CAD内核的确定性能力。

Method: ReliCAD通过显式设计意图建模，将用户需求转换为结构化设计规范，显式建模几何关系、拓扑依赖和特征构建顺序；生成约束感知的参数化指令，并通过Agent-ready API调用CAD内核执行几何构建与约束求解；同时记录运行时证据，采用验证-反馈机制评估生成模型与设计规范的一致性，实现错误定位与迭代修复。

Result: 在公开HistCAD生成数据集及自建多粒度CAD编辑数据集上，ReliCAD显著优于基线方法，达到99.8%有效性率和0.8753 IoU。

Conclusion: ReliCAD为自然语言交互式CAD建模提供了一种可验证、可修复且可泛化的方法。

Abstract: Large language models have shown considerable potential for natural-language-driven parametric CAD modeling. However, a fundamental contradiction exists between their probabilistic generation and the deterministic requirements of CAD modeling, resulting in limitations in reliability, design-intent preservation, and geometric validity. Existing methods typically rely on large-scale annotated datasets, lack explicit modeling of design intent, and underutilize the deterministic capabilities of CAD kernels. To address these limitations, we propose ReliCAD, a unified framework that transforms uncertain LLM generation into reliable parametric CAD modeling. Through explicit design-intent modeling, ReliCAD converts user requirements into structured design specifications and explicitly models geometric relations, topological dependencies, and feature construction order. It then generates constraint-aware parametric instructions and invokes the CAD kernel through an Agent-ready API to perform geometric construction and constraint solving. ReliCAD further records runtime evidence and employs a verification-feedback mechanism to assess consistency between the generated model and the design specifications, enabling error localization and iterative repair. Experiments on the public HistCAD generation dataset and our multi-granularity CAD editing dataset demonstrate that ReliCAD significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving 99.8\% validity rate and 0.8753 IoU. ReliCAD provides a verifiable, repairable, and generalizable approach to natural-language-interactive CAD modeling.

</details>


### [59] [AffordanceWAM: Affordance-Aware Joint World-Action Modeling for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22332)
*Jiadi You,Qize Yu,Yue Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhide Zhong,Yuran Wang,Bowen Ping,Jiaqi Liang,Zhenhao Shen,Haodong Yan,Yinchuan Li,Ruihai Wu,Xiaojuan Qi,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AffordanceWAM，一种具备可供性感知能力的生成式“世界-动作”模型，通过以物体为中心的时空可供性（标量可供性 + 可供性热力图）统一预测未来世界状态与机器人动作，实现无需人类动作标签与重定向的人到机器人迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作需要预测场景演化、识别可行交互位置并决定动作。带动作标注的机器人视频可直接监督控制，但采集成本高、多样性有限；第一人称人类视频交互多样，却缺少机器人动作且存在具身与外观差异。因此需要一个能弥合两者差距的共享表征与训练范式。

Method: AffordanceWAM 将可供性表示为物体中心的时空表征，包含 Scalar Affordance（标量可供性）与 Affordance Heatmap（可供性热力图），并将其嵌入生成的未来世界。模型基于预训练视频扩散 Transformer，采用独立参数化的 World Expert 与 Action Expert，通过 Masked Joint Self-Attention 耦合，在统一的 flow-matching 目标下联合预测未来 RGB 观测、标量可供性场、可供性热力图与连续机器人动作。人类视频监督三条未来世界流，机器人轨迹额外提供动作监督，从而实现无需人类动作标签或重定向的迁移。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、CALVIN ABC→D 及真实世界操作任务上，方法相较仅 RGB 与仅机器人数据基线取得一致性提升。在机器人监督固定的条件下，RoboCasa 性能随可供性标注人类视频规模增加而单调提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明可供性可作为视觉-语言-动作学习与人到机器人迁移的有效接口。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires predicting how a scene will evolve, identifying where interactions are feasible, and determining how to act. Action-labeled robot videos directly supervise control but are costly and limited in diversity, whereas egocentric human videos capture diverse interactions but lack robot actions and differ in embodiment and appearance. We introduce AffordanceWAM, an affordance-aware generative World Action Model that represents object-centric spatiotemporal affordance through Scalar Affordance and Affordance Heatmap, within the generated future World. This representation grounds visual prediction in task-relevant objects and interaction regions for action generation, and provides shared interaction targets across human and robot videos. Built on a pretrained video diffusion Transformer, AffordanceWAM uses separately parameterized World and Action Experts, coupled through Masked Joint Self-Attention, to jointly predict future RGB observations, Scalar Affordance fields, Affordance Heatmaps, and continuous robot actions under a unified flow-matching objective. Human videos supervise all three future-World streams, whereas robot trajectories additionally provide action supervision, enabling transfer without human action labels or retargeting. Experiments on RoboCasa, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over RGB-only and robot-data-only baselines. Under fixed robot supervision, RoboCasa performance improves monotonically as affordance-annotated human video scales. These results support affordance as an effective interface for both vision-language-action learning and human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [60] [Active Spatial Inspection for Effective and Efficient Embodied Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22385)
*Wenbin Wang,Xiang Bai,Yizhao Wang,Hang Sun,Dong Ren,Jie Qin,Qingquan Li,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间显式决策的ACE框架（证据锚定感知+暴露知情移动），在导航与问答探索任务上显著提升成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身探索框架通过空间粗糙且间接的提示线索与方向评估来引导智能体，难以判断线索充分性与是否需要继续检查，导致过早终止或不必要延续，降低任务成功率与效率。

Method: 从空间显式视角重构具身探索，提出ACE框架：将证据锚定感知与暴露知情移动耦合，建立空间解析决策范式。证据锚定感知结合细粒度定位与聚焦验证，把提示线索转化为精确的决定性视觉支撑；暴露知情移动通过前瞻性优先级排序与回溯性抑制实现方向判断，选择有前景的方向以高效推进。

Result: 大量实验表明，ACE相较此前SOTA基线在导航任务成功率上提升18.0%，在问答探索效率上提升10.3%。

Conclusion: ACE缓解了“防止过早终止”与“避免不必要延续”之间的张力，推动了高效且有效的具身探索。

Abstract: Achieving high task success and efficiency remains a central pursuit in embodied exploration. Existing frameworks typically guide agent behavior through a spatially coarse and indirect assessment of suggestive cues and directions, yet such designs may struggle to judge cue sufficiency and the need for further inspection, leading to early termination or excessive continuation and ultimately reducing task success and efficiency. This work rethinks embodied exploration from a spatially explicit standpoint and introduces the spatial-inspection-guided ACtive Exploration (ACE) framework coupling evidence-grounded perception with exposure-informed movement and establishing a spatially resolved decision paradigm. Evidence-grounded perception strengthens inferential validity by integrating granular localization with focused verification, turning suggestive cues into precise decisive visual support. Exposure-informed movement promotes directional judgment through prospective prioritization and retrospective suppression, selecting promising directions for efficient spatial progress. ACE alleviates the tension between preventing early termination and avoiding unnecessary continuation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE achieves 18.0% higher navigation task success and 10.3% higher exploration efficiency for question answering than prior state-of-the-art baselines, advancing effective and efficient embodied exploration.

</details>


### [61] [Behavior Trees for Robotic Systems: An Empirical Study on Practices and Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22404)
*Razan Ghzouli,Jennifer Horkoff,Daniel Strüber,Rebekka Wohlrab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过混合方法研究（汽车公司技术行动研究+34名机器人从业者调查），揭示行为树（BT）在机器人系统中的实际应用现状、价值与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为树已成为机器人系统任务协调的主流行为模型，但缺乏来自学术与工业界从业者的实证证据。证据匮乏导致研究社区可能开发出理论合理却与实际挑战脱节的指南和工具，并催生临时性实践，阻碍软件复用、维护与演化。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：在一家汽车公司开展技术行动研究，并对34名机器人从业者进行问卷调查。

Result: 行为树改善了团队沟通和机器人决策逻辑的可理解性，体现其超越任务协调的实用价值。但从业者面临诸多非平凡的设计与集成决策，因缺乏足够指南和工具支持而更复杂，涉及架构与语言选择及ROS集成（报告了观察到的模式）。此外还面临多因素粒度决策和现有库的混合体验：最常用BT库存在实现挑战与文档缺口；规划算法中节点排序决策困难；可扩展性看法尚无定论。

Conclusion: 当前库与实践的局限阻碍了行为树的更广泛采用。作者提出跨领域观察结论，为采用行为树的从业者和致力于推进BT采用实证理解的研究者提供启示。

Abstract: Over the last decade, behavior trees (BT) have become one of the dominant behavior models for coordinating missions of robotic systems. Yet empirical evidence on BT adoption in real-world contexts remains limited, especially regarding practitioners experiences and practices. Without practitioner-grounded evidence from both academic and industrial settings, the research community risks developing guidelines and tools that are plausible in principle but only partly aligned with real challenges. This scarcity of evidence leads to ad-hoc practices, impeding software reuse, maintenance, and evolution. This paper reports a mixed-methods study combining a technical action research investigation at an automotive company with a survey of 34 robotics practitioners. Our results indicate that BTs improve team communication and the understandability of robotic decision-making logic, reflecting BTs practical value beyond mission coordination. At the same time, practitioners face multiple non-trivial design and integration decisions, complicated by the lack of adequate guidelines and tool support. These decisions span architectural and language choices when implementing BTs and integrating them within ROS, for which we report observed patterns. Additionally, practitioners faced multi-factor granularity decisions and mixed experiences with current libraries. For the most used BT libraries, they reported implementation challenges and documentation gaps. Regarding planning algorithms, practitioners find deciding optimal BT node ordering challenging. Views on scalability remained inconclusive, and limitations in current libraries and practices hinder broader adoption. We conclude with cross-cutting observations and implications for both practitioners adopting BTs in robotic systems and researchers aiming to advance empirical understanding of BT adoption.

</details>


### [62] [VLPSA: Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions for Full-Body Safety of Learned Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22462)
*Meg Wilkinson,Emily Fourney,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLPSA 是一种无需重训练的安全滤波框架，通过在线合成泊松安全函数构建 CBF-QP，实现对 VLA 策略的全身实时避障，将碰撞规避率从 23.1% 提升至 91.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽提升了机器人操作的通用性，但其学习策略不提供碰撞规避的安全保证，尤其在训练分布之外的环境中安全性难以保障。

Method: 从感知数据在线合成泊松安全函数（PSF），导出控制屏障函数（CBF），并通过 CBF-QP 安全滤波器在全身及视为末端连杆延伸的抓取物体上施加约束；同时利用布尔 CBF 组合将 PSF 的双分辨率融合，在保证关键任务区域空间分辨率的同时实现实时部署。

Result: 在 SafeLIBERO 上对比安全滤波基线，取得最高碰撞规避率：将基础 π0.5 策略的避障率从 23.1% 提升至 91.2%，且任务成功率超过基线；并在 Franka FR3 上于含动态障碍与人为干扰的杂乱场景中完成部署，验证了操作任务中的实时全身安全。

Conclusion: VLPSA 证明无需重训练即可为 VLA 策略提供全身、实时、适用于杂乱与动态环境的安全保证，兼顾避障性能与任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable increasingly general-purpose robotic manipulation, but such learned policies do not provide safety guarantees for collision avoidance---especially in environments outside of training distributions. This work presents Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions (VLPSA), a safety filtering framework that provides full-body safety for VLA policies in cluttered and dynamic environments without retraining. VLPSA synthesizes Poisson Safety Functions (PSF) online from perception data, yielding a Control Barrier Function (CBF) that is enforced through a CBF-QP safety filter over the full body and any grasped object, treated as an extension of the final robot link. To enable real-time deployment while maintaining fine spatial resolution in critical task regions, VLPSA combines dual resolutions of this PSF using Boolean CBF compositions. We evaluate VLPSA on SafeLIBERO against safety-filtering baselines, where it achieves the highest collision avoidance rate among the evaluated methods, increasing collision avoidance from 23.1% for the base $π_{0.5}$ policy to 91.2% while surpassing its task success rate. We further deploy VLPSA on a Franka FR3 in cluttered scenes with dynamic obstacles and human interference, demonstrating real-time full-body safety during manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [63] [Tracker-Free Robotic Ultrasound Calibration with a Spherical-Marker Phantom and Threshold-Free Center Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22483)
*Kyoungmo Koo,Guangshen Ma,Xueding Wang,Mark Draelos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需外部跟踪器的机器人超声标定框架，利用球形标记体模与全自动感知流程实现无阈值球心定位，单标记标定精度与三标记相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声标定需准确关联US图像与机器人坐标系，但现有方法依赖复杂体模或外部3D跟踪器，难以实现准确且自动化的标定。

Method: 采用球形标记体模与高度自动化的球心定位感知流程；提出无阈值图像处理方法，依据每幅US图像的灰度分布定位球形特征，免除手工调节强度阈值；利用多姿态观测估计US到末端执行器(EE)的变换，无需外部跟踪或机器人基座坐标系中球心的先验定位。

Result: 在12折交叉验证中，单标记标定的球心精度与重复性为1.75±0.51 mm和0.85±0.11 mm，三标记为1.72±0.51 mm和0.84±0.12 mm；三组重建中，单标记标定的注册后点到面MAE±SD为圆锥0.41±0.35 mm、三棱柱0.50±0.41 mm，三标记为0.40±0.35 mm和0.48±0.40 mm。

Conclusion: 该无跟踪器框架在机器人超声平台上通过重复球体扫描及已知CAD模型的几何相异体模验证，单标记标定性能与三标记相当，且无需重新调参即可适应不同成像设置。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound (US) calibration is essential for accurately relating US images to the robot coordinate system, but accurate and automated calibration remains challenging because existing methods often require complex phantoms or external 3D trackers. In this work, we develop a tracker-free robotic US calibration framework using a spherical-marker phantom and a highly automated perception pipeline for sphere-center localization. The proposed threshold-free image-processing method localizes the spherical feature based on each US image's intensity distribution, eliminating hand-tuned intensity thresholds and improving robustness across imaging settings without per-system retuning. Multi-pose observations of the spherical fiducial are then used to estimate the US-to-EE transformation without external tracking or prior localization of the sphere center in the robot base frame. We validate the proposed framework on a robotic US platform through repeated sphere scans and further assess the calibrated system using geometrically distinct phantoms with known CAD models. Across 12 cross-validation folds, single-marker calibration achieved sphere-center accuracy and precision of $1.75\pm0.51$ mm and $0.85\pm0.11$ mm, respectively, compared with $1.72\pm0.51$ mm and $0.84\pm0.12$ mm for three-marker calibration. Across the three reconstruction sets, the single-marker calibration yielded pooled post-registration point-to-surface MAE$\pm$SD values of $0.41\pm0.35$ mm for the cone and $0.50\pm0.41$ mm for the triangular prism, closely matching the three-marker results of $0.40\pm0.35$ mm and $0.48\pm0.40$ mm, respectively.

</details>


### [64] [Layered e-skin for Shear Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22493)
*Qingzheng Cong,Alexis W. M. Devillard,Abu Bakar Dawood,Xinxin Zhang,Wen Fan,Neri Niccolò Dei,Cem Suulker,Kaspar Althoefer,Etienne Burdet,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于机器人指尖的双层堆叠FSR阵列，通过层间压力中心位移实现剪切力估计，并结合CNN-GRU模型实现三轴力感知、接触运动跟踪与滑移事件检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FSR阵列主要用于法向力感知，难以提供剪切力、接触运动与滑移信息，而这对于机器人精细操作至关重要。作者希望在不显著增加硬件复杂度的情况下，扩展FSR阵列的感知能力。

Method: 设计双层堆叠FSR阵列，中间加入柔性点阵弹性体隔离层，将剪切载荷转化为层间位移并引起两层压力中心（CoP）的相对偏移；用基于物理的力矩平衡建立层间CoP位移与剪切力的关系，同时采用端到端CNN-GRU模型以两层信号为输入，建模负载相关压缩与接触重分布等非线性效应；并开展机器人操作实验验证。

Result: 双层输入模型对剪切力估计达到 R²=0.914（Fx）与 R²=0.944（Fy），持续优于单层基线。接触运动跟踪中，深层跟踪机械臂施加的平移与旋转，浅层跟踪接触面滑移；两层总压力响应差值的瞬态变化在滑移事件检测中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 双层FSR阵列可超越传统法向力感知，实现三轴力估计、接触运动跟踪和滑移事件检测，为机器人指尖提供更丰富的触觉感知能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a stacked two-layer force-sensing resistor (FSR) array designed for robotic fingertips that combines high-resolution pressure mapping with shear-force estimation. A compliant lattice elastomer spacer converts shear loading into a measurable inter-layer displacement, producing relative center-of-pressure (CoP) shifts between layers. A physics-based moment balance links inter-layer CoP displacement to shear force, while an end-to-end CNN--GRU model captures nonlinear effects from load-dependent compression and contact redistribution. This model, with both layers as input, achieves coefficients of determination $R^2 = 0.914$ for $F_x$ and $R^2 = 0.944$ for $F_y$, consistently outperforming single-layer baselines for shear-force estimation. Robotic manipulation experiments show that, for contact-motion tracking, the deep layer tracks the translation and rotation imposed by the robot arm, whereas the superficial layer tracks the slip at the contact surface. Transient changes in the difference between the total pressure responses of the two layers provide the best slip-event detection performance among the tested cues. These results demonstrate that two-layer FSR arrays can provide three-axis force estimation, contact-motion tracking, and slip-event detection beyond conventional normal-force sensing.

</details>


### [65] [Latent Policy Steering: An Efficient and Flexible Framework for Cross-Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22521)
*Yiqi Wang,Mrinal Verghese,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Latent Policy Steering（LPS）框架，通过在世界模型潜空间搜索动作实现跨具身迁移，仅需少量目标具身演示即可显著提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉运动策略性能依赖大规模高质量演示数据，但真实机器人数据采集成本高昂；已有的机器人与人类数据集存在具身差异和动作空间不匹配，难以直接利用。因此需要跨具身迁移，即复用其他具身经验来提升目标具身的学习效果，从而突破单一机器人数据采集的规模瓶颈。

Method: LPS包含具身无关的预训练阶段：在多样具身上用光流训练基于图像的世界模型（WM），捕捉跨具身共享的视觉动力学，即世界对运动的响应；随后用目标具身的机器人动作对WM进行微调；在测试时通过在WM潜空间中搜索既接近微调数据又能引导基础策略趋向更优动作的规划，实现策略引导。该框架与策略无关，可灵活适配不同策略而无需重新训练。

Result: 在Robomimic和真实世界评估中，仅使用50条未见目标具身的演示，LPS将Diffusion Policy平均性能相对提升16%（仿真）和62%（真实世界），将Pi0.5相对提升8%（仿真）和14%（真实世界）。

Conclusion: 跨具身迁移可通过学习跨具身共享的视觉动力学，并有效利用测试时稀缺的目标具身数据来实现；LPS作为策略无关框架，能在极少目标具身演示下显著提升多种现有策略的性能，为超越单机器人数据收集的机器人学习规模化提供有效路径。

Abstract: The performance of learned robot visuomotor policies depends heavily on the size and quality of their training data, yet collecting high-quality demonstrations remains costly for robots in the real world. Although large-scale robot and human datasets are increasingly available, embodiment gaps and mismatched action spaces make them difficult to leverage directly. Cross-embodiment transfer, reusing experience from other embodiments to improve learning on a target embodiment, is therefore crucial for scaling robot learning beyond per-robot data collection.
  In this work, we find that efficient transfer can be achieved by learning from what is shared across embodiments, the visual dynamics of how the world responds to motion, and by effectively exploiting the scarce target-embodiment data at test time. The proposed framework, called Latent Policy Steering (LPS), implements an embodiment-agnostic pretraining phase, which trains an image-based World Model (WM) with optical flow across diverse embodiments. The resulting WM is finetuned on the target embodiment with robot actions. It then steers the base policy toward better actions by searching in the WM's latent space for plans that stay close to the finetuning data. LPS is a policy-agnostic framework: it can flexibly accommodate different policies without having to retrain them. In Robomimic and real-world evaluations, LPS improves the average performance of Diffusion Policy relatively by 16% and 62%, and Pi0.5 by 8% and 14%, with only 50 demonstrations on an unseen target embodiment.

</details>


### [66] [FRAMES: Failure Recovery And Monitoring of Embodied Skills for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22538)
*Ajay Vikram Periasami,Xinyuan Luo,Haoyu Li,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRAMES 是一个面向 Unitree G1 人形机器人、位于 CEER 全身控制器之上的故障感知监督框架，通过规划器、监控器与恢复代理实现技能级执行监控，监控模块在 MuJoCo 的 100 次试验中达到 94.0% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 规划器虽能分解指令并选择可复用技能，但选对技能并不保证物理执行成功；在人形 loco-manipulation 中，接近、抓取、搬运或放置阶段的错误会导致整个长时程规划失效，因此需要执行层面的故障感知与监督。

Method: FRAMES 运行于 CEER 全身控制器之上：Planner Agent 通过参数化中层技能选择子任务；基于 VLM 的 Monitor Agent 利用时序多视角观测与结构化机器人及接触证据评估每个技能；检测到故障时终止当前技能并向 Recovery Agent 提供有依据的反馈；框架还包含复用历史技能经验的 Memory Module，以及基于深度与分割的几何接地。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 中对监控模块独立评估，涵盖 5 个任务的 100 次试验（50 次失败、50 次成功）；监控器检出 48/50 次失败，正确接受 46/50 次成功执行，总体准确率为 94.0%。

Conclusion: 结果为监控组件提供了初步证据，完整恢复闭环的端到端评估仍在进行中。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) planners can decompose natural-language instructions and select reusable robot skills, but choosing the correct skill does not guarantee successful physical execution. This gap is especially important in humanoid loco-manipulation, where errors during approach, grasping, transport, or placement can invalidate the remainder of a long-horizon plan. We present FRAMES, a failure-aware supervisory framework for the Unitree G1 humanoid that operates above the CEER whole-body controller. A Planner Agent selects subtasks through parameterized mid-level skills, while a vision-language-model-based Monitor Agent evaluates each skill using temporal multi-view observations and structured robot and contact evidence. Detected failures stop the active skill and provide grounded feedback to a Recovery Agent. The framework further includes a Memory Module for reusing prior skill experience, and geometric grounding via depth and segmentation. We independently evaluate the monitoring module of the framework in MuJoCo using 100 trials comprising 50 failed and 50 successful executions across five tasks. The monitor detects 48 of 50 failures, correctly accepts 46 of 50 successful executions, and achieves 94.0% overall accuracy. These results provide initial evidence for the monitoring component, while end-to-end evaluation of the complete recovery loop remains ongoing.

</details>


### [67] [React When You Need To: Event-Triggered Asynchronous Inference for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22587)
*Yansong Wu,Huaqing Li,Tianding Hou,Lingyun Chen,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略，根据场景变化自适应调整推理间隔，兼顾运动一致性与实时响应，在静态和动态真实场景中平均成功率95%，超最强基线55个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常预测动作块，限制了执行期间对环境变化的响应能力；已有异步推理方法虽提升了响应性，但依赖固定的推理间隔，无法兼顾运动一致性与及时反应。

Method: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略，基于上一次推理以来观察到的场景变化，自适应地调整推理间隔。

Result: 在静态和动态真实场景中均表现最佳，平均成功率95%，超过最强基线55个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过按场景变化动态调节推理间隔，该方法同时保持运动一致性与快速响应，验证了动态推理策略在真实环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly predict action chunks, limiting their ability to react to environmental changes during execution. Existing asynchronous inference methods improve reactivity but typically rely on a fixed inference gap. In this paper, we propose an event-guided dynamic inference strategy that adapts the inference gap according to scene changes observed since the previous inference. Thereby, it simultaneously preserves motion consistency and prompt reactivity. Across static and dynamic real-world settings, our method consistently performs best, averaging 95% success and exceeding the strongest baseline by 55 percentage points. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance. The project page is available at https://react-when-you-need-to.github.io/.

</details>


### [68] [REBOOT: From Failure to Recovery - A Dataset and Benchmark for Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22591)
*Nana Yaw Owusu Ofori-Ampofo,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Joseph Thekinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 REBOOT，首个以失败为一等信号的机器人操作基准，覆盖18个精密装配任务、2160条演示，含专家与恢复轨迹，支持阶段级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习数据集多由成功演示构成，真实基准又常将性能简化为二值成功率，导致既缺乏恢复监督，也无法定位失败发生在何处。亟需一个以失败为核心的基准来支撑恢复学习与细粒度失败分析。

Method: 构建 REBOOT，18个精密装配任务，每个分解为 Align(pick)、Engage(pick)、Transport、Align(place)、Engage(place) 五个共享阶段；在各阶段注入失败，并配对专家恢复轨迹使系统回到有效可续状态；标注旋转对称性、啮合间隙精度等级及安装方向（通过配对安装-移除）；失败片段标注阶段与类别化失败模式；数据含四视角同步 RGB-D 与阶段级成败条件的自然语言描述；一半为专家演示，另一半为按模仿学习策略 rollout 中观测到的失败采样的恢复演示。评估 action-chunked transformer、diffusion、π0-FAST 策略的阶段级完成率。

Result: 基准揭示此前被二值成功率掩盖的模型专属失败点：不同策略在阶段级完成率上表现各异，可归因到具体运动学阶段与容差违规。

Conclusion: REBOOT 将失败提升为一等信号，提供阶段级评估与恢复监督，为分析机器人操作策略的失败机理与提升鲁棒性提供新基准。

Abstract: Robot learning policies fail in characteristic ways: they stall in uncertain states, drift during contact-rich alignment, and miss targets by millimetres in precision tasks. Yet training datasets consist largely of successful demonstrations, while real-world benchmarks often reduce performance to binary success. This limits both supervision for recovery and analysis of where failures occur. We introduce REBOOT (Recovery Episode Benchmark for Off-nominal Trajectories), the first robot manipulation benchmark designed around failure as a first-class signal. REBOOT contains 2,160 demonstrations across 18 precision assembly tasks, each decomposed into five shared phases: Align(pick), Engage(pick), Transport, Align(place), and Engage(place), enabling phase-level evaluation beyond terminal success.
