The camera sees only the robot's motion relative to the elevator, while the IMU senses that motion plus the elevator's motion relative to the world. This conflict is challenging for existing visual-inertial estimators. If vision dominates, the estimator tracks only the robot's motion within the elevator and misses the elevator's rise, and if the conflict remains, the estimator diverges. We observe that the conflict comes from forcing both observations into a single coordinate frame. We instead estimate the robot's pose in the elevator's coordinate frame, and the elevator's motion relative to the world as a per-keyframe transport state, the elevator's rise and vertical velocity, within dense visual-inertial bundle adjustment. Elevator-VIGS detects rides zero-shot with a vision-language model and a depth network, and constrains the transport state at the departure and the arrival. We record real-world and simulated elevator sequences. On these sequences, Elevator-VIGS achieves state-of-the-art tracking and rendering performance. On four elevator-free public benchmarks it keeps the state-of-the-art performance of VIGS-SLAM. Project page: https://ruizhou-cn.github.io/elevator-vigs/.

</details>


### [138] [Imagine then Verify: Affordance-Targeted Active Perception for Task-Oriented Grasping in Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23504)
*Jingzhi Cui,Xuefeng Liu,Feng Han,Xinyu Liu,Wei Chen,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATAP是一个面向任务抓取的主动感知框架，将视点规划从"全物体扫描"转变为"目标化可供性验证"：借助生成式形状先验想象被遮挡的目标几何并预测可供性分布，再用不确定性感知的视点规划器联合优化熵减与可供性验证增益，迭代直到可供性被充分确认，从而显著提升功能抓取成功率并减少NBV步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向抓取需要抓取物体的功能部位（如倒水时抓杯柄），但这些可供性区域在杂乱场景中常被遮挡。现有NBV方法在规划视点时把目标物体当作整体，不区分任务相关部位；若简单改为完整扫描目标物体后再预测可供性，会把大部分视点预算浪费在任务无关表面（如倒水时的杯身）上。

Method: 提出ATAP（Affordance-Targeted Active Perception）框架：1）用生成式形状先验对被遮挡的目标几何进行假设，并在想象出的完整表面上预测可供性分布；2）针对杂乱场景中严重遮挡导致隐藏可供性位置存在多解的问题，设计不确定性感知的视点规划器，联合优化对竞争假设的期望熵减与真实观测带来的期望可供性验证增益；3）迭代上述过程直至可供性被充分验证后执行抓取。

Result: 在仿真与真实杂乱场景实验中，ATAP相比固定视点的任务导向抓取基线大幅提升功能抓取成功率，并且相比基于重建的主动感知方法减少了超过57%的NBV步数。

Conclusion: 以可供性验证为目标的主动感知可在杂乱遮挡场景中高效定位任务相关部位，兼顾抓取成功率与感知效率，优于固定视角抓取与重建驱动的主动感知基线。

Abstract: Task-oriented grasping (TOG) requires robots to grasp functional parts of objects (e.g., the handle of a mug for pouring), yet these affordance regions are frequently occluded in cluttered scenes. Active perception via next-best-view (NBV) planning can resolve such occlusions by moving the camera for more informative observations. However, existing NBV methods typically optimize viewpoints for grasping the target object as a whole without distinguishing which part is task-relevant. A naive adaptation, fully scanning the target object before predicting the affordance, wastes most of the viewpoint budget on task-irrelevant surfaces (e.g., the mug body for pouring). To address this, we propose ATAP, an Affordance-Targeted Active Perception framework that shifts viewpoint planning from exhaustive target scanning to targeted affordance verification. ATAP hypothesizes the occluded target geometry via a generative shape prior and predicts the affordance distribution over the imagined complete surface. In cluttered scenes, severe occlusion can make the location of the hidden affordance ambiguous, leaving multiple locations plausible given the partial observation. ATAP therefore introduces an uncertainty-aware viewpoint planner that jointly optimizes expected entropy reduction over these competing hypotheses and expected affordance verification gain from real observations. This process iterates until the affordance is sufficiently verified for grasp execution. Experiments in simulation and real-world cluttered scenes show that ATAP substantially improves the functional grasp success rate over fixed-view TOG baselines, and outperforms reconstruction-based active perception with over 57% fewer NBV steps.

</details>


### [139] [PINGU: Extending Air-Bearing Spacecraft Emulators with Open-Source Actuators and Learned Control for Contact-Rich Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23554)
*Ricard Marsal I Castan,Akiyoshi Uchida,Aman Arora,Pedro Lima,Matteo El-Hariry,Anrej Orsula,Francesco Grella,Antoine Richard,Cedric Pradalier,Miguel A. Olivarez-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出PINGU系统，在开源ATMOS平面气浮平台上集成反作用轮与两条力/力矩感知机械臂（LEVION），通过统一ROS 2抽象层将新增执行器纳入控制体系，并构建强化学习训练环境与数字孪生。在四项基准任务（点到位姿导航、机械臂质心偏移下的扰动抑制、反作用轮动量稳定、力控对接）上验证了经典最优控制与学习策略可无修改互换，将ATMOS级仿真器扩展至接触丰富的操作领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本平面气浮台已成为自由飞行航天器GNC的常用代理平台，但多数仅配备推力器，缺乏面向接触丰富、惯量耦合操作的能力。作者希望弥补这一空白，使气浮平台能够支撑接触操作与多执行器耦合控制研究，并在同一硬件上打通经典最优控制与强化学习两条技术路线。

Method: 基于开源ATMOS测试台进行扩展：增加一个反作用轮与两条带力/力矩传感的LEVION机械臂，末端执行器可互换；通过统一的ROS 2抽象层将其集成为一等控制执行器。在软件栈之上构建强化学习训练环境与数字孪生，并设计可充分利用新增自由度的控制器，使经典最优控制器与学习策略能在同一硬件上无修改替换。

Result: 在四项基准任务上进行验证：1）点到位姿导航，对比经典LQR与sim-to-real PPO；2）机械臂引起质心偏移下的动态扰动抑制；3）反作用轮动量稳定；4）力控对接。结果表明新增组件将ATMOS级仿真器拓展至接触丰富区间，并实现经典最优控制与强化学习的统一验证。

Conclusion: PINGU作为一个可复现的集成平台，证明在低成本平面气浮测试台上可以同时支持接触丰富操作、惯量耦合动力学与多执行器协同控制，并实现经典最优控制与强化学习策略的即插即用切换，为航天器GNC研究提供更通用的地面验证手段。

Abstract: Low-cost planar air-bearing testbeds have matured into a standard proxy for free-flying spacecraft GNC, but they remain largely thruster-only and are rarely equipped for contact-rich, inertia-coupled manipulation. Building on the open-source ATMOS testbed, we contribute a reaction wheel and two force/torque-sensed robotic arms (LEVION) with interchangeable end-effectors, integrated as first-class control actuators through a unified ROS 2 abstraction layer. On top of the software stack we build a reinforcement-learning training environment and digital twin, and a controller that exploits these added degrees of freedom, letting classical optimal controllers and learned policies be swapped on the same hardware without modification. We validate the integrated system, PINGU, across four benchmark tasks: point-to-pose navigation (classical LQR vs. sim-to-real PPO), dynamic disturbance rejection under arm-induced center-of-mass shifts, reaction-wheel momentum stabilization, and force-controlled docking. The results show that these additions extend an ATMOS-class emulator into the contact-rich regime and bridge classical optimal control and reinforcement learning on one reproducible platform.

</details>


### [140] [AR-WAM: A Visual-Conditioned Agent-Ready World Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23578)
*Yicheng Jiang,Zesen Gan,Xiaobo Wang,Tianlun He,Chenxu Zhao,Minghui Wu,Xinyue Wang,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Jianan Wang,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AR-WAM，一个视觉条件、面向智能体的世界动作模型，用视觉接地提示（目标框）和可学习操作令牌替代自然语言指令，0.5B参数，在标准操作任务上匹配最强基线，在记忆依赖和真实长程任务上表现更优，推理延迟最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型和世界动作模型（WAMs）依赖自然语言指令指定操作任务，而语言作为智能体驱动的控制接口并不合适：存在指代歧义、空间不精确、与智能体自身语言理解冗余，且将意图与执行纠缠在一起。因此需要一种更适合智能体驱动的控制范式。

Method: 提出AR-WAM，以两种互补条件替代语言：(1)视觉接地提示，即目标物体的边界框，指示交互对象与位置；(2)可学习的操作令牌，指定要执行的原子技能。模型仅0.5B参数，使用冻结的预训练视觉编码器且无语言编码器，在紧凑的潜在状态中预测场景演化并解码动作，通过显式可监督的推理信号暴露策略意图。此外提供模型无关的兼容层，含detect、execute、query三个原语，使本地VLM或在线智能体API可直接驱动策略，长程记忆与闭环错误恢复交由智能体侧处理。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、RMBench及真实Astribot S1双臂平台上，AR-WAM在标准操作任务上匹配最强基线（平均成功率87.2%），在记忆依赖任务和真实机器人长程任务上分别提升5.9%和36.7%成功率，同时保持最低推理延迟14.1 ms。

Conclusion: 用视觉接地与操作令牌替代自然语言，可构建更紧凑、高效且面向智能体的世界动作模型，在保持标准任务性能的同时显著提升记忆与长程任务表现，并降低推理延迟，验证了视觉条件接口在智能体驱动机器人控制中的有效性。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable, agent-driven robotic control is emerging as a compelling paradigm. However, prevailing vision-language-action (VLA) models and world action models (WAMs) still rely on natural-language instructions to specify manipulation tasks, an ill-suited interface for agent-driven control: referentially ambiguous, spatially imprecise, redundant with the agent's inherent language understanding, and entangling intent with execution. We present AR-WAM, a visual-conditioned, agent-ready world action model that replaces language with two complementary conditions: a visual grounding prompt (a bounding box of the target) denoting the interaction object and location, and a learnable operation token dictating the atomic skill to execute. Our compact 0.5B-parameter model, with a frozen pretrained visual encoder and no language encoder, predicts scene evolution within compact latent states while decoding actions, exposing the policy's intent through explicit, supervisable reasoning signals. A model-agnostic compatibility layer provides three primitives (detect, execute, and query) so that local VLMs or online agent APIs can drive the policy directly, with long-horizon memory and closed-loop error recovery delegated to the agent side. On RoboTwin 2.0, RMBench, and a real Astribot S1 dual-arm platform, AR-WAM matches the strongest baselines on standard manipulation (87.2% average success) and outperforms them on memory-dependent and real-robot long-horizon tasks, improving success rates by 5.9% and 36.7%, respectively, while maintaining the lowest inference latency (14.1 ms).

</details>


### [141] [TaskAnchor: Grounding Task State in Reactive VLAs for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23580)
*Hengyan Liu,Wenlve Zhou,Bo Yue,Yongyi Su,Ruixiang Wang,Zhanqi Zhang,Dekun Lu,Wei Gao,Xiaofen Xing,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TaskAnchor——一种轻量适配器，通过引入执行历史缓解 VLA 模型的任务状态混叠问题，在多个基准上显著提升长程操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式视觉—语言—动作（VLA）模型在长程操作中表现不佳：视觉上相似的观测在不同任务阶段或交互历史下可能对应不同动作，作者将此歧义定义为"任务状态混叠"（task-state aliasing）。

Method: TaskAnchor 由两部分组成：一是历史条件化的视觉精炼，二是里程碑监督的任务状态坐标（一个表征执行语义阶段的标量）。两者分别通过原生视觉与语言接口注入，不引入显式规划器，也不修改动作生成机制。

Result: 在 RMBench 上，平均成功率约为已发表 π_{0.5} 与 X-VLA 基线的 4.9–5.5 倍；在 RoboMemArena 和真实机器人上也取得一致增益。对 π_{0.5}，每个动作块仅增加 2.08 ms 延迟。

Conclusion: 通过在执行历史中锚定预训练 VLA，可在几乎不增加计算开销、不改变原有架构的前提下有效解决任务状态混叠，显著提升长程操作的成功率与泛化性。

Abstract: Reactive vision--language--action (VLA) models struggle with long-horizon manipulation when visually similar observations can correspond to different actions depending on the task stage or interaction history. We refer to this ambiguity as task-state aliasing and introduce TaskAnchor, a lightweight adapter that grounds pretrained VLAs in execution history. TaskAnchor combines history-conditioned visual refinement with a milestone-supervised task-state coordinate, a scalar representing the semantic stage of execution. These signals are injected through the native visual and language interfaces, respectively, without introducing an explicit planner or modifying the action-generation mechanism. On RMBench, TaskAnchor achieves approximately 4.9--5.5$\times$ the average success rates of the published $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA baselines, with consistent gains on RoboMemArena and real robots. The added latency is only 2.08\,ms per action chunk for $π_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [142] [PRIMO: Prior-Informed Odometry from Human-Motion Tracking for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23610)
*Xu Han,Angsong Li,Shaopeng Zhang,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Yuan Zhuang,Haiyu Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PRIMO框架，通过人类动作跟踪生成训练数据并引入先验信息估计器，解决人形机器人本体感知里程计的仿真到现实迁移难题，实机平均误差较最强基线降低31.6%-61.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的人形机器人本体感知里程计面临两大迁移挑战：一是基于特定部署控制策略生成的训练轨迹覆盖的运动范围有限；二是仿真到现实（sim-to-real）差异使无约束预测不可靠。

Method: 数据层面，让仿真中的人形机器人跟踪多样化的重定向人类动作以生成里程计监督，使监督与部署策略解耦并扩展训练运动分布；模型层面，采用"先验知情"估计器，利用物理与对称性先验构建速度与旋转预测，并通过粗粒度原始上下文通路在编码特征之外保留传感器上下文，从而增强仿真到现实的泛化能力。

Result: 在统一的实机协议下，PRIMO在各域-指标对比中相较最强外部基线将平均误差降低31.6%-61.7%；跨两个运动策略版本，策略专家模型呈对称交叉现象，而Tracking-Locomotion训练使跨策略仿真平均误差降低86.8%-94.6%；在真实动态运动上相较两个部署策略并集训练降低平均误差69.2%-81.7%；先验知情估计器在仿真与实机中均一致优于无约束版本。

Conclusion: 通过将数据生成与部署策略解耦并引入物理/对称性先验，PRIMO有效缓解了训练分布受限与sim-to-real差异两大难题，在仿真与实机评测中均显著提升人形机器人本体感知里程计的精度与跨策略泛化性。

Abstract: Simulation-trained humanoid proprioceptive odometry faces two transfer challenges: training trajectories generated by specific robot control policies intended for deployment cover only a limited range of motions, while sim-to-real mismatch can make unconstrained predictions unreliable. We address both with Prior-Informed Odometry from Human-Motion Tracking (PRIMO). On the data side, we generate odometry supervision by having the humanoid track diverse retargeted human motions in simulation, decoupling supervision from the deployment policies and broadening the training motion distribution. On the model side, a Prior-Informed estimator uses physics- and symmetry-informed priors to structure velocity and rotation prediction and a coarse raw-context pathway to preserve sensor context alongside encoded features, thereby strengthening sim-to-real generalization. Under a unified real-robot protocol, PRIMO reduces mean error by 31.6%-61.7% relative to the strongest evaluated external baseline in each domain-metric comparison. Across two locomotion-policy revisions, policy specialists exhibit symmetric crossover, whereas Tracking-Locomotion training reduces mean opposite-policy simulation error by 86.8%-94.6%. On real dynamic motion, Tracking-Locomotion training reduces mean error by 69.2%-81.7% relative to training on the union of both deployment policies. Across the tested motion compositions, the Prior-Informed estimator consistently lowers mean trajectory errors relative to its Unconstrained counterpart in both simulation and real-robot evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/Agibot-Spatial-Intelligence/PRIMO.

</details>


### [143] [CompVLA: A Variable Compliance Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23614)
*Jongmin Kim,Junsu Ha,Che-Sang Park,Minchang Song,Hyeokju Jeong,Himchan Hwang,Jianlong Fu,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CompVLA，一个从RGB与语言输入联合预测运动与刚度矩阵的统一VLA框架，通过专用柔顺专家输出时变刚度与虚拟位移，结合几何阻抗控制，在接触丰富任务上取得最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作要求机器人不仅调节运动，还需调节对外部力的顺应方式，是VLA模型的下一个前沿。但现有VLA仅输出纯运动学指令，导致在真实世界接触丰富任务上性能下降，因此需要让VLA同时具备力/柔顺控制能力。

Method: 提出CompVLA统一框架，从RGB图像和语言输入联合预测运动与刚度矩阵。在传统架构基础上增加一个专用的柔顺专家（Compliance Expert），输出时变刚度与虚拟位移剖面，并通过几何阻抗控制执行。

Result: 在多种接触丰富任务上，CompVLA取得最高的平均成功率，优于普通VLA和具备柔顺感知能力的VLA基线；消融实验证实每个组件都是必要的。

Conclusion: 将刚度/柔顺预测纳入VLA输出可显著提升接触丰富操作表现，CompVLA为接触丰富任务提供了一种有效的统一运动与柔顺控制方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation, requiring robots to regulate not only motion but also how they yield to external forces, has emerged as the next frontier for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, existing VLAs output purely kinematic commands, degrading performance on real-world contact-rich tasks. In this paper, we introduce CompVLA, a unified VLA framework that jointly predicts motion and stiffness matrix from RGB and language inputs. Our approach augments the conventional architecture with a dedicated Compliance Expert, which outputs time-varying stiffness and virtual displacement profiles executed via geometric impedance control. We demonstrate that CompVLA achieves the highest average success rate across diverse contact-rich tasks, outperforming both vanilla and compliance-aware VLA baselines, with ablations confirming each component is essential.

</details>


### [144] [Spiking Neural Network Actor-Critic Proximal Policy Optimization Control for Autonomous UAV Navigation Through Constrained Openings in Civil Infrastructure and Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23643)
*Francis Noah Walugembe,Maciej Wielgosz,Tomaž Goričan,Matej Mertik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将脉冲神经网络（SNN）与近端策略优化（PPO）结合的算法，用于无人机在受限三维环境中的自主导航，以减少深度强化学习的计算开销，实验在3000余回合中成功完成1913回合，平均每回合通过2.10个窗口，成功率达63.77%，后期阶段成功率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在受限三维环境中的自主导航是机器人领域的难题，尤其应用于桥梁、隧道和结构等民用基础设施检测。深度强化学习虽在受限环境导航中取得成功，但其高计算成本限制了实际应用，因此需要降低计算开销的算法方案。

Method: 提出基于脉冲神经网络的近端策略优化算法（SNN-PPO），将基于脉冲的actor-critic强化学习与PPO算法相结合，并采用随机高斯策略，用于受限序列环境下无人机的自主导航，并在受限三维环境中实现。

Result: 算法在超过3000个回合中成功完成1913个回合，平均每回合通过2.10个窗口，总体成功率为63.77%，在算法后期阶段成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: 将脉冲神经网络与PPO结合可有效支持无人机在受限三维环境中的自主导航，并展现出后期性能显著提升的学习效果，为降低深度强化学习计算成本提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles in constrained three-dimensional environments has been a challenge in the robotics domain. The application of autonomous unmanned aerial vehicles in civil infrastructure inspection involves the use of such vehicles in bridge inspection, tunnel inspection, and structural inspection. The use of deep reinforcement learning in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles has been successful in constrained environments. However, the computational cost of the algorithm limits the application of the algorithm in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles. This paper proposes the use of the spiking neural network-based Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles in constrained sequential environments. The proposed algorithm integrates the use of spike-based actor-critic reinforcement learning with the Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm. The proposed algorithm uses the stochastic Gaussian policy in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles. The proposed algorithm was implemented in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles in constrained 3D environments. The proposed algorithm was successful in completing 1913 episodes out of more than 3000. The proposed algorithm was successful in passing an average of 2.10 windows per episode. The proposed algorithm was successful in achieving a success rate of 63.77%. The proposed algorithm was successful in achieving success rates of more than 90% in the later stages of the algorithm.

</details>


### [145] [Beyond Appearance Shifts: Task-Semantic Action Calibration for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23650)
*Shuaijun Liu,Feiyang You,Chengyu Wu,Shuyang Hao,Chenglong Zhang,Jingyao Cai,Xingwei Chen,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BAS-VLA，一个基于冻结VLA基座的任务语义动作校准框架，通过"破坏中心"校准核心与选择性证据门控的保持辅助机制，平衡行为稳定性与任务语义敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在具身操作中表现良好，但缺乏明确的机制来平衡行为稳定性与任务语义敏感性。存在两类互补的失败模式：任务保持变化下出现不必要的动作漂移，语义破坏变化下无法产生足够区分的行为而沿袭原轨迹。

Method: BAS-VLA构建于冻结的基座VLA之上，默认路径采用"破坏中心"的校准核心，并引入选择性证据门控的保持辅助，仅当检测到干扰变化而任务语义保持一致时激活。

Result: 在OpenPI-pi0.5 / LIBERO-Object Milk-Swap基准上，清洁条件成功率98.0%，语义保持条件97.5%，而在刻意目标物体替换下清洁准则成功率降至0.0%，显示强陈旧任务抑制；在风格保持偏移上成功率由42%提升至70%且不损害清洁性能。

Conclusion: 可靠的VLA行为需要超越外观鲁棒性，转向显式的任务语义动作校准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in embodied manipulation, but still lack a clear mechanism to balance behavioral stability with task-semantic sensitivity. We identify two complementary failure modes. Under task-preserving changes, where task semantics remain unchanged but scene appearance varies (e.g., style, illumination, clutter, or paraphrasing), policies often exhibit unnecessary action drift. Conversely, under semantic-breaking changes, where key task semantics such as the target object or constraint are altered, policies frequently fail to produce sufficiently distinct behaviors and instead follow the original trajectory. To address this gap, we propose BAS-VLA, a task-semantic action calibration framework built on top of a frozen base VLA. BAS-VLA adopts a breaking-centered calibration core as the default path, and introduces a selective evidence-gated preserving auxiliary that activates only when nuisance variation is detected while task semantics remain consistent. On the OpenPI-pi0.5 / LIBERO-Object Milk-Swap benchmark, BAS-VLA maintains high success on clean (98.0%) and semantics-preserving conditions (97.5%), while reducing clean-criterion success to 0.0% under deliberate target-object swaps, demonstrating strong stale-task suppression and task-semantic separation. On validated style-preserving shifts, it improves success from 42% to 70% without degrading clean performance. These results highlight that reliable VLA behavior requires moving beyond appearance robustness toward explicit task-semantic action calibration.

</details>


### [146] [WOLF: World Model Guided LiDAR Exploration with Predictive Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23656)
*Yuyang Tian,Penghui Yang,Pengyuan Wu,Haoran Yang,Chenhui Li,Pengfei Han,Dong Wang,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WOLF——一个基于世界模型引导的 LiDAR 无人机自主探索框架，通过预测未来观测来提升探索效率，在仿真与真实飞行中均取得显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LiDAR 无人机探索仅依据已测量地图决策，对遮挡区域之外的空间延续缺乏预见性，导致潜在高信息量方向被忽略。为此需要一种能预测未来观测的机制来增强自主探索的认知能力。

Method: 1) 训练阶段用循环世界模型从探索轨迹中学习观测动力学，循环记忆保留跨连续视角解读部分观测所需的空间上下文；2) 结合观测历史与候选运动，预测局部占据与可见性；3) 提出预测前沿生成机制，依据置信度、分支一致性和观测质量对齐融合预测，识别有前景区域；4) 将预测前沿与实测前沿联合用于几何视点选择与轨迹生成，新扫描持续更新后续预测。

Result: 仿真中，在 Garage 场景于覆盖率相当条件下平均终止时间较 EPIC 降低 10.9%；在 Tunnel 场景平均覆盖率由 42.12% 提升至 98.35%。真实实验进一步验证了所学习模型可在实体飞行中机载部署并在线推理。

Conclusion: 世界模型引导的预测式探索能有效弥补遮挡导致的前瞻不足，通过预测前沿融合提升采样效率与覆盖率，并具备实际机载在线运行的可行性。

Abstract: LiDAR-based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) exploration builds maps by continually selecting where to observe next. However, decisions based on the measured map provide limited foresight into spatial continuations behind occlusions, leaving potentially informative directions unrecognized. We present WOLF, a world-model-guided framework that predicts future observations to enhance autonomous exploration. In the training stage, a recurrent world model learns observation dynamics from exploration trajectories, with recurrent memory retaining the spatial context needed to interpret partial observations across successive views. Building on this context, the model combines observation history with candidate motions during exploration to predict local occupancy and visibility. To guide further sensing, a predictive frontier generation mechanism then aligns and fuses these predictions using confidence, branch agreement, and observation quality to identify promising regions. The resulting predictive frontiers join measured ones to guide geometric viewpoint selection and trajectory generation, while new scans update subsequent predictions. In simulations, our method reduces mean terminal time by 10.9% relative to EPIC in Garage at comparable coverage and increases mean coverage from 42.12% to 98.35% in Tunnel. Real-world experiments further demonstrate onboard deployment of the learned model for online inference during physical flight.

</details>


### [147] [UniPoint: Unified Point-Level Sensor Fusion for Humanoid Locomotion Across Challenging Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23666)
*Sicen Li,Zhen Chu,Chao Li,Qiuguo Zhu,Jun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniPoint：基于多源点级传感器融合的人形机器人全身运动框架，将360° LiDAR与两路深度相机早期融合为单一基座坐标点云，经体素化与线性自注意力编码，实现一次训练覆盖八种地形并机载实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境部署要求人形机器人安全穿越高度异构地形，感知需同时满足广覆盖、局部精度与传感器故障冗余。现有方法难以兼顾：单前向深度相机或近邻高度采样覆盖不足；里程计校正的高程图在剧烈运动下漂移且遗漏细薄垂直结构；图像级编码开销随相机数量增长。

Method: 提出UniPoint全身运动框架，采用多源点级传感器融合：将360° LiDAR与两路深度相机测量早期融合为统一基座坐标系点集；通过体素化重采样为固定数量token，使用线性自注意力编码，并以本体感受查询的交叉注意力聚合特征，使前向计算开销与传感器数量解耦；训练中引入地形感知奖励、感知退化注入与域随机化，单次训练得到统一策略。

Result: 在DR02人形机器人上，于七类地形的九个真实场景各进行20次试验，验证了70厘米高平台、100厘米沟隙、细薄障碍物及稀疏或狭窄落脚点；策略零样本泛化至室外环境；在机载RK3588上无需微调即可部署；单一模态失效仅移除部分token，性能平滑退化。

Conclusion: 点级早期融合与固定token编码在保持细薄垂直结构信息的同时，实现了覆盖范围、局部精度与故障冗余的统一，且计算开销不随传感器数量增长，单策略可跨地形部署并具备零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Open-world deployment requires humanoid robots to cross highly heterogeneous terrain safely, with perception that simultaneously provides wide coverage, local accuracy, and redundancy against sensor failure. Existing approaches struggle to satisfy all three: one forward depth camera or nearby height sampling covers too little; odometry-corrected elevation maps drift under aggressive motion and miss thin vertical structures; image-level encoding costs grow with camera count. We present UniPoint, a humanoid whole-body locomotion framework built on multi-source point-level sensor fusion. Measurements from a 360° light detection and ranging (LiDAR) sensor and two depth cameras are early-fused into one base-frame point set. Voxelization resamples it to a fixed number of tokens encoded by linear self-attention and proprioception-queried cross-attention, decoupling forward cost from sensor count. The point set retains standing thin barriers; a single-modality failure removes only part of the tokens, so the policy degrades gracefully. A single training run with terrain-aware rewards, perception-degradation injection, and domain randomization produces one policy for all eight terrain types, deployed on an onboard RK3588 without fine-tuning. On a DR02 humanoid, 20 trials at each of nine real-world settings over seven terrain types validate the policy on 70-cm-high platforms, 100-cm gaps, thin barriers, and sparse or narrow footholds; it also generalizes zero-shot outdoors.

</details>


### [148] [Marginal Calibration Does Not Compose: Hidden Dependence in Modular Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23731)
*Rista Baral*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人系统由多个独立开发的模块组合而成，本文指出即便各模块单独标定良好，组合后系统级不确定性标定也可能失效，并通过仿真证明建模联合协方差可恢复下游标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统由独立开发的感知、预测、动作模块构成。各模块单独测试时表现可靠，但模块组合后系统级的不确定性标定未必得到保持，独立验证模块的做法存在根本性局限。

Method: 以移动障碍物预测流程为案例，分别考察位置估计器与速度估计器的标定情况，分析其误差相关性差异对未来状态不确定性估计的影响；在仿真中对比三种处理方式：假设独立、建模联合协方差、以及依赖鲁棒的不确定性界。

Result: 位置与速度估计器各自均表现为标定良好，但由于误差相关性不同，导致未来状态不确定性估计差异显著。假设独立会使系统过度自信或过度保守，直接影响下游规划决策与安全性；建模联合协方差可恢复下游标定并提升系统性能，依赖鲁棒的不确定性界则以增加保守性为代价提升安全性。

Conclusion: 组件接口标定良好并不能保证组合后的下游行为标定良好，这揭示了独立验证机器人模块的根本局限，说明接口需传递依赖信息或支持直接的系统级标定。

Abstract: Robotic systems are typically composed of multiple independently developed modules that work together to perceive, predict, and act in the environment. Although each module may perform reliably in isolation, composing them does not necessarily preserve uncertainty calibration at the system level. In this work, we show that well-calibrated component interfaces do not necessarily produce calibrated downstream behavior after composition. Using a moving-obstacle prediction pipeline, we demonstrate that position and velocity estimators can each appear well calibrated individually, yet differences in how their error are correlated lead to substantially different estimates of future-state uncertainty. Consequently, assuming independence can make the system either overly confident or unnecessarily conservative, directly influencing downstream planning decisions and safety. Through simulations, we show that modeling the joint covariance restores downstream calibration and improves system performance, whereas dependence-robust uncertainty bounds enhance safety at the cost of increased conservatism. Our findings reveal a fundamental limitation of independently validating robotic modules and highlight the need for interfaces that communicate dependence information or support direct system-level calibration.

</details>


### [149] [FlockDiffusion: Assignment-Conditioned Diffusion for Multi-Drone Task Allocation and Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23745)
*Iana Zhura,Satenik Akopyan,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FlockDiffusion 框架，统一场景图编码、任务分配与扩散式轨迹生成，实现多无人机集群在密集任务与复杂环境下的高效协同规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多无人机导航需在有限算力下服务分布式目标；分立的代价估计、任务分配与执行求解器导致冗余图搜索和突兀路径，效率低、路径质量差。

Method: FlockDiffusion 组合场景图编码器、显式分配头、以分配为条件的扩散变换器和闭式轨迹解码器；用自回归教师模型提供离线监督，实现集群轨迹的并行生成。

Result: PyBullet 消融：任务捆绑使完成率由 50% 升至 100%，完整教师模型相较带捆绑的 MAGNNET 降低路径代价 8.4%。可扩展性基准（每密度 100 场景、10 架无人机）：推理快 6.2–7.6 倍，路径短约 37%；任务数 20→40 时延 7.8→11.1 ms，基线为 48.0→75.8 ms。Gazebo 五环境：规划覆盖率 100%，路径代价较带捆绑基线降低 15.4%。

Conclusion: 在任务密度递增的场景中实现高效规划，适用于难以用实体无人机集群复现的配置。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-drone navigation requires fleets to service distributed objectives in cluttered environments under tight computational budgets. Efficient coordination depends on task bundling, where each drone visits multiple objectives along its route. Separate solvers for cost estimation, assignment, and execution incur redundant graph search and produce long, abrupt paths. We propose FlockDiffusion, a learned framework combining a scene graph encoder, an explicit allocation head, an assignment conditioned diffusion transformer, and a closed form trajectory decoder. An autoregressive teacher provides offline supervision for parallel fleet trajectory generation. PyBullet ablations show that bundling increases task completion from 50% to 100%, while our complete teacher further reduces route cost by 8.4% relative to MAGNNET with bundling. In the optimized scalability benchmark, evaluated on 100 scenes per density with ten drones, FlockDiffusion achieves 6.2 to 7.6 times faster inference and approximately 37% shorter routes than the classical pipeline. As nominal task counts increase from 20 to 40, latency rises from 7.8 to 11.1 ms, compared with 48.0 to 75.8 ms for the baseline. In a separate evaluation across five Gazebo environments, FlockDiffusion achieves 100% planner coverage and reduces planned route cost by 15.4% relative to the baseline with bundling. These results demonstrate efficient planning under increasing task density in configurations that are demanding to reproduce with physical drone fleets.

</details>


### [150] [EgoWild2Dex: Learning Dexterous Robotic Manipulation from In-the-Wild Human Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23755)
*Kunyang Lin,Xutao Wen,Jingxi Lin,Lanyong Lin,Jiaming Liu,Tianshuo Yang,Xianchi Chen,Yue Han,Yiduo Li,Zhanpeng Zhang,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoWild2Dex，利用野外第一人称人类数据训练双臂灵巧机器人，并发布538.9小时EgoWild数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧机器人操作缺乏可扩展监督信号；现有第一人称数据多在受限或专门搭建环境中采集，难以覆盖真实世界的长尾物体与技能分布，而野外采集又带来视角抖动与场景杂乱等视觉挑战。

Method: 在家庭、工厂、药店等真实场景中让佩戴头戴相机的人执行日常任务，采集野外第一人称演示；提出EgoWild2Dex，将不稳定第一人称视角与人体动作分别对齐到机器人观测与动作。核心为GeoFormer——可微几何Transformer，将带噪人类观测形变对齐至机器人观测；并设计人-机器人训练方案以弥合具身差距。

Result: 在真实机器人上，三项长时程双臂灵巧操作任务平均成功率96.7%，物体级零样本平均成功率33.3%；发布EgoWild数据集，含538.9小时、179,049条episode、125,961条独特任务描述、1,282个物体类别。

Conclusion: 野外第一人称人类演示可作为灵巧机器人操作的可扩展监督来源，通过视角与动作的联合对齐有效弥合人-机器人具身差距，并在有限机器人监督下实现高任务成功率。

Abstract: Egocentric human data provide a principled source of supervision for learning dexterous robot manipulation. Unlike prior approaches that often collect such data in constrained or specially constructed environments, we collect in-the-wild egocentric demonstrations in real-world settings, including homes, factories, and pharmacies, etc., where people perform their ordinary tasks while wearing head-mounted cameras. This collection protocol captures diverse workflows and hand-object interactions across long-tailed object and skill distributions, but also yields visually challenging observations due to scene clutter and head-motion-induced viewpoint changes (a mean cumulative rotation of $15.93^{\circ}$/s). To address these issues, we introduce EgoWild2Dex, which transfers in-the-wild ego-human experience to dual-arm robots with dexterous hands by jointly aligning unstable egocentric views and human motions with robot observations and actions, respectively. This work offers three benefits. First, we introduce GeoFormer, a differentiable geometric transformer that warps noisy human observations toward robot observations. Second, we design a human-robot training scheme to bridge the embodiment gap, enabling high task success with limited robot supervision. Third, we release EgoWild, a 538.9-hour in-the-wild egocentric human dataset comprising 179,049 episodes, 125,961 unique task descriptions, and 1,282 object categories. On real robots, EgoWild2Dex achieves an average success rate of 96.7% across three long-horizon bimanual dexterous manipulation tasks and an average object-level zero-shot success rate of 33.3%. The data, models, and code will be released.

</details>


### [151] [PackLab: A Comprehensive Framework for Developing, Training, and Evaluating MLLMs in Robotic Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23784)
*Donghao Zhou,Jia-Hui Pan,Fan Zhang,Xingyuan Bu,Shilong Li,Xiaojie Gao,Yun-Hui Liu,Chi-Wing Fu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PackLab框架，用于开发、训练和评估面向闭循环机器人装箱的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），包含仿真平台PackLab-Suite、专用模型PackLab-VLM和多难度评测基准PackLab-Bench，实验证明其在多种物体集与容器配置下优于几何启发式、传统强化学习和通用MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装箱是长时程序贯决策问题，每个物体的放置都会影响后续可用空间。现有方法主要依赖手工几何启发式（优化预设目标）或通过试错在预设训练配置上学习的强化学习策略；尽管MLLM近期被用于该任务，但其在异构装箱配置下进行闭循环序贯决策的潜力仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 作者提出PackLab综合框架，包含三个组件：1）PackLab-Suite，基于物理的仿真平台，可规模化生成多样化训练装箱轨迹并评估其物理结果；2）PackLab-VLM，一种装箱专用MLLM，能够理解不断变化的物体与容器状态，以闭循环方式联合选择物体并预测放置位置；3）PackLab-Bench，提供多难度等级的标准化装箱场景以进行系统评测。

Result: 大量实验表明，在不同物体集与容器配置下，PackLab-VLM平均而言优于传统装箱启发式方法、传统强化学习方法以及通用MLLM，凸显了MLLM在长时程机器人装箱中的潜力。

Conclusion: MLLM具备解决长时程机器人装箱闭循环序贯决策问题的潜力。代码、模型、数据集与基准已在 https://github.com/Correr-Zhou/PackLab 开源。

Abstract: Robotic bin packing requires long-horizon sequential decision-making, as each object placement affects the available space for subsequent packing. Existing methods primarily rely on hand-crafted geometric heuristics that optimize predefined objectives or reinforcement learning policies learned through trial and error over predefined training configurations. Despite recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for this task, their potential for closed-loop sequential decisions across heterogeneous packing configurations remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce PackLab, a comprehensive framework for developing, training, and evaluating MLLMs for closed-loop robotic bin packing. PackLab-Suite provides a physics-based simulation platform for scalable generation of diverse training packing trajectories and evaluation of their physical outcomes. PackLab-VLM is a packing-specialized MLLM that understands the evolving object and container states to jointly select objects and predict placements in a closed-loop manner. PackLab-Bench provides standardized packing scenarios at multiple difficulty levels for systematic evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, on average, PackLab-VLM outperforms conventional packing heuristics, traditional reinforcement learning methods, and general-purpose MLLMs across object sets and container configurations, highlighting the potential of MLLMs for long-horizon robotic packing. The code, model, dataset, and benchmark are available at https://github.com/Correr-Zhou/PackLab .

</details>


### [152] [Risk-Aware Motion Planning and Control under Unknown Dynamics with Hybrid Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23792)
*Zhiquan Zhang,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究未知动力学且状态观测仅在部分状态空间可用的机器人运动规划与控制问题，针对"盲区"提出基于名义动力学和开环控制序列的随机转移系统建模方法，将高层规划形式化为随机最短路径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作采用分层框架（系统辨识、预测可达性、图搜索与控制器综合），通过多面体状态空间划分上的局部仿射近似模型实现，但依赖状态观测进行辨识和反馈控制。当状态测量在部分状态空间缺失（盲区）时，该方法无法直接应用，因此需要解决盲区下的规划与控制问题。

Method: 基于该分层框架，通过选择名义动力学并在观测丢失前预计算开环控制序列来处理盲区。由于真实动力学可能与所选名义模型存在偏差，机器人可能通过非预期的面离开盲多面体。作者量化这种转移风险，并将可能的结果纳入随机转移系统。高层规划问题被形式化为随机最短路径问题，其策略指导控制器综合。

Result: 案例研究表明，该方法能够引导机器人从初始状态到达目标，同时在路径效率与穿越盲区相关风险之间进行权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为混合状态观测下未知动力学的机器人规划提供了一种处理盲区的方法，通过随机最短路径公式化在效率与风险之间取得平衡。

Abstract: We consider robotic motion planning and control under unknown dynamics with hybrid state observations, where state measurements are available only in parts of the state space. Existing work combines system identification, predicted reachability, graph search and controller synthesis in a hierarchical framework using local affine approximated models over polytopic state space partitioning, but requires state observations for identification and feedback control. Based on this framework, we address blind regions by selecting nominal dynamics and precomputing open-loop control sequences before observation is lost. Since the true dynamics may differ from the selected nominal model, the robot may exit a blind polytope through an unintended facet. We quantify this transition risk and incorporate the possible outcomes into a stochastic transition system. The high-level planning problem is formulated as a stochastic shortest path problem, whose policy guides controller synthesis. A case study demonstrates that the method guides the robot from an initial state to a target while balancing route efficiency and the risks associated with traversing blind regions.

</details>


### [153] [ContactDP: Contact-Guided Diffusion Policy for Tight Insertion Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23800)
*Chengyi Xing,Shaoxiong Yao,Diego Romeres,Devesh K. Jha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContactDP——一种面向紧配合连接器插入任务的多模态扩散策略框架，融合视觉、触觉与力/力矩信息，并结合混合位置-力控制器，实现接触丰富场景下的高精度插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度连接器插入因机械公差紧、接触过程部分可观测、遮挡与接触歧义带来的多模态不确定性而极具挑战。成功插入需要闭环接触引导，持续整合全局对齐线索与局部接触反馈，以在交互中产生稳定的修正动作。现有仅依赖视觉的扩散策略在紧配合接触任务中性能、可靠性与泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出 ContactDP（Contact-Guided Diffusion Policy），联合集成腕部 RGB 观测、指尖触觉传感与腕部力/力矩测量，用于推断接触状态并生成时序一致的修正运动；学习到的策略与混合位置-力控制器协同工作，提供柔顺的底层交互。

Result: 在多种连接器几何、抓取条件与初始错位的工业级连接器插入任务上评估，ContactDP 在性能、可靠性与泛化性方面均显著优于仅视觉的扩散策略。

Conclusion: 多模态接触反馈与扩散策略及柔顺控制相结合，可有效应对紧配合插入中的不确定性与接触歧义，提升插入任务的表现与鲁棒性。

Abstract: High-precision connector insertion remains challenging for robotic systems due to tight mechanical tolerances, partial observability during contact, and multimodal uncertainty arising from occlusion and contact ambiguity. Successful insertion requires closed-loop contact guidance that continuously integrates global alignment cues with local contact feedback to produce stable corrective actions under interaction. In this work, we present ContactDP (Contact-Guided Diffusion Policy for Tight Insertion Tasks), a multimodal diffusion-policy framework for contact-rich insertion. ContactDP jointly integrates wrist RGB observations, fingertip tactile sensing, and wrist-mounted force-torque measurements to infer contact state and generate temporally consistent corrective motions during insertion. To ensure stable execution under contact, the learned policy operates together with a hybrid position-force controller that provides compliant low-level interaction. We evaluate our approach on a suite of industrial-grade connector insertion tasks with varying connector geometries, grasp conditions, and initial misalignment. Across all tasks, ContactDP significantly outperforms vision-only diffusion policies for performance, reliability and generalization.

</details>


### [154] [Structured World-State Reasoning for Agentic Robotic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23841)
*Finley R. Holt,Luis A. Pabon,John Irvin Alora,Jonas Frey,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WORLDS 框架通过持久化图结构与并行推理器，将自然语言目标与地图先验、实时观测对齐，在 CityNav 上取得 51.8% 导航成功率，较此前最佳提升 15.7 个百分点，并在四旋翼上完成实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人搜索需将自然语言与异构、不完整且常有歧义的证据（文本、先验地图、随时间到达的观测）对齐。核心难点在于语境化这些信息流，并在选定目标前决定在何处采集证据。

Method: 提出 WORLDS 框架：以地理空间先验初始化持久化图，并由感知持续更新；多个并行推理器（Parallel Reasoners）维护相互竞争的候选解释并主动请求证据以区分；多模态检查器（Examiner）采集并处理被请求的观测；裁判（Judge）据此选择有据可依的目标或请求下一轮。

Result: 在 CityNav 全部 5,311 个测试回合上达到 51.8% 导航成功率，为已报告最高，超此前公开最佳 15.7 个百分点（OSM-only、高分辨率正射协议）。在 1,000 个共享回合上为 50.0%，而同等模型、先验、感知栈与移动预算下最强适配基线为 27.9%。Examiner 的基于观测的验证贡献 5.9 个百分点；在降低推理开销设置下仍超适配 GeoNav 基线 18.8 个百分点，且生成 token 更少。四旋翼实机飞行生成的感知航点，仅用机载影像成功定位三个语言目标，包括一个地图中不存在的车辆。

Conclusion: 以持久化世界状态图加并行推理、多模态证据检验与裁判判定的闭环，可在证据稀疏且有歧义时显著提升长时程语言引导搜索的性能，并具备实机部署可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic search must resolve natural language against heterogeneous, incomplete, and often ambiguous evidence: textual information, prior maps, and observations arriving over time. The core challenge is to contextualize these streams and decide where to gather evidence before selecting a target. We present WORLDS: World-state Observation and Reasoning for Language-guided Discovery and Search, a framework that grounds reasoning in a persistent graph initialized from geospatial priors and updated by perception. Parallel Reasoners maintain competing candidate interpretations and request evidence to distinguish between them. We collect and process the requested observations with a multimodal Examiner, after which a Judge selects a grounded target or requests another pass. WORLDS achieves 51.8% navigation success across all 5,311 CityNav test episodes, the highest reported success rate, exceeding the previous published best by 15.7 percentage points under an OSM-only, high-resolution orthographic protocol. On 1,000 shared episodes, it achieves 50.0% versus 27.9% for the strongest adapted baseline using the same model, prior, sensing stack, and movement budget. Observation-based verification by the Examiner contributes 5.9 points of this success, and at a reduced reasoning-effort setting WORLDS still exceeds the adapted GeoNav baseline by 18.8 points while generating fewer tokens. We also demonstrate WORLDS on a quadrotor, which flies the generated sensing waypoints and grounds three language targets, including a vehicle absent from the map, from its onboard imagery.

</details>


### [155] [Object-Centered Reconstruction for Vision-Based 3D Force Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23856)
*Zhonghao Zhang,Mingyeung Wu,Hao Yang,Ayberk Acar,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉的立体内窥镜视频软组织形变3D交互力估计流程，在体模与离体猪结肠上取得0.77 N和1.30 N的平均RMSE，并初步验证了活体手术序列的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人结直肠手术中过大操作力会损伤组织并增加吻合口漏风险。达芬奇5虽具备力感知能力，但早期达芬奇系统及多数其他手术机器人平台均不具备该功能，因此需要一种无需力传感器的力估计方法。

Method: 构建视觉驱动流程：在以物体为中心的坐标系中动态重建组织点云，在几何约束下跟踪组织点，并用神经网络预测3D力向量；在橡胶手套体模、离体猪结肠及活体结直肠手术视频序列上逐步评估。

Result: 在不同组织朝向、内窥镜视野内位置及相机视角下，体模与猪结肠上的平均RMSE分别为0.77 N和1.30 N。相比相机坐标系表示，物体中心表示将平均RMSE降低51.3%和56.7%；相比CoTracker，几何约束跟踪将RMSE降低19.8%和25.3%。并在活体结直肠手术序列上定性验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了基于视觉、无传感器的力估计在结直肠手术中的潜力，是迈向临床转化的一步。

Abstract: Excessive force may damage tissue and increase the risk of anastomotic leakage in robotic colorectal surgery. Although the da Vinci 5 provides force sensing, this capability is unavailable on earlier da Vinci systems and many other surgical robotic platforms. In this work, we present a vision-based pipeline for estimating 3D interaction forces from soft-tissue deformation in stereo endoscopic video. We dynamically reconstruct the tissue point cloud in an object-centered coordinate frame, track tissue points with geometric constraints, and predict the 3D force vector with a neural network. We progressively evaluate the pipeline on rubber-glove phantoms, ex vivo porcine colons, and in vivo colorectal surgical video sequences. Under varying tissue orientations and positions within the endoscopic view, as well as different camera viewpoints, the proposed method achieves average root mean square error (RMSEs) of 0.77 N and 1.30 N on the phantom and porcine colon, respectively. Compared with the camera-frame representation, the object-centered representation reduces average RMSE by 51.3% and 56.7%, while geometry-constrained tracking reduces RMSE by 19.8% and 25.3% compared with CoTracker. We further qualitatively demonstrate the feasibility of vision-based force estimation on an in vivo colorectal surgical sequence, as a step toward clinical translation of vision-based, sensorless force estimation.

</details>


### [156] [Grounded Action Model: 3D Grounding as a Foundation for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23863)
*Gehao Zhang,Weikai Huang,Shailesh Shailesh,Yiyan Peng,Jiafei Duan,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Grounded Action Models (GAM)，通过在机器人基础模型中引入3D grounding（语言/点/框提示→对象中心表示），实现目标感知与度量几何结合的动作预测，在仿真与真实机器人任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人基础模型（如VLA、WAM）依赖的预训练骨干网络（视觉语言模型、视频生成）并不直接要求度量级的物体定位（metric grounding），只能从机器人演示中隐式学习"哪些物体重要、位于何处"，导致泛化与扰动鲁棒性不足。

Method: GAM将语言、点或框提示统一转化为共享的对象中心表示，该表示包含目标聚焦的视觉特征与度量物体几何；再通过多流（multi-stream）transformer与机器人状态历史混合，预测动作块（action chunks）。GAM可自主运行，也可作为高层规划器控制的低层控制器，支持多模态输入与长程、依赖记忆的操作。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0：50任务平均成功率55.3%（Spatial Forcing 52.0%），场景随机化下47.6%（Abot-M0 30.4%），且仅在干净场景演示上训练；LIBERO-PRO：16种扰动设置下SOTA平均61%（π_{0.5} 53%），目标重定位或重新指定时增益最大；真实机器人：双臂YAM视觉偏移下17/20成功（π_{0.5} 4/20），Franka上结合Molmo2规划器在长程与记忆依赖任务中达64.7% ID与49.8% OOD步骤完成率。

Conclusion: 显式3D grounding可有效弥补现有机器人基础模型的度量定位缺失，显著提升对场景随机化、目标重定位与视觉偏移等扰动的鲁棒性，并可与高层规划器组合实现长程、依赖记忆的操作，验证了该新范式的有效性。

Abstract: Manipulation policies must know which objects matter and where they are, yet the pretrained backbones that current robot foundation models build on, from language in vision-language-action models (VLAs) to video generation in world-action models (WAMs), do not directly require this metric grounding, leaving it to be learned implicitly from robot demonstrations. We propose Grounded Action Models (GAMs), a new paradigm of robot foundation models built with 3D grounding. GAM can be conditioned using language, points, or box prompts, which are first transformed into a shared object-centric representation of the selected objects. This representation captures target-focused visual features and metric object geometry, which is mixed with robot state history through a multi-stream transformer to predict action chunks. Although GAMs can be run autonomously, they can also serve as a low-level controller that a high-level planner controls using its various input modalities, allowing for long-horizon and memory-dependent manipulation. On RoboTwin 2.0, GAM achieves an average success rate of 55.3% across 50 tasks (vs. 52.0% for Spatial Forcing), including 47.6% under scene randomization (vs. 30.4% for Abot-M0), with its action policy trained only on clean-scene demonstrations. On LIBERO-PRO, it achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate of 61% (vs. 53% for $π_{0.5}$) across 16 perturbation settings, with the largest gains when targets are relocated or newly designated. On two real robots, GAM retains 17/20 successes under visual shift on a bimanual YAM versus 4/20 for $π_{0.5}$, while its composition with a Molmo2 planner on a Franka achieves 64.7% ID and 49.8% OOD step completion on long-horizon and memory-dependent tasks.

</details>


### [157] [HumynexSurg-1: A Curated Expert Liposuction Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23885)
*Rhea Huang,David L. Matlock,Laurence Reich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Humynex Robotics 发布 HumynexSurg-1，首个带同步力/压力感知与专家口述的抽脂手术数据集（14 段、42,738 帧、35.6 分钟），并验证 NVIDIA Isaac GR00T N1.7 可在无自定义代码下于 1 小时内微调学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型的示教语料普遍缺失手术场景：780 小时 Open-H 手术集合中仅一个数据集含同步力信息，且无任何美学类手术数据。抽脂是其中最难的情形，因为器械在皮下工作，医生依靠手感与判断操作，难以被常规视觉数据刻画。因此需要专门采集的手术示教数据集。

Method: 由资深抽脂医生在猪腹部组织上操作，并同步口述每一次决策；采集同步的负压、六轴手部力/力矩、俯视 RGB-D 视频、侧视视频与领夹麦克风音频。共 14 个 episode、42,738 帧、35.6 分钟、356 条语音，其中 95% 可编译为抽脂专用标签 schema。采集遵循专利待审的传感方案，围绕策略所需物理量组织；本版本以侧视视频中的工具-手轨迹表示器械运动，力通道以状态形式提供，后续资助采集将补充实测 6-DoF 手柄位姿、经验证的力通道、脂肪层超声成像与触诊感知。概念验证使用 NVIDIA Isaac GR00T N1.7 微调。

Result: GR00T N1.7 无需自定义代码即可在每次运行 1 小时内完成微调并学会所录会话。同批 episode 上的扩展性探测表明：每新增一段会话，都能降低在未见会话上的误差，说明数据规模扩大可带来持续收益。

Conclusion: 数据集本身、其标签 schema、质量保证报告与评估协议即为交付产品。探测结果指向下一阶段采集方向：在各类脂肪区域上进行大量短会话，并配备文中所列传感器。

Abstract: Robot foundation models learn manipulation from large demonstration corpora, but surgery is missing from those corpora: across the 780-hour Open-H surgical collection, one dataset carries synchronized force and none covers an aesthetic procedure. Liposuction is the hard case, because the instrument works under the skin and the surgeon operates by feel and by judgment. Humynex Robotics builds curated expert datasets for this kind of procedure. HumynexSurg-1 is the first release: a master liposuction surgeon performing on porcine abdominal tissue while narrating every decision, recorded with synchronized suction pressure, six-axis hand force/torque, top-down RGB-D video, side video and a lavalier microphone -- 14 episodes, 42,738 frames, 35.6 minutes, 356 utterances of which 95% compile into a liposuction-specific label schema. The capture follows a patent-pending sensing plan organized around the quantities a policy needs, so a channel captured today by a model can be upgraded to a sensor tomorrow without changing the data format. This release captures the instrument motion as a tool-hand track in the side video and provides the force channel as state; the funded capture adds a measured 6-DoF handle pose, a validated force channel, ultrasound imaging of the fat layer, and palpation sensing. As a proof of concept, NVIDIA Isaac GR00T N1.7 fine-tunes on the dataset with no custom code in under an hour per run and learns the recorded sessions; scaling probes on the same episodes show where further gains come from: every new session lowers the error on an unseen session. The dataset, its label schema, its quality-assurance reports and its evaluation protocol are the product; the next capture, many short sessions across fat regions with the sensors named here, is what the probes point to.

</details>


### [158] [HapticWAM: Distilling Imagined Touch into a World-Action Model without Inference-Time Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23888)
*Mikhail Sannikov,Ilya Mikhalchuk,Konstantin Gubernatorov,Petr Kovalev,Ogunwoye Faith Oluwatobi,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapticWAM 是一个世界-动作模型，通过异构触觉编码、结构化接触预测与师生蒸馏，在部署时无需指尖观测即可实现富接触操作，学生模型在三个真实拾放任务上取得 77% 的平均单任务成功率（合并 82%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富接触操作需要估计力、滑动与接触几何，但这些信息在场景图像中往往具有歧义。光学触觉传感器虽同时提供接触表面的视觉观测与力学测量，但从这些信号中学习存在两大挑战：一是如何表征超越外观的接触信息，二是如何将其收益迁移到部署时不需要指尖观测的策略上。

Method: 提出 HapticWAM 世界-动作模型，包含三部分：(1) 异构触觉编码，教师模型将凝胶图像与形变、剪切、分布式力、合力旋量及派生接触状态一起编码进冻结的视频骨干网络；(2) 结构化接触预测，模型联合生成动作与描述未来事件及力学的“接触包”，而非仅预测触觉像素；(3) Anticipatory Contact Coupling 利用先前想象的接触包来调制注意力，在无直接触觉观测时仍保留与接触相关的输入；(4) Haptic-Imagination Distillation 将接触未来与动作预测迁移到学生模型，学生保留生成式接触头但移除指尖输入分支。

Result: 在真实世界实验设置下，针对三个富接触拾放任务，HapticWAM Student 取得 77% 的单任务平均成功率（50 次起始中成功 41 次，合并成功率 82%），其中某一任务达到 95%，优于所评估的教师模型与基线配置。

Conclusion: HapticWAM 表明，将异构触觉信号编码为结构化接触预测，并通过师生蒸馏迁移至无指尖观测的学生策略，能够有效提升富接触操作的成功率，且学生模型可超越教师与基线方法。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires estimating forces, slip and contact geometry that can remain ambiguous in scene images. Optical tactile sensors provide both visual observations of the contact surface and mechanical measurements, yet learning from these signals raises two challenges: representing contact beyond appearance and transferring its benefits to a policy that does not require fingertip observations at deployment. We introduce HapticWAM, a world-action model that combines heterogeneous tactile encoding, structured contact prediction and teacher-student distillation. Its teacher encodes gel images together with deformation, shear, distributed forces, resultant wrench and derived contact state into a frozen video backbone. Rather than predicting tactile pixels alone, the model jointly generates actions and a contact package describing future events and mechanics. Anticipatory Contact Coupling uses the previously imagined package to condition attention, preserving a contact-related input when direct tactile observations are unavailable. Haptic-Imagination Distillation transfers both contact futures and action predictions to a student that retains the generative contact head but removes its fingertip input branches. On a real-world setup, across three contact-rich pick-and-place tasks, HapticWAM Student achieves a 77% per-task mean success rate (41 of 50 starts, 82% pooled), reaching 95% on one of the tasks, outperforming the evaluated teacher and baseline configurations.

</details>


### [159] [BarrierFormer: Transformer-Guided Predictive Barrier Enforcement for Safe Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23896)
*Anandsingh Chauhan,Kunal Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BarrierFormer 提出一种屏障监督的 transformer 框架，通过在学习无模型安全策略时编码 rollout 级别的 CBF 约束，实现无在线优化、无模型知识的实时预测性安全控制，在安全率与推理延迟上优于 RL、扩散、MPC 和 transformer 基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 CBF 方法仅约束当前时刻安全性，具有短视性，可能使系统逼近安全集边界而未来无可行的安全控制；MPC 类方法虽在滚动时域上施加约束，但需已知模型并逐步求解约束优化问题，实时部署计算代价高。因此需要一种兼具预测性安全约束与实时无模型部署能力的方法。

Method: 提出 BarrierFormer：1）因果 transformer 编码观测-动作历史；2）通过动力学头自回归生成预测性 rollout 以替代真实模型；3）通过动作头为标称控制器提供残差修正以替代在线计算；4）屏障评论器基于局部观测评估 rollout 上的 CBF 约束违反；5）安全教师计算满足约束的屏障一致动作，作为学习控制策略的直接监督目标。推理时策略直接将观测-动作历史映射为控制动作。

Result: 在线性与非线性、2D 与 3D 动力系统上的安全目标导航评测中，BarrierFormer 在安全率和推理延迟两方面均优于现有 RL、扩散、MPC 及 transformer 类方法。

Conclusion: BarrierFormer 将 rollout 级 CBF 约束以监督方式注入无模型策略学习，兼顾了 MPC 的预测性安全与无模型策略的实时性，可在无在线优化与模型知识条件下实现实时预测性安全约束执行。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) have become one of the most popular tools for encoding and enforcing state constraints in safety-critical robotics. Standard CBF approaches are inherently myopic in nature as they enforce safety only at the current time step. Consequently, the system can be driven toward the boundary of the safe set where no feasible safe control exists at a future timestep. Model predictive control (MPC) based approaches address this by enforcing state constraints over a receding horizon. However, such approaches generally require the model to be known for solving a constrained optimization problem at every step, which is computationally expensive for real-time deployment. We propose BarrierFormer, a barrier-supervised transformer framework that addresses these limitations by encoding rollout-level CBF constraints in learning a model-free safe policy. A causal transformer encodes observation-action history, autoregressively generates a predictive rollout through the dynamics head to replace the model, and provides a residual correction to a nominal controller through the action head to replace the online computation. A barrier critic operating on local observations evaluates CBF constraint violations along this rollout, and a safety teacher computes barrier-consistent actions satisfying these constraints as direct supervision targets for the learned control policy. During inference, the policy maps observation-action history to control actions without any online optimization or model knowledge, enabling real-time model-free predictive safety enforcement. Evaluations across linear and nonlinear, 2D and 3D dynamical systems for safe goal-directed navigation demonstrate that BarrierFormer outperforms existing reinforcement learning (RL)-based, diffusion-based, MPC-based, and transformer-based approaches in safety rate and inference latency.

</details>


### [160] [ReVeal: A Reconstruction-Aware Real-to-Sim Framework for VLA Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23910)
*Xinyi Wang,Heng Hao,Wenjun Hu,Anna Enyu Li,Dizhi Ma,Karthik Ramani,Hankyu Moon,Yeong-Dae Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReVeal——一个real-to-sim评估框架，通过工作空间重建、重建级评估与匹配的闭环策略评估，衡量重建环境对VLA策略评测的适用性。作者提出NVMF与APGF两项保真度指标，并设计引入单目深度监督的重建流程PGSR-D。实验表明，重建保真度越高，真实与仿真下的策略表现越一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于仿真的VLA策略评估具备可扩展性与可复现性，但重建误差会导致仿真性能偏离真实表现，因此需要评估重建环境是否适合下游VLA策略评测。

Method: 提出ReVeal框架，包含工作空间重建、重建级评估和匹配的闭环策略评估三部分；提出NVMF（新视角网格保真度）评估观测保真度，APGF（标注平面几何保真度）评估平面几何保真度；并开发PGSR-D重建流程，通过单目深度监督改善多视角视觉线索受限区域的几何质量。

Result: 在8个评估场景中，NVMF与APGF能稳定区分2DGS、PGSR和PGSR-D的重建保真度；在8个类人操作任务上对GR00T、SmolVLA和pi0.5进行匹配评估，三种流程下重建保真度与real-sim性能一致性呈一致排序；进一步分析表明更高的保真度对应更强的实仿一致性。

Conclusion: ReVeal能够量化重建环境对VLA策略评测的适用性，重建保真度可作为real-sim性能一致性的有效预测指标，为基于仿真的策略评估提供可靠依据。

Abstract: Simulation-based evaluation provides a scalable and repeatable alternative to real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies. However, reconstruction errors can cause simulated policy performance to diverge from real-world performance, motivating the need to assess reconstructed environments for downstream VLA policy evaluation. We present ReVeal, a real-to-sim assessment framework combining workspace reconstruction, reconstruction-level assessment, and matched closed-loop policy evaluation. Novel-View Mesh Fidelity (NVMF) and Annotated Planar Geometry Fidelity (APGF) assess observation and planar geometric fidelity, respectively. We also develop PGSR-D, a reconstruction pipeline incorporating monocular depth supervision to improve geometry where multi-view visual cues are limited. Across 8 assessment scenes, NVMF and APGF consistently distinguish the fidelity of 2DGS, PGSR, and PGSR-D. Matched evaluations of GR00T, SmolVLA, and pi0.5 across 8 humanoid manipulation tasks show consistent ordering between reconstruction fidelity and real-sim performance agreement across pipelines. Further analysis of the evaluation workspaces shows that higher fidelity is associated with stronger real-sim agreement.

</details>


### [161] [FinsSim: A Reality-Aligned Integrated Simulation Platform for Underwater Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23943)
*Yu Zhang,Yuanmingqing Song,Xiangyun Rao,Pangkit Fong,Kunhao Zhang,Chongrong Fang,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FinsSim：面向水下机器人Sim-to-Real学习的现实对齐集成仿真平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人学习依赖仿真器，需同时具备高保真水动力学、便捷学习接口与可信的真机迁移能力，但现有方案难以兼顾。

Method: FinsSim提供可选后端的高保真仿真，配备标准控制基线与统一学习工作流；采用多传感器融合实现低成本精确定位，实现标定的推进器-水动力学模型与带约束的力分配算法，并通过ROS 2串联为完整Sim-to-Real管线。

Result: 匹配的仿真与实验表明，借助FinsSim可实现水下机器人控制策略的可靠Sim-to-Real迁移；消融研究验证各模块从不同方面解决关键问题。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在弥合理论研究与实际应用的差距，推动水下机器人领域发展。

Abstract: Underwater robot learning relies on simulators that integrate high-fidelity hydrodynamics, convenient learning interfaces, and a credible transition to real scenarios. In this work, we present FinsSim, a reality-aligned integrated simulation platform for Sim-to-Real underwater robot learning. FinsSim first constructs high-fidelity simulation with selectable backends to adapt to diverse requirements. To facilitate underwater robot research, it further offers standard control baselines, alongside with unified robot learning workflows. For reliable Sim-to-Real transfer, FinsSim adopts a multi-sensor fusion scheme to provide low-cost yet precise localization. Moreover, it implements calibrated thruster-hydrodynamics models and a constrained wrench allocation algorithm. Bridging these modules by ROS~2, FinsSim establishes a complete Sim-to-Real transfer pipeline. Through matched simulations and experiments, it is demonstrated that reliable Sim-to-Real transfer of underwater robot control policies can be achieved with the FinsSim framework. Separate ablation studies also validate that the modules of FinsSim can address the pivotal issues of underwater Sim-to-Real from different aspects. Overall, this work aims to bridge the gap between theoretical research and practical applications, ultimately driving advancements in the field of underwater robotics.

</details>


### [162] [Topology-Informed Visual Prompting For Vision Language Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23944)
*Haoyang Wu,Abhinav Kumar,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出拓扑引导的视觉提示框架，用仿真规划增强VLA微调数据集，并结合VLM预测拓扑签名与末端执行器路点作为视觉提示，提升复杂障碍几何下的操作任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在复杂障碍几何的操作任务中受部分可观测性困扰，相似视觉观察或机器人构型却需要本质不同的动作，这类区别可用拓扑签名量化；但部署时通常缺乏环境几何与物体状态的完整信息，运动规划器无法直接应用。

Method: 使用Gauss-Linking-Integral拓扑签名表示捕获环境重要拓扑性质；借助仿真近似环境中的特权几何信息，增强VLA微调数据集，加入将系统移动到已演示签名并从新构型恢复任务执行的轨迹；在同一数据集上微调VLM，使其从实时相机观察预测签名与末端执行器路点，并将路点渲染为观察上的视觉提示以引导VLA。

Result: 在三个仿真双臂任务和一个真实世界盒子拾取任务上，方法优于仅在名义演示上微调的VLA以及可移除拓扑相关信息的VLM提示基线；在硬件上，任务成功率比最强基线高出40%。

Conclusion: 拓扑签名引导的视觉提示框架可有效缓解部分可观测性带来的动作歧义，通过仿真特权信息增强与部署时视觉提示，显著提升VLA在复杂障碍几何操作任务中的成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can struggle with manipulation tasks with complex obstacle geometries due to partial observability. These complex geometries can lead to similar visual observations or robot configurations requiring qualitatively different actions, a distinction that can be quantified using topological signatures. While motion planners with full knowledge of environment geometries and object states can reason about these signatures in planning, this information is often not known at deployment. To address this issue, we present a topology-guided visual-prompting framework that uses simulation-based planning to augment a nominal demonstration dataset and provides vision-based guidance at deployment. Our method uses a Gauss-Linking-Integral topological signature representation to capture important topological properties of the environment. Using privileged geometry information from a simulation approximation of our environment, we augment a VLA fine-tuning dataset with trajectories that move the system to a demonstrated signature and, from the new configuration, resume task execution. A vision-language model (VLM) is fine-tuned on the same dataset to both predict signatures from live camera observations and predict end-effector waypoints, which are rendered as visual prompts on the observations to guide the VLA. Across three simulated bimanual tasks and a real-world box pickup task, our method outperforms a VLA fine-tuned only on nominal demonstrations and a VLM-prompting baseline that can remove topology-relevant information from observations. On hardware, it exceeds the strongest baseline by 40% in task success. Project website: https://topology-vla.github.io.

</details>


### [163] [Opt2VLA: Force-Aware Vision-Language-Action for Contact-Rich Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23968)
*Fukang Liu,Yipu Chen,Jaehwi Jang,Danfei Xu,Zsolt Kira,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Opt2VLA，一种力感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，在VLA与控制器的接口中显式引入接触力指令，通过多任务策略联合预测几何运动目标与连续力参考，并用轨迹优化生成接触一致的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人系统主要以几何运动目标表示动作，依赖专注运动跟踪的全身控制器，缺乏对交互力的显式推理与控制；在接触密集任务中，几何相似的运动可能对应不同力模式，且接触后视觉观测可能不可靠。

Method: 在VLA与全身控制的接口处引入显式力指令；单一多任务VLA策略同时预测几何运动目标与连续接触力参考，由任务特定的基于强化学习（RL）的全身控制器跟踪；通过带显式力参考的全身轨迹优化（TO）生成动力学可行且接触一致的训练数据，提供物理落地的监督信号。

Result: 在三个人形机器人接触密集任务上评估，显式力条件化相比仅运动控制能实现更准确、更一致的力调节；由TO提供的物理落地力矩监督进一步提升力跟踪精度与稳定性；闭环评估在仿真与真机硬件上展示了语言条件化的力调制能力。

Conclusion: 将接触力作为显式指令纳入VLA-控制接口，并结合轨迹优化提供的物理监督，可有效提升人形机器人接触密集操作中的力调节精度与稳定性，并支持语言驱动的力调制。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are expected to perform diverse human-level tasks in daily environments, many of which require precise regulation of interaction forces. While recent vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown promise for semantic planning and visuomotor control, existing humanoid systems primarily represent actions through geometric motion goals and rely on whole-body controllers focused on motion tracking, with limited explicit reasoning or control of interaction forces. This limitation is particularly relevant in contact-rich tasks, where geometrically similar motions may require different force regimes depending on the task context and where visual observations may become unreliable after contact. In this work, we present Opt2VLA, a force-aware VLA framework that introduces explicit force commands at the VLA-to-control interface for humanoid whole-body manipulation. A single multi-task VLA policy jointly predicts both geometric motion goals and continuous contact-force references, which are tracked by task-specific reinforcement learning (RL)-based whole-body controllers. To provide scalable and physically grounded supervision, we generate dynamically feasible and contact-consistent training data via whole-body trajectory optimization (TO) with explicit force references. We evaluate Opt2VLA on three contact-rich humanoid tasks and show that explicit force conditioning enables more accurate and consistent force regulation than motion-only control, while physically grounded torque supervision from TO further improves force tracking accuracy and stability. Closed-loop evaluations further demonstrate language-conditioned force modulation with Opt2VLA in simulation and on humanoid hardware.

</details>


### [164] [Anticipatory Robot Goalkeeping via Monotone Optimal Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23976)
*Hao E. Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yisen Li,Yaru Niu,Yikai Wang,Raihan Haque,Khalil Zbiss,Guanyang Luo,Hui-ping Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将机器人快速物理交互中的"何时启动动作"问题建模为策略条件下的有限时域最优停止问题，提出单调最优停止（MOS）方法，用于四足机器人动态扑救的释放时机决策，仿真中将平均扑救率从67.7%提升至74.4%，反转扑救从52.1%提升至66.5%，并在真机上验证了快速拦截与释放后方向修正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人高速物理交互常需在对方意图尚未完全确定时就开始动作。以守门扑救为例，等待可获得更可靠的目标信息，但会压缩可拦截的物理窗口；提前启动可保留可达性，却要在不确定性下行动，二者存在权衡。作者希望为固定闭环扑救控制器解决"何时启动"的决策问题。

Method: 将启动时机决策形式化为策略条件下的有限时域最优停止问题，提出单调最优停止（MOS）：四足扑救策略用强化学习训练，MOS则从演化的机器人状态与目标信念中决定何时激活策略。MOS不直接预测释放时间、也不仅依赖置信度，而是学习"立即行动相对于再多等待一次观测"的回报优势，并针对该"行动—等待"边际推导直接Bellman递推，仅对物理紧迫性施加单调性约束（体现随时间的不可逆拦截机会损失）。在单交叉条件下，MOS具有阈值释放边界及有界近似误差。

Result: 仿真实验表明，MOS相较参数匹配的可学习门控，将平均扑救率由67.7%提升至74.4%，反转扑救由52.1%提升至66.5%。真机实验进一步展示了在人类射门方向假动作下的快速拦截与释放后方向修正能力。

Conclusion: MOS通过结构化建模"行动—等待"边际并结合物理紧迫性单调性，兼顾了动态困难扑救的提前启动与后续观测变化下的闭环自适应，仿真与真机结果验证了其在动态机器人拦截中的有效性。

Abstract: Robots engaged in fast physical interactions often need to act before the intent of another agent is fully known. Anticipatory goalkeeping illustrates this challenge. Waiting provides more reliable information about the target but reduces the physical opportunity for interception, whereas acting early preserves reachability but requires initiating motion under uncertainty. Given a fixed closed-loop save controller, we formulate the decision of when to initiate motion as a policy-conditional finite-horizon optimal stopping problem. Building on this formulation, we propose monotone optimal stopping (MOS), a structured release-timing method for dynamic robotic interception. The quadruped save policy is trained with reinforcement learning, while MOS determines when the policy should be activated from the evolving robot state and target belief. Rather than predicting a release time or relying on confidence alone, MOS learns the return advantage of acting now over waiting for one more observation. We derive a direct Bellman recursion for this act-versus-wait margin and impose monotonicity only with respect to physical urgency, reflecting the irreversible loss of interception opportunity as time elapses. This structure enables early activation for dynamically demanding saves while preserving closed-loop adaptation when later observations change the predicted target. Under a single-crossing condition, MOS admits a threshold release boundary with a bounded approximation error. Extensive simulation studies show that MOS improves the mean save rate from 67.7% to 74.4% over a parameter-matched learned gate and increases reversal saves from 52.1% to 66.5%. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate rapid interception and post-release direction correction under human shot-direction feints.

</details>


### [165] [RoboTalk: Learning Multi-Robot Communication and Coordination from Multimodal Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23997)
*Dorian Benhamou Goldfajn,Mason Nakamura,Saaduddin Mahmud,Justin Svegliato,Kyle H. Wray,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboTalk，一个用于训练小型视觉语言模型（VLM）在部分可观测条件下实现多机器人协作的合成数据生成管线与数据集，包含 7,950 条多模态轨迹，覆盖 53 个移动操作厨房任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作可提升复杂机器人任务的效率与可扩展性，但部分可观测条件下的协作仍具挑战。自然语言通信是缓解该问题的有效途径，然而在去中心化操作场景中，针对可端侧部署的小型 VLM，如何从多模态演示中联合学习显式的机器人间通信与技能级动作选择，仍缺乏充分研究。

Method: 构建 RoboTalk 合成数据生成管线与数据集：7,950 条多模态轨迹、53 个移动操作厨房任务；数据集包含 leader-follower 规划协议、工具调用（感知、操作、导航、通信）、推理轨迹（rationale traces）以及多样化的自然语言通信内容，用于微调小型 VLM 学习通信与协作。

Result: 在未见过的留出任务上，微调后的开源模型成功率可达 77%，显著优于未微调的开源模型（成功率约 2%）。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了去中心化操作中联合学习显式机器人间通信与技能级动作选择的数据空白，表明通过合成多模态轨迹数据微调小型 VLM 可大幅提升部分可观测多机器人协作任务的成功率，为端侧部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-robot collaboration could enable more efficient and scalable solutions to complex robotic tasks, but collaboration under partial observability remains challenging. Natural-language communication offers a promising approach to coordinating robots under partial observability. However, in decentralized manipulation, jointly learning explicit inter-robot communication and skill-level action selection from multimodal demonstrations remains underexplored for small vision-language models (VLMs) intended for on-device deployment. To address this gap, we introduce RoboTalk, a synthetic data-generation pipeline and dataset of 7,950 multimodal trajectories spanning 53 mobile-manipulation kitchen tasks for training small VLMs to communicate and coordinate. The dataset includes a leader-follower planning protocol, tool calls (perception, manipulation, navigation, and communication), rationale traces, and diversified natural-language communication. Fine-tuning open-source models on our dataset can reach 77% success on novel held-out tasks, a significant improvement over the untuned open source models, which had a success rate of around ~2%.

</details>


### [166] [Imagine-RL: Residual-Confidence-Guided Cross-Attention for World-Model-Augmented VLA Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24033)
*Kejia Hu,Wentong Zhai,Bo Zhao,Shuai Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine-RL 在噪声空间 VLA 后训练中引入动作条件化的视觉-力矩想象，通过冻结的视觉-力矩潜世界模型自回归预测未来紧凑表征，并利用上一窗口预测残差提供 token 级置信度先验以抑制不可靠未来 token，从而让动作评论家同时基于当前证据与预测后果评估候选动作并监督 actor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作中的可靠动作评估不能只看当前观测，还需考虑未来的视觉与接触后果。现有噪声空间强化学习虽能高效引导冻结的 VLA 策略，但其评论家基本忽略了这些未来后果，导致动作评估不充分。

Method: 提出 Imagine-RL：1) 对每个候选动作块，用冻结的视觉-力矩潜世界模型（VTLWM）自回归预测紧凑未来表征，不做像素重建；2) 以当前图像-状态-动作查询注意力机制，同时关注观测历史与预测未来；3) 用上一窗口预测残差作为 token 级置信度先验，抑制不可靠的未来 token；4) 融合当前证据与预测后果，训练动作评论家以评估候选动作并监督 actor；VLA 与 VTLWM 均保持冻结。

Result: 在四个真实机器人任务上、每任务 50 次评估试验中，Imagine-RL 仅使用 100 条 RL 轨迹，平均成功率相比 DSRL 提升 23.6%，相比 VLA 基线提升 60%。

Conclusion: 将动作条件化的视觉-力矩想象引入噪声空间 VLA 后训练，可在不微调 VLA 与世界模型的前提下显著提升接触丰富操作的成功率，说明显式建模未来视觉与接触后果对动作评估至关重要。

Abstract: Reliable action evaluation in contact-rich manipulation requires looking beyond the current observation to future visual and contact consequences. Existing noise-space reinforcement learning efficiently steers a frozen Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, but its critics largely ignore these consequences. We present Imagine-RL, which augments noise-space VLA post-training with action-conditioned visual-torque imagination. For each candidate action chunk, a frozen visual-torque latent world model (VTLWM) autoregressively predicts compact future representations without pixel reconstruction. A current image-state-action query attends to observed histories and predicted futures, while previous-window prediction residuals provide token-wise confidence priors that suppress unreliable future tokens. By combining current evidence with predicted consequences, the action critic better evaluates candidate actions and supervises the actor, while the VLA and VTLWM remain frozen. Across four real-robot tasks with 50 evaluation trials per task, Imagine-RL uses only 100 RL trajectories and improves the average success rate by (23.6%) over DSRL and by (60%) over VLA baselines.

</details>


### [167] [What Matters in Designing World Action Models: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24048)
*Chao Tang,Haoqing Wang,Zilang Cen,Weishi Mi,Wei Xia,Fangcheng Liu,Anda Cheng,Yeqing Shen,Xiaohui Cui,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yehui Tang,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对World Action Models（WAMs）的设计选择进行受控解耦研究，围绕因果结构、世界建模潜空间与训练目标三个核心问题，在三个仿真基准与真实机器人数据上系统比较六种因果结构、八种潜表示与四种训练目标，揭示各设计选择的影响机理并为未来WAM系统开发提供原则性指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM系统多以统一框架打包架构与训练策略等多项设计选择，难以隔离单项贡献、也无法系统比较不同设计方案的优劣，导致对WAM核心设计要素的作用机理缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 采用受控实验（controlled study）解耦设计选择，聚焦三个基本问题：(1)世界建模与动作生成之间应采用何种因果结构；(2)世界建模应在何种潜空间中进行；(3)不同世界-动作建模目标如何影响模型行为与性能。在RoboCasa-GR1、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus三个代表性基准上进行结构受控实验，系统比较6种因果结构、8种潜表示与4种训练目标，并在DROID真实机器人数据上验证关键发现。

Result: 在三个仿真基准中系统比较了六种因果结构、八种潜表示与四种训练目标，覆盖现有WAM的主流设计选择；关键结论在DROID真实机器人数据上得到验证，实现了对设计选择经验效果及其作用机制的分析。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了关于核心设计选择如何影响世界-动作建模的系统性理解，并提炼出可指导未来WAM系统开发的原则。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalizable robot control. Despite the growing number of WAM systems, existing works often introduce unified systems that bundle together multiple design choices, such as architecture and training strategy, making it difficult to isolate individual contributions and systematically compare alternative designs. In this work, we present a controlled study that disentangles these design choices and analyzes not only their empirical effects, but also how and why they shape WAMs. More specifically, we focus on three fundamental questions in building WAMs: (1) what causal structure should govern the interaction between world modeling and action generation? (2) in which latent space should world modeling be performed? and (3) how do different world-action modeling objectives affect model behavior and performance? Through structurally controlled experiments on three representative benchmarks, RoboCasa-GR1, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus, we systematically compare six causal structures, eight latent representations, and four training objectives, covering popular design choices in existing WAMs. We further validate our key findings on real-robot data from the DROID dataset. We hope to provide a systematic understanding of how core design choices affect world-action modeling and what principles can guide the development of future WAM systems.

</details>


### [168] [AquaOrbit: Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Underwater Target Orbiting under Intermittent Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24054)
*Kanzhong Yao,Jinyi Leng,Hao Zhang,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AquaOrbit——带恢复模块的强化学习控制器，用于视觉反馈中断下的水下目标环绕，实现零样本仿真迁移与实机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环绕时视觉反馈间歇性中断会破坏目标相对反馈，导致难以维持协调运动并重新捕获移动目标，现有方法缺乏对检测丢失的恢复能力。

Method: 在Isaac Sim中进行动力学、观测与视觉丢失随机化训练；恢复模块在检测丢失期间利用锁存的视线、横滚与深度参考实现稳定与目标重捕获；随后在Gazebo/ROS2（不同物理引擎与感知扰动）下不重训练评估，并进行全机载感知与控制的零样本实物部署。

Result: 在未见过的变深度三维轨迹上，静态与移动目标各20/20次环绕全部完成；移动目标下平均视线误差较带恢复的PID视觉伺服降低约46%，路径跟踪精度相当；移除恢复模块后完成率降至9/20。实物实现椭圆、8字与变深度圆形轨迹，后两者未在训练中出现；手动遮挡达8s仍保持姿态稳定，姿态引起视场丢失时2.5s内重捕获目标。

Conclusion: 恢复模块对中断视觉下的稳定与重捕获至关重要；该策略具备跨物理引擎、跨感知扰动的零样本泛化与实机迁移能力。

Abstract: Intermittent visual loss disrupts target-relative feedback during underwater orbiting, making it difficult to maintain coordinated motion and reacquire a moving target. We present AquaOrbit, a reinforcement-learning controller with a recovery module for underwater target orbiting under interrupted visual feedback. During detection loss, the recovery module uses latched line-of-sight, roll, and depth references to support stabilization and target reacquisition. We train the controller in Isaac Sim with dynamics, observation, and vision-loss randomization. Evaluated without retraining in Gazebo/ROS2 under a different physics engine and perception perturbations, AquaOrbit completes 20/20 orbiting trials in each of the static- and moving-target conditions on an unseen variable-depth 3-D trajectory. In the moving-target condition, it reduces mean line-of-sight error by approximately 46% relative to a PID-based visual servoing controller with recovery while maintaining comparable path-tracking accuracy; removing the recovery module reduces completion to 9/20. Zero-shot physical deployment with fully onboard perception and control demonstrates elliptical, figure-eight, and variable-depth circular trajectories, including the latter two trajectory types absent from training. The robot maintains attitude stability during manual occlusions lasting up to 8s and reacquires the target within 2.5s in the reported attitude-induced field-of-view loss events.

</details>


### [169] [Toward Human-in-the-Loop Robot Failure Recovery: Bridging Communication Gaps in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24055)
*Promise Ekpo,Teju Vijay,Dhruv Mandalik,Tisha Jain,Arman Ibrayeva,Sunishka Sil,Stefanie A. Tellex,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LD-HRI 游戏、数据集与基准，用听众实际表现来评测求助请求；发现模型生成的请求对新手成功率更高，但专家—新手差距依然显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可通过向旁观者求助从失败中恢复，但有效的人在回路恢复依赖能应对人们知识差异的沟通。既有逆语义研究只用单一听众模型生成请求，未检验听众知识差异。

Method: 构建 LD-HRI 游戏、数据集与基准，通过人类听众的实际表现来评测发言者。在受控的听众信息差异下，评估请求属性、大语言模型发言者以及逆语义请求选择算法。语料含 446 条人类撰写请求与 1,302 次听众试验；另评测 24 条冻结的 LLM 撰写请求，70 名人类听众，共 560 次试验。

Result: 在全部四项任务中，模型撰写请求的新手成功率在描述性统计上更高；但两种来源的请求均存在明显的专家—新手差距，LLM 请求的差距达 16 个百分点。

Conclusion: LD-HRI 使这些差距可测量，为人机与人—智能体交互中设计更稳健的沟通奠定基础。

Abstract: Robots can recover from failures by asking bystanders for help, but effective human-in-the-loop recovery requires communication that accounts for differences in people's knowledge. Prior inverse-semantics work generates requests using a single listener model, leaving differences in listener knowledge untested. We introduce Listener Differences in Human-Robot Interaction (LD-HRI), a game, dataset, and benchmark that evaluates speakers through human listener performance. Our evaluation examines request properties, large language model (LLM) speakers, and inverse-semantics request-selection algorithms under controlled differences in listener information. The corpus contains 446 human-written requests and 1{,}302 listener trials. We additionally evaluated 24 frozen LLM-written requests with 70 human listeners across 560 trials. Novice success is descriptively higher with model-written requests across all four tasks, yet both request sources leave substantial expert--novice gaps, including 16 percentage points for LLM requests. LD-HRI makes these gaps measurable, providing a foundation for designing more robust communication in human-robot and human-agent interaction.

</details>


### [170] [Automatic Labelling for Bimanual Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24059)
*Yupu Lu,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动子任务标注流程，将时间定位交给确定性轨迹分析、语义解释交给视觉语言推理，并在29个真实双臂移动操作任务上验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义化的子任务标签能为长时程策略提供有效上下文，但自动识别可靠的时间边界与宽泛的语义描述仍很困难。

Method: 将同步的运动学信号分割为阶段，对每个阶段进行局部化的视觉语言（VL）推理以描述内容，并分别针对基座、左臂和右臂动作聚合输出；VL推理重复三次以检验一致性。

Result: 在29个真实Galaxea双臂移动操作任务上，重复三次VL推理在所选任务上有87.4%产生相同输出；九名参与者对全部29个任务的评审显示，对时间划分、身体标签和手臂标签的接受度分别为90.5%、90.7%和78.7%。

Conclusion: 分割-VL设计能够生成结构化标注，同时保留异步双臂行为，为更丰富的语义子任务识别与基于状态的验证提供了基础。

Abstract: Semantically meaningful subtask labels can provide useful contexts for long-horizon policies, but automatically identifying both reliable temporal boundaries and broad semantic descriptions for annotations remains difficult. We present an automatic labelling pipeline that assigns temporal localisation to deterministic trajectory analysis and semantic interpretation to vision-language (VL) reasoning. The pipeline segments synchronised kinematic signals into phases, performs phase-localised VL reasoning to describe the contents, and aggregates the outputs for the base, left arm, and right arm actions. We evaluate this pipeline primarily on 29 real Galaxea bimanual mobile-manipulation tasks. Repeating the VL reasoning three times first produces the same output value for 87.4% on selected tasks. A review by nine participants across all 29 tasks then judgements on the labelled phases and shows positive acceptance of temporal divisions (90.5%), body labels (90.7%), and arm labels (78.7%). The results indicate that the segmentation-VL design can produce structured annotations while preserving asynchronous bimanual behaviour, providing a basis for richer semantic subtask identification and state-based verification.

</details>


### [171] [Safety Control of a Hyper-redundant Robot via Adaptive Weighted Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24062)
*Zijian Cai,Kiwan Wong,Wenci Xin,Wei Xiao,Daniela Rus,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出加权控制障碍函数（W-CBFs）框架，在保证安全约束的同时降低超冗余机器人因载荷不均导致的跟踪误差，并通过仿真与实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冗余机器人适合受限空间操作，但在杂乱环境中安全运行仍具挑战；其细长结构易导致载荷分布不均，使沿机身的跟踪误差不均匀。

Method: 提出加权控制障碍函数（W-CBFs）框架，在施加安全约束的同时减小载荷不均引起的跟踪误差。先在圆形路径跟踪任务、不同障碍配置下评估固定权重方法；再基于仿真与实验性能差异研究不同映射函数下的自适应加权策略；最后在矩形区域清洁任务中与人工遥操作对比。

Result: 固定权重下，相比非加权方法，RMS跟踪误差最大降幅为仿真59.6%、实验87.7%。自适应加权使RMS误差进一步分别降低21.9%和8.5%，但当障碍靠近机身时误差增大。清洁任务中，未针对覆盖率优化的自主策略仍取得与人工遥操作相当或更好的覆盖性能，并避免与周围框架碰撞，而人工操作发生碰撞。

Conclusion: W-CBFs框架能有效兼顾安全约束与跟踪精度，自适应加权可进一步提升性能，但障碍贴近机身时存在局限；自主策略在复杂任务中较人工遥操作更安全可靠。

Abstract: Hyper-redundant robots are well suited for confined-space manipulation due to their high dexterity, but safe operation in cluttered environments remains challenging. In addition, their slender structures often lead to uneven load distributions and nonuniform tracking errors along the body.
