Method: 提出SE-LLM-OCP统一框架：LLM负责高层离散机动决策，最优控制模块负责底层车辆动力学与碰撞约束。在线阶段，LLM提出稀疏机动计划，将泊车任务分解为一系列短时域轨迹优化问题，由底层求解器顺序求解；求解失败时，LLM聚合来自求解器与验证阶段的失败证据以指导重规划。离线阶段，SE-LLM-OCP由累积的在线失败驱动，从零自动演化结构化决策知识库。

Result: 在类车车辆模型与差速驱动机器人上进行了仿真验证，结果表明SE-LLM-OCP能在狭窄场景中实现更安全的自动泊车，并证明同一机动表示可迁移至不同运动学平台。

Conclusion: LLM高层推理与最优控制底层约束执行的结合可兼顾语义决策与物理可行性，借助失败驱动的在线重规划与离线知识演化提升狭窄非凸泊车安全性，且机动表示具备跨平台迁移性。

Abstract: Autonomous parking in nonconvex and narrow environments remains challenging. Although optimal-control methods can explicitly enforce vehicle dynamics and collision constraints, nonconvexity compromises solver robustness and can cause failures. Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic reasoning capabilities, but directly generating dense trajectories makes it difficult to guarantee physical feasibility. We introduce SE-LLM-OCP, a unified framework in which LLMs make high-level discrete maneuver decisions, while an optimal-control module enforces low-level vehicle dynamics and collision constraints. Online, the LLM proposes sparse maneuver plans, decomposing the parking task into a sequence of short-horizon trajectory-optimization problems. A low-level solver then sequentially solves optimal-control problems. If the solver fails, the LLM aggregates failure evidence from the solver and validation stages to guide replanning. Offline, SE-LLM-OCP automatically evolves a structured decision-making knowledge base from scratch, driven by accumulated online failures. We validate our proposed framework in simulation on a car-like vehicle model and on a differential-drive robot. Our experimental results show that SE-LLM-OCP enables safer autonomous parking in narrow scenarios and demonstrates transfer of the same maneuver representation to a different kinematic platform.

</details>


### [208] [Feasibility Distance Fields for Heterogeneous Constraints in Robot Configuration Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24632)
*Xijing Cui,Huayan Pu,Jun Luo,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可行性距离场（FDF）：在固定正定关节空间度量下，将异构约束统一为到不可行配置集并集的标量距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人约束由各自指标监控，单位与梯度尺度不可比，缺乏衡量"距离违规还剩多少"的统一度量。

Method: 以固定正定关节空间度量定义到不可行集并集的距离；用经典距离集理论获得1-Lipschitz、几乎处处可微、单位对偶梯度范数；给出外部/自碰撞、关节限位、灵巧度、笛卡尔与任务投影柔顺、负载下关节力矩、动态可操作度的可行公式；同度量下用逐点最小值合成，条件约束保持距离空间，多机约束产生块稀疏梯度；用投影标签加距离损失与Eikonal惩罚训练神经近似。

Result: UR5e与双臂单元仿真，7个场按值、投影、符号、梯度、合成、移动障碍诊断评估：8000配置下最大可行侧割线比0.920，学习梯度范数均值0.994–0.998，投影残差0.011–0.034 rad；24条随机路径中外部与合成碰撞场3 cm内成功率90.4%与91.6%，符号错误率低于2%。

Conclusion: FDF提供统一配置空间裕度；近似误差主要出现在中轴附近与边界稀疏采样处。

Abstract: Robot manipulators are monitored by constraint-specific indicators whose units and gradient scales are not comparable, so they do not provide a common measure of the configuration-space motion remaining before violation. We define the feasibility distance field (FDF) as the distance, under a fixed positive-definite joint-space metric, to the union of infeasible configuration sets. Classical distance-to-set theory gives 1-Lipschitz continuity, almost-everywhere differentiability, and unit dual-gradient norm wherever the nearest projection is unique. The robotics contribution is an admissibility analysis showing when practical constraints define non-empty closed sets. We derive admissible formulations for external and self-collision, joint limits, dexterity, Cartesian and task-projected compliance, joint torque under payload, and dynamic manipulability. Since every field uses the same metric, heterogeneous constraints compose by a pointwise minimum, conditioned constraints retain a fixed distance space, and multi-robot constraints produce block-sparse gradients that identify which robots must react. We generate projection-based labels and train neural approximations with a distance loss and an Eikonal penalty. Simulations on a UR5e and a dual-arm cell evaluate seven fields using value, projection, sign, gradient, composition, and moving-obstacle diagnostics. Across 8,000 configurations, the largest feasible-side secant ratio is 0.920, mean learned gradient norms range from 0.994 to 0.998, and projection residuals range from 0.011 to 0.034 rad. Across 24 random obstacle paths, the external and composed collision fields achieve 90.4% and 91.6% success within 3 cm, with sign-error rates below 2%. The results support a common configuration-space margin and identify approximation errors near medial axes and sparsely sampled boundaries.

</details>


### [209] [Touch2Robot: Robot Touch in the Human Demonstration Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24660)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiaoyang Cheng,Hong Ying,Xiaoying Zhou,Jiaming Jiang,Haoran Guo,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Robot 在人演示采集回路中引入目标机器人手的接触可视化，通过采集人手运动、触觉手套与物体运动，训练物体专属强化学习策略并进行实时重定向蒸馏，从而提升灵巧操作数据采集的质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手演示便于规模化采集操作数据，但其接触在迁移至机器人手时可能不稳定或不可行；直接在目标机器人上采集虽可避免这种不匹配，却大幅提高采集成本。因此需要一个兼顾数据质量与采集效率的方案。

Method: 采集人类操作时的人手运动、触觉手套测量与物体运动数据；以这些记录引导物体专属的强化学习策略，在复现演示的物体运动的同时偏好与所记录人类触觉一致的接触；将所学行为蒸馏为统一的实时重定向器，把传入的人体观测与物体几何映射为机器人手构型；采集过程中将预测的机器人构型与仿真中跟踪的物体位姿同步，重建机器人-物体接触并可视化，帮助演示者调整后续交互。

Result: 在四个真实世界任务上，相比仅视觉反馈，Touch2Robot 将真实机器人回放完成率从 37.9% 提升至 72.1%，并把每次回放成功的演示采集时间由 58.6 s 降至 18.2 s；重建的目标手接触对真实机器人触觉测量达到 44.2% F1；用 Touch2Robot 演示训练的策略使下游 Diffusion Policy 性能相较仅视觉反馈提升 29.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将机器人触觉引入人类演示回路，可同时提升可扩展灵巧操作数据采集的质量与效率。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer a scalable way to collect manipulation data, but their contacts may be unstable or infeasible when transferred to a robot hand. Collecting demonstrations directly on the target robot avoids this mismatch, but substantially increases the cost of data collection. To address this trade-off, we present \textbf{Touch2Robot}, a framework that lets humans collect demonstrations while seeing how the target robot hand would contact the object. We capture human hand motion, tactile-glove measurements, and object motion during human manipulation. These recordings guide object-specific RL policies to reproduce the demonstrated object motion while favoring contacts consistent with the recorded human touch. We distill the learned behaviors into a unified real-time retargeter that maps incoming human observations and object geometry to robot hand configurations. During collection, the predicted robot configuration is synchronized with the tracked object pose in simulation to reconstruct robot-object contacts, which are visualized to help the demonstrator adapt subsequent interactions to the target hand. Across four real-world tasks, Touch2Robot improves average real-robot replay completion from 37.9\% to 72.1\% over visual-only feedback, while reducing the collection time per replay-successful demonstration from 58.6~s to 18.2~s. Reconstructed target-hand contacts achieve 44.2\% F1 against real-robot tactile measurements, and policies trained on Touch2Robot demonstrations improve downstream Diffusion Policy performance by 29.1 percentage points over visual-only feedback. These results show that bringing robot touch into the human demonstration loop improves both the quality and efficiency of scalable dexterous data collection. \textit{Project webpage: \href{https://Touch2Robot.github.io/}{https://Touch2Robot.github.io/}.}

</details>


### [210] [Think Like a World Model, Act Like a VLA: Distilling World-Model Representations into Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24682)
*Trung Dao,Sankalp Yamsani,Jaden Park,Joohyung Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量特征对齐方法，将冻结世界模型的内部表征蒸馏进 VLA 策略，使策略在不增加推理成本的前提下获得世界模型的物理接地先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型缺少关于世界如何响应的目标，鲁棒性受数据覆盖限制；世界模型具备该目标但推理需数秒，无法进入控制回路。作者希望在不承担生成式推理开销的情况下继承世界模型的物理接地能力。

Method: 在常规 VLA 训练中加入一个特征对齐项：先用冻结的世界模型对训练帧做一次前向，缓存其特征，学生策略学习与该缓存一致。训练时不加载教师模型，对齐投影器用后即弃，部署策略与未蒸馏基线完全一致（32 ms、1.86 GB，消费级 RTX 5090）。

Result: 0.8B 学生模型在 LIBERO 达 97.9%；在 RoboCasa-GR1 人形操作上从 48.2% 提升至 50.5%；同一目标可迁移到真实硬件（单臂与双臂平台）。增益在学生规模、骨干、对齐层与教师变化下均保持。

Conclusion: 世界模型关于物理场景的知识存于内部特征，而生成未来只是产生这些特征的目标，因而可只继承接地表征、舍弃生成机制。该增益体现为广泛的表征先验，而非两个特定网络间的脆弱对齐。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map observations to actions with no objective that accounts for how the world responds, so their robustness is bounded primarily by data coverage. World models carry precisely that missing objective and are better grounded for it, yet rolling the future forward costs seconds per decision and rules them out of the control loop. We show the two can be separated. What a world model knows about physical scenes lives in its \emph{internal features}; generating the future is merely the objective that produced them, so the grounding can be inherited while the generative machinery is left behind. We add one feature-alignment term to ordinary VLA training: a frozen world model is run over the training frames once and cached, and the student learns to agree with that cache. No teacher is loaded during training, the projector is discarded after it, and the deployed policy is identical to the undistilled baseline, running in $32$~ms and $1.86$~GB on a consumer RTX~5090, so every gain is attributable to the representation rather than to added capacity or test-time compute. A $0.8$B student reaches $97.9\%$ on LIBERO, improves from $48.2\%$ to $50.5\%$ on RoboCasa-GR1 humanoid manipulation, and the same objective carries over to real hardware, on both a single-arm and a bimanual platform. The gain survives changes of student scale, backbone, alignment layer, and teacher, indicating a broad representational prior rather than a fragile alignment between two particular networks. Project page: https://thaw-vla.trung-dt.com/.

</details>


### [211] [AC-DC: Adaptive Communication for Scalable Dynamic Average Consensus in Multi-Robot Ergodic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24702)
*Robin Inho Kee,Begum Cannataro,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AC-DC 方法，面向多机器人系统在有限通信范围、有限速率与干扰约束下的可扩展分布式动态平均一致性，实现对“与谁通信、何时通信、通信状态的哪些部分”的联合自适应；在动态优先级遍历搜索任务中，较先进基线降低 27.5% 的 AUC 并减少 8.7 倍通信量，且通信负载随机器人数量增加趋近理想集中式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在有限通信范围、有限速率和干扰约束下难以实现可扩展的分布式动态平均一致性；现有方法通常需要全体机器人同步完成通信轮次，且通信负载随团队规模增长，难以扩展到大规模集群。

Method: 提出自适应通信动态平均一致性（AC-DC）：利用本地输入与成功接收的邻居信息，联合自适应决定通信对象（Who）、通信时机（When）以及共识状态的哪些部分（What）进行交换；机器人对之间无需等待所有机器人完成通信轮次即可更新，所选状态消息在固定状态表示下其坐标维度与团队规模无关；将 AC-DC 应用于动态优先级多机器人遍历搜索，其中一路一致性流估计团队访问分布用于运动协调，另一路融合区域量测信息以更新不确定度地图与搜索目标。

Result: 在多达 80 个机器人、12 种设置、每种设置 20 组配对试验中，AC-DC 在归一化协方差迹 AUC 与建模通信负载两项指标上均优于所比较的分布式方法；跨设置平均取得 27.5% 的配对 AUC 降低，通信流量减少 8.7 倍；在 600×600 m 固定区域、120 个机器人的扩展测试中，AC-DC 通信量为 19.3 MB，理想集中式基线为 19.2 MB，且 AC-DC 仍保持点对点架构。

Conclusion: AC-DC 在保持纯点对点架构的前提下，实现了兼具高协调精度与极低通信开销的可扩展动态平均一致性，其通信负载随团队规模增长趋近理想集中式基线，适用于大规模多机器人协同任务。

Abstract: We study scalable peer-to-peer dynamic average consensus (DC) for multi-robot systems under finite-range, finite-rate, and interference-constrained communication. We introduce Adaptive Communication for Dynamic Average Consensus (AC-DC), which jointly adapts Who communicates with whom, When, and over What parts of the consensus state, using local inputs and successfully received neighbor information. Each robot's consensus state estimates the current average of the robots' local inputs. AC-DC updates these estimates as local inputs change and averages the values exchanged between robot pairs. In AC-DC, robot pairs update without waiting for every robot to complete a communication round, and the selected-state messages carry consensus state coordinates independent of team size for a fixed state representation. We apply AC-DC to dynamic-priority multi-robot ergodic search: one consensus stream estimates team visitation for motion coordination, while the other fuses regional measurement information to update uncertainty maps and search targets. Across twelve settings with up to 80 robots and 20 paired trials per setting, AC-DC has the lowest mean (i) normalized covariance-trace area under the curve (AUC) and (ii) attempted modeled communication payload among the compared decentralized methods. Averaged across settings, AC-DC achieves paired AUC reductions of 27.5% relative to state-of-the-art baselines, with 8.7x less communication traffic. As the number of robots increases, we observe that AC-DC's communication payload approaches that of the ideal centralized baseline (one ground compute-station communicating directly with all robots): with 120 robots in a fixed 600 x 600 m scaling test, AC-DC uses 19.3 MB versus 19.2 MB for the ideal centralized baseline, while remaining peer-to-peer.

</details>


### [212] [SPARSER: Sparse Variable Projection by Exploiting Separable Structure in Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24708)
*Nikolas R. Sanderson,Andrew Fishberg,Haoyu Han,Heng Yang,Jonathan P. How,Hanumant Singh,Michael Everett,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARSER：面向规范对称问题的变量投影（VarPro）框架，同时利用稀疏性与可分离性，用无矩阵Schur补算子高效计算降维代价/梯度/Hessian-向量积，在SLAM/SNL/SfM上较SOTA平均加速5–7倍，鲁棒变体较GNC快2–16倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知常需求解大规模非线性最小二乘（NLS）。稀疏性已被广泛用于扩展求解器规模，但另一互补且未被充分利用的结构是"可分离性"：部分变量（如视觉路标）在残差中线性出现，在其余变量（如位姿）固定时可闭式求解。变量投影（VarPro）可解析消去线性变量，得到计算性质更优的降维问题；但其在机器人感知中的应用受制于规范对称性（如对全局平移与旋转的不变性），给标准VarPro带来挑战。

Method: 提出SPARSER（Sparsity Preserving Analytic Reduction for Separable Robotic Perception），一个针对规范对称问题的VarPro框架，联合利用可分离性与稀疏性。构造无矩阵（matrix-free）Schur补算子，用于高效计算降维后的代价、梯度与Hessian-向量积，从而可与迭代NLS求解器集成；刻画了适用问题类别，识别了可进一步解析简化的常见情形，并证明基于IRLS的鲁棒代价可保留大部分可利用结构。

Result: 在合成与真实的SLAM、SNL、SfM基准上，SPARSER在CPU与GPU上平均比SOTA基线快5–7倍，在个别数据集上加速超过40倍。在含外点的多机器人SLAM数据上，鲁棒变体比SOTA的GNC求解器快2–16倍。开源了C++代码与全部数据集。

Conclusion: 通过联合利用可分离性与稀疏性，并以无矩阵Schur补算子处理规范对称性，VarPro可有效应用于机器人感知中的大规模NLS问题，带来显著的精度保持下的效率提升，并开源代码与数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Robotic perception often requires solving large nonlinear least-squares (NLS) problems. While sparsity has been widely exploited to scale solvers, a complementary and underused structure is \emph{separability}: some variables, such as visual landmarks, appear linearly in the residuals and admit a closed-form solution once the remaining variables, such as poses, are fixed. Variable projection (VarPro) exploits this structure by analytically eliminating the linear variables, yielding a reduced problem with favorable computational properties. However, its use in robotic perception has been limited by gauge symmetries, such as invariance to global translations and rotations, which introduce challenges for standard VarPro methods.
  We present SPARSER (\textbf{S}parsity \textbf{P}reserving \textbf{A}nalytic \textbf{R}eduction for \textbf{S}eparable \textbf{R}obotic \textbf{P}erception), a VarPro framework for gauge-symmetric problems that jointly exploits separability and sparsity. Our method constructs a \emph{matrix-free Schur complement operator} for efficient evaluation of reduced costs, gradients, and Hessian-vector products, enabling integration with iterative NLS solvers. We characterize the applicable problem class, identify common cases admitting further analytical simplifications, and show that IRLS-based robust costs preserve most of the exploitable structure.
  Across synthetic and real SLAM, SNL, and SfM benchmarks, SPARSER is on average $5\times$--$7\times$ faster than state-of-the-art baselines on CPU and GPU, with gains exceeding $40\times$ on individual datasets. On outlier-corrupted multi-robot SLAM data, the robust variant is $2\times$--$16\times$ faster than a state-of-the-art GNC solver. We release open-source C++ code and all datasets.

</details>


### [213] [LLM-based Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for MyBuddy Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24742)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的多功能对话式AI知识助手，部署于Raspberry Pi驱动的13轴MyBuddy人形机器人上，融合语音识别、互联网知识检索、对话管理与语音合成，实现智能多轮对话与情感支持型人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在以人为中心的应用中日益普及，但其智能对话与自然交互式知识辅助能力受限于传统规则式对话系统、预定义响应和有限的知识库，亟需突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 利用LLM实现自然、自适应、情境感知的人机交互（HRI），将其与实时语音识别、通过可扩展互联网引擎（如Wikipedia、arXiv）的知识检索、灵活对话管理以及自然语音合成相集成，构建支持多轮连续对话和情感支持的机器人知识助手。

Result: 系统在Raspberry Pi驱动的13轴MyBuddy人形机器人上完成实现，能够实现更智能的多轮连续对话以及高级的情感支持型人机交互。

Conclusion: 将LLM驱动的语言理解与AI推理同机器人硬件及多模态交互组件结合，可有效克服传统对话系统的局限，为人形机器人提供自然、情境感知且知识丰富的交互能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly being popular and developed for human-centered applications, yet their ability to provide intelligent conversations and natural interactive knowledge assistance remains constrained by traditional rule-based dialogue systems, pre-defined responses and limited knowledge repositories. Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful foundation for enabling natural, adaptive, and context-aware Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), which provides a significant opportunity to address such limitations by enabling robots to understand natural speech language, reason over complicated queries, maintain high-quality conversational context, and generate knowledge-rich responses. In this work, we originally present and implement an LLM-based versatile Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for the Raspberry-Pi-powered 13-Axis MyBuddy humanoid robot, which integrates LLM-driven language understanding and AI reasoning with real-time speech recognition, knowledge retrieval via extensible access of internet engines (e.g., Wikipedia, arXiv), flexible dialogue management, and natural speech synthesis to enable much more intelligent multi-turn continuous conversations and advanced emotional-support Human-Robot Interaction.

</details>


### [214] [Beyond Visual Quality: A Study of Test-Time Planning with World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24745)
*Jianhao Yuan,Yu Yuan,Benjamin Ramtoula,Lukas Vierling,Paul Newman,Lars Kunze,Philip Torr,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实证考察世界动作模型（WAM）利用想象未来指导动作选择的规划潜力，发现候选采样中存在可观的选择增益（oracle 上界从 68.9% 提升至 79.2%），但现有基于视觉质量、物理一致性、任务进展的选择器仅能回收其中很小一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型同时生成动作与视觉预测，理论上可通过采样多个动作、比较想象结果来规划。但如何用想象未来来指导动作选择尚不明确，需要量化这一规划潜力并检验现有选择准则的有效性。

Method: 1) 用 oracle 上界估计选择潜力：在同一状态下采样多个候选动作，选取真实结果最优者。2) 将基于视觉质量、物理一致性、任务进程的选择器作为受控干预进行测试。3) 通过同一状态的反事实分支分析，检验采样动作是否导致不同结果、差异是否在预测中可见、评分是否识别这些差异；在不同轨迹阶段进行完整动作执行的进一步评估。

Result: 同状态受控分析中，oracle 选择将成功率从均匀随机选择的 68.9% 提升至 79.2%。测试的选择器虽部分提升观测成功率，但增益不均衡，且距机会上界仍有很大差距。反事实分支显示选择机会集中在初始候选集合中相对少数的决策上，动作分散度与结果覆盖度并不必然同步增长。在完整动作执行的分阶段评估中，所测评分仍只能回收很少的提升，仅学习价值函数带来小幅增益。

Conclusion: 产出有后果的动作选择与在生成未来中识别这些选择是两种不同能力，二者需被区分；应通过预测对决策的有用性而非仅凭视觉质量来评估 WAM。

Abstract: World action models generate actions together with visual predictions of their consequences. These paired outputs create the potential for planning by sampling multiple actions from one state, comparing their imagined outcomes, and choosing the action with the most promising predicted outcome. However, how to use imagined futures to guide action selection remains unclear. We examine this planning potential empirically. First, we estimate an oracle upper bound on selection by choosing the sampled candidate whose realised outcome is best. In a controlled same-state analysis, this choice raises success from 68.9% under uniform random selection to 79.2%. We then test selectors based on visual quality, physical consistency, and task progression as controlled interventions. Some tested selectors yield higher observed success, but the gains are uneven and the matched selectors leave much of the measured opportunity unrecovered. To investigate this gap, we examine whether sampled actions lead to different outcomes, whether these differences are visible in the predictions, and whether a score recognises them. Counterfactual branching from the same states shows that selection opportunity is concentrated in relatively few decisions in the initial candidate sets. Action spread and outcome coverage need not increase together. In a further evaluation across trajectory phases with complete action execution, the tested scores again recover little of the available improvement despite a small gain from learned value. These findings distinguish producing consequential action choices from recognising them in generated futures, motivating the evaluation of WAM predictions through their usefulness for decisions rather than visual quality alone.

</details>


### [215] [D-JEPA: A Decision-Aligned Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24749)
*Shuaijun Liu,Chengyu Wu,Qifu Wen,Feiyang You,Chenglong Zhang,Shuyang Hao,Xi Lin,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 D-JEPA，一种决策对齐的潜在世界模型，通过学习已执行结果中候选未来之间的决策相关关系，弥合预测精度与动作选择效果之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型能预测动作后果，但预测准确并不保证潜在距离能反映候选动作的实际执行成功率。作者识别出"决策局部预测鸿沟"：在少数竞争执行的未来中，被预测更接近目标的候选却可能产生比备选更差的真实结果。

Method: D-JEPA 从已执行结果中学习候选未来间的决策相关关系。采用有界、置换等变算子，对目标相对的预测特征与序数证据进行联合推理，在最关乎动作选择的区域精炼预训练预测几何。通过受限预测器适配与共享序数接口，将这种对齐扩展到互补的预测几何上；并将学到的决策结构体现在 JEPA 兼容的未来表示中，支持原生潜在距离规划部署。

Result: 在潜在控制、操作、预训练动作生成模型、物理机器人和自动驾驶等评测中改进动作选择：PushT 成功率 87.89%，RoboTwin 平均提升 15.04 个百分点，物理机器人任务提升 17 个百分点。

Conclusion: 决策相关的关系结构可作为连接预测式世界建模与有效控制的直接桥梁。

Abstract: Latent world models predict the consequences of actions, but accurate prediction does not guarantee that latent distance reflects which candidate will execute successfully. We identify a decision-local prediction gap: among the few futures competing for execution, a candidate predicted closer to the goal can produce a worse realized outcome than an available alternative. We introduce D-JEPA, a decision-aligned latent world model that learns decision-relevant relations among candidate futures from executed outcomes. A bounded, permutation-equivariant operator jointly reasons over goal-relative predictive features and ordinal evidence, refining pretrained predictive geometry where action choices are most consequential. Restricted predictor adaptation and a shared ordinal interface extend this alignment across complementary predictive geometries. D-JEPA further realizes the learned decision structure in JEPA-compatible future representations, enabling deployment through native latent-distance planning. Evaluations across latent control, manipulation, pretrained action-producing models, physical robots and autonomous driving demonstrate improved action selection, including 87.89% success on PushT, a 15.04-point average gain on RoboTwin, and a 17-point gain on physical robot tasks. These results establish decision-relevant relational structure as a direct bridge between predictive world modeling and effective control.

</details>


### [216] [A Switched Adaptive Control Framework for Aerial Manipulators Under Dynamic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24761)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Saksham Gupta,Amitabh Sharma,Sarthak Mishra,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy,Simone Baldi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于切换的自适应控制框架，用于空中机械臂，在无需先验知识的情况下应对系统动态突变与状态相关不确定性，并保证过渡阶段稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在执行复杂交互任务时会出现动态转换，导致系统动力学发生突变或不连续，适合采用切换方法；但现有空中操作方法并非为切换工况设计，且难以处理飞行器与机械臂之间的紧密耦合以及由此带来的状态相关不确定性。

Method: 提出基于切换的自适应控制框架，不依赖飞行器—机械臂耦合及状态相关不确定性的先验知识；通过提供一类切换信号，刻画能够保证系统保持稳定的过渡阶段。

Result: 对比实验验证了所提基于切换的框架相较现有方法更有效。

Conclusion: 该切换式自适应框架可在系统动态变化下保证稳定操作，为空中机械臂在动态过渡中的控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators represent the forefront of aerial robotics. Although potentially capable of complex interaction tasks, controlling aerial manipulators throughout the dynamic transitions occurring during task execution presents significant challenges. Abrupt or discontinuous changes in system dynamics generated by the transitions suggest the use of a switched approach, yet the available aerial manipulation methods are not designed for coping with switched regimes. In addition, most available methods fall short in coping with the tight couplings between the aerial vehicle and the manipulator, as well as in coping with the state-dependent uncertainties arising from the difficulty in modeling such couplings. We propose a switched-based adaptive control framework for aerial manipulators not relying on a priori knowledge of the vehicle-manipulator couplings and of state-dependent uncertainties. To guarantee stable manipulation despite changes in system dynamics, the framework provides a class of switching signals characterizing those transition phases for which the system is guaranteed to remain stable. Comparative experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed switched-based framework over the state of the art.

</details>


### [217] [H2RBench: A Real-to-Sim Benchmark for Evaluating Human-to-Robot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24778)
*Chuyang Xiao,Haotian Zhan,Sriram Krishna,Peilin Meng,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 H2RBench，一个基于 Real2Sim 的标准化基准，用于公平比较人-机器人（H2R）操作策略迁移方法，并验证仿真性能对真实机器人性能的预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 H2R 迁移方法在任务套件、场景布局、物体实例和机器人监督量等设置上各不相同，导致难以进行公平比较，缺乏统一的评估协议。

Method: 构建 H2RBench，基于真实人类视频示范与仿真机器人示范的标准化协议，涵盖四个具有多样化交互需求的操作任务；并在此基准上评估多种采用不同具身差距弥合策略的代表性 H2R 迁移方法。

Result: 系统刻画了各方法随人类示范数据量的扩展规律，发现不同方法利用额外人类数据的能力差异显著；仿真性能对真实机器人性能总体具有预测性，Pearson 相关系数 r = 0.89，Spearman 相关系数 ρ = 0.85，平均最大排名违例（MMRV）为 0.06。

Conclusion: H2RBench 为真实部署前的 H2R 方法对比评估提供了实用且可扩展的基准。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies from human video demonstrations constitutes a promising avenue for scalable robot learning. However, comparing different human-to-robot (H2R) transfer methods remains challenging, as existing approaches are evaluated under different settings, including differing task suites, scene layouts, object instances, and amounts of robot supervision. To address this challenge, we present H2RBench, a Real2Sim benchmark for evaluating H2R transfer methods. H2RBench provides a standardized protocol built on real human video demonstrations and simulated robot demonstrations, and includes four manipulation tasks spanning diverse interaction requirements. We evaluate multiple representative H2R transfer methods, each adopting a different strategy for bridging the embodiment gap. Using H2RBench, we systematically characterize how each method scales with the amount of human demonstrations, revealing that methods differ substantially in their ability to leverage additional human data. We further show that simulation performance is broadly predictive of real-world robot performance, with an overall Pearson correlation of r = 0.89, Spearman correlation of \r{ho} = 0.85 and Mean Maximum Rank Violation (MMRV) of 0.06 across method-task configurations. These results establish H2RBench as a practical and scalable benchmark for comparative H2R evaluation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [218] [Uranus: Building the Next-Generation Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24815)
*Wenkang Qin,Yukun Zhou,Noah Shen,Jisong Cai,Dongxiao Mao,Baicheng Li,Yue Zhang,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uranus：基于关节轨迹条件的自回归扩散模型的数据驱动机器人仿真器，支持流式开放式生成、低延迟(24 FPS)与可扩展多视角机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展仿真对机器人数据生成、策略训练、评估与安全迭代至关重要，但真实交互成本高昂，而传统仿真器需要大量人工建模。

Method: 提出 Uranus 数据驱动机器人仿真器，核心为关节轨迹条件（joint-trajectory-conditioned）的自回归扩散模型：在线接收未来关节位置轨迹，逐步自回归生成每个潜在帧（对应四帧RGB），无固定生成时长；经推理优化实现低延迟。

Result: 实现 24 FPS 的生成速度；提供统一接口，支持跨多种机器人形态与相机配置的同步多视角生成；在分布内与分布外数据上完成定量与定性评测。

Conclusion: Uranus 具备流式开放式生成、低延迟与可扩展机器人控制能力，评测客观并明确其当前局限，开源代码与模型权重以支持社区应用与研究。

Abstract: Scalable simulation is essential for robot data generation, policy training, evaluation, and safe iteration, yet real-world interaction is costly and conventional simulators require labor-intensive construction. We present Uranus, a data-driven robot simulator built around a joint-trajectory-conditioned autoregressive diffusion model. Uranus offers three key capabilities: (1) streaming, open-ended rollout, which receives future joint-position trajectories online and autoregressively generates one latent frame per step, corresponding to four RGB frames, without a fixed horizon; (2) low-latency generation, achieving 24 FPS after inference optimization; and (3) scalable, extensible robot control, providing a unified interface for synchronized multi-view generation across diverse robot embodiments and camera configurations. We conduct comprehensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data, providing an objective assessment of Uranus and clearly identifying its current limitations. We release the code and model weights to empower the community with practical tools and insights.

</details>


### [219] [Minimum Time Trajectories for a Car-Like Mobile Robot Moving with Rigid Wheels Under Non-Sliding Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24832)
*Joseph Ben-Asher,Elon Rimon,Leeor Ravina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无遮挡环境下类车移动机器人最小时间轨迹问题，在运动学模型时间最优路径基元的基础上，基于动力学引入非滑动约束，新增三种非滑动路径基元，与原十二种基元共同构成类车机器人时间最优轨迹，并给出近似解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作针对运动学类车机器人求解了无遮挡环境下的时间最优轨迹，但运动学模型假设车轮与地面接触处为纯滚动，这需要前后轮的非滑动约束，而该约束只能由机器人动力学处理，因此需要将非滑动约束纳入问题建模以提升轨迹的可行性。

Method: 以具有前进与后退速度、受有界前轮加速度与前轮转向速率限制的类车机器人为对象；基于机器人动力学表述非滑动约束，并在运动学模型的时间最优路径基元上增补三种与非滑动约束相关的路径基元；对三种非滑动路径基元给出近似解析解；通过代表性机动的算例分析其影响。

Result: 得到由三种非滑动路径基元与运动学模型十二种路径基元共同组成的类车机器人时间最优轨迹，并提供了非滑动路径基元的近似解析解；算例展示了非滑动约束如何影响类车机器人的时间最优轨迹。

Conclusion: 将基于动力学的非滑动约束纳入类车机器人时间最优轨迹构造，可使轨迹更符合实际滚动条件，并扩展了原有运动学时间最优路径基元体系。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum time trajectoriesvof a car-like mobile robot navigating in an obstacle free environment. The robot, with forward and backward speeds, is controlled by bounded front-wheels acceleration and limited front-wheels steering rate. The paper extends previous results which solved this problem for the kinematic car-like robot. However, the kinematic model assumes pure rolling at the wheels ground contacts. This assumption requires non-sliding constraints for the front and rear wheels that can only be handled by the robot dynamics. This paper formulates the non-sliding constraints based on the robot dynamics then augments the kinematic model time-optimal path primitives with three new path primitives associated with the non-sliding constraints. The three non-sliding path primitives together with the kinematic model twelve path primitives form the car-like robot time optimal trajectories. Approximate analytic solutions for the non-sliding path primitives are also provided. Examples study the time-optimal path primitives along representative maneuvers, illustrating how the non-sliding constraints influence the time optimal trajectories of the car-like robot.

</details>


### [220] [PredActor: Predictive Action Diffusion for Steerable Onboard Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24840)
*Lei Ye,Haibo Gao,Yitang Li,Peng Xu,Zetong Jing,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Ziqi Han,Xue Wang,Jianhua Sun,Cewu Lu,Hao Zhao,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PredActor 是一种仅用本体感受观测、直接执行的预测式动作扩散策略，联合生成可执行动作与内部未来状态轨迹，支持文本条件引导和测试时目标引导，并在仿真与 Unitree G1 实机上验证了实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将扩散模型的灵活运动生成能力转化为可反馈响应的人形控制存在困难：分层系统依赖可能超出跟踪器能力的参考轨迹，恢复与物理执行交给跟踪器；纯动作扩散缺少显式未来状态轨迹，难以在测试时施加运动目标；联合状态-动作扩散虽提供该表示，但代表性控制器依赖特权全身状态，且学习式行为选择与测试时运动引导的支持较为零散。

Method: 提出 PredActor，一种预测式动作扩散策略。以本体感受历史与可选任务上下文为条件，联合生成可执行动作与内部未来状态轨迹。采用无分类器引导增强文本条件行为，用分类器引导将预测状态导向测试时目标。仅执行动作，不使用独立运动参考跟踪器，也不将外部估计的全身状态作为策略输入。为便于机载部署，引入滚动去噪与保计算的运行时优化。

Result: 仿真中 PredActor 到达全部 15 个目标点，文本检索得分为 0.580，条件动作扩散为 0.373，抗扰动存活表现相近。运行时优化将完整回调在 Jetson Orin NX 上降至中位数 16.790 ms、p95 19.383 ms，均低于 20 ms 控制周期。在 Unitree G1 上部署，仿真与实机评测展示了文本条件运动、扰动响应、手柄控制和语义插值。

Conclusion: PredActor 将互补的引导能力整合进单一直接执行策略，仅凭本体感受即可实现测试时运动引导与行为选择，并满足机载实时控制要求，具备实机部署可行性。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer flexible motion generation, but translating this flexibility into feedback-responsive humanoid control remains challenging. Hierarchical systems steer motion through references that may exceed a separate tracker's capabilities, leaving recovery and physical execution largely to the tracker. Action-only diffusion generates actions directly but lacks an explicit future-state trajectory for test-time motion objectives. Joint state-action diffusion provides this representation, yet representative controllers often depend on privileged full-body states, and support for learned behavior selection and test-time motion steering remains fragmented. We present PredActor, a predictive action diffusion policy that brings these complementary steering capabilities into one directly executed policy using proprioceptive observations. Conditioned on proprioceptive history and optional task context, PredActor jointly generates executable actions and an internal future-state trajectory. Classifier-free guidance strengthens text-conditioned behavior, while classifier guidance steers predicted states toward test-time objectives. Only actions are executed, without a separate motion-reference tracker or externally estimated full-body states as policy inputs. In simulation, PredActor reaches all 15 destination targets and achieves a text retrieval score of 0.580, compared with 0.373 for conditional action diffusion, with similar observed disturbance survival. To make this guided policy practical onboard, rolling denoising and computation-preserving runtime optimizations reduce the complete callback to 16.790 ms median and 19.383 ms p95 on a Jetson Orin NX, both below the 20 ms control period. We deploy PredActor on a Unitree G1; evaluations across simulation and physical hardware demonstrate text-conditioned motion, disturbance response, joystick control, and semantic interpolation.

</details>


### [221] [CAST: Collision-Aware Assembly with Construction Robots using Simultaneous Trajectory Estimation and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24841)
*Karthik Shaji,Chisung Kim,John D'Amato,Edvard Bruun,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单因子图的轨迹估计与规划框架，融合机器人状态测量、显式碰撞约束与学习到的缆索约束，用于多机器人协同建筑施工中的高维运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在建筑施工中具有减少人员暴露于危险任务的潜力，但其工作空间配置空间高维，且需满足涉及其他机器人、装配对象和工作空间边界的碰撞避免约束；同时重型机器人系统存在刚性引起的振动不确定性，需要一种能适应变化工作空间并实现同步高维运动规划的方案。

Method: 采用单一因子图统一进行轨迹估计与规划，将测量得到的机器人状态与显式的碰撞约束、学习得到的缆索约束共同纳入图中，从而支持不断变化的工作空间，并实现同步的高维机器人运动规划，同时处理重型机器人系统振动引起的刚度相关不确定性。

Result: 该框架在一个柱梁（post-and-lintel）结构建造任务中得到验证：一台机械臂作为木材抓取器，另一台机器人作为钉 fastener 完成紧固作业，实验表明框架取得成功。

Conclusion: 单一因子图可有效统一多机器人施工中的轨迹估计与规划，兼顾碰撞避免、缆索约束与振动不确定性，并在实际建筑施工任务中验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems have shown increasing viability in construction due to their ability to execute high-precision actions while reducing human exposure to hazardous tasks. However, these environments have high-dimensional configuration spaces and possess substantial collision-avoidance constraints, which include other robots, assembly objects, and workspace boundaries. We utilize a single factor graph for trajectory estimation and planning that incorporates measured robot states together with explicit collision and learned cable constraints. This supports changing workspaces and enables synchronized, high-dimensional robot motion planning while accounting for the stiff, vibration-induced uncertainty of heavy robotic systems. We demonstrate the success of our framework on the construction of a post-and-lintel structure using one robot arm as a timber gripper, and a second robot as a nail-fastener.

</details>


### [222] [Range-Aided SLAM Initialization Exploiting Accurate Heading Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24846)
*Isabel Lougheed,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向测距辅助SLAM（RA-SLAM）的新型初始化方法：在已知高精度航向的前提下，先用GTRS求解应答器相对AUV的位置，再通过线性最小二乘联合估计应答器与AUV位置，为非线性RA-SLAM提供可靠初值，并用真实AUV数据集验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用SLAM问题具有可分离结构，在机器人航向已知时，路标与机器人位置可线性求解。AUV导航场景中惯导可提供高精度航向，因而可将该结构用于测距应答器与AUV位置的求解，但非线性测距下的初值问题仍需可靠方法，以避免非线性RA-SLAM陷入局部最优。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一步，基于非线性测距量，将应答器相对AUV的位置求解建模为广义信赖域子问题（GTRS）；第二步，利用求得的应答器相对位置与已知AUV航向，通过线性最小二乘联合估计应答器与AUV的绝对位置。所得估计结合高精度航向，作为通用非线性RA-SLAM问题的初值。

Result: 在真实AUV数据集上进行了验证，该数据集包含长基线（LBL）测距量以及由惯性导航系统（INS）提供的高精度航向信息，结果表明所提方法有效。

Conclusion: 在高精度航向可得（如AUV导航）的条件下，GTRS加线性最小二乘的两步方案可为RA-SLAM提供可靠的初始化，适用于实际LBL测距与INS航向数据。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel initialization method for range-aided simultaneous localization and mapping (RA-SLAM). The general SLAM problem has a well-known separable structure where landmark and robot positions can be solved for in a linear fashion given known robot headings. This paper considers the case where highly accurate heading information is available, which is typical of autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) navigation, to solve for the range transponder and robot positions. The proposed approach consists of two steps. First, using a generalized trust region subproblem (GTRS), the positions of the transponders relative to the AUV are solved for given the nonlinear range measurements. Second, the relative transponder positions and the known heading of the AUV are used to estimate the transponder and AUV positions by solving a linear least-squares problem. These transponder and AUV position estimates, combined with the highly accurate heading information, provide a reliable initialization method for the general nonlinear RA-SLAM problem. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is tested on a real-world AUV dataset where long baseline (LBL) range measurements are provided in concert with highly accurate heading information provided by an inertial navigation system (INS).

</details>


### [223] [SE(3) Neural Potential Fields for 6-DoF Trajectory Planning Directly from Images Without Explicit 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24864)
*Jeffrey Eiyike,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从已定位RGB图像中学习的SE(3)神经势场，用导航函数（自由空间中到抓取位姿的测地距离）监督，消除了传统势场的局部极小值与贴障问题，实现无碰撞6-DoF抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中达到6-DoF抓取位姿需要无碰撞轨迹，传统方法通过三维重建再规划，代价是精度与算力开销；直接从图像学习势场则摆脱重建依赖，但继承了人工势场的固有缺陷：吸引与排斥梯度相消处下降轨迹会擦过障碍而非绕行，甚至停滞于目标之前。

Method: 提出SE(3)神经势场，从已定位（posed）的RGB图像学习；训练时以导航函数监督，即从同一批图像中恢复的自由空间内到抓取位姿的测地距离。

Result: 在两个桌面场景、障碍阻挡的起始位姿下于UR10上执行：势场从每个起点均在3 cm内收敛至抓取位姿，所有执行路径相对真值几何均无碰撞，而仅用图像监督时为25%和0%；平均间隙从不足1 cm提升至8.6-8.8 cm；机械臂连杆接触从执行构型的20.6-50.4%降至2.7-5.5%；两场景抓取成功率分别为90.0%和40.0%，剩余失败源于笛卡尔执行器的拒绝而非势场失效；规划约2 s，而基于同批图像重建的RRT*需67-133 s；但在统一离线测试框架下两者相当，部署时的差距主要来自对稠密重建做碰撞检测的开销，而非规划器复杂度。

Conclusion: 以测地距离导航函数监督的SE(3)神经势场可同时消除局部极小值与贴障下降两类失效，在真实机器人上实现高成功率、无碰撞的杂乱环境抓取，并显著降低在线规划时间；相较于重建加规划的方法，其优势主要来自避免稠密重建的碰撞检测开销。

Abstract: Reaching a 6-DoF grasp pose in clutter requires a collision-free trajectory, conventionally obtained by reconstructing the scene in 3D and planning inside that reconstruction, at the cost of its accuracy and compute. Potential fields learned directly from images remove that dependency but inherit the classical weakness of artificial potential fields: where attractive and repulsive gradients cancel, the descent grazes the obstacle instead of going around it, and can stall short of the goal. We present an SE(3) neural potential field learned from posed RGB images and supervised with a navigation function, the geodesic distance to the grasp through free space recovered from those same images during training, which removes both failures. On two tabletop scenes, from obstacle-blocked starts executed on a UR10, the field converges within 3 cm of the grasp from every start and every path it executes is collision-free against the ground-truth geometry, against 25% and 0% under image supervision alone; mean clearance rises from under a centimeter to 8.6-8.8 cm and arm-link contacts fall from 20.6-50.4% to 2.7-5.5% of executed configurations. Executed grasp success is 90.0% and 40.0% on the two scenes, the residual failures being refusals of the Cartesian executor rather than of the field. Planning takes about 2 s against 67-133 s for RRT* on a reconstruction of the same images, though under a common offline harness the two are comparable: the deployed margin is the cost of collision-checking a dense reconstruction, not planner complexity.

</details>


### [224] [DualWAM: Dual-System World Action Models for Asynchronous Global Planning and Local Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24868)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yuntian Deng,Kai Liu,Yizhou Zhou,Yizhou Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao,He Wang,Zhizheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双系统世界动作模型（WAM），通过解耦全局规划与局部精化，在保持长时域世界-动作生成的同时实现高频闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM因未来视觉预测计算开销大，依赖长动作块摊薄推理成本，牺牲了闭环响应性。

Method: 双系统异步共享去噪轨迹：System 2周期性对广域世界-动作块做高噪声双向去噪以建立全局规划；System 1仅用腕部观测，从中间去噪状态截取时间对齐的短窗口完成低噪声精化，并持续引入最新交互反馈。

Result: 在Franka与Galbot零样本操作任务上，成功率平均超最强基线4.5个百分点，关键路径加速16.6倍；角色匹配的自我中心与UMI数据提升成功率14个百分点；支持低通信开销的边缘-云部署。

Conclusion: 解耦全局规划与局部精化可兼顾广域世界-动作生成与高频闭环响应，并天然适配边缘-云部署。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate robot actions and predict future world states, transferring priors from video pretraining to robot control. However, future visual prediction is computationally expensive, so existing WAMs often rely on long action chunks to amortize inference cost across control steps, at the cost of closed-loop responsiveness. We present \method, a dual-system WAM that preserves broader-horizon world-action generation while enabling high-frequency closed-loop action updates by decoupling global planning and local refinement. \systwo periodically performs high-noise bidirectional denoising over a broader world-action chunk to establish a global plan, while wrist-only \sysone extracts a temporally aligned short window from the intermediate denoising state and completes low-noise refinement using the latest wrist observations, which provide action-aligned cues about local geometry, motion, and contact during interaction. The two systems operate asynchronously along a shared denoising trajectory: each global plan is reused across multiple local updates, while \sysone repeatedly incorporates fresh interaction feedback. Across zero-shot manipulation tasks on Franka and Galbot, \method improves success over the strongest evaluated baseline by 4.5 percentage points on average, while achieving a 16.6$\times$ critical-path speedup. Further studies show that role-matched egocentric and UMI data improve success by 14 percentage points, and that the decoupled design naturally supports edge--cloud deployment with substantially lower communication overhead than the baseline.

</details>


### [225] [Steerable and Reactive Grasping Through Modular Design with a Three-Point Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24896)
*Andrew Nguyen,Yonghyeon Lee,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种模块化灵巧抓取框架，通过"三点接触接口"将全局几何推理与局部接触控制解耦：先从预计算的抓取可供性热图中采样接触三元组，再由基于模型的反应式控制器完成接近，最后用仅依赖本体感觉的强化学习策略稳定抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取需同时解决"抓哪里、如何到达、如何保持稳定接触"三个问题。端到端方法将全部环节耦合，泛化性与可解释性差；纯几何规划难以应对到达与感知误差。作者希望以简洁接口分离全局几何推理与局部接触控制，从而兼顾泛化性与稳定性。

Method: 1) 从预计算的抓取可供性热图中采样接触三元组作为三点接口，可结合物体几何与可选语言指令；2) 模型驱动的反应式控制器跟踪目标、避碰并引导机械手靠近所选接触点；3) 最后数厘米由RL策略接管，仅以手指关节状态与近期动作为输入（无目标点、视觉与物体几何），依靠本体感觉反馈修正误差并稳定抓取，单一策略跨物体与抓取构型共享。

Result: 仿真中与squeeze基线及端到端基线比较抓取-提升成功率，刻画到达收敛性，并展示抓取转向（grasp steering）能力；硬件上在两个训练物体和一个未见物体上验证完整流程。

Conclusion: 模块化设计有效：以几何信息引导到达、以局部反馈确保抓取稳定；紧凑的三点接口使单一RL策略可跨物体与抓取构型复用。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping requires deciding where to grasp, reaching the target, and maintaining stable contact. We connect these stages through a compact three-point interface that separates global geometric reasoning from local contact control. Given object geometry and optional language commands, our framework samples contact triples from a precomputed grasp-affordance heatmap. A model-based reactive controller tracks the object, avoids collisions, and guides the hand toward the selected contacts. In the final centimeters, a Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy uses proprioceptive feedback to refine and stabilize the grasp despite reaching and perception errors. It observes only finger joint states and its recent actions, with no target points, visual observations, or object geometry, so a single policy is shared across objects and grasp configurations. In simulation, we compare grasp-and-lift success against squeeze and end-to-end baselines, characterize reaching convergence, and demonstrate grasp steering; hardware demonstrations on two training objects and one unseen object illustrate the full pipeline. Our modular framework uses geometry to guide the reach and local feedback to secure the grasp.

</details>


### [226] [Visuomotor Robotic Pruning in Planar Orchards Using Hybrid Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24906)
*Abhinav Jain,Cindy Grimm,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出端到端流水线学习闭环视觉运动控制器用于机器人修剪，完全基于仿真与合成数据训练，实现零样本真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 休眠期果树修剪劳动强度大但对现代高产果园至关重要；平面化树形系统（V-Trellis苹果、UFO樱桃）的修剪亟需自动化，而仿真到现实的零样本迁移是降低数据采集成本的关键挑战。

Method: 构建端到端流水线：合成生成平面果园树网格、搭建基于物理的果园仿真器、通过运动规划自动采集成功修剪轨迹、并用新型混合强化学习算法（结合离线示范与在线仿真回放）训练策略。控制器仅使用腕部相机的光流输入，无需完整三维重建，持续引导切割器穿越杂乱枝干环境到达指定切点并保持正确刀具姿态。

Result: 在超过3,000个修剪点的仿真任务空间评估中，V-Trellis苹果成功率49.9%，UFO樱桃46.0%；38次物理试验（28次商业与实验果园户外试验、10次室内实验室测试）验证了零样本仿真到现实迁移；实验室真机测试中策略优于经典RRT-Connect基线。

Conclusion: 该工作证明纯仿真训练的闭环视觉运动策略可零样本部署于真实果园修剪任务，为平面树形系统的机器人修剪提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Dormant tree pruning is labor-intensive yet essential for maintaining modern high-productivity fruit orchards. In this work, we focus on pruning of modern planar tree training systems - V-Trellis apples and UFO cherries - where trunks and primary branches are trained into approximately planar walls. We introduce an end-to-end pipeline to learn a closed-loop visuomotor controller for robotic pruning. This controller is trained entirely using simulation and synthetically generated data and deployed in real orchards in a zero-shot manner. The pipeline comprises synthetic generation of planar orchard tree meshes, construction of a physics-based orchard simulator, automated collection of successful pruning trajectories via motion planning, and policy learning with a novel hybrid reinforcement-learning algorithm that combines offline demonstrations with online simulated rollouts. The controller uses optical-flow inputs from a wrist-mounted camera - avoiding the need for full 3D-reconstruction - and continuously guides the cutter through cluttered branch environments to a specified cutpoint with correct tool orientation. In exhaustive simulated task-space evaluations over 3,000 pruning points, the policy attains 49.9% success on V-Trellis apples and 46.0% on UFO cherries. We validate the learned controller across 38 physical trials - comprising 28 outdoor field trials in commercial and experimental orchards and 10 indoor laboratory tests - demonstrating zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. The learned policy also outperforms a classical RRT-Connect baseline on physical hardware in laboratory trials.

</details>


### [227] [Learning to Drive on Mars: Visual Multimodal Traversability Estimation for Off-World Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24952)
*Darren Chiu,Cole Wilson,Andrei Tumbar,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Steven Myint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布火星车500 sol、45km多模态数据集，并提出不确定性感知的可通行性估计框架，在火星2020数据集上实现最优性能并可集成至ENav路径规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星自主导航需在多样且视觉困难环境中区分可通行地形，但地外环境学习型导航受限于缺乏大规模数据集，因此需要构建真实火星行驶数据并据此开发可通行性估计方法。

Method: 基于毅力号在Jezero陨石坑采集的500 sol、45km数据，包含灰度立体图像对、位姿、加速度计读数、摇臂-转向架角度及倾斜与打滑估计；据此提出不确定性感知的可通行性估计框架，从多模态驾驶经验中学习地形表征。

Result: 与现有方法相比，本方法在火星2020数据集上AUROC达0.874、F1为0.758，分别超过最强基线0.058与0.156，并取得最高平均精度与召回率；视觉表征可在实物火星车测试平台上集成至ENav等路径规划器。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了大规模真实火星行驶数据集与不确定性感知可通行性估计方法，验证了其在真实平台与路径规划器中的实用性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation on Mars requires vehicles to distinguish between traversable terrains across diverse and visually challenging environments. However, progress in learning-based navigation for off-world environments has been limited by the lack of large-scale datasets. Since landing in Jezero Crater, the Mars 2020 Perseverance rover has traversed terrain ranging from sandy dunes, rocky patches, and flat bedrocks. As a result, this paper presents a dataset spanning 500 sols and 45km of trajectories driven by both human operators and the onboard planner, ENav. Our dataset contains grayscale stereo image pairs, poses, accelerometer readings, rocker-bogie angles, and estimates of tilt and wheel slip. Building on this dataset, we introduce an uncertainty-aware traversability-estimation framework that learns terrain representations from multimodal driving experience. We compare our proposed method against existing approaches on the Mars 2020 dataset and show that our method achieves an AUROC of 0.874 and an F1 score of 0.758, outperforming the strongest baseline by 0.058 and 0.156, respectively, while also achieving the highest average precision and recall. Finally, we show that the visual representations can be integrated into path planners, such as ENav, on a physical rover test bed. Videos, code, and the M2020 dataset will be available at https://darren-chiu.github.io/learning-to-drive-on-mars.

</details>


### [228] [DexTacWAM: A Visuo-Tactile World-Action Model for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24976)
*Haoran Yuan,Zekai Wang,Boning Shao,Haoran Lu,Trevor Darrell,Ismini Lourentzou,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexTacWAM，一种视觉-触觉世界-动作模型，将触觉潜变量注入视频扩散世界模型进行联合建模，在6个富接触双手灵巧操作任务上平均得分70.6（最强基线38.0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖接触动力学，而接触动力学往往只能被视觉部分观测。现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）将预测式视频世界建模与动作生成耦合，但仍以视觉为中心，无法直接建模接触动力学。

Method: DexTacWAM对每个指尖独立编码，通过手指与位姿感知的触觉压缩器聚合特征，并将触觉潜变量注入视频扩散世界模型，实现视觉-触觉联合世界建模；采用冻结预训练视觉VAE、仅适配触觉编码器的持续视觉到触觉学习范式。

Result: 在22-DoF双臂平台的6个富接触灵巧操作任务上均取得最高分，平均70.6 vs 最强基线38.0。消融显示：移除触觉世界建模后四任务均值从74.7降至26.6（触觉特征与动作专家保持不变），说明增益来自将接触演化作为预测世界状态的一部分。触觉编码器适配4小时、冻结预训练视觉VAE、无触觉中训练，每任务约100条演示即可扩展至触觉，视觉预测质量保持在纯视觉版本0.5 dB以内。压缩器保留89.4%融合前接触召回率，训练加速2.26倍、推理加速1.29倍。

Conclusion: 预训练视频先验可以以数据高效、计算高效的方式扩展到分布式多指接触动力学建模。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact dynamics that are often only partially observable from vision. Recent World-Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive video world modeling with action generation, but remain largely vision-centric and therefore cannot directly model these contact dynamics. We present DexTacWAM, a visuo-tactile WAM that encodes each fingertip independently, aggregates the resulting features through a finger- and pose-aware tactile compressor, and injects the tactile latent into a video diffusion world model for joint visuo-tactile world modeling. Across six contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks on a 22-DoF bimanual platform, DexTacWAM achieves the highest score on every task, averaging 70.6 versus 38.0 for the strongest baseline. Ablations attribute the gain to modeling contact evolution as part of the predicted world state rather than tactile conditioning alone: removing tactile world modeling reduces the four-task mean from 74.7 to 26.6 while keeping the same tactile features and action expert. After four hours of tactile-encoder adaptation with a frozen pretrained vision VAE, our continual vision-to-touch learning extends the pretrained video model to touch using roughly 100 demonstrations per task without tactile midtraining, while retaining visual prediction quality within 0.5 dB of vision-only counterparts. The compressor retains 89.4% of pre-fusion contact recall while enabling 2.26x faster training and 1.29x faster inference. Together, these results show that pretrained video priors can be extended to distributed multi-finger contact dynamics in a data- and compute-efficient manner.

</details>


### [229] [MIGU: Multimodal Instruction Grounding under Uncertainty for Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24995)
*Mingke Lu,Anxing Xiao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化框架MIGU，将语言与手势的语义、几何证据以贝叶斯式融合为统一接地信念，并接入移动操作与桌面任务规划，真实基准上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在以人为本的环境中部署机器人需要理解自然人类指令。语言与手势提供互补但不确定的线索，现有方法对多模态不确定性建模不足，难以可靠接地并驱动操作规划。

Method: MIGU构建3D几何似然：通过眼-指几何传播视线方向与深度不确定性，并考虑手部方向估计误差；同时用视觉语言模型（VLM）提供候选物体与区域的语义先验；二者经贝叶斯式融合形成统一接地信念。该信念支持行为规划：直接进入下游规划或请求澄清。接地的目标随后用于定义移动操作与桌面任务-运动规划的Goal。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，MIGU优于所有被评估的基线；消融实验支持显式多模态不确定性建模带来的收益。

Conclusion: 显式建模语言与手势的多模态不确定性，并将语义与几何证据统一于接地信念，可有效提升指令接地与机器人操作规划性能。

Abstract: Understanding natural human instructions is crucial for deploying robots in human-centric environments. We study multimodal instruction grounding, where language and gesture provide complementary but uncertain cues. We present MIGU, a modular framework that combines semantic and geometric evidence into a unified grounding belief and connects it to manipulation planning. MIGU constructs a 3D geometric likelihood by propagating viewing-direction and depth uncertainty through eye-finger geometry while accounting for hand-direction estimation error. A vision-language model (VLM) provides semantic priors over candidate objects and regions, which are combined with the geometric likelihood through Bayes-inspired fusion. The resulting belief supports behavior planning to either proceed directly to downstream planning or request clarification. Grounded targets then define goals for mobile manipulation and tabletop task-and-motion planning. On a real-world benchmark, MIGU outperforms all evaluated baselines, while ablations support the benefit of explicit multimodal uncertainty modeling. Project website: multimodal-instruction.github.io

</details>


### [230] [Learning Beyond What Humans Can Demonstrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24996)
*Yuchen Song,Aditya Mittal,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLIDE通过从任务描述中推断失效模式并生成可执行护栏，解决了"不可行演示"场景下的数据收集与策略部署问题，将数据收集成功率从0-10%提升至70-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作的行为克隆依赖专家演示，但对需要动态稳定、精确接触时序或灵巧协调的任务，人类操作者难以甚至无法收集数据。作者研究这一"不可行演示"（infeasible-demonstration）场景。

Method: 提出GLIDE（Guardrails for Learning from Infeasible Demonstrations Efficiently）框架：给定任务描述与条件化遥操作代码，推断任务特定的失效模式，并将其转化为可执行护栏——利用系统状态过滤遥操作与策略指令、约束易失效动作，并基于轨迹反馈迭代改进。

Result: 在三个任务中，GLIDE发现了超越领域专家硬编码护栏的新兴护栏，优于朴素VR遥操作与专家硬编码护栏的数据收集；精炼后数据收集成功率从0-10%提升至70-90%；策略执行阶段，混合数据+护栏策略在Tomato plate transfer、Marker handover and stand、Wine serving上分别达到70%、60%、60%的成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明GLIDE能在直接演示不可行的情况下支撑策略学习，护栏机制可有效替代人工演示以产生高质量训练数据。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for robot manipulation relies on expert demonstrations. However, for tasks that require dynamic stability, precise contact timing, or dexterous coordination, human operators may find it hard or even impossible to collect data. We study this infeasible-demonstration regime and propose GLIDE: Guardrails for Learning from Infeasible Demonstrations Efficiently, a framework that infers task-specific failure modes and converts them into executable guardrails for data collection and policy deployment. Given a task description and the conditioning teleoperation code, GLIDE writes guardrails that use system states to filter teleoperation and policy commands, constrain failure-prone actions, and iteratively improve from trajectory feedback. Across three tasks, GLIDE discovers emergent guardrails that go beyond domain-expert hardcoded ones, improving data collection over naive VR teleoperation and domain-expert hardcoded guardrails. After refinement, GLIDE raises data-collection success from 0-10 percent to 70-90 percent across the three tasks. During policy execution, mixed-data guarded policies reach 70 percent, 60 percent, and 60 percent success on Tomato plate transfer, Marker handover and stand, and Wine serving tasks. These results show that GLIDE can support policy learning when direct demonstrations are infeasible. Project website: http://guardrail-policy.github.io/

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Domain-decomposed Evolutional Deep Neural Network with Random Features for Transient Pressure Diffusion with Discontinuous and High-Contrast Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22349)
*Peiqi Li,Jie Chen,Hui Zhang,Simon Hands*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出域分解随机特征演化深度神经网络（DD RF-EDNN），将空间逼近与时间演化分离，实现非均质多孔介质瞬态压力扩散的高效结构保持降阶求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当渗透率不连续且跨越多个数量级时，非均质多孔介质中的瞬态压力扩散难以高效求解，需要兼顾精度、效率与物理结构保持的降阶方法。

Method: 构建DD RF-EDNN：将渗透率感知的随机特征压缩为正交压力空间，把守恒型有限体积算子投影到该空间，仅对降阶坐标进行时间推进；避免重复的神经网络优化，并进行字典与奇异值截断误差分析、在能量一致耗散修正下证明收缩性，以及推导齐次动力学与稳态提升数据的条件性网格级估计。

Result: 在低渗夹杂、高导通道与块体以及三维网格上均表现一致精度；主基准最终时刻相对L2误差为9.54×10⁻⁴，渗透率反差10³时误差保持10⁻²量级；数值诊断显示误差平衡与时间收敛性与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: DD RF-EDNN是一种结构保持、可解释的降阶格式，适用于给定非均质介质上的瞬态模拟。

Abstract: Transient pressure diffusion in heterogeneous porous media becomes difficult to resolve efficiently when permeability is discontinuous and spans several orders of magnitude. We develop a domain-decomposed random-feature evolutional deep neural network (DD RF-EDNN) that separates spatial approximation from temporal evolution. Permeability-informed random features are compressed into an orthonormal pressure space, and a conservative finite-volume operator is projected onto this space so that only the reduced coordinates are advanced in time. This formulation retains the dissipative structure of the discrete flow problem without repeated neural-network optimization. The analysis quantifies dictionary and singular-value truncation errors, proves contractivity under an energy-consistent dissipativity correction, and derives a conditional grid-level estimate for homogeneous dynamics and time-independent data admitting a steady lifting. Experiments on low-permeability inclusions, high-conductivity channels and blocks, and three-dimensional grids demonstrate consistent accuracy across distinct coefficient structures. The method reaches a final-time relative $L^2$ error of $9.54\times10^{-4}$ in the principal benchmark and maintains errors on the order of $10^{-2}$ at a permeability contrast of $10^3$. Numerical diagnostics further show that the observed error balance and temporal convergence are consistent with the analysis. These results support DD RF-EDNN as a structure-preserving and interpretable reduced formulation for transient simulations on prescribed heterogeneous media.

</details>


### [232] [Bernstein Constraint Complexes for Multivariate Splines on Triangulated Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22355)
*Shelvean Kapita*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出 Bernstein 约束复形：以 Bernstein–Bézier 系数局部表示有限元微分复形，通过边/顶点的光滑泛函施加全局连续性，无需全局基；核心是系数级交换恒等式，可用于子复形判定与约束 Galerkin 求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元微分复形需要构造全局基来施加连续性，代价高且难以统一处理标量样条、切向/法向连续向量场及高阶光滑性。作者希望以不构造全局基的方式，在系数层面统一表达和验证这些连续性约束。

Method: 每个三角形保留自身 Bernstein–Bézier 系数，全局连续仅通过边与顶点上的光滑泛函施加；不同连续性（标量连续、切向/法向连续、高阶光滑）仅体现在边泛函差异上。证明系数级交换恒等式：边的光滑泛函经一元 Bernstein 差分矩阵显式组合。据此用行空间测试检验候选光滑剖面，并用精确光滑方程在 broken 系数上求解约束 Galerkin 问题，结合伪逆处理半定元算子。

Result: 给出光滑矩阵核构成子复形的局部证明（de Rham 迹与分量 C^r 样条）；实现最低阶 Powell–Sabin 正合序列并验证正合性；证明约束方法与协调方法等价；给出有限谱为约束 Maxwell 谱的奇异 pencil；在协变/逆变曲面 Piola 映射下参考泛函保持不变。实验比较零空间法、Awanou–Lai–Wenston 增广拉格朗日迭代与鞍点直接解。

Conclusion: Bernstein 约束复形为有限元微分复形提供了一种无全局基、系数级、可计算的统一框架，兼容平面与曲面、低阶到高阶光滑问题，并有数值实验验证其等价性与效率。

Abstract: We introduce Bernstein constraint complexes, a representation of finite element differential complexes in which every triangle of a triangulation keeps its own Bernstein--Bézier coefficients and global continuity is imposed only through smoothness functionals attached to edges and vertices. Continuity of scalar splines, tangential continuity of spline vector fields, normal continuity of spline vector fields, and higher smoothness differ only in the edge functionals. No global basis is constructed. The structural result is a coefficient-level commuting identity: on every edge the smoothness functionals of a Bernstein derivative are explicit combinations of the smoothness functionals of its argument, through univariate Bernstein difference matrices. This gives a local proof that the kernels of the smoothness matrices form a subcomplex, for the de Rham traces and for componentwise $C^r$ spline profiles, and a computable row-space test for candidate smooth profiles. We realize the lowest-order Powell--Sabin exact sequence in this form and confirm its exactness by ranks. The constrained Galerkin problem is solved in the broken coefficients with exact smoothness equations; we prove equivalence with the conforming method, treat semidefinite element operators through a pseudoinverse interface system, and give a singular pencil whose finite spectrum is the constrained Maxwell spectrum. Under the covariant and contravariant surface Piola maps the reference functionals of the de Rham sequence transfer unchanged to conforming curved triangulations. Experiments compare the null-space method, the augmented Lagrangian iteration of Awanou, Lai and Wenston, and direct solution of the saddle system on the same problems, and cover planar source and eigenvalue problems, a $C^1$ biharmonic solve, the exact sphere, and an embedded hyperboloid.

</details>


### [233] [Conformal Spherical Splines for the Laplace--Beltrami Operator on Genus-Zero Surfaces: Construction, Algorithm, and Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22356)
*Shelvean Kapita*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文用球面样条求解共形度量的Laplace–Beltrami算子的Poisson方程与特征值问题，利用二维Dirichlet能量的共形不变性，使刚度矩阵退化为单位球矩阵，共形因子仅进入载荷向量、均值约束与加权质量矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零亏格光滑闭曲面均等距于带共形因子的单位球，因此可用共形因子唯一描述几何。作者希望在此统一框架下，仅以共形因子为几何输入，高效、精确地求解Poisson方程与特征值问题，并避免重复装配刚度矩阵。

Method: 将未知量设为固定球面三角剖分上的分片Bernstein–Bézier样条，通过边泛函代数施加C^r光滑性，并用一次计算、可复用的零空间矩阵实现协调空间；由于二维Dirichlet能量共形不变，刚度矩阵恒为单位球矩阵，共形因子仅影响载荷向量、均值约束与加权质量矩阵；共形因子在离散方程前先用面积恒等式校验。

Result: 在单位球度量、长球面、旋转哑铃、Evans–Fung红细胞剖面及无嵌入的给定因子等算例上，样条次数d达到L^2收敛率d+1与能量收敛率d，特征值收敛率2d，并可将单位球的偶数阶特征值重现到次数d（达舍入误差）；在相同三角剖分下，与参数曲面有限元相比，四次与六次样条在相近未知量数下误差更小。

Conclusion: 以共形因子为唯一几何描述、结合球面样条与共形不变刚度矩阵的方法，为任意零亏格共形度量上的Poisson方程和特征值问题提供了统一、高精度且可复用的离散框架。

Abstract: Every smooth closed Riemannian surface of genus zero is isometric to the unit sphere carrying a conformal metric, the round metric multiplied by a positive factor. This paper solves the Poisson equation and the eigenvalue problem of the Laplace--Beltrami operator of such a metric with smooth spherical splines, taking the conformal factor as the only description of the geometry. Because the Dirichlet energy is conformally invariant in two dimensions, the stiffness matrix is the round-sphere matrix for every conformal metric, and the factor enters only the load vector, the mean-value constraint and the weighted mass matrix. A surface embedded in space with a conformal parametrization, a surface of revolution, and a surface given only as a triangle mesh are instances of the same problem that differ in how the factor is obtained; the first two are treated here, and the factor is checked against an area identity before any equation is discretized. The unknown is a spline on a fixed spherical triangulation in broken Bernstein--Bézier form, with $C^r$ smoothness imposed algebraically through edge functionals and the conforming space realized by a null-space matrix computed once and reused for every metric. Each step of the computation is stated with the formulas it needs. Experiments on the round metric, a prolate spheroid, a dumbbell of revolution, an Evans--Fung red-blood-cell profile and a prescribed factor with no embedding show the rates $d+1$ in $L^2$ and $d$ in energy for spline degree $d$, the rate $2d$ for eigenvalues with reproduction of the even-order eigenvalues of the round metric up to degree $d$ to roundoff, and, against parametric surface finite elements on the same triangulations, smaller errors at comparable numbers of unknowns for degrees four and six.

</details>


### [234] [Update to: Spectral sets: Numerical range and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22460)
*Michel Crouzeix,Anne Greenbaum*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文利用Lorist与Schwenninger关于Crouzeix猜想的最新证明结果，更新了Crouzeix与Greenbaum 2019年关于谱集与数值域的论文，对其中所有区域给出了更小的K-谱集常数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Crouzeix与Greenbaum 2019年论文（Spectral sets: Numerical range and beyond）建立了各类区域的K-谱集估计，但这些界并非最优。Lorist与Schwenninger（arXiv:2608.03841v2）给出了Crouzeix猜想的证明，为改进原有K值提供了新的工具，因此有必要重新审视并更新此前的估计结果。

Method: 以Lorist与Schwenninger关于Crouzeix猜想的解为理论工具，针对2019年论文所考虑的全部区域重新推导K-谱集常数，比较并改进原有界。重点分析了环形区域与带圆孔或切口的凸区域两类情形。

Result: 对2019年论文中的所有区域，均得到比原文更小的K-谱集常数界。具体地，证明多种环形区域是2-谱集；更一般的带圆形孔洞或切口的凸区域是4-谱集。

Conclusion: 借助Crouzeix猜想的证明，2019年论文中的K-谱集估计可被系统性改进，环形区域与带孔凸区域的谱集常数分别优化至2和4，显著提升了原结果的紧致性。

Abstract: We use the results of Lorist and Schwenninger, {\em A solution to Crouzeix's conjecture}, arXiv:2608.03841v2, https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03841, to update the 2019 paper of Crouzeix and Greenbaum, {\em Spectral sets: Numerical range and beyond}, SIAM J.~Matrix Anal.~Appl., 40(3):1087-1101. For all regions considered in the 2019 paper, we are able to show that the region is a $K$-spectral set with a bound on $K$ that is smaller than that established in the 2019 paper. In particular, we show that various annular regions are $2$-spectral sets and that a more general convex region with a circular hole or cutout is a $4$-spectral set.

</details>


### [235] [Efficient Rigorous Continuation via Chebyshev Series Expansion I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22689)
*Maxime Breden,Olivier Hénot*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Chebyshev级数展开的解流形全局延拓方法,通过加权ℓ¹空间上的拟牛顿算子压缩性获得显式误差界,并应用于伪弧长延拓与多参数延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动力系统中解流形的全局延拓问题,需要严格的延拓方法与更精细的误差控制。

Method: 用高阶Chebyshev插值多项式近似分支,并通过验证该近似附近拟牛顿算子的压缩性获得显式误差界;压缩性在加权ℓ¹空间上表述,给出比一致压缩定理所得C⁰误差界更精细的控制;方法自然适用于伪弧长延拓并扩展至多参数延拓。

Result: C⁰误差界可由本文压缩算子直接推出;计算了Cahn--Hilliard方程的双参数稳态族,以及Shigesada--Kawasaki--Teramoto系统中经历鞍结分岔的单参数稳态族。

Conclusion: 所提Chebyshev级数延拓方法为解流形提供严格且精细的误差控制,适用于伪弧长与多参数延拓,并在两类方程中得到验证。

Abstract: We study the global continuation of solution manifolds arising in dynamical systems. We present a rigorous continuation method based on a Chebyshev series expansion of the solution manifold. The branch is first approximated by a high-order Chebyshev interpolation polynomial, and an explicit error bound is then obtained by verifying the contraction of a quasi-Newton operator near this approximation. The contraction is formulated on a weighted $\ell^1$ space, giving a finer control than the typical $C^0$-error bound obtained from the uniform contraction theorem. In fact, the latter follows directly from our contraction operator. Furthermore, we discuss how our strategy applies naturally to pseudo-arclength continuation, where the continuation parameter fails to provide a valid local coordinate, and extends to multi-parameter continuation. Lastly, we detail two applications in which we compute a two-parameter family of steady-states for the Cahn--Hilliard equation, and a one-parameter family of steady-states undergoing saddle-node bifurcations for the Shigesada--Kawasaki--Teramoto system.

</details>


### [236] [Asymptotic-Preserving Exponential Integrators applied to the Hyperbolic Korteweg-de Vries System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22864)
*Abhijit Biswas,Sebastiano Boscarino,David I. Ketcheson,Giovanni Russo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究指数时间积分结合Fourier伪谱空间离散求解双曲KdV（KdVH）系统，分析其渐近保持（AP）性质并给出高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究指数时间积分方法在双曲KdV系统上的适用性，特别是其渐近保持性质，以厘清Lawson方法与ETD方法在刚性/奇异极限下的表现差异。

Method: 采用Fourier伪谱空间离散，耦合指数时间积分；理论分析Lawson方法与指数时间差分（ETD）方法的渐近保持（AP）性质；基于该系统矩阵指数的精确公式实现高效数值格式，并与已有的ImEx Runge-Kutta方法对比。

Result: 一般情况下Lawson方法不满足渐近保持，因为其辅助（导数近似）变量不满足极限平衡流形；而ETD方法对所有分量（包括解变量与辅助变量）均具有AP性质。数值实验表明指数方法在此背景下具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 在KdVH系统的数值求解中，ETD类指数时间积分在渐近保持性上优于Lawson方法，且借助矩阵指数精确公式可实现高效计算，性能可与ImEx Runge-Kutta相当。

Abstract: We study the application of exponential time integration methods, coupled with Fourier pseudospectral space discretization, to the hyperbolic Korteweg-de Vries (KdVH) system. We investigate the asymptotic preserving (AP) properties of such discretizations, showing that, in general, Lawson methods are not asymptotic preserving, because the auxiliary (derivative-approximating) variables do not satisfy the limit equilibrium manifold, whereas exponential time differencing (ETD) methods are AP for all components, including both the solution variable and the auxiliary variables. We also present an efficient numerical implementation based on an exact formula for the matrix exponential of this system, and compare it with previously-proposed ImEx Runge-Kutta time integration, showing that exponential methods can be competitive in this context.

</details>


### [237] [The Zipped Finite Element Method on polyhedral meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22955)
*Stefano Berrone,Gioana Teora*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将高阶Zipped有限元方法扩展到三维多面体单元，采用QR策略构造基函数，实现最优收敛阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二维高阶Zipped有限元方法推广到三维多面体单元，并克服二维启发式构造方法的局限性，提供更系统、鲁棒的基函数构造方案。

Method: 通过对多边形面三角化并连接至合适内点构建局部子四面体剖分；高阶形函数由标准有限元基函数的线性组合构成，系数选取保证面与单元内部的多项式精确重构并保持全局C0连续性；引入基于QR的构造策略替代启发式方法，该策略与空间维数无关，避免几何对齐测试和问题相关容差；方法针对扩散-反应问题建立。

Result: 三维收敛性测试验证了该方法达到预期的多项式最优收敛阶。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于QR策略的三维高阶Zipped有限元方法系统性强、鲁棒性好，能够实现最优精度，适用于多面体单元上的扩散-反应问题。

Abstract: We extend the high-order Zipped Finite Element Method to three-dimensional polyhedral elements. The construction relies on a local sub-tetrahedralization obtained by triangulating the polygonal faces and connecting the resulting vertices to a suitable interior point. High-order shape functions are constructed as linear combinations of standard finite element basis functions, with coefficients chosen to ensure exact polynomial reproduction on the faces and in the element interior while preserving global C0-conformity. We further introduce a QR-based strategy as a systematic alternative to the heuristic procedure that was adopted for the two-dimensional case. The QR-strategy is more systematic and robust than the heuristic strategy, while being independent of the spatial dimension and avoiding geometric alignment tests and problem-dependent tolerances. The resulting method is formulated for a diffusion-reaction problem and numerically assessed through three-dimensional convergence tests, confirming the expected optimal polynomial order of accuracy.

</details>


### [238] [NeSTR: Neural S-Transform Reconstruction for Gaussian SPDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23001)
*Nacira Agram,Fred Espen Benth,Jan Rems*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出神经S变换重构（NeSTR）方法，针对高斯随机偏微分方程（SPDE），不是直接学习随机解，而是在白噪声检验空间有限维子空间上学习其S变换，通过逆S变换原理与有限模态学习恢复Wiener混沌系数，并在数值实验中验证其稳定性与误差分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接学习SPDE的随机解（如学习随机轨迹）存在高维、随机性强、难以稳定获取高阶混沌系数等问题，且神经近似与Wiener混沌展开之间缺乏直接联系。作者希望将学习对象从随机解转向其S变换，从而将神经近似与解的Wiener混沌展开直接挂钩，并为Wick型SPDE建立确定性的参数化学习框架。

Method: 方法核心是NeSTR：学习对象为随机解在白噪声检验空间有限维子空间上的S变换，而非随机解本身。对Wick型SPDE，NeSTR给出限制S变换所满足的确定性参数化PDE，并由该变换在原点处的Taylor系数恢复随机解。方法结合逆S变换重构原理与有限模态学习，证明恢复的混沌系数的一致性，并将噪声模态误差、混沌截断误差、确定性求解器误差与神经近似误差分离，给出限制变换的确定性学习形式。

Result: 在加性随机热方程与乘性Wick热方程上的数值测试表明，变换变量中的多项式谱表示能够稳定地获取高阶混沌系数。所得重构将依赖方程的确定性系数场与可复用的Gaussian–Hermite基分离。

Conclusion: NeSTR学习的是一个解析生成函数，其导数可恢复Wiener混沌系数，而非直接学习随机轨迹，从而为高斯SPDE提供了一种将神经近似与Wiener混沌展开直接联系的确定性学习框架。

Abstract: We introduce Neural $S$-Transform Reconstruction (NeSTR) for Gaussian stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs). The learned object is not the random solution itself, but its $S$-Transform on a finite dimensional subspace of the white noise test space. For Wick type SPDEs, NeSTR yields a deterministic parametric PDE for the restricted $S$-Transform, and the stochastic solution is recovered from the Taylor coefficients of this transform at the origin. Thus the neural approximation is tied directly to the Wiener chaos expansion of the solution. The main novelty of NeSTR is an inverse $S$-Transform reconstruction principle combined with finite mode learning: we prove consistency of the recovered chaos coefficients, separate noise mode, chaos truncation, deterministic solver, and neural approximation errors, and give a deterministic learning formulation for the restricted transform. Numerical tests on an additive stochastic heat equation and a multiplicative Wick heat equation show that polynomial spectral representations in the transform variables provide stable access to higher order chaos coefficients. The resulting reconstruction separates the equation dependent deterministic coefficient fields from a reusable Gaussian--Hermite basis. Consequently, NeSTR learns an analytic generating function whose derivatives recover the Wiener chaos coefficients, rather than learning stochastic trajectories directly.

</details>


### [239] [Asymptotic preservation of an exponential Euler method under Hamiltonian fast advection with multiple critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23045)
*Jianbo Cui,Guozhen Li,Derui Sheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了指数Euler方法对R²中含快速Hamiltonian平流（多临界点）的随机反应-扩散-平流方程的渐近保持性质，极限为定义在非紧度量图上的随机偏微分方程，并在加权L²框架下获得了接近1/2（部分条件下恰为1/2）的时间收敛阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速平流极限下，原始方程退化为非紧度量图上的SPDE。图上算子具有非一致椭圆性、顶点退化、多边多顶点等特点，使得既有数值格式的收敛性与渐近保持分析变得困难，需要新的分析工具来刻画指数Euler格式的收敛阶。

Method: 研究指数Euler方法的渐近保持性：分析极限（图上）指数Euler格式的强收敛性；采用加权L²框架结合解析半群光滑性估计处理图的非紧性、多边多顶点结构以及算子的非一致椭圆性与顶点退化；再将图上收敛估计与快速平流极限结合。

Result: 对加权L²初值，证明时间收敛阶可任意接近1/2；在半阶分数域条件下，收敛阶恰为1/2。将上述估计与快速平流极限结合，得到多临界点情形下的渐近保持性质。数值实验验证了渐近行为并确认了预测的时间收敛率。

Conclusion: 指数Euler方法在多临界点快速平流极限下具有渐近保持性，极限格式在加权L²框架下以接近1/2（或恰为1/2）的时间阶强收敛，数值实验与理论预测一致。

Abstract: We establish the asymptotic-preserving property of the exponential Euler method for stochastic reaction-diffusion-advection equations in $\mathbb R^2$ under fast Hamiltonian advection with multiple critical points. In the fast-advection limit, the dynamics reduces to a stochastic partial differential equation on a noncompact metric graph. A key ingredient is the strong convergence analysis of the limiting exponential Euler scheme, which is complicated by the graph's noncompactness, its multiple edges and vertices, and the nonuniform ellipticity and vertex degeneracy of the graph operator. Using a weighted $L^2$ framework and analytic-semigroup smoothing estimates, we prove a temporal convergence order arbitrarily close to $1/2$ for weighted $L^2$ initial data and exactly $1/2$ under a half-order fractional-domain condition. Combining these estimates with the fast-advection limits yields the asymptotic-preserving property in the multiple-critical-point setting. Numerical experiments illustrate the asymptotic behavior and confirm the predicted temporal convergence rate.

</details>


### [240] [TASE-Stabilized Time-Evolving Natural Gradient Methods for Diffusion-Dominated PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23054)
*Zihao Shi,Dongling Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出 TASE–TENG 方法，将高稳定性的 TASE 算子 T_p(hW_0) 应用于每个 Runge–Kutta 阶段增量，并结合可学习、网格无关的刚性扩散算子代理 W_0，从而在相同时间步长下提升刚度扩散及反应–扩散问题的稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TENG 方法通过局部自然梯度迭代将时间离散的目标状态投影到神经网络流形上，但在刚性、扩散主导问题中，显式 Runge–Kutta 方法受严重稳定性限制。高频扩散分量会使阶段目标超出神经流形的局部可达区域，恶化局部最小二乘投影的条件数并使参数更新失稳。因此需要一种能抑制刚性扩散模态并稳定 TENG 积分的机制。

Method: 提出 TASE–TENG：在每个 Runge–Kutta 阶段增量投影前，施加高稳定显式算子 T_p(hW_0)，其中 W_0 近似主导刚性扩散算子。为在无网格设置下构造该代理，设计可学习算子，其局部相互作用系数由共享的、依赖几何的神经规则生成；通过结构化分解强制满足离散自伴随性、耗散性与常数保持。该算子离线训练，在线积分期间固定不变，仅用于 TASE 稳定化算子内部。

Result: 在刚性扩散与反应–扩散问题上的数值实验表明，在相同时间步长下稳定性和精度均有提升，来自高频扩散模态的误差降低。此外，学习到的规则使稳定化算子能够在不同空间点分辨率下重建而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: TASE–TENG 通过将高稳定显式 TASE 算子与可学习的、具备结构性约束的刚性扩散代理相结合，有效缓解了 TENG 在刚性扩散主导问题中的稳定性瓶颈，并在保持时间步长的前提下提高精度；其几何依赖的学习规则还带来了跨空间分辨率的可迁移性。

Abstract: The Time-Evolving Natural Gradient (TENG) method evolves neural approximations of time-dependent partial differential equations by projecting time-discrete target states onto the neural-network manifold through local natural-gradient iterations. For stiff diffusion-dominated problems, however, explicit Runge--Kutta methods face severe stability restrictions. High-frequency diffusive components can drive stage targets beyond the locally reachable region of the neural manifold, worsening the conditioning of local least-squares projections and destabilizing parameter updates.
  We propose TASE--TENG, which applies the highly stable explicit operator $T_p(hW_0)$ to each Runge--Kutta stage increment before projection, where $W_0$ approximates the dominant stiff diffusion operator. To construct this surrogate in a mesh-free setting, we develop a learnable operator whose local interaction coefficients are generated by a shared geometry-dependent neural rule. A structured factorization enforces discrete self-adjointness, dissipativity, and constant preservation. The operator is trained offline and remains fixed during online integration, where it is used only within the TASE stabilization operator. Numerical experiments on stiff diffusion and reaction--diffusion problems demonstrate improved stability and accuracy at the same time-step size, with reduced errors from high-frequency diffusive modes. The learned rule also allows the stabilization operator to be reconstructed at different spatial point resolutions without retraining.

</details>


### [241] [A fast spectral particle method for the Landau equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23086)
*Giacomo Borghi,Lorenzo Pareschi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种求解空间齐次Landau方程的快速确定性粒子方法，将粒子表示与碰撞通量的傅里叶近似结合，利用非均匀快速傅里叶变换实现粒子与密度/通量之间的高效转换。每步计算量为O(N+M^d log M)，并给出重构速度场的一致性估计，二维数值实验验证了方法的精度、效率及相对直接blob实现的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间齐次Landau方程的数值求解需要在保证精度的同时控制计算成本；传统粒子方法或谱方法在耦合粒子表示与非线性碰撞项时，往往面临高昂的计算开销或精度损失。作者希望构造一种兼具确定性、快速且精度可控的算法。

Method: 方法将解的粒子表示与非线性碰撞通量的傅里叶近似相结合；使用非均匀快速傅里叶变换（NUFFT）从粒子重建密度，并在粒子位置处评估通量与密度；利用通量的卷积结构，通过FFT实现其高效计算。对固定变换容差，每时间步复杂度为O(N+M^d log M)，其中N为粒子数，M为每个速度维度的傅里叶模态数。

Result: 在参考密度远离零的区域建立了重构速度场的一致性估计，将谱截断误差与粒子密度重构误差分离，并表明对足够光滑的密度后者可能占主导。二维Maxwellian相互作用数值实验展示了方法的精度与效率、对粒子与谱分辨率的敏感性以及滤波效果；与直接blob实现相比，在显著降低计算成本的同时提高了精度。

Conclusion: 该方法为空间齐次Landau方程提供了一种快速、确定性的数值求解途径，兼具谱精度与粒子方法的灵活性；一致性估计揭示了误差来源，数值实验支持其高效性与准确性。

Abstract: We propose a fast deterministic particle method for the spatially homogeneous Landau equation. The method combines a particle representation of the solution with a Fourier approximation of the nonlinear collision flux. Nonuniform fast Fourier transforms are used to reconstruct the density from the particles and to evaluate the flux and density at the particle locations, while the convolutional structure of the flux enables its efficient computation by FFTs. For a fixed transform tolerance, the cost per time step is $\mathcal{O}(N+M^d\log M)$, where $N$ is the number of particles and $M$ the number of Fourier modes per velocity dimension. We establish a consistency estimate for the reconstructed velocity field on regions where the reference density is bounded away from zero. The estimate separates the spectral truncation error from the particle-density reconstruction error, showing how the latter can dominate for sufficiently smooth densities. Two-dimensional numerical experiments with Maxwellian interactions illustrate the accuracy and efficiency of the method, its sensitivity to particle and spectral resolution, and the effects of filtering. Comparisons with a direct blob implementation demonstrate improved accuracy at a strongly reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [242] [Sparsely connected neural network representation of Lagrange finite element function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23299)
*Jiaxiong Hao,Yunqing Huang,Nianyu Yi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种由网格诱导的稀疏连接神经网络框架，可精确复现单纯形网格上任意阶Lagrange有限元空间，并用于非匹配网格插值与自适应有限元求解抛物型偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络代理模型（black-box surrogates）缺乏可解释性和理论保证，无法与经典有限元逼近理论对应。作者希望构造一种网络架构，使其函数空间与目标有限元空间完全一致，从而继承完整的有限元逼近理论，同时利用自动微分替代传统有限元中单独的梯度计算子程序。

Method: 构建网格诱导的稀疏连接神经网络：局部计算由单纯形几何与重心坐标变换决定，全局一致性通过共享自由度实现；线性Lagrange单元由仿射重心层直接实现，高阶多项式基分解为重心乘积组合并用定制的ReLU^p模块实现；配合单元指示分支与乘法单元将局部模块全局组装成稀疏连接神经网络。为分段激活函数定制反向传播微分规则，使函数值及空间梯度可在统一计算图中通过自动微分同时提取。

Result: 数值实验验证了该神经网络表示Lagrange有限元的精度；利用其固有的无网格特性，实现了非匹配网格间的有限元函数插值，并成功应用于求解抛物型偏微分方程的自适应有限元方法；开源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 该网络架构严格对应有限元离散规则，函数空间与目标有限元空间精确一致，可继承经典有限元逼近理论；同时通过自动微分统一提取函数值与梯度，避免了独立梯度计算子程序，并为非匹配网格插值和自适应有限元方法提供了新途径。
