Abstract: We construct a mesh-induced sparsely connected neural network framework that exactly reproduces arbitrary-order Lagrange finite element spaces over simplicial meshes. Unlike conventional black-box neural surrogates, the proposed network architecture is fully dictated by finite element discretization rules: local computations stem from simplex geometry and barycentric coordinate transformations, while global consistency is enforced through shared degrees of freedom. For linear Lagrange elements, local basis functions are directly implemented via affine barycentric layers, and high-order polynomial bases are explicitly decomposed into barycentric product compositions realized by specially designed $\mathrm{ReLU}^p$ modules. Equipped with element indicator branches and multiplication units, these modular local components are globally assembled into a sparsely connected neural network whose function space coincides exactly with the target finite element space, thereby inheriting the complete classical finite element approximation theory. By prescribing customized backward differentiation rules for piecewise activations, function values and their spatial gradients can be simultaneously extracted via automatic differentiation within a unified computational graph, eliminating the separate gradient calculation subroutines required in standard finite element implementations. Numerical experiments verify the accuracy of the neural network representation of Lagrange finite elements. Furthermore, by virtue of the intrinsic mesh-free nature of this neural network representation, finite element functions can be interpolated between non-matching meshes, and the proposed scheme can be applied to adaptive finite element methods for solving parabolic partial differential equations. An open-source code implementation of the proposed architecture is made publicly available.

</details>


### [243] [Super-approximation and optimal $L^2$ convergence of the stabilized BGN method with piecewise linear parametric finite elements for curve-shortening flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23326)
*Genming Bai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文引入双同伦论证估计一致性泛函的离散物质时间导数，结合分部求和得到法向误差速度测试下的方向超收敛估计，最终证明稳定BGN方法在分段线性参数有限元下投影误差的L_t^∞H_x^1超收敛与轨迹误差的L_t^∞L_x^2最优收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线缩短流的BGN方法虽有良好数值表现，但其投影误差的H^1范数超收敛与轨迹误差的L^2最优收敛缺乏严格理论保证，需要新的分析工具来刻画离散物质时间导数与一致性贡献的收敛行为。

Method: 提出双同伦（bihomotopy）论证来估计一致性泛函的离散物质时间导数；结合分部求和（summation-by-parts）技巧，推导以法向误差速度为检验函数时的方向性超收敛估计；并利用反向加权离散法向的超逼近性质进行分析。

Result: 在分段线性参数有限元框架下，针对带稳定项的BGN方法求解曲线缩短流，证明了投影误差的L_t^∞H_x^1超收敛以及轨迹误差的L_t^∞L_x^2最优收敛。

Conclusion: 双同伦论证与分部求和相结合为参数化曲线演化方程的有限元分析提供了有效工具，成功建立了稳定BGN方法的最优误差理论。

Abstract: We develop a bihomotopy argument to estimate the discrete material time derivatives of consistency functionals. Combined with summation-by-parts, this argument yields a directional super-convergence estimate for the consistency contribution when tested with the normal error velocity. Together with the super-approximation of the reversely weighted discrete normal, these techniques establish $L_t^\infty H_x^1$ super-convergence of the projection error and optimal $L_t^\infty L_x^2$ convergence of the trajectory error of a stabilized BGN method for curve-shortening flow with piecewise linear parametric finite elements.

</details>


### [244] [High-order mass-, energy- and momentum-conserving methods for the nonlinear Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23344)
*Georgios Akrivis,Buyang Li,Rong Tang,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出非线性薛定谔方程的时空有限元新格式，全离散隐式格式在离散层面保持质量、能量与动量守恒，并设计高效牛顿迭代求解，数值实验验证高阶收敛与长时间模拟精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性薛定谔方程数值模拟中，传统方法难以在离散层面同时保持质量、能量和动量守恒，导致长时间模拟误差累积。本文旨在构造一种内在守恒的算法。

Method: 提出新的时空有限元格式；采用全离散隐式格式，并证明其质量和能量、动量守恒性；基于等价形式设计迭代方案——非线性系统用牛顿迭代，拉格朗日乘子不做迭代。

Result: 大量数值算例显示该方法具有高阶收敛性；在一维马孤子、双孤子及二维孤子的模拟中有效保持质量、能量和动量；与不守恒格式相比，长时间模拟中显著降低数值解误差。

Conclusion: 所提时空有限元方法能内在保证离散守恒律，兼具高阶精度与长期稳定性，适合非线性薛定谔方程的长时间数值模拟。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel formulation and an associated space-time finite element method for simulating solutions to the nonlinear Schrödinger equation. A major advantage of the proposed algorithm is its intrinsic ability to preserve the conservation of mass, energy, and momentum at the discrete level. This is proved for the numerical solutions determined by the fully discrete implicit scheme. An effective iterative scheme is proposed for solving the nonlinear system based on an equivalent formulation which suggests using Newton's iteration for the solution and no iteration for the Lagrange multipliers in the nonlinear system. Extensive numerical examples are provided to demonstrate the high-order convergence and effectiveness of the proposed algorithm in conserving mass, energy, and momentum in the simulation of one-dimensional Ma-solitons and bi-solitons, as well as of two-dimensional solitons governed by the nonlinear Schrödinger equation. The numerical results show that the mass-, energy- and momentum-conserving method designed in this paper also significantly reduces the errors of the numerical solutions in long-time simulations compared with methods which do not conserve these quantities.

</details>


### [245] [Dynamic Ritz projection of mean curvature flow and optimal ${\bf L^2}$ convergence of parametric FEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23361)
*Buyang Li,Rong Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出通过动态Ritz投影进行误差分析的新方法，证明了闭曲面平均曲率流的参数化有限元逼近在$L^\infty(0,T;L^2)$范数下的最优阶收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上分析参数化有限元逼近平均曲率流的收敛性时，常将数值解与平均曲率流的插值进行比较，这种方法在误差分析上存在局限，难以获得最优阶收敛结果。

Method: 引入动态Ritz投影作为比较基准，将数值解与该投影对比而非与插值对比进行误差分析；建立动态Ritz投影逼近平均曲率流在$L^2$和$W^{1,p}$范数下的误差估计，并在此基础上证明收敛性。

Result: 建立了动态Ritz投影在$L^2$和$W^{1,p}$范数下逼近平均曲率流的误差估计，并据此证明了参数化有限元方法在$L^\infty(0,T;L^2)$范数下的最优阶收敛，包括分片线性有限元的情形。

Conclusion: 动态Ritz投影为分析参数化有限元逼近平均曲率流的收敛性提供了有效工具，成功获得最优先验误差界，适用于闭曲面三维平均曲率流。

Abstract: A new approach is developed to study the convergence of parametric finite element approximations to the mean curvature flow of closed surfaces in three-dimensional space. In this approach, the error analysis is conducted by comparing the numerical solution to a dynamic Ritz projection of the mean curvature flow introduced in this paper, rather than an interpolation of the mean curvature flow, as commonly used in the literature. The errors associated with the dynamic Ritz projection in approximating the mean curvature flow are established in the $L^2$ and $W^{1,p}$ norms. Leveraging these results, optimal-order convergence of parametric finite element methods for mean curvature flow of closed surfaces in the $L^\infty(0,T;L^2)$ norm is proved, including the convergence of parametric finite element methods with piecewise linear finite elements.

</details>


### [246] [A novel monotonous finite volume element scheme for convection dominant diffusion problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23546)
*Cunyun Nie,Xiaoling Chen,Zhujun wang,Zhikun Tian,Chengjie Xia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的单调有限体积元（MFVE）格式，用于对流占优扩散问题，通过对流项的上风体积离散、梯度函数的渐近展开与扰动系数修正，构造了一阶与二阶非线性离散算子，并结合保正有限体积元扩散算子，给出了L2误差估计与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对流占优扩散问题中，经典有限体积方法在强对流情形下易产生非物理振荡、难以兼顾单调性与高精度。作者希望构造既具上风性质（保证单调、抑制振荡）又具二阶精度的有限体积元格式，并给出严格误差分析。

Method: 1) 引入上风子控制体积（upwind volume），用于对流项离散，从而获得上风性质；2) 在上风体积内通过数值积分，直接得到平衡方程中对流项的一阶线性离散算子，区别于经典有限体积方法；3) 利用一个延拓插值函数，推导每个节点对偶单元内梯度函数的渐近展开，并结合FVE解的误差估计构造对流项的二阶非线性离散算子，其中巧妙引入扰动系数作为高阶修正；4) 结合保正有限体积元（PFVE）扩散算子，设计一阶与二阶MFVE格式。

Result: 推导出近似解的L2范数误差估计；数值实验验证了理论结论，表明所提格式具有单调性（上风性质）与二阶精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的MFVE格式通过对流项的上风体积离散与基于渐近展开的二阶非线性修正，结合PFVE扩散算子，能够在保证单调性的同时实现高阶精度，理论误差估计与数值结果一致。

Abstract: One novel monotonous finite volume element (MFVE) scheme is put forward for convection dominant diffusion problem. The main contributions of this paper include four aspects. Firstly, one upwind sub-control volume, called upwind volume, is introduced for the discretization of the convection term similar to the upwind element, which leads to the upwind property. Secondly, one first-order linear discrete operator for the convection term in the balance equation is directly obtained by numerical integration in upwind volume, which is different from the classical finite volume methods.Thirdly, one asymptotic expansion of gradient functions in the dual element of each node is derived by one extension interpolation function. Then, one second-order nonlinear discrete operator for the convection term is constructed by the asymptotic expansion and error estimation of its FVE solution, where some perturbed coefficients are skillfully put into the expansion, which serves as a high-order correction. Fourthly, one novel two-order MFVE scheme, also accompanied by another one-order MFVE, is designed for convection-dominated diffusion problems together with one positivity-preserving finite volume element (PFVE) discrete diffusive operator. The L2 norm of the error of the approximate solution is derived. Finally, numerical results validate theoretical conclusions.

</details>


### [247] [Numerical analysis of parabolic equations with Prandtl--Ishlinskii hysteresis of play type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23676)
*Shu Xu,Liqun Cao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为具有Prandtl–Ishlinskii（play型）滞回的抛物型方程建立了隐式Euler与P1有限元离散的O(h+τ)误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带有滞回的抛物型方程数值逼近的严格误差分析仍很有限，即便是广泛使用的play型Prandtl–Ishlinskii滞回也如此，已有分析往往需要滞回变量的高阶时间正则性或额外空间正则性，而这些在滞回演化中一般无法成立。

Method: 对时间离散，利用加权Hilbert空间中的凸次梯度流结构，结合相应的耗散与强制性次梯度余项得到一阶收敛；对空间离散，仅将扩散场限制在有限元空间，并通过保约束比较得到O(h)的半离散估计。分析直接建立在空间Hilbert空间上表述的play型Prandtl–Ishlinskii算子，涵盖经典逐点模型及更一般的空间结构化约束。

Result: 在不需要滞回变量高阶时间正则性、也不需要其额外空间正则性的条件下，得到隐式Euler P1有限元离散的O(h+τ)误差界（时间一阶收敛，空间O(h)半离散估计）。

Conclusion: 该工作为play型Prandtl–Ishlinskii滞回抛物方程的数值逼近提供了严格且无需额外正则性假设的误差分析框架，并适用于空间Hilbert空间上的更一般模型。

Abstract: Rigorous error analysis for numerical approximations of parabolic equations with hysteresis remains limited, even for the widely used Prandtl--Ishlinskii hysteresis of play type. In this work, we establish an $O(h+τ)$ error bound for an implicit Euler $P_1$ finite element discretization. The analysis requires neither higher-order temporal regularity of the hysteresis variables, which cannot in general be expected in hysteretic evolutions, nor additional spatial regularity of these variables. For the temporal discretization, we exploit a convex subgradient-flow structure in a weighted Hilbert space together with the associated dissipation and coercive subgradient remainder to obtain first-order convergence. For the spatial discretization, only the diffusive field is restricted to the finite element space, and a constraint-preserving comparison yields an $O(h)$ semidiscrete estimate. The analysis is developed for play-type Prandtl--Ishlinskii operators formulated directly on a spatial Hilbert space, encompassing the canonical pointwise model as well as more general spatially structured constraints.

</details>


### [248] [ELIPPS: Exact Learning for Inverse Problems from Partial Self-supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23683)
*Benjamin Walder Markus Haltmeier,Lukas Neumann,Nadja Gruber,Gyeongha Hwang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出 ELIPPS 自监督训练范式，仅用不完整的前向测量即可求解欠采样逆问题，无需真值图像或全采样测量，理论证明掩码经验风险与完整自监督风险等价，并在 CT 上验证性能接近全监督参考模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠采样逆问题（如稀疏视角 CT）中测量数少，可降低辐射、时间与成本，并缓解某些采集布置不可及的问题。但现有基于学习的方法通常需要全采样测量与真值图像作为监督，这类数据获取成本高甚至不可行。因此需要一种无需真值图像和全采样测量的训练范式。

Method: 提出 ELIPPS（Exact Learning for Inverse Problems from Partial Self-supervision）：一种不完整自监督训练范式，仅从固定不完整监督集上的不完整前向测量中学习。理论上，当数据分布在特定变换下不变时，最小化掩码经验风险等价于最小化完整自监督风险（即针对完整无噪测量的风险）；该等价性对任意等变假设类严格成立，且适用于信号相关噪声（如低剂量 CT 的 pre-log Poisson 统计），对对数变换计数模型则成立至一个可量化偏差。当底层覆盖条件在离散网格上仅近似满足时，给出以相关框架常数和逆问题不可约误差表示的稳定性估计。在 CT 中通过利用旋转与反射不变性来实现 ELIPPS。

Result: 实验中 ELIPPS 显著优于朴素掩码监督，并达到与使用完整干净测量训练的全监督参考模型相同量级的精度。

Conclusion: ELIPPS 为欠采样逆问题提供了一种无需真值图像与全采样测量的自监督训练方案，理论保证其在不变性条件下与完整自监督风险等价并具稳定性，实验证明其在 CT 任务上可媲美全监督参考模型。

Abstract: In undersampled inverse problems (such as sparse-view computed tomography), only a small number of measurements are collected, which reduces radiation exposure and acquisition time and cost, and can also address the inaccessibility of certain acquisition arrangements. Most learning-based methods for such problems require supervision in the form of fully sampled measurements and ground-truth images, which are costly or even infeasible to acquire. To overcome this issue, we propose \emph{Exact Learning for Inverse Problems from Partial Self-supervision} (ELIPPS), an incomplete self-supervised training paradigm for undersampled inverse problems that requires neither ground-truth images nor fully sampled measurements. ELIPPS learns solely from incomplete forward measurements on a fixed incomplete supervision set. Our theory shows that when the data distribution is invariant under certain transformations, minimizing a masked empirical risk is equivalent to minimizing the full self-supervised risk, i.e. the risk against the complete, noise-free measurement. The equivalence is exact for arbitrary equivariant hypothesis classes and holds for signal-dependent noise such as the pre-log Poisson statistics of low-dose tomography, and for the log-transformed count model up to a quantifiable bias. When the underlying coverage condition is only approximately satisfied on a discrete grid, we give a stability estimate in terms of the associated frame constants and the irreducible error of the inverse problem. We realize ELIPPS for computed tomography by exploiting rotation and reflection invariance. In our experiments, ELIPPS substantially outperforms naive masked supervision and reaches the same order of accuracy as a reference model trained with full clean measurements.

</details>


### [249] [Blind error estimation for CUR approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23810)
*Lorenzo Lazzarino,Katherine J. Pearce,Nathaniel Pritchard*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对CUR分解在仅能观测部分矩阵条目约束下的精度评估难题，推导了一种"盲"Frobenius误差估计器，给出了所需列查询数的下界及不确定性量化方法，并在合成与真实谱显微问题上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩近似是规模化矩阵计算的基础工具，随机数值线性代数方法（如CUR分解）能以较低代价达到与截断SVD相当的精度，且仅需访问矩阵的部分行和列，适合无法进行矩阵-向量乘积的场景（如谱显微实验设计）。然而这类方法的随机性带来一个关键挑战：在只能观测部分矩阵条目的严格约束下，如何高效评估近似精度。

Method: 作者针对CUR分解推导了一个"盲"的Frobenius误差估计器（即在无法访问完整矩阵、只能查询部分列的条件下估计误差），并推导了该估计器所需列查询数的最坏情况下界，同时提出了量化其不确定性的技术。

Result: 在合成问题与真实谱显微（spectro-microscopy）实例的混合测试中，验证了所提误差估计器及不确定性集合的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为在仅能访问部分矩阵条目的受限场景下评估CUR分解精度提供了理论保证与实用方法，适用于谱显微等无法进行矩阵-向量乘积的应用。

Abstract: Low-rank approximation is a fundamental tool for scalable matrix computations. While such approximations have classically been formed via the truncated SVD, recent advances in randomized numerical linear algebra have produced methods of comparable accuracy at a fraction of the cost. A key advantage of these approaches is their ability to operate with limited access to the full matrix. A prominent example is the CUR decomposition, which builds an approximation from only a subset of columns and rows, making it particularly well-suited to settings where global matrix access is unavailable, such as in the design of spectro-microscopy experiments where even matrix-vector products cannot be computed. However, the randomness inherent to these methods introduces a key challenge: efficiently assessing approximation accuracy under the stringent constraint that only a subset of matrix entries can be observed. We derive a "blind" estimator of the Frobenius error of a CUR decomposition. For this estimator, we derive a worst-case lower bound on the number of required column queries, and provide techniques for quantifying its uncertainty. Finally, we demonstrate the performance of the estimator and the uncertainty sets on a mix of synthetic problems and real spectro-microscopy examples.

</details>


### [250] [An Implementation-Friendly SDG Scheme based on Cartesian Grids for Stokes Equations with Pressure Robustness and Superconvergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24232)
*Bohan Yang,Eric Chung*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于笛卡尔网格的交错间断伽辽金(SDG)格式求解Stokes方程，实现简单、本质压力鲁棒且所有变量二阶超收敛，并推广至Navier-Stokes方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SDG使用复合网格，实现复杂；且需要一种实现简单、压力鲁棒并具超收敛性的格式，同时可扩展到非线性流动问题。

Method: 从笛卡尔网格构造交错四边形网格；以速度、压力和速度梯度为未知量，采用分片常数空间并设计交错连续性；梯度未知量经静力凝聚局部消除，并可通过质量集中进一步移除；推导显式逐点格式；引入二阶相容的离散对流项，结合SAV方法与Crank-Nicolson格式扩展至Navier-Stokes方程。

Result: 严格证明了压力鲁棒性和二阶超收敛性，且在一般非均匀笛卡尔网格上成立；Navier-Stokes格式无条件能量稳定且二阶精度；数值实验验证理论与精度鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该SDG格式简单、压力鲁棒、超收敛，并成功扩展到Navier-Stokes方程，兼具稳定性与精度。

Abstract: This paper develops a staggered discontinuous Galerkin (SDG) scheme based on Cartesian grids for Stokes equations that is simple to implement, intrinsically pressure-robust, and superconvergent for all variables. Instead of the composite meshes used in standard SDG, we construct staggered quadrilateral meshes from Cartesian grids. The scheme takes velocity, pressure, and velocity gradient as unknowns, and employs piecewise-constant spaces with carefully designed staggered continuity. The additional gradient unknowns are locally eliminated via static condensation and can be further removed by mass lumping without loss of accuracy. An explicit pointwise formulation of the scheme is derived, which facilitates implementation and enables a detailed pointwise analysis. We rigorously prove pressure robustness and second-order superconvergence, which holds on general non-uniform Cartesian grids. The scheme is further extended to Navier-Stokes equations by introducing a novel discrete convection term with second-order consistency. Combined with the scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) approach and the Crank-Nicolson (CN) scheme, this yields an unconditionally energy-stable and second-order accurate scheme. Numerical experiments validate the theory and demonstrate accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [251] [Numerical differentiation of functions on the half-line in a weighted uniform metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23833)
*S. G. Solodky,Y. A. Volynets*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究半直线上的数值微分问题：由带扰动 Fourier–Laguerre 系数恢复 $f^{(r)}$，误差以 $\ell_p$ 度量、精度以权重 $t^\alpha e^{-t}$ 下的一致度量衡量，建立 Wiener 类 $W^\mu_s$ 的适定框架，并构造达到最优精度阶且输入信息量渐近最小的最优方法类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半直线上的函数数值微分缺乏统一的适定框架与最优方法。本文旨在刻画使该类问题适定的函数类条件，并在给定扰动系数条件下确定可实现的最优精度阶，同时尽可能减少所需的输入信息量。

Method: 考虑从有限个扰动 Fourier–Laguerre 系数恢复 $f^{(r)}$，系数误差用 $\ell_p$ 范数度量，逼近误差用加权一致范数 $\|t^\alpha e^{-t}(\cdot)\|_\infty$ 度量；证明 Wiener 类 $W^\mu_s$ 在 $\mu>r+1-1/s$ 时构成适定设置；构造方法类 $\mathcal{S}^\theta$ 并分析其最优性。

Result: 证明当 $\mu>r+1-1/s$ 时，Wiener 类 $W^\mu_s$ 允许上述数值微分设置，即问题在该类上适定。所构造的方法类 $\mathcal{S}^\theta$ 在 $W^\mu_s$ 上实现数值微分的最优精度阶，且按阶使用最少的输入信息。

Conclusion: Fourier–Laguerre 系数框架下，半直线函数的数值微分在 Wiener 类 $W^\mu_s$（$\mu>r+1-1/s$）上适定，方法类 $\mathcal{S}^\theta$ 同时达到最优精度阶与渐近最优信息效率。

Abstract: We consider the problem of numerical differentiation of functions defined on the half-line: the derivative $f^{(r)}$, $ r=1,2,\ldots$, is recovered from a finite set of perturbed Fourier--Laguerre coefficients of a function $f$, whose error is measured in $\ell_p$, while the accuracy of approximation is measured in the uniform metric with the weight $t^αe^{-t}$. It is established that the Wiener class $W^μ_{s}$ admits such a setting for $μ>r+1-1/s$. A class of methods $\mathcal{S}^θ$ is constructed that realize the optimal order of accuracy of numerical differentiation on $W^μ_{s}$ and use the smallest amount of input information in order.

</details>


### [252] [Fourth-Order Energy-Consistent Average Vector Field Discrete Gradient Methods for Dissipative Hamiltonian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24692)
*Lucas Lautwein,Nicole Marheineke,Håkon Noren Myhr,Kevin Schäfers*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将针对Poisson系统的扰动原理推广到耗散Hamilton系统，通过基于合同变换的补全保持耗散矩阵半正定性，构造出四阶平均向量场离散梯度格式，并给出保结构有理逼近与Padé分析，数值实验验证其能量一致性与效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散梯度方法虽能实现能量一致的时间积分，但通常最多只有二阶收敛精度。作者希望在耗散Hamilton系统中突破这一阶数限制，获得高阶且无条件能量耗散的保结构格式。

Method: 将此前针对Poisson系统发展的扰动原理扩展到耗散情形，推导四阶平均向量场离散梯度格式；采用基于合同变换的补全策略保持耗散矩阵的半正定性，从而保证任意正步长下的无条件能量耗散性。针对纯耗散系统，进一步构造指数补全与保结构有理逼近，并刻画能保持半正定性、可嵌入能量相关分裂格式的Padé逼近。

Result: 在阻尼单摆与耗散Fermi–Pasta–Ulam系统上的数值实验表明：半正定补全对避免非物理数值不稳定、正确刻画定性动力学至关重要；所提方法达到预期精度与能量一致性，计算成本与现有Galerkin类方法相当；四阶格式在所考察精度范围内效率优于传统二阶离散梯度方法，尽管单步成本更高。

Conclusion: 基于合同变换的补全为构造高阶能量一致格式提供了系统化途径，四阶平均向量场离散梯度方法在保持无条件能量耗散性的同时兼顾精度与效率，适用于耗散Hamilton系统的长时间保结构模拟。

Abstract: Discrete gradient methods provide a structure-preserving approach to energy-consistent time integration of dissipative Hamiltonian systems. In their conventional formulation, however, they generally attain at most second-order convergence. Extending the perturbation principle previously developed for Poisson systems to the dissipative setting, we derive fourth-order average vector field discrete gradient schemes. A congruence-based completion preserves the positive semi-definiteness of the dissipation matrix, thereby guaranteeing unconditional energy dissipativity for all positive step sizes. This completion concept provides a systematic route to higher-order energy-consistent schemes. For purely dissipative systems, an alternative exponential completion is derived, together with structure-preserving rational approximations. In particular, we characterize Padé approximants that preserve positive semi-definiteness and can be incorporated into energy-associated splitting schemes. Numerical experiments on a damped physical pendulum and a dissipative Fermi--Pasta--Ulam system demonstrate the importance of the positive semi-definite completion for avoiding nonphysical numerical instabilities and capturing the correct qualitative dynamics. They further show that the proposed methods achieve the desired accuracy and energy consistency at a computational cost competitive with existing Galerkin-type approaches. Moreover, the fourth-order schemes outperform the conventional second-order discrete gradient method in computational efficiency over the considered accuracy range, despite their higher per-step cost.

</details>


### [253] [Time-uniform accuracy of ensemble Kalman filters with localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23927)
*Xiaoou Cheng,Daniel Sanz-Alonso,Nathan Waniorek*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为确定性平方根集合卡尔曼滤波（EnKF）在完美模型下的线性高斯状态空间模型中建立了时间一致（time-uniform）的精度保证。对双曲可检测系统，证明集合均值与协方差在高概率下随时间一致地逼近卡尔曼滤波对应量，且无需inflaion或重采样；所需集合规模取决于初始协方差的有效秩与不稳定子空间维数，而非环境状态维数。对弱耦合空间系统，分析了局部化平方根EnKF，导出将采样误差与局部化偏差分离的精度界：局部化滤波所需集合规模取决于局部内在维数并仅对数依赖于空间块数，局部化偏差随耦合强度缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合卡尔曼滤波（EnKF）在实践中广泛使用，但其精度保证多为有限时间或渐近结果，缺乏对确定性平方根EnKF的时间一致（uniform-in-time）非渐近精度刻画；同时，高维空间系统中局部化（localization）虽被广泛采用，但其采样误差与局部化偏差的定量分离尚不清晰，尤其所需集合规模与状态维数、空间块数的依赖关系有待严格分析。

Method: 针对完美模型动力学下的线性高斯状态空间模型，对双曲、可检测系统分析确定性平方根EnKF。核心工具包括：对可能奇异的Riccati递推的稳定性与遗忘（forgetting）分析、非渐近协方差集中不等式，以及局部化协方差动力学的扰动估计。对弱耦合空间系统进一步分析带局部化的平方根EnKF，利用稳定化卡尔曼协方差从动力学中继承的空间衰减性质，将总误差分解为采样误差与局部化偏差分别界定。

Result: 证明对双曲可检测系统，集合均值与协方差随时间一致、高概率地逼近卡尔曼滤波对应量，无需inflation或重采样；所需集合规模仅依赖于初始协方差的有效秩和不稳定子空间维数，而非环境状态维数。对局部化滤波，证明稳定化卡尔曼协方差继承空间衰减，并得到分离采样误差与局部化偏差的精度界：所需集合规模取决于局部内在维数、仅对数依赖于空间块数，局部化偏差随相互作用强度缩放。

Conclusion: 该工作为确定性平方根EnKF提供了时间一致的非渐近精度理论，表明其样本复杂度由系统内在低维结构（有效秩、不稳定子空间、局部内在维数）决定，而非环境维数，从理论上解释了EnKF与局部化在高维系统中高效的根本原因，并为集合规模与局部化强度的选取提供了定量指导。

Abstract: This paper establishes time-uniform accuracy guarantees for deterministic square-root ensemble Kalman filters in linear--Gaussian state-space models under perfect-model dynamics. For hyperbolic, detectable systems, we prove that the ensemble means and covariances approximate their Kalman filter counterparts uniformly in time, with high probability and without inflation or resampling. The required ensemble size depends on the effective rank of the initial covariance and the dimension of the unstable subspace, rather than on the ambient state dimension. We also analyze a localized square-root ensemble Kalman filter for weakly coupled spatial systems. We show that the stabilizing Kalman covariance inherits spatial decay from the dynamics and derive accuracy bounds that separate sampling error from localization bias. For the localized filter, the required ensemble size depends on the local intrinsic dimension and only logarithmically on the number of spatial blocks, while the localization bias scales with the interaction strength. Our analysis combines stability and forgetting for possibly singular Riccati recursions, nonasymptotic covariance concentration, and perturbation estimates for localized covariance dynamics.

</details>


### [254] [Cost-Accuracy Trade-offs: Neural Operator vs Classical Numerical Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24021)
*Daniel Zhengyu Huang,Andrew M. Stuart*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过可复现基准测试，系统比较了神经算子代理模型与经典数值求解器在成本-精度上的表现，界定了神经算子何时、为何优于经典求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为数据驱动代理模型可用于多次查询场景，但学界尚不清楚其在给定精度下的成本何时及为何能超越经典数值求解器；作者希望在训练成本完全摊销的最有利条件下，厘清二者的适用边界。

Method: 聚焦训练后、多查询极限，将数据获取与训练成本视为固定并完全摊销；通过可复现基准，在计算科学与工程的代表性问题上，将神经算子与问题匹配的经典求解器进行对比，评价指标包括预测误差、单次查询浮点运算成本与墙钟运行时间。

Result: 神经算子在低到中等精度需求下最具竞争力；其浮点成本优势强烈依赖问题结构，主要出现在避免时间推进或非线性迭代、或仅预测降维的目标量而非完整解场时；稠密张量运算契合现代硬件可带来额外墙钟加速。随目标精度提高，神经算子达标愈发困难，经典求解器反超。

Conclusion: 神经算子代理模型在中低精度区间具有成本优势，但其优势依赖问题结构与精度要求；高精度计算中经典求解器仍不可替代，将继续在验证与高精度求解中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Neural operators are data-driven models that learn mappings from inputs that parameterize partial differential equations, such as spatially varying coefficients, initial conditions, forcing terms, boundary conditions, or geometries, to solution fields or quantities of interest. Once trained, they can serve as surrogates for classical numerical solvers in many-query settings that require repeated evaluations for varying inputs. We address the question of when, and then why, neural operator surrogates outperform classical numerical solvers, in terms of cost for a given accuracy. We focus on the post-training, many-query limit, in which data-acquisition and training costs are treated as fixed and fully amortized. Even in this deliberately favorable regime for neural operators, there are regimes in which classical solvers outperform the surrogate models. We compare the cost-accuracy performance of neural operator surrogates and classical numerical solvers through a reproducible benchmark study comparing neural operators with problem-matched classical solvers on representative problems in computational science and engineering, focusing on prediction error, per-query floating-point cost, and wall-clock runtime. Neural operators are most competitive at low-to-moderate accuracy requirements. Their floating-point cost advantage depends strongly on the problem structure, arising when they avoid temporal or nonlinear iterations or predict a reduced quantity of interest rather than a full solution field. Additional wall-clock speedups result from dense tensor operations that are well suited to modern hardware. As the target accuracy is tightened, achieving the required accuracy with neural operators becomes increasingly challenging, and classical solvers outperform surrogates in this regime; thus classical solvers will remain important for verification and high-accuracy computation.

</details>


### [255] [Accurate wall shear stress in immersed flow analysis with application to point cloud-based CFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24069)
*Monu Jaiswal,Ming-Chen Hsu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在点云CFD中精确获取壁面剪应力(WSS)的新方法，采用非对称Nitsche公式结合近壁建模和基于patch的应力恢复技术，在典型基准、球体湍流绕流及患者主动脉案例上验证了精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云CFD避免了曲面重建和贴体网格生成，但局部壁面剪应力(WSS)的精确预测仍是难点：点云与背景网格任意相交产生的切割单元缺乏梯度评估所需的正则性；对称Nitsche公式中弱边界条件的稳定项参数依赖于切割构型，并影响由变分一致定义的牵引力，从而影响WSS计算。

Method: 提出新方法：采用非对称Nitsche公式（避免对称公式中依赖切割构型的稳定参数），引入近壁建模，并设计基于patch的应力恢复方法以保证牵引力相容性，从而在浸没式流动分析中精确计算WSS。

Result: 方法在典型基准算例上得到验证，并应用于球体湍流绕流与患者个性化主动脉算例，结果与参考结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 所提非对称Nitsche结合近壁建模与patch应力恢复的方法能够有效解决点云CFD中WSS预测的精度瓶颈，具有良好的准确性与适用性。

Abstract: Point cloud-based CFD enables flow analysis directly on discrete points obtained from 3D scanning and medical imaging, bypassing surface reconstruction, geometry cleanup, and boundary-fitted mesh generation. Derived from immersogeometric analysis, the method immerses the point cloud in a background mesh and enforces no-slip conditions on discrete points through a Nitsche-based weak boundary condition (BC). The framework delivers accurate velocity fields, pressure distribution, and integrated loads; however, accurate prediction of the local wall shear stress (WSS) has remained a critical challenge. The geometry intersects the background mesh arbitrarily, producing cut elements that lack the regularity required for consistent gradient evaluation. The issue is compounded by the stabilization term of the weak BC, whose parameter estimation in the symmetric Nitsche formulation is dependent on the cut configuration and affects the variationally consistent definition of traction from which the WSS is computed. In this work, we propose a new method to obtain accurate wall shear stress in immersed flow analysis with application to point cloud-based CFD, using a non-symmetric Nitsche's formulation with near-wall modeling and a patch-based stress recovery approach with traction compatibility. The method is validated on canonical benchmarks and applied to turbulent flow past a sphere and to a patient-specific aorta, showcasing excellent agreement with reference results.

</details>


### [256] [A mesh- and horizon-robust preconditioner for finite element discretizations of volume-constrained nonlocal diffusion problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24143)
*Jiashu Lu,Yufeng Nie,Haolun Zhang,Pingrui Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对体积约束非局部扩散问题的有限元离散，构造了对网格尺寸与相互作用视界均稳健的预条件子 B_h=G_h+βδ²D_h^{-1}，并证明预处理系统谱一致有界且远离零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部扩散问题有限元离散后形成的线性系统条件数同时受网格尺寸和相互作用视界影响，随视界变小或网格加密而急剧恶化，导致迭代求解效率低下，亟需对视界与网格均稳健的预条件方法。

Method: 以非局部算子的Fourier乘子为动机，构造预条件子 B_h=G_h+βδ²D_h^{-1}，其中 G_h 为对称局部Poisson求解器，D_h 为集中质量矩阵，β 为依赖核的常数；严格证明预处理系统谱上界有界且一致远离零；为保持预条件子与PCG所需的对称正定性，提出对单元（pair-cell）装配格式；并给出有限元离散的 L² 误差估计。

Result: 理论上证明预处理系统谱关于网格尺寸和视界一致有界且下有界；数值实验中 G_h 采用单个对称几何多重网格V循环，结果验证了预测的收敛率，并在所测试的网格、视界和核下展现出有界的条件数与PCG迭代次数。

Conclusion: 所提预条件子对网格尺寸与相互作用视界均稳健，配合多重网格V循环可高效求解体积约束非局部扩散问题的有限元系统，实现与网格和视界无关的良好收敛性。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a preconditioner that is robust with respect to both the mesh size and the interaction horizon for finite element discretizations of volume-constrained nonlocal diffusion problems. Motivated by the Fourier multiplier of the nonlocal operator, we construct the preconditioner $B_h=G_h+βδ^2D_h^{-1}$, where $G_h$ is a symmetric local Poisson solver, $D_h$ is the lumped mass matrix, and $β$ is a kernel-dependent constant. We rigorously prove that the spectrum of the $B_h$-preconditioned system is bounded above and away from zero uniformly with respect to the mesh size and horizon. Moreover, we develop a pair-cell assembly for the finite element matrix that preserves the symmetry and positive definiteness required by the preconditioner and PCG, and establish an $L^2$-error estimate for the finite element discretization. Finally, we use a single symmetric geometric multigrid V-cycle for $G_h$ in the numerical experiments, and the corresponding numerical results confirm the predicted convergence rate and show bounded condition numbers and PCG iteration counts over the tested meshes, horizons, and kernels.

</details>


### [257] [A Discretely Entropy Conserving/Stable Flux Reconstruction Scheme via Hybridized Split Form Differential Operator and Generalized Entropy Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24257)
*Geng Liang,Junjie Wang,Hui Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的离散熵守恒/稳定的通量重构(FR)格式，用于非线性守恒律，通过混合分裂型微分算子与广义熵投影实现任意节点分布与修正函数下的离散熵守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性守恒律的FR格式在任意节点分布和修正函数下难以同时保持守恒性与离散熵守恒，且通常需修改原求积权重，限制了通用性与稳定性。

Method: 将混合分裂型(split-form)微分算子与广义熵投影相结合，直接构建于经典FR框架之上，无需修改原始求积权重，适用于任意节点分布与任意修正函数，并分析其守恒性与半离散熵守恒性质。

Result: 等熵涡数值实验验证了理论预测的精度，并展示了在熵耗散界面通量下的稳健熵行为。

Conclusion: 该格式在保持经典FR守恒特性的同时实现了半离散熵守恒，具有良好的通用性与稳定性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel discretely entropy conserving/stable flux reconstruction (FR) scheme for nonlinear conservation laws. The scheme combines a hybridized split-form differential operator with a generalized entropy projection, enabling discrete entropy conservation for arbitrary nodal distributions and arbitrary correction functions without modifying the original quadrature weights. The formulation is built directly upon the classical FR framework and is shown to preserve conservation properties while achieving entropy conservation in the semi-discrete sense. Numerical experiments using the isentropic vortex problem confirm the theoretically predicted accuracy and demonstrate robust entropy behavior with entropy-dissipative interface fluxes.

</details>


### [258] [A Nonlinear Physics-based Reduced Order Model with Convolutional-based Operator Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24300)
*Anna Ivagnes,Giovanni Stabile,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出将非线性引入控制算子表示的新范式：用卷积网络学习全阶算子的非线性低维表示，保留显式降阶方程组，并借连续卷积与RBF插值实现大规模稀疏算子压缩与在线预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影类降阶模型依赖低维线性表示，其精度受解流形Kolmogorov N-width限制；当N-width衰减缓慢时需大量线性模态。现有非线性降阶模型多只改进解场的非线性潜表示，算子仍为线性，本文转而将非线性直接引入控制算子的表示以突破该瓶颈。

Method: 学习全阶算子的非线性低维表示，同时保留显式降阶方程组；采用卷积架构利用微分算子的局部性与空间结构；训练目标兼顾解的重构精度与降阶解误差；针对精细/非结构离散产生的大规模稀疏算子，使用连续卷积直接作用于非零元；再用径向基函数插值预测未见解参数下的压缩算子，实现无需组装全阶系统的在线阶段。

Result: 在三个参数化问题上的数值实验表明，非线性算子压缩作为基于方程的降阶模型替代策略具有潜力。

Conclusion: 非线性算子压缩可绕过N-width瓶颈，为基于方程的降阶建模提供一条区别于解场非线性压缩的新路径；其在线阶段高效且适用于大规模稀疏算子。

Abstract: Reduced-order models (ROMs) are widely used to accelerate the solution of parametrized partial differential equations, with projection-based methods relying on a linear representation of the governing equations in a low-dimensional space. Their efficiency, however, is fundamentally limited by the Kolmogorov N-width of the solution manifold: when the N-width decays slowly, accurate approximations require a large number of linear modes. This limitation has motivated nonlinear ROMs, which typically replace the linear representation of the solution field with nonlinear latent representations learned through autoencoder architectures. In this work, we propose a different perspective by introducing nonlinearity directly into the representation of the governing operators. Rather than compressing only the solution field, we learn nonlinear low-dimensional representations of the full-order operators while retaining an explicit reduced system of equations. Convolutional architectures are employed to exploit the locality and spatial structure of differential operators, and the resulting compressed operators are coupled with a nonlinear representation of the solution through a training strategy that accounts for both reconstruction accuracy and the error of the reduced-order solution. To address large sparse operators arising from fine and unstructured discretizations, continuous convolutions are used to operate directly on their non-zero entries. Finally, radial basis function interpolation enables the prediction of the compressed operators at unseen parameter configurations, providing an efficient online stage without assembling the corresponding full-order system. Numerical experiments on three parametrized problems demonstrate the potential of nonlinear operator compression as an alternative strategy for equation-based ROMs.

</details>


### [259] [Autoencoders vs. Numerical Analysis--Informed Manifold Learning for Navier--Stokes Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24332)
*Alessandro Della Pia,Lucia Russo,Ioannis Kevrekidis,Constantinos Siettos*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文重新审视基于流形学习的降阶建模替代方案，使用简约扩散映射（PDMs）对二维旋转圆柱绕流进行基准测试，在重构与预测精度上与POD及卷积自编码器（AE）相当甚至更优，且训练耗时低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自编码器（AE）已成为数据驱动降阶建模（ROM）中非线性潜空间构造的主流方法，其解码器可将潜表示映射回原始状态空间。然而这种主导地位掩盖了另一类根植于经典数值分析的流形学习方法。作者希望重新评估这一被忽视的替代路径，检验其在复杂分岔流动系统上能否兼具精度与效率优势。

Method: 采用简约扩散映射（PDMs）识别简约且可解释的内蕴潜坐标，并直接从数据估计潜空间维度；潜动力学由高斯过程回归学习，前像映射通过PDMs空间中的凸K近邻（K-NN）插值构造，并对该插值建立逐点一致性。基准对象为二维不可压缩旋转圆柱绕流——一个由余维2的Bogdanov-Takens点及其相关Hopf、鞍结与同宿分岔组织的Navier-Stokes系统，并与基于POD的ROM及多种卷积AE架构进行对比。

Result: 所得非线性ROM显著优于基于POD的ROM，其重构与预测精度与基于AE的ROM相当，且在某些分岔区间内更优；同时，PDMs的潜变量学习所需计算时间比AE训练低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 基于经典数值分析的流形学习方法（PDMs）可作为AE的有力替代方案，在复杂分岔流动的降阶建模中同时实现竞争性精度与显著的计算效率优势，值得作为非线性潜空间构造的重要选项被重新重视。

Abstract: Autoencoders (AEs) have become a dominant approach to nonlinear latent-space construction in data-driven reduced-order modelling (ROM), with their decoders lifting latent representations back to the ambient state space. Their prominence, however, has overshadowed an established alternative: manifold-learning methods grounded in classical numerical analysis. We revisit this alternative using Parsimonious Diffusion Maps (PDMs), benchmarking them against Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD)-based ROMs and several convolutional AE architectures for the two-dimensional incompressible flow past a rotating cylinder ---a bifurcating Navier-Stokes (NS) system organized by a codimension-2 Bogdanov-Takens point and its associated Hopf, saddle-node, and homoclinic bifurcations. Our approach uses PDMs to identify a parsimonious and interpretable set of intrinsic latent coordinates and to estimate their dimension directly from data. Gaussian process regression then learns the latent dynamics, while convex K-nearest-neighbor (K-NN) interpolation in PDMs space constructs the pre-image map, for which we establish pointwise consistency. The resulting nonlinear ROM substantially outperforms POD-based ROMs and achieves reconstruction and prediction accuracy comparable to ---and, in some bifurcating regimes, better than--- that of AE-based ROMs. At the same time, latent-variable learning with PDMs requires orders of magnitude less computational time than AE training.

</details>


### [260] [Morley element for the linear strain gradient elasticity problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24340)
*Xiao Li,Shudan Tian,Hua Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了满足二阶弱连续性条件的有限元空间上的离散 $H^1$-Korn 不等式，证明经典 $H^2$-非协调元满足离散 Korn 不等式，并据此提出无需罚参数的稳定化 Morley 有限元方法求解线性应变梯度弹性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性应变梯度弹性问题的数值求解通常需要 $H^2$ 正则性或 $C^1$ 连续性，经典 Morley 元等 $H^2$-非协调元自然适用，但需要离散 Korn 不等式以保证稳定性；以往方法往往引入罚参数且难以同时关于材料参数稳健，因此本文旨在建立一个更一般的离散 Korn 不等式框架，从而构造无罚参数的稳定化方法。

Method: 首先在满足二阶弱连续性条件的有限元空间上建立离散 $H^1$-Korn 不等式；由此推出经典 $H^2$-非协调元也满足该不等式。基于此结果，仅使用分片二次多项式构造线性应变梯度弹性的稳定化 Morley 有限元方法，利用离散 Korn 不等式替代罚项来保证稳定性。随后进行关于参数 $λ$ 与 $ι$ 的稳健性分析。

Result: 所提方法无需任何罚参数，并证明其对 $λ$ 和 $ι$ 均具有稳健性；数值实验验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 离散 $H^1$-Korn 不等式为 $H^2$-非协调元提供了稳定性理论基础，使无罚参数的稳定化 Morley 方法可行且关于材料参数稳健，为应变梯度弹性问题提供了高效数值方案。

Abstract: We establish a discrete $H^1$-Korn inequality for finite element spaces satisfying a second order weak continuity condition. This implies that classical $H^2$-nonconforming elements satisfy the discrete Korn inequality. Based on this result, we develop a stabilized Morley finite element method for linear strain gradient elasticity using only piecewise quadratic polynomials. Thanks to the discrete $H^1$-Korn inequality, this method does not require any penalty parameter. We prove that the method is robust with respect to both $λ$ and $ι$. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results.

</details>


### [261] [The transposition method in saddle-point problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24478)
*Dietmar Gallistl,Zhen Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将二阶椭圆偏微分方程理论中的转置方法推广至满足Brezzi分裂结构假设及椭圆正则性映射性质的鞍点问题，构建了一个可用标准（未修改）Galerkin型方法进行逼近的框架，允许方法相对于转置算子所容许的更一般数据是非协调的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典转置方法源于二阶椭圆偏微分方程理论，用于处理数据正则性不足的问题。作者希望将其推广到鞍点问题，并借助Brezzi分裂结构与反映具体微分算子（含边界条件）椭圆正则性的映射性质，来放宽对数据正则性的要求。

Method: 在Brezzi分裂的结构假设下，结合刻画微分算子带边界条件时椭圆正则性的映射性质，为鞍点问题构造转置方法的框架；该框架保证可使用标准、未修改的Galerkin型方法进行逼近，且允许逼近方法相对于转置算子所容许的更一般数据是非协调的。

Result: 所构造的框架能够覆盖多个具体实例，包括Laplacian、Stokes算子、线弹性、双调和算子（bi-Laplacian），以及带L²边界数据的定常Maxwell问题；一个特点是数据无需正则化即可处理，这是鞍点方法特有的优势。

Conclusion: 转置方法可成功推广到满足特定结构与正则性假设的鞍点问题，并借助标准Galerkin型方法实现逼近，且无需对数据进行正则化；该框架在Laplacian、Stokes、线弹性、双调和及Maxwell等问题上具有适用性。

Abstract: The transposition method known from the theory of second-order elliptic partial differential equations is formulated for saddle-point problems under certain structural assumptions known from the Brezzi splitting plus mapping properties that reflect elliptic regularity in concrete instances of differential operators with boundary conditions. A framework is devised that grants approximation by standard, unmodified Galerkin-type methods, which may be nonconforming with respect to the more general data allowed by the transposed operator. That the data need not be regularized is an advantage particular to the saddle-point approach. Examples covered by the framework include the Laplacian, the Stokes operator, linear elasticity, the bi-Laplacian, and the stationary Maxwell problem with $L^2$ boundary data.

</details>


### [262] [Numerical Realization of an Entropy-Based Selection Principle in the Example of Kelvin-Helmholtz Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24479)
*Shaoshuai Chu,Michael Herty*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将基于参数化Young测度与线性规划的数值格式推广到二维，并应用于二维Euler方程描述的Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性，比较了1至9阶Young测度格式与LLF及LLF-ELCD格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证二维Young测度格式的可行性，并考察线性规划目标函数能否作为耗散弱解的有效选择机制；同时检验将熵直接嵌入传统格式是否能够复现Young测度格式所表现出的熵偏好。

Method: 构建一阶、二阶、三阶、五阶、七阶与九阶参数化Young测度格式，在二维空间离散中结合线性规划求解；将其与标准LLF通量分裂格式及带熵基局部特征分解的LLF-ELCD格式对比，考察瞬时与时间平均密度剖面、跨空间重构阶数的累积平均、区域经验密度分布、平均密度边缘分布以及若干耗散弱解选择准则。

Result: 数值结果显示流动结构依赖具体格式，尤其体现在剪切层卷起生成的小尺度结构上。在每一种重构阶数下，Young测度格式均给出最大的时间平均物理熵；LLF-ELCD格式的熵并不系统性地高于标准LLF格式，因而不构成一致的最大熵选择机制。Young测度的平均密度边缘分布集中于若干相邻密度态，且各Young测度格式的后期累积平均趋于接近。

Conclusion: 结果证明了二维Young测度表述的可行性，并表明线性规划问题中的目标函数可作为有效的选择机制。所观察到的熵偏好与期望物理熵的局部优化一致，且仅通过将熵纳入传统数值构造无法复现。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend numerical schemes based on parameterized Young measures and linear programming to two space dimensions and apply them to the Kelvin-Helmholtz instability governed by the two-dimensional Euler equations of gas dynamics. We construct first-, second-, third-, fifth-, seventh-, and ninth-order Young-measure schemes and compare them with corresponding standard local Lax-Friedrichs (LLF) flux-splitting schemes and LLF schemes equipped with an entropy-based local characteristic decomposition (LLF-ELCD). We examine instantaneous and time-averaged density profiles, cumulative averages across spatial reconstruction orders, regional empirical density distributions, averaged density marginals, and several selection criteria for dissipative weak solutions. The numerical results reveal scheme-dependent flow patterns, particularly in the small-scale structures generated during the roll-up of the shear layers. At every reconstruction order considered, the Young-measure schemes yield the largest time-averaged physical entropy. By contrast, the LLF-ELCD schemes do not systematically yield larger entropy than the standard LLF schemes and therefore do not constitute a consistent maximum-entropy selection mechanism. The averaged density marginals of the Young measures are concentrated on several neighboring density states, and the later cumulative averages are closer for all Young-measure schemes. These results demonstrate the feasibility of the two-dimensional Young-measure formulation and show that the objective function in the linear-programming problem can act as an effective selection mechanism. The observed entropy preference is consistent with the local optimization of the expected physical entropy. It cannot be reproduced by merely incorporating entropy into a conventional numerical construction.

</details>


### [263] [Error Analysis and Precision Selection for Mixed-Precision DEIM-CUR Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24509)
*Ioannis Thanasis,Erin Carson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析不精确SVD及有限精度算术对CUR近似（特别是DEIM-CUR方法）质量的影响，给出SVD与DEIM索引选择的工作精度选取准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUR近似（多基于SVD）的质量界通常假设全部或部分SVD被精确计算，这一假设在实际有限精度计算中并不成立，因此需要放松该假设以刻画误差影响。

Method: 放松“SVD精确计算”的假设，建立不精确SVD对CUR近似质量影响的理论分析；进一步分析有限精度算术对DEIM-CUR方法的影响，并据此推导SVD计算与DEIM索引选择算法的精度选择准则。

Result: 理论分析表明，在许多情况下可对SVD和DEIM索引选择使用很低的精度，而不会显著损害近似质量；并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 不精确SVD与有限精度算术虽会影响CUR近似质量，但在合理选定的低精度下影响可控，为低精度CUR/DEIM-CUR实现提供了精度选取指导。

Abstract: CUR approximation is a popular approach for constructing an interpretable low-rank approximation of a matrix. There are many approaches for constructing the CUR approximation, many of which are based on the SVD. Results in the literature bounding the quality of the approximation usually rely on the assumption that all or part of the SVD is computed exactly. In this work, we relax this assumption and show how the quality of the approximation is affected by an inexact SVD. Moreover, we analyze the effect of finite precision arithmetic on the DEIM-CUR method. Our analysis suggests guidelines for selecting the working precisions for the SVD and the DEIM index selection algorithm. In particular, it suggests that in many cases one can use very low precisions for these computations without significantly impacting the approximation quality. A set of numerical experiments is presented to demonstrate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [264] [A priori regularity of the reverse heat flow and dimension-dependent complexity of higher-order diffusion samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24622)
*Xixian Wang,Zhongjian Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为分数扩散模型概率流ODE（逆向热流）在Ornstein-Uhlenbeck框架下建立任意阶先验正则性估计，常数与维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向热流（即分数扩散模型的概率流ODE）在数值离散化时的正则性缺乏系统性理论，尤其需要与维度无关的估计来支撑高维采样算法的收敛性与复杂度分析。

Method: 针对紧支撑、可能奇异的、满足支撑-容量测度维度一致加倍条件的目标分布，对流的迭代物质导数及其一阶空间导数给出逐点界。证明基于两个要素：法向后验涨落的空间一致界（目标几何唯一进入处），以及物质微分下封闭的中心后验矩正规形；结合加权正则性演算得到各阶定量界，包括Runge-Kutta阶段所需的混合时空方向导数。

Result: 对前向时间δ>0处停止的精确分数采样，p阶Taylor与显式Runge-Kutta格式在总变差距离下以Õ(d^{1/p}ε^{-1/p})步达到精度ε，Taylor格式在W_2下以Õ(d^{1/(2p)}ε^{-1/p})步达到精度ε。

Conclusion: 建立了逆向热流的任意阶、维度无关的先验正则性理论，为分数扩散模型高维采样的高阶数值格式提供了严格复杂度保证。

Abstract: We establish arbitrary-order a priori regularity estimates for the reverse heat flow in the Ornstein--Uhlenbeck setting, that is, for the probability-flow ODE of score-based diffusion models. For compactly supported, possibly singular targets satisfying a dimension-uniform doubling condition on supporting-cap masses, we bound the iterated material derivatives of the flow and their first spatial derivatives pointwise, with constants independent of the dimension $d$ and explicit in time and support radius. The proof rests on two ingredients: a spatially uniform bound on normal-direction posterior fluctuations, which is the only point where the geometry of the target enters, and a centered posterior-moment normal form closed under material differentiation. This algebraic structure, together with a weighted regularity calculus, yields quantitative bounds at every order, including the mixed space--time directional derivatives required by Runge--Kutta stages. As an application, for exact-score sampling stopped at forward time $δ>0$, order-$p$ Taylor and explicit Runge--Kutta schemes reach accuracy $\varepsilon$ in $\widetilde O(d^{1/p}\varepsilon^{-1/p})$ steps in total variation, and the Taylor scheme in $\widetilde O(d^{1/(2p)}\varepsilon^{-1/p})$ steps in $W_2$.

</details>


### [265] [Edge-based Katz centralities for spatio-temporal multiplex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24638)
*Kai Bergermann,Francesco Gravili,Valeria Simoncini,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向空间网络的故障感知时序多层网络模型，用于计算边基Katz中心性，并用块三对角/带状超邻接矩阵与两种截断方法实现高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Katz中心性研究多关注节点基量，边基度量探索不足；时序网络Katz中心性易因结构小变化导致大范围中心性剧变，且缺乏面向实时基础设施监测的故障感知与高效计算方案。

Method: 构建故障感知的时序多层网络模型，基于线图表示空间网络；采用块三角、块带状超邻接矩阵，将层间连接建模为低秩矩阵，对更新或过时边施加空间约束权重；分析块对角线上方块的逆矩阵元素衰减结构，据此提出两种截断方法以降低计算量并控制误差。

Result: 在规模达2×10^8的真实时空网络上验证，方法在保持精度的同时显著提升效率，运行时间最高加速679倍。

Conclusion: 该模型与截断算法可高效、可控地计算空间时序网络中的边基Katz中心性，适用于水管/燃气管破裂或道路阻塞等实时基础设施监测场景。

Abstract: Katz centrality is a well-established measure to identify and rank the most important nodes in complex networks by means of a linear system solve. Recent works have developed notions of Katz centrality for temporal, i.e., time-evolving networks. Their drawback is that small changes in the network structure may drastically change centralities across large parts of the network. Moreover, an unproportional effort in the network science community has been devoted to the study of node-based quantities while edge-based measures are far less explored. In this manuscript, we introduce a novel failure-aware temporal multiplex network model for computing edge-based Katz centralities for spatial networks. This class of networks admits the use of the line graph as network representation. We use a block-triangular and -banded supra-adjacency matrix representation, modeling inter-layer connections as low-rank matrices, which assigns a spatially constrained emphasis on up- or downdated edges. These could represent, e.g., breakages of water/gas pipes or obstructions in street networks in real-time infrastructure monitoring applications. We analyze the structure of the of the block matrix inverse with respect to entry decay in the blocks above the block-diagonal. This gives rise to two truncation approaches that allow drastic computational runtime reductions at the cost of introducing a controlled truncation error. Numerical experiments on a range of real-world spatio-temporal networks of size up to $2\cdot 10^8$ illustrate accuracy and efficiency with runtime gains of up to a factor of $679$.

</details>


### [266] [Constraints on admissible behavior of GMRES applied to tridiagonal Toeplitz systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24751)
*Fei Chen,Kirk M. Soodhalter*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文利用Greenbaum–Strakoš与Arioli–Pták–Strakoš的收敛曲线参数化理论，分析非Hermitian三对角Toeplitz矩阵下GMRES的可行与可达收敛行为，证明Toeplitz结构虽施加约束，但仍可达到连续统的收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Greenbaum与Strakoš证明给定特征值集合下任意收敛曲线皆可能，Arioli、Pták与Strakoš随后参数化了对应的矩阵–右端项对，表明GMRES行为无法仅由特征值完全刻画。本文动机是借助该理论理解具有结构约束矩阵（特别是一类非Hermitian三对角Toeplitz矩阵）时GMRES的可行与可达行为。

Method: 采用Greenbaum–Strakoš及Arioli–Pták–Strakoš的矩阵–右端项参数化框架，并将该理论应用于非Hermitian（非对称）三对角Toeplitz矩阵，分析其结构约束如何限制该理论的实现方式。

Result: 研究表明Toeplitz结构必然对该理论所能呈现的方式施加约束，但仍可达到连续统的可行收敛行为。

Conclusion: 在非Hermitian三对角Toeplitz矩阵情形下，尽管结构约束限制了GMRES收敛行为的自由度，但可行行为的集合仍具有连续统的丰富性。

Abstract: The result of Greenbaum and Strakoš that for a given set of eigenvalues, any convergence curve is possible [SIMAX 1996] and the subsequent parameterization of such matrix-right-hand side pairs $(A,\mathbf{b})$ of Arioli, Pták, and Strakoš [BIT 1998] demonstrated that the behavior of the \gmres could not be completely characterized by the eigenvalues of $A$ alone. In this paper, we consider how to use this theory to understand the admissible and attainable \gmres behavior for matrices with constrained structure, focussing on non-Hermitian (non-symmetric) tridiagonal Toeplitz matrices. We show that Toeptliz structure necessarily constrains the how the theory from these papers can manifest but that a continuum of admissible behaviors is still attainable.

</details>


### [267] [An adaptive localized orthogonal decomposition method for Helmholtz problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24776)
*Yuwen Li,Chuxin Que*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应局部正交分解(ALOD)方法求解含局部振荡与奇异性的Helmholtz问题，给出了首个在标准分辨率、过采样与离散稳定性框架下波数鲁棒的残差型后验误差估计，并据此实现粗空间、细校正网格与过采样层级的分离自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helmholtz问题在局部振荡与奇异性区域需要精细离散，但全局细网格代价高昂；现有LOD后验误差估计缺乏对波数的鲁棒性，且未能将误差控制与在线降维（如POD）有机耦合，难以分别自适应粗空间、校正网格与过采样水平。

Method: 构建ALOD框架：推导双向残差型后验误差估计，含可计算的校正子局部化误差界；将指示子已计算的局部残差Riesz代表元复用为区域加性Schwarz校正，其张成构成共享富集空间，再用能量加权POD压缩以供在线使用；采用核提升(kernel-lifted)检验函数实现统一过采样条件下的稳定拟最优耦合，并通过逐成员检查控制压缩扰动。

Result: 得到首个在标准分辨率、过采样与离散稳定性机制下对Helmholtz LOD离散波数鲁棒的残差后验误差估计，估计相对细网格解双向成立，并可实现在线粗空间、细校正网格与过采样层级的分离自适应；估计对满足原始LOD检验空间Galerkin正交性的富集解仍有效。二维数值实验验证了针对给定载荷族的在线压缩与低秩复用。

Conclusion: ALOD将后验误差控制机制直接作为降维空间生成器，实现误差估计、富集与压缩的统一，为含局部振荡和奇异性的Helmholtz问题提供了波数鲁棒且计算高效的自适应求解途径。

Abstract: We develop an adaptive localized orthogonal decomposition (ALOD) method for Helmholtz problems with localized oscillations and singularities. The main result is the first wavenumber-robust residual a posteriori error estimate for a Helmholtz LOD discretization within the standard resolution, oversampling, and discrete-stability regime. The estimate is two-sided relative to the fine-grid solution and includes a computable bound for the corrector-localization error, enabling separate adaptation of the online coarse space, the fine corrector mesh, and the oversampling level. For a prescribed load family, we reuse the local residual Riesz representatives already computed by the indicator as regional additive Schwarz corrections. Their span forms a shared enrichment, which is then compressed by energy-weighted proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) for online use. Thus, the reduced space is generated directly by the adaptive error-control mechanism. The error estimate remains valid for enriched solutions satisfying Galerkin orthogonality on the original LOD test space. Kernel-lifted tests provide stable, quasi-optimal coupling under a unified oversampling condition without additional global fine-grid solves, and memberwise checks control the perturbation caused by compression. Two-dimensional experiments demonstrate online compression and low-rank reuse for the prescribed load families.

</details>


### [268] [A Domain Derivative for Electromagnetic Scattering by Perfect Conductors in the Time Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24822)
*Marvin Knöller*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立完美导体时变电磁散射的域导数，借助Laplace域分析导出Maxwell方程解的频率相关估计，用于证明域导数的时间正则性及Runge–Kutta卷积求积半离散的收敛性，并给出时间离散结合空间Galerkin法的逐点收敛分析，最后应用于迭代形状重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变（时间依赖）电磁散射中，形状重构需要域导数（形状导数）作为优化/迭代的核心工具，但现有工作多集中于频域或稳态问题，缺乏对时变问题域导数的严格数学刻画，尤其是其时间正则性与数值离散的收敛性保证。作者希望填补这一空白，为基于近场测量的时域形状重构提供理论基础与可实现的算法。

Method: 1) 通过Laplace域（拉普拉斯变换）处理时变电磁散射问题，推导Maxwell方程解的频率相关范数界。2) 利用这些界建立域导数的时间正则性质。3) 提出Runge–Kutta卷积求积（Runge–Kutta convolution quadrature）对时间进行半离散，并证明其收敛性。4) 将该时间离散与空间Galerkin方法结合，对域导数的逐点取值进行完整收敛分析。5) 将域导数嵌入迭代形状重构算法，输入数据为若干接收点处（远离完美导体）的电场近场测量。

Result: 数值实验验证了迭代形状重构算法的可行性，并表明该算法具有鲁棒性，在数据中加入额外噪声时仍能有效工作。

Conclusion: 该工作为完美导体时变电磁散射提供了域导数的严格时域理论，涵盖频率相关估计、时间正则性以及Runge–Kutta卷积求积与Galerkin全离散的收敛分析，并将理论成功应用于具有抗噪能力的迭代形状重构，证明了方法的可行性与稳健性。

Abstract: A domain derivative for time-dependent electromagnetic scattering from perfect conductors is established. By proceeding through the Laplace domain, frequency-dependent bounds on solutions to Maxwell's equations are derived. These bounds are used both for establishing time regularity properties of the domain derivative and for proving convergence of the proposed Runge--Kutta convolution quadrature semi-discretization in time. A full convergence analysis is also carried out for pointwise evaluations of the domain derivative, when this time discretization is combined with a Galerkin method in space. Eventually, the domain derivative is applied in an iterative shape reconstruction algorithm, in which measurements of the electric near field at some receiver positions, away from the perfect conductor are measured. Numerical examples show the feasibility of this algorithm and in particular highlight its robustness, when additional noise is applied to the data.

</details>


### [269] [GradAgent: A Knowledge-Guided Multi-Agent System for Structure-Preserving Gradient-Flow Computation with an Application to Multicomponent Vesicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24871)
*Zhenlin Guo,Jiale Meng,Shuqi Tang,Haiyan Su,Maosheng Jiang,Kaiwen Shi,Meng Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: GradAgent是一个知识引导的多智能体系统，通过模型分析、算法设计与证明、数值实现与验证三个智能体的协同，结合独立审计与可扩展知识图谱GradAgent-KG，自动构建并验证高阶微分算子与非线性耦合系统的守恒、能量稳定格式；在全耦合多组分囊泡相场-流体模型上生成六个无条件守恒与能量耗散的格式，并验证了精度与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合梯度流系统因高阶微分算子和非线性耦合，构造守恒且能量稳定的格式极具挑战。现有方法依赖人工推导与实现，缺乏可复用知识、跨阶段一致性与独立校验机制，易出现数学错误和证明漏洞。

Method: 提出GradAgent多智能体系统：三个智能体分别负责模型分析、算法设计与证明、数值实现与验证；引入独立审计发现数学错误与证明缺口并指导修订。Reconstruction Mode重建20篇已发表研究，将模型结构、离散策略与证明、实现及数值证据组织为可扩展知识图谱GradAgent-KG；Design Mode评估检索知识的适用性并据此设计新格式。应用于全耦合多组分囊泡相场-流体模型，产出三个算法族的三个一阶和三个二阶格式（含四个线性解耦格式）。

Result: 在给定假设下，六个格式均无条件守恒膜组分质量与囊泡体积，并耗散各自的时间离散能量。有/无GradAgent-KG的对比表明，知识图谱提升了该目标模型结构保持格式设计的多样性。数值测试确认二阶空间精度、预期时间阶、守恒性与时间离散能量耗散，三维剪切流模拟与实验定性一致。

Conclusion: GradAgent通过结合可复用知识、协同推理与独立审计，能够为复杂耦合系统开发并验证结构保持算法，为高阶耦合梯度流系统的守恒与能量稳定格式自动设计提供了可行范式。

Abstract: High-order differential operators and nonlinear coupling make it challenging to construct conservative and energy-stable schemes for coupled gradient-flow systems. We present GradAgent, a knowledge-guided multi-agent system that coordinates three agents across model analysis, algorithm design and proofs, and numerical implementation and validation. Independent audits strengthen reliability by uncovering mathematical errors and proof gaps, guiding revisions, and maintaining consistency across stages. In Reconstruction Mode, GradAgent reconstructs 20 published studies and organizes audited knowledge in an extensible knowledge graph (KG), GradAgent-KG, linking model structures, discretization strategies and proofs, implementations, and numerical evidence. In Design Mode, the agents assess the applicability of retrieved knowledge and develop new schemes informed by relevant discretization strategies. Applied to the fully coupled multicomponent vesicle phase-field-fluid model, GradAgent yields three first-order and three second-order schemes across three algorithmic families, including four linear, decoupled schemes. Under stated assumptions, all six schemes conserve membrane component mass and vesicle volume and dissipate their respective temporally discrete energies unconditionally. Comparisons with and without GradAgent-KG show that it promotes diversity in structure-preserving scheme design for this target model. Numerical tests confirm second-order spatial accuracy, the expected temporal orders, conservation, and temporally discrete energy dissipation, while three-dimensional shear-flow simulations agree qualitatively with experiments. These results demonstrate GradAgent's ability to combine reusable knowledge, coordinated reasoning, and independent auditing to develop and validate structure-preserving algorithms for complex coupled systems.

</details>


### [270] [A Contour Method for Multiparameter Eigenvalue Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24943)
*Emil Graf,Alex Townsend*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向解析多参数特征值问题的围道法，基于多变量矩阵值解析函数的新留数公式，可在指定区域一次性求出全部特征值，避免构造超大规模矩阵且易于并行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数特征值问题广泛出现于边值问题、稳定性分析与延迟微分方程中，但现有方法要么需解极大全局问题，要么依赖局部迭代只能逐个求得少量特征值，缺乏高效求解指定区域内全部特征值的手段。

Method: 理论核心是提出多变量矩阵值解析函数的新留数公式，将Beyn基于Keldysh定理的亚纯算子函数留数定理推广到多复变情形；据此推导出Beyn围道法的多维推广，在C^d中给定区域内计算多参数特征值问题的全部特征值。

Result: 所得算法无需构造超大矩阵即可求解区域内的特征值，并行性极佳（embarrassingly parallelizable）；数值实验表明，对现有方法难以处理的大规模多参数问题可以成功求解。

Conclusion: 首次将围道法拓展到多参数特征值问题，通过多复变留数理论实现区域整体求解，兼顾计算规模与并行效率，为应用中的大规模多参数问题提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Multiparameter eigenvalue problems arise in boundary value problems, stability analysis, and delay-differential equations. Despite their importance, existing methods either require solving extremely large global problems or rely on local iterative techniques that only recover a few eigenvalues at a time. In this work we develop the first contour method for analytic multiparameter eigenvalue problems. The key theoretical ingredient is a new residue formula for multivariate matrix-valued analytic functions, extending Beyn's Keldysh-based residue theorem for meromorphic operator functions to several complex variables. Using this, we derive a multidimensional analogue of Beyn's contour method that computes all the eigenvalues of a multiparameter eigenvalue problem contained in a prescribed region of $\mathbb{C}^d$. The resulting algorithm targets eigenvalues in a region without constructing preposterously large matrices and is embarrassingly parallelizable. Numerical experiments demonstrate that we can now successfully solve large multiparameter problems arising in applications where existing approaches struggle.

</details>

### [271] [Certified Residual Quasi-Newton Methods for Distributed Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22481)
*Ewsey R. Obzherin,Roman M. Mozhaev,Alexander V. Gasnikov,Martin Takáč,Artem A. Agafonov,Dmitry I. Kamzolov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式单调变分不等式的高效二阶方法。作者利用服务器Jacobian与全局Jacobian的相似性以仅一阶通信代价获得改进速率，并通过沿候选步长验证代理Jacobian的机制，最终在不传输矩阵的情况下达到精确最优速率O(L₁D³T^{-3/2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式二阶方法虽能达到最优速率O(T^{-3/2})，但传输精确Jacobian的通信量是传输算子值的d倍。作者希望利用服务器与全局Jacobian之间的相似性，在保持一阶通信代价的前提下提升收敛速度，从而缓解通信瓶颈。

Method: 利用服务器Jacobian与全局Jacobian偏差不超过β的性质构造代理模型，获得O(L₁D³T^{-3/2}+βD²T^{-1})的速率且仅需一阶通信；进一步用已通信的割线构造残差Jacobian∇F−∇F₁的拟牛顿近似；最终采用沿候选步长的认证策略——用一个Jacobian-向量乘积检验代理模型，若检验失败则复用为精确修正。

Result: 基于相似性的代理模型达到O(L₁D³T^{-3/2}+βD²T^{-1})，但拟牛顿近似无法消除T^{-1}项，因为Jacobian误差的任意一致上界都会保留该项；沿候选步长的认证方法在仅传输向量的条件下达到精确速率O(L₁D³T^{-3/2})。在LIBSVM和合成数据集上的实验测量了精度与通信量之间的关系。

Conclusion: 利用Jacobian相似性可以免费完成部分工作，而沿候选步长对代理模型进行验证与修正，能够在仅传输向量的分布式设置下达到二阶方法的精确最优速率，实现精度与通信的良好权衡。

Abstract: Second-order methods for smooth monotone variational inequalities reach the optimal rate $O(T^{-3/2})$, but a distributed exact Jacobian costs $d$ times more communication than an operator value. We show that similarity does part of the work for free: if the server's Jacobian differs from the global one by at most $β$, using it gives $O(L_1D^3T^{-3/2}+βD^2T^{-1})$ at first-order communication cost. A quasi-Newton approximation of the residual Jacobian $\nabla F-\nabla F_1$, built from secants already communicated, improves the model but cannot remove the $T^{-1}$ term, because any uniform bound on the Jacobian error leaves it in the rate. We therefore certify the surrogate only along the candidate step: one Jacobian-vector product tests it, and a failed test is reused as an exact correction. This attains the exact rate $O(L_1D^3T^{-3/2})$ while transmitting only vectors. Experiments on LIBSVM and synthetic instances measure accuracy against communication.

</details>


### [272] [Combinatorial Optimization Augmented Machine Learning for Dynamic Electric Autonomous Dial-a-Ride Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22495)
*Yue Su,Léa Ricard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于决策周期的动态电动自主需求响应交通问题（Dyn-EADARP），并开发ML-CO策略（COAML框架）求解，在320个测试实例上验证了高效性与优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态需求响应场景中，请求按周期到达，服务方不仅需决定如何服务请求，还需决定何时派发；同时电动车辆带来路径、调度与充电的联合决策复杂性，现有方法难以兼顾实时性与解质量。

Method: 定义Dyn-EADARP：在每个决策周期收集并处理新请求，决定服务或推迟，并联合确定车辆路径、时间表与充电决策，已有计划未执行部分可随新信息修正。采用组合优化增强机器学习（COAML）框架，构建ML-CO策略：统计模型预测可用请求的prize，prize-collecting E-ADARP据此联合优化请求选择、路径、调度与充电，统计模型直接以优化层决策质量为目标进行训练。

Result: 在320个测试实例上，ML-CO平均约6秒求解近500个请求的实例；目标值比基准策略低8.7%–14.3%，与预见性参考解平均差距4.8%。

Conclusion: 管理启示：立即服务请求并非总是最优，选择性推迟可创造更好共乘机会；修正已有计划能保持运营灵活性并显著提升解质量；决策频率越高并不必然提升性能，需选择合适决策频率。

Abstract: This study introduces a decision-epoch-based dynamic electric autonomous dial-a-ride problem (Dyn-EADARP), in which incoming requests are collected and processed at periodic decision epochs. A key decision is not only how to serve requests, but also when to dispatch them. At each decision epoch, the service provider decides which requests to serve and which to postpone, while jointly determining vehicle routes, schedules, and charging decisions. Unexecuted parts of existing plans can be revised as new information becomes available. To solve this problem, we develop an ML--CO policy following the combinatorial optimization augmented machine learning (COAML) framework, which combines a statistical model with a combinatorial optimization layer for decision making. The statistical model predicts prizes for available requests, and a prize-collecting E-ADARP uses these prizes to jointly determine request selection, routing, scheduling, and charging. The statistical model is trained directly to improve the decisions produced by the optimization layer. Computational experiments on 320 test instances demonstrate the efficiency of ML--CO, which solves instances with nearly 500 requests in about 6 seconds on average. It achieves 8.7%--14.3% lower objective values than benchmark policies and an average gap of 4.8% to the anticipative reference. The results provide several managerial insights. First, serving requests immediately is not always best, as selectively postponing some requests can create better ride-sharing opportunities. Second, revising existing plans preserves operational flexibility and substantially improves solution quality. Finally, more frequent decision making does not necessarily improve performance, highlighting the importance of choosing an appropriate decision frequency.

</details>


### [273] [Scalable Incremental Robustness Analysis of Neural Network Feedback Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22576)
*Zichen Wang,Peter Seiler,Geir Dullerud,Bin Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一且可扩展的框架，用于含高维神经网络与未建模动态的反馈互联系统的增量鲁棒稳定性与性能分析，通过结构化分解全阶SDP条件并结合可扩展的Lipschitz常数估计算法，得到维度仅依赖网络最后两层宽度、与深度无关的简化LMI验证条件，并利用多轮交替更新降低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有含深度神经网络的反馈系统SDP证书规模随神经元总数增长，难以扩展；而小增益测试虽可扩展但过于保守。因此需要一个兼具可扩展性与低保守性的分析框架。

Method: 将全阶SDP条件进行结构化分解，结合可扩展的Lipschitz常数估计算法，导出化简的验证条件；所得LMI维度仅依赖网络最后两层的宽度而与深度无关；保留被控对象与神经网络的耦合，并将增量小增益条件作为特例包含；进一步提出多轮交替更新方案迭代精化耦合变量。

Result: 所提框架可为大规模神经网络反馈系统给出当前最优的增量ℓ2增益界，同时保持可扩展性，并涵盖增量小增益条件作为特例。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了对含高维神经网络反馈互联系统的可扩展、低保守的增量鲁棒稳定性与性能分析，验证条件规模与网络深度无关，并通过交替更新进一步提升性能界。

Abstract: Semidefinite programming (SDP) certificates for feedback systems containing deep neural networks (NNs) typically scale with the total number of neurons, whereas small-gain tests are scalable but can be highly conservative. This paper develops a unified and scalable framework for incremental robust stability and performance analysis of feedback interconnections involving high-dimensional NNs and unmodeled dynamics. By combining a structured decomposition of the full-order SDP condition with scalable Lipschitz constant estimation algorithms, we derive reduced verification conditions that certify incremental convergence and incremental $\ell_2$-gain bounds. The dimensions of the resulting control-analysis linear matrix inequalities (LMIs) depend only on the widths of the last two network layers and are \textit{independent of network depth}. The framework preserves the coupling between the plant and the NN, with the incremental small-gain condition recovered as a special case. To further reduce conservatism, we develop a multi-round alternating update scheme that iteratively refines the coupling variables while preserving scalability. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art incremental $\ell_2$-gain bounds for large-scale NN feedback systems.

</details>


### [274] [Joint Observer-Based Output-Feedback Robust Guidance and Control Funnel Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22616)
*Cheng Chang,Shiva Shakeri,Mehran Mesbahi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个联合制导与鲁棒观测器的输出反馈综合框架，用于受有界过程扰动和测量噪声影响的离散时间非线性系统，实现参考轨迹、时变Luenberger观测器与输出反馈增益的协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对离散时间非线性系统在过程扰动和测量噪声下，参考轨迹设计、状态估计与输出反馈控制通常分离处理，难以保证跟踪与估计误差的联合不变界，因此需要一种协同综合方法来同时保证鲁棒跟踪与约束满足。

Method: 方法协同设计参考轨迹、时变Luenberger观测器、基于观测器的线性输出反馈增益以及耦合椭圆漏斗，用以给出跟踪与估计误差的条件不变界。非线性残差通过由局部Lipschitz常数参数化的增量二次约束（QCs）进行界定，Lipschitz常数通过采样方法估计。耦合稳定性条件被表述为线性矩阵不等式（LMI）代理形式，并通过带近端正则化的序列凸规划（prox-SCP）结合半定规划（SDP）求解。

Result: 在持续传感噪声与系统扰动下的单轮车模型仿真表明，方法能够实现鲁棒跟踪，并相对于联合设计的参考轨迹满足漏斗认证的约束。

Conclusion: 所提联合制导与鲁棒观测器输出反馈综合框架可在扰动与噪声下实现跟踪与估计误差的耦合不变界保证，并通过prox-SCP与SDP有效求解，仿真验证了其鲁棒跟踪与约束满足能力。

Abstract: A joint guidance and robust observer-based output-feedback synthesis framework is developed for discrete-time nonlinear systems subject to bounded process disturbances and measurement noise. The method co-designs a reference trajectory, time-varying Luenberger observer and observer-based linear output-feedback gains, and coupled ellipsoidal funnels that certify conditioned-invariant bounds on tracking and estimation errors. Nonlinear residuals are bounded by incremental quadratic constraints (QCs) parameterized by local Lipschitz constants, estimated via sampling approaches. The coupled stability conditions are formulated as linear matrix inequality (LMI) surrogates and solved using sequential convex programming with proximal regularization (prox-SCP) via semidefinite programming (SDP). Simulations of a unicycle model under persistent sensory noise and system disturbances demonstrate robust tracking and funnel-certified constraint satisfaction with respect to the jointly designed reference trajectory.

</details>


### [275] [Riemannian Density-Driven Optimal Control: Tangent-Space LQR for Second-Order Multi-Agent Systems on Curved Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22678)
*Kooktae Lee,Ruchika Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将密度驱动最优控制(D2OC)从欧氏域推广至黎曼流形上的二阶多智能体系统,提出R-D2OC框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有D2OC方法主要针对欧氏域设计,无法直接刻画流形的内在几何结构,难以处理在黎曼流形上演化的二阶多智能体系统的分布控制问题。

Method: 通过指数/对数映射在每个智能体的切空间中构造局部分布目标,以其加权中心作为有限时域LQR的参考;控制量经由内在二阶动力学与并行传输在流形上执行,并采用滚动时域(receding-horizon)方案。理论上建立了依赖于曲率的局部误差界,量化切空间约简引入的近似误差及目标偏差,并推导了在局部速度对齐条件下的离散下降结果。

Result: 推导了曲率相关的近似误差界与目标偏差刻画,给出闭环目标在速度对齐条件下的条件离散下降结论;在3D椭球流形上的数值仿真实现了分布级控制,并经验性地支持了近似与下降理论结果。

Conclusion: R-D2OC成功将密度驱动最优控制扩展到黎曼流形上的二阶系统,为流形空间中的多智能体分布控制提供了具有理论保证的框架。

Abstract: Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) provides an effective framework for steering multi-agent systems toward prescribed spatial distributions. However, existing D2OC formulations are primarily developed for Euclidean domains and do not directly account for intrinsic manifold geometry. This paper extends D2OC to second-order multi-agent systems evolving on Riemannian manifolds. The proposed Riemannian D2OC (R-D2OC) constructs a local distribution objective in the tangent space of each agent through logarithmic maps and uses its weighted center as the reference for a finite-horizon LQR. The resulting control is executed on the manifold through intrinsic second-order dynamics and parallel transport within a receding-horizon scheme. We establish local curvature-dependent bounds that quantify the approximation introduced by the tangent-space reduction and characterize the resulting target bias. Furthermore, we derive a conditional discrete-descent result showing that the closed-loop objective decreases when a local velocity-alignment condition is satisfied. Numerical simulations on a 3D ellipsoidal manifold demonstrate distribution-level control and empirically support the proposed approximation and descent results.

</details>


### [276] [The logarithmic spiral is optimal for shoreline search: a computer-assisted proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24454)
*Alexander Temerev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明了 Baeza-Yates–Culberson–Rawlins 猜想：在既不知距离也不知方向的直线搜索问题中，比率为 C_sp ≈ 13.8111351794611… 的对数螺线是最优路径，借助计算机辅助证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题为船舶以单位速度从平面一点出发，需到达一条未知直线，且该直线的距离与方向均未知。路径的竞争比定义为在所有直线上取上确界：到达直线的时间除以该直线到出发点的距离。Baeza-Yates、Culberson 与 Rawlins 猜想比率为 C_sp 的对数螺线为最优，本文旨在给出这一猜想的证明。

Method: 允许任意路径（离出发点距离与极角均可减小）。证明将已找到方向的集合提升到圆的万有覆叠上，在那里极角的展开只能增大（直线上的 Kneser–Poulsen 性质）；再用一个带单调最终源的记账不等式，将覆盖测度用三状态松弛控制问题的回报来界定，其中向内运动为普通控制、低于保证圆盘的偏移为脉冲。构造一个显式 C^1 存储函数（张量三次 B 样条加闭式项），使其在该问题的螺线水平上满足耗散不等式，且仅在螺线处取等。

Result: 所构造的存储函数满足三状态松弛控制问题的耗散不等式，其水平与螺线一致且仅在螺线处紧；该结论通过对约 10^6 个 Arb 球算术盒子、一个精确 jet 以及螺线处的区间 Hessian 的验证得到确认。由此覆盖测度受控于螺线回报，从而完成对数螺线最优性的证明。

Conclusion: 本文给出 Baeza-Yates–Culberson–Rawlins 猜想的计算机辅助证明，确认比率为 C_sp = 13.8111351794611… 的对数螺线在直线搜索问题中是最优路径。

Abstract: A ship starts at a point of the plane and moves at unit speed; it has to reach an unknown straight line, of which neither the distance nor the direction is known. The competitive ratio of a path is the supremum, over all lines, of the time at which the line is reached divided by its distance. Baeza-Yates, Culberson and Rawlins conjectured that a logarithmic spiral, with ratio $C_{\mathrm{sp}} = 13.8111351794611\ldots$, is optimal. We give a computer-assisted proof. Paths are arbitrary: the distance from the start and the polar angle may both decrease. The proof lifts the set of found directions to the universal cover of the circle, where unfolding the polar angle can only increase it (Kneser-Poulsen on the line); a bookkeeping inequality with a monotone final source then bounds the covered measure by the reward of a three-state relaxed control problem, in which inward motion is an ordinary control and excursions below the guaranteed disk are impulses. An explicit $C^1$ storage function, a tensor cubic B-spline plus a closed-form term, satisfies the dissipation inequalities of that problem at the spiral's level and is tight only at the spiral; this is verified with about $10^6$ boxes of Arb ball arithmetic, an exact jet and an interval Hessian at the spiral.

</details>


### [277] [Exact Truncation and Radial Rigidity in Time-Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22802)
*Changqin Quan,Gengsheng Wang,Lijuan Wang,Qishu Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究线性系统点目标与容差球目标最优控制的精确截断相容性，证明其等价于刚性条件BB^⊤=βI、A+A^⊤=2aI（β>0, a≤0），也等价于点目标最小时间函数的欧氏径向性，并给出显式最优时间与反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点目标与球目标的最小时间控制问题各自是经典问题，但两者最优控制何时精确相容（球目标最优控制恰为点目标最优控制的限制）尚不明确，本文旨在刻画这一相容性的充要条件及几何本质。

Method: 针对线性系统ẑ=Az+Bu、控制受L∞范数约束≤1，考察对所有非零初态x及充分小ε>0，容差球B̄_ε(0)的最优控制是否恰为点目标最优控制的限制，即"精确截断"性质；通过将该性质与刚性条件及点目标最小时间函数的欧氏径向性建立等价关系进行分析。

Result: 证明精确截断性质等价于刚性条件BB^⊤=βI_n、A+A^⊤=2aI_n（β>0, a≤0），也等价于点目标最小时间函数的欧氏径向性；即精确截断成立的充要条件是点目标最小时间函数的次水平集为以原点为中心的欧氏球，与容差目标的几何相匹配。

Conclusion: 精确截断的局部性质可自动延拓至所有0<ε<|x|，且所得最优时间与点目标最优反馈均可显式表达；该结果揭示了精确截断成立时系统几何与目标几何的一致性。

Abstract: We consider minimum-time control for the linear system $$ \dot{z}(t)=Az(t)+Bu(t),\qquad \|u\|_{L^\infty(0,\infty;\mathbb{R}^m)}\leq 1, $$ with $A\in\mathbb R^{n\times n}$ and $B\in\mathbb R^{n\times m}$. While the individual point-target and ball-target problems are classical, we study a different question: when are their optimal controls exactly compatible, in the sense that, for every nonzero initial state $x$ and all sufficiently small $\varepsilon>0$, the optimal control for the tolerance ball $\overline{B}_\varepsilon(0)$ is precisely the restriction of the point-target optimal control? We prove that this {\it{exact truncation}} property is equivalent to the rigidity conditions $$
  B B^\top=βI_n,\qquad A+A^\top=2aI_n,
  \qquad β>0,\; a\leq 0, $$ and also to Euclidean radiality of the point-target minimum-time function. Thus exact truncation holds precisely when the sublevel sets of the point-target minimum-time function are Euclidean balls centered at the origin, matching the geometry of the tolerance targets. Moreover, the local property automatically extends to every $0<\varepsilon<|x|$, and the resulting optimal times and point-target optimal feedback are both explicit.

</details>


### [278] [Data-Driven MPC with Adaptively Sampled Non-Expert Demonstrations: Performance Guarantees and Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22828)
*Shijie Pan,Agustin Castellano,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于记忆的非参数策略，通过学习代理有限时域MPC生成的轨迹构造Lipschitz正则化上包络，在无需运行时显式优化的情况下近似无限时域最优控制策略，并给出相对最优性保证与样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限时域最优控制策略通常需要昂贵甚至不可行的在线优化。作者希望利用有限时域MPC产生的轨迹作为"非专家示范"，以数据驱动方式构造可部署策略，同时获得可计算的性能证书并避免运行时显式优化。

Method: 将代理有限时域MPC生成的轨迹视为非专家示范；提出一种基于记忆的非参数策略，构造有限时域代理值函数的Lipschitz正则化上包络，作为所得策略值的显式上界，从而提供可计算的性能证书；理论上建立相对于无限时域最优值函数的相对最优性保证，并推导满足给定相对误差所需的MPC时域充分条件与样本复杂度界。

Result: 在火箭着陆任务上的数值实验验证了理论预测，策略表现出接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，可从有限时域MPC轨迹中直接构造具有性能证书的非参数策略，在保证相对最优性的同时规避在线优化，为数据驱动最优控制提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We study data-driven policy construction for dynamical systems using trajectories generated by surrogate finite-horizon Model Predictive Control (MPC), with the goal of approximating an infinite-horizon optimal control policy. We interpret these trajectories as non-expert demonstrations and propose a memory-based, nonparametric policy that constructs a Lipschitz-regularized upper envelope of the finite- horizon surrogate value function, which serves as an explicit upper bound on the value of the resulting policy, thereby providing a computable performance certificate while bypassing explicit optimization at runtime. We establish relative optimality guarantees with respect to the infinite-horizon optimal value function and derive sufficient MPC horizon conditions, together with sample complexity bounds, for achieving a prescribed relative error. Numerical experiments on a rocket landing task validate the theoretical predictions and demonstrate near-optimal performance.

</details>


### [279] [Presolve heuristics in HiGHS: implementation and computational study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22938)
*Simon Spoorendonk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在开源求解器 HiGHS 中集成了四种已发表的混合整数规划原始启发式，统一接口与并行预算调度，并在 mipfeas 基准上验证其可将原始积分平均改善 23.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将多种已发表的 MIP 原始启发式统一集成到开源求解器 HiGHS 中，通过共享接口、独立预算与并行种子工作线程来提升求解器尽早获得可行解的能力，并对其实际效果进行独立评估。

Method: 集成四种启发式：Feasibility Jump、fix-propagate-repair（FPR）及其 LP 引导的 dive-time 变体、LocalMIP、以及一阶 LP 可行性泵 Scylla。它们共享一个集成接口，具备各自的预算与耐心参数，并在独立播种的工作线程上并行运行。评估在 mipfeas 基准（233 个实例、600 秒时限）上进行，与单独构建的未修补 HiGHS 在同一机器上对比。

Result: 原始积分在全集上改善 23.7%，在未用于调参的实例上改善 12.9%；修补版求解器更快达到较好解，在时限处略微领先。修补版在 4 个实例上找到解而未修补版没有，反向情况仅 1 个；其最终 gap 更小，但仅在全集上显著。Scylla 与 LP 引导 dive 在任何携带它们的完整时限运行中均未产生被接受的 incumbent，故默认禁用发布，但实现保留。

Conclusion: 将多种原始启发式以统一接口和并行预算机制集成进 HiGHS 可显著提升早期可行解质量（原始积分），部分启发式在实测中无效但实现仍保留；整体以开源形式发布。

Abstract: We present reference implementations of four published primal heuristics for mixed-integer programming inside the open-source solver HiGHS (Huangfu and Hall, 2018): Feasibility Jump (Luteberget and Sartor, 2023), fix-propagate-repair (FPR; Salvagnin, Roberti and Fischetti, 2025) with its LP-guided dive-time variant, LocalMIP (Lin, Zou and Cai, 2024), and the first-order-LP feasibility pump Scylla (Mexi et al., 2025). They share one integration interface with per-heuristic budgets and patiences, and each runs in parallel on independently seeded workers. The evaluation is on the mipfeas benchmark (Bussieck and Dirkse, 2026), 233 instances at 600 s, against a separately built unpatched HiGHS on the same machine. The primal integral improves by 23.7% over the full set and by 12.9% on the instances not used for tuning: the patched solver reaches a good solution sooner. At the time limit it is slightly ahead. It finds a solution on 4 instances where the unpatched solver finds none, against 1 the other way, and its final gap is smaller, significantly so only over the full set. Scylla and the LP-guided dive produced no accepted incumbent in any full-limit run that carried them and ship disabled; their implementations remain. The implementation is released as open source.

</details>


### [280] [Searching for Primes: A Neural AlphaZero Approach to a Factoring Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22968)
*Marcel Crasmaru*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究一个在 N×N 棋盘上的单人令牌游戏，证明当目标矩形未知时求解等价于整数分解，而当目标矩形已知时求解可化为多项式时间步骤，并将由此产生的约束搜索空间用于 AlphaZero 风格的神经树搜索实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望构造一个组合游戏，将其难度精确定位到数论分解这一核心环节，从而把整数分解的困难性嵌入一个可用于神经启发式搜索研究的等价环境。

Method: 定义 N×N 棋盘上的令牌滑动/复制游戏，通过守恒整数权重 W'（2N 位）与严格单调量保证解长度上界为 O(N²)，从而落入 NP；论证到达终局等价于将 W' 分解为两个 N 位因子 V,M；当目标矩形 R×S 已知时，用强制向下芯片流与基于 Cohn 定理的 0/1 多项式分解两步求解；在给定因子 popcount 的承诺下，用学习到的策略/价值网络与 AlphaZero 风格蒙特卡洛树搜索进行实验。
