Result: 求解平衡半素数目标的游戏等价于整数分解；目标矩形已知时求解归约为两个多项式时间步骤，其中第二步依赖 Cohn 定理的 0/1 多项式因子分解；提供因子 popcount 承诺可保持渐近难度但压缩目标搜索空间，该受限空间被策略/价值网络与 MCTS 用于实证探测神经前瞻的极限。

Conclusion: 该游戏的完整难度被隔离在初始的数论分解步骤上，其余部分为多项式时间可解；这一等价环境为研究神经网络前瞻在分解类困难问题上的表现提供了可控测试平台。

Abstract: We study a one-player token game on an $N\times N$ board where tokens slide along diagonals or duplicate onto neighbouring ones to form a combinatorial rectangle $R\times S$. A conserved integer weight $W'$ and a strict monovariant guarantee $O(N^2)$-length solutions, placing the game in $\mathsf{NP}$. We prove that reaching a final position factors this $2N$-bit $W'$ into two $N$-bit factors $V,M < 2^N$ that encode the rectangle's rows and columns. Consequently, solving the game for a balanced-semiprime target is equivalent to integer factoring. However, if the target rectangle is known, the solution reduces to two polynomial-time steps: a forced downward chip-flow and a $0/1$-polynomial factorisation leveraging Cohn's theorem. The game's entire difficulty is thus isolated to the initial number-theoretic split. Supplying the popcounts of the factors as a promise preserves this asymptotic hardness but bounds the target search space. We exploit this constrained space using a learned policy/value network and an AlphaZero-style Monte-Carlo tree search, empirically probing the limits of neural look-ahead on a factoring-equivalent environment.

</details>


### [281] [C^{2,α} solution to the proportional transaction cost problem with two risky assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23028)
*Xinfu Chen,Shuaijie Qian*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将比例交易成本下投资组合选择产生的奇异控制变分不等式（一个梯度约束PDE）通过价值函数的凹性转化为障碍问题，证明了其解具有 C^{2,α} 正则性，突破了文献中通常仅有 W^{2,∞} 正则性的限制，并指出方法可推广至更一般的多维凹价值函数奇异控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类梯度约束PDE源自比例交易成本下的投资组合选择等奇异控制问题，文献一般只能保证解的 W^{2,∞} 正则性，且已有反例表明更高正则性一般不成立。因此需要寻找新的结构性工具来提升正则性结论。

Method: 利用价值函数的凹性这一结构性质，将梯度约束问题与障碍问题（obstacle problem）建立联系，借助障碍问题的正则性理论来分析原变分不等式。

Result: 证明了解具有 C^{2,α} 正则性，优于文献中通常给出的 W^{2,∞} 结果，且不受既有高正则性反例的限制（因利用了凹性这一附加结构）。

Conclusion: 价值函数凹性是提升奇异控制变分不等式正则性的关键；方法在二维投资组合选择设定下建立，但可望推广到价值函数为凹的更一般多维奇异控制问题。

Abstract: This paper concerns the variational inequality arising from a specific singular control problem: portfolio selection under proportional transaction costs. This variational inequality is a gradient-constrained partial differential equation (PDE). Generally, the literature only guarantees the W{2,\infty} regularity of the solution to such a PDE, and counterexamples exist for higher regularity. In this paper, by exploiting the concavity of the value function, we connect this gradient-constrained problem to an obstacle problem, and finally show that the solution is C^{2,α}. This paper concerns a two-dimensional portfolio selection setup, but our approach can potentially be extended to more general multi-dimensional singular control problems when the value function is concave.

</details>


### [282] [Exact Controllability of Backward-Structured Mean-Field SDEs: Hautus Criteria and Initial-Time Dichotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23072)
*Jingrui Sun,Lvning Yuan,Xurun Zuo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有后向结构的线性平均场随机微分方程的精确可控性，建立了Hautus判据并揭示了初始时刻为零与正时刻之间的二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清线性平均场随机微分方程（后向结构）的精确可控性刻画，特别是不同初始时刻下可控性条件的差异，以及平均场SDE与ODE、SDE系统之间的可控性关系。

Method: 建立Hautus判据：在零初始时刻，用均值动态的Hautus条件刻画，并结合由中心化随机动态产生的额外控制方向；在任意正初始时刻，额外要求中心化系统的随机Hautus条件，该条件以Lyapunov型算子的半正定特征矩阵形式表述。

Result: 揭示了零初始时刻与正初始时刻之间的尖锐二分法：零时刻仅需均值动态的Hautus条件加中心化动态的额外控制方向；正时刻还需中心化系统的随机Hautus条件（基于半正定特征矩阵）。二分法源于初始sigma域的平凡性。

Conclusion: 刻画了后向结构线性平均场SDE的精确可控性，并阐明了相应ODE、SDE与平均场SDE系统之间的可控性关系。

Abstract: This paper studies exact controllability of linear mean-field stochastic differential equations with backward-structure. We establish Hautus criteria and uncover a sharp dichotomy between the zero initial time and positive initial times. At time zero, exact controllability is characterized by a Hautus condition for the mean dynamics, with additional control directions generated by the centered stochastic dynamics. At any positive initial time, exact controllability further requires a stochastic Hautus condition for the centered system. Unlike the classical eigenvector-based condition, this stochastic criterion is formulated in terms of positive-semidefinite eigenmatrices of an associated Lyapunov-type operator. The dichotomy arises from the triviality of the initial sigma-field. We also clarify the controllability relations among the corresponding ODE, SDE, and mean-field SDE systems.

</details>


### [283] [A scenario-cluster-based and enhanced progressive hedging algorithm for the two-stage stochastic quadratic knapsack problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23176)
*Ibrahim Dan Dije,Franklin Djeumou Fomeni,Leandro C. Coelho,Janosch Ortmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对利润与重量不确定的两阶段随机二次背包问题，提出融合渐进对冲算法（EPHA）与聚类情景缩减的求解框架，实验表明其间隙显著小于CPLEX。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSSQKP同时具有随机、二元与二次结构，计算难度大，传统求解器（如CPLEX）在大规模实例上最优性间隙很高，需要更高效的求解框架。

Method: 提出融合渐进对冲算法改进版（EPHA）与聚类情景缩减的框架。EPHA包含取整过程及基于振荡与停滞检测的动态惩罚更新策略；聚类框架基于情景间机会成本距离，导出medoid下界（MLB）、聚类下界（CLB）与聚类上界（CUB）；并用EPHA启发式求解聚类子问题，得到估计聚类上界（ECUB）及估计最优性间隙。

Result: 在800个生成的TSSQKP实例上，所提框架在相当计算条件下间隙小于CPLEX。基于EPHA的聚类框架中，CLB、EPHA与MLB对应平均估计间隙分别为1.65%、1.88%和2.41%，而CPLEX平均最优性间隙为35.86%。MLB计算最快，平均执行时间420.88秒；EPHA在解质量与计算代价间取得良好折中，使聚类下界相关平均计算时间减少约52.7%，而平均估计间隙仅增加0.23个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提EPHA与聚类边界框架在TSSQKP上显著优于CPLEX，EPHA在计算效率与解质量之间提供了有利折中，聚类边界与情景缩减有效提升了求解性能。

Abstract: This paper introduces the two-stage stochastic quadratic knapsack problem (TSSQKP) with uncertainty in profits and weights. To overcome the computational difficulties arising from the combined stochastic, binary, and quadratic structure, we propose a solution framework that integrates an adaptation of the progressive hedging algorithm with clustering-based scenario reduction. The enhanced progressive hedging algorithm (EPHA) incorporates a rounding procedure and a dynamic penalty update strategy driven by the detection of oscillation and stagnation patterns. In parallel, we propose a clustering framework based on opportunity-cost distances between scenarios, from which we derive the medoid lower bound (MLB), the cluster lower bound (CLB) and the cluster upper bound (CUB). To further improve computational efficiency, EPHA is also used to solve the cluster subproblems heuristically; in this case, the resulting upper bound is referred to as the estimated cluster upper bound (ECUB), and the corresponding gaps are interpreted as estimated optimality gaps. Computational experiments on 800 generated TSSQKP instances show that the proposed framework yields smaller gaps than CPLEX under comparable computational conditions. In the EPHA-based clustering framework, the average estimated gaps associated with the CLB, EPHA and MLB 1.65%, 1.88% and 2.41% respectively, compared with an average optimality gap of 35.86% for CPLEX. MLB is fastest to compute, with an average execution time of 420.88s, while EPHA provides a favourable compromise between solution quality and computational effort, reducing the average computational time associated with the cluster bounds by approximately 52.7%, while increasing the average estimated gap by only 0.23 percentage points.

</details>


### [284] [A General Multicriteria Optimization Perspective on Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23188)
*Stephan Helfrich,Gabriela Ciolacu,Jan Boeckmann,Emilia Grass*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文将弹性（resilience）建模为多准则时变网络流问题，提出基于包络的启发式方法近似非支配解集，并表明弹性决策的权衡高度依赖具体情境，应以多准则视角而非单一标量指标来处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性在本质上是多准则概念，涵盖准备、抵抗、吸收与恢复等可能相互冲突的目标。然而现有模型多聚焦单一准则，未显式分析准则间的潜在冲突、权衡及其对决策的影响；同时系统还需在日常运行中保持效能并按需调整准备水平，这一需求未获充分刻画。

Method: 作者将反弹（rebound）、抵抗（resistance）、损失（loss）与最大性能退化（maximum performance degradation）定义为独立的弹性准则，构建整合准备（preparedness）、效能（effectiveness）与响应（response）的一般两阶段多准则时变网络流模型；证明该类多准则优化模型一般不可解，并设计基于包络（enclosure-based）的启发式算法近似非支配解集。

Result: 计算结果表明，不同的准备手段与激活时机对应不同的弹性准则，且相应的权衡强烈依赖于具体实例，缺乏普适性。

Conclusion: 弹性应从显式的多准则视角加以处理，而不是通过一个与偏好无关的普适标量指数来刻画。

Abstract: Resilience is a system's capability to prepare for, resist, absorb, and recover from adverse events. By definition, resilience therefore encompasses multiple criteria that can conflict and may prescribe different decisions. Importantly, a system must also maintain its effectiveness during routine operations and appropriately scale its preparedness for adverse events. Although resilience is inherently multicriteria, existing models often focus on a single criterion and do not explicitly analyze potential conflicts, the associated trade-offs, and their implications for decision-making.
  We formulate rebound, resistance, loss, and maximum performance degradation as separate resilience criteria in a general two-stage multicriteria model of network flows over time that integrates preparedness, effectiveness, and response. We show that, in general, such multicriteria optimization models are intractable and develop an enclosure-based heuristic for approximating the nondominated set. Our computational results show that different preparedness instruments and activation timings are associated with different resilience criteria and that the corresponding trade-offs are strongly instance-specific. Thus, resilience should be approached from an explicit multicriteria perspective rather than through a universal, preference-independent scalar index.

</details>


### [285] [On Control of Drawdown: Robust Invariance and Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23272)
*Chung-Han Hsieh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多资产随机系统在有限时域内对最大回撤比例的鲁棒控制问题，提出了精确的鲁棒不变性定理，将鲁棒安全控制刻画为"回撤缓冲×归一化方向"的回撤调制形式，并在阶段独立收益下将优化问题化简为一维Bellman递归，给出最优鲁棒安全状态反馈策略，同时刻画了满足回撤限制的LTI增益并证明回撤调制不降低期望收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回撤（财富自峰值下降的比例）是路径依赖风险控制中的经典难题。现有方法缺乏在多资产随机系统中对预定最大回撤比例进行严格鲁棒性保证的有限时域控制框架，且如何在鲁棒安全约束下进行最优策略设计、以及与线性时不变策略的比较尚不清晰。

Method: 建立有限时域控制框架以施加预定最大百分比回撤限制。首先证明精确的鲁棒不变性定理，刻画所有能在任意支持收益下保持回撤限制的控制动作；其次在阶段独立收益假设下，将鲁棒安全因果策略上的优化化简为一维Bellman递归；最后刻画满足回撤限制的LTI增益，并分析回撤调制的期望收益性质。

Result: 证明每个鲁棒安全控制都可表示为回撤缓冲与可行归一化方向的乘积（回撤调制形式），从而给出鲁棒回撤安全策略的完整参数化；在阶段独立收益下得到一维Bellman递归及最优鲁棒安全状态反馈策略；刻画满足回撤限制的LTI增益，并证明最优回撤调制在相同限制下期望收益不低于LTI策略，当LTI策略单阶段期望净收益为正且时域不少于两阶段时严格更优。

Conclusion: 该框架为路径依赖的回撤风险控制提供了完整的鲁棒策略参数化与最优性理论，表明回撤调制在保证最大回撤限制的同时不牺牲、并可严格提升期望收益，为多资产随机系统中的回撤约束控制提供了理论与方法基础。

Abstract: Mitigating \emph{drawdown}, the decline in wealth from its running peak, presents a canonical problem in path-dependent risk control. In this paper, we develop a finite-horizon control framework that enforces a prescribed maximum percentage drawdown limit in multi-asset stochastic systems. Our first result is an exact robust-invariance theorem characterizing every control action that preserves a prescribed drawdown limit against all supported returns. We show that every robustly safe control admits a \emph{drawdown-modulated} form: the product of the current drawdown \emph{cushion} and a feasible \emph{normalized direction}. This yields a complete parameterization of robustly drawdown-safe policies. Additionally, under stagewise-independent returns, we show that optimizing over all robustly safe causal policies reduces to a one-dimensional Bellman recursion and yields an optimal robustly safe state-feedback policy. Finally, we characterize the linear time-invariant (LTI) gains satisfying a prescribed drawdown limit and prove that optimal drawdown modulation achieves no lower expected return under the same limit. Strict expected-return improvement holds for horizons of at least two stages whenever the LTI policy has positive expected one-stage net return.

</details>


### [286] [On a Non-Prox-Regularity Example by Rockafellar and Wets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23279)
*Isaac Jasper,Xianfu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文指出Rockafellar与Wets 1998年专著《Variational Analysis》中关于非prox-正则函数的反例并不成立，并通过建立集值映射连续性的一般定理予以证明，同时给出修正后的构造使其成为有效反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rockafellar与Wets在《Variational Analysis》中给出了一个非prox-正则函数的反例，但该反例可能存在缺陷，需要澄清其有效性并提供一个正确的版本，以维护该领域基础文献的严谨性。

Method: 首先建立关于集值映射连续性的一般定理，然后利用该定理分析Rockafellar-Wets反例，证明其不具备所声称的性质；最后对原构造进行轻微调整，并证明修正版本确实满足所需的非prox-正则反例性质。

Result: 证明了经典的Rockafellar-Wets反例并不具有其预期的性质，即该反例是无效的；提出的修正构造则成功充当了所期望的非prox-正则函数反例。

Conclusion: 经典文献中的该反例存在错误，集值映射连续性可用于揭示其缺陷；经轻微修正后可得到正确的非prox-正则函数反例，从而保证相关理论的表述准确。

Abstract: We analyze a counterexample concerning non-prox-regular functions provided by Rockafellar and Wets in their 1998 foundational monograph, \textit{Variational Analysis}. By establishing a general theorem on the continuity of set-valued mappings, we show that the counterexample does not have its intended properties. We propose a slight adjustment to the construction and prove that the corrected version serves as the desired counterexample.

</details>


### [287] [A Combinatorial Benders Decomposition Framework for Two-Dimensional Irregular Bin Packing Problems with Convex Polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23461)
*Jianming Wang,Zhouwang Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种精确组合Benders分解框架，用于凸多边形的二维不规则装箱问题，结合基于模式的主问题与单箱几何可行性判定子问题，并通过定制分支定价求解，在540个基准实例中于12个类别318个实例上取得最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维不规则装箱问题将组合装箱分配决策与困难的几何可行性约束耦合在一起，精确优化极具挑战性，现有方法在大规模实例上难以求解到最优。

Method: 采用精确组合Benders分解框架：主问题为基于模式的模型，子问题为精确的单箱几何可行性判定预言机；利用子问题反馈的几何信息动态生成Benders可行性割，逐步收紧主问题与定价问题；主问题由定制精确分支定价算法求解，包含目标分层搜索与自适应精确定价。

Result: 在来自18个类别的540个基准实例上，所提方法在3600秒时限内对12个类别的318个实例求得最优解，求解实例数优于直接混合整数规划模型和基线组合Benders分解方法。

Conclusion: 几何可行性判定与组合Benders分解的深度耦合能有效处理不规则装箱中的几何约束，动态割与定制分支定价显著提升了精确求解能力。

Abstract: Two-dimensional irregular bin packing combines combinatorial bin-assignment decisions with difficult geometric feasibility constraints, making exact optimization challenging. This paper develops an exact combinatorial Benders decomposition framework for the two-dimensional irregular bin packing problem with convex polygons, coupling a pattern-based master problem with an exact single-bin geometric feasibility oracle. The dynamically strengthened Benders master is solved by a tailored exact branch-and-price procedure that incorporates objective-layered search and adaptive exact pricing. Geometric information obtained from the oracle is further fed back to the master and pricing processes through dynamically generated Benders feasibility cuts, which progressively restrict the subsequent pricing problems. Computational experiments are conducted on 540 benchmark instances from 18 classes. The proposed method obtains the optimal solution for 318 instances across 12 classes within a 3600-second time limit. On these classes, the proposed method solves more instances than a direct mixed-integer programming formulation and a baseline combinatorial Benders decomposition approach.

</details>


### [288] [Semidefinite Implementations of the Proximal Point Algorithm and Consequences for Progressive Decoupling in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23519)
*Xudong Li,R. Tyrrell Rockafellar,Defeng Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种半定邻近点算法扩展，通过对投影单调映射施加正定邻近点算法并结合补全步骤，在更广范围内保证（通常线性）收敛，且允许变量度量下的非精确子问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典邻近点算法要求邻近项正定，但实际中半正定邻近项有助于预处理。本文旨在将算法推广到半正定情形，在更广范围内保证收敛（通常线性收敛），并允许非精确最小化以支持拟牛顿类方法。

Method: 对由给定极大单调映射导出的投影单调映射应用一种改进的正定邻近点算法，并结合补全步骤；重点分析并给出保证投影映射极大性的条件与便捷判据；进一步在变量度量实现中允许子问题非精确最小化。

Result: 建立了半定邻近点算法的收敛性，通常具有线性收敛，适用范围超出以往预期；明确了投影映射极大性的保证情形并给出便捷判据；将该扩展算法应用于凸inf-投影、增广拉格朗日步骤以及基于Spingarn部分逆的带括号渐进解耦。

Conclusion: 半定扩展邻近点算法在更广范围内保证收敛并支持非精确变量度量实现，为增广拉格朗日方法与分裂/分解方案提供了更具弹性的理论框架。

Abstract: The proximal point algorithm for finding a zero of a maximal monotone mapping is the engine that drives augmented Lagrangian methods and various splitting schemes in convex optimization, where proximal terms that are merely positive semidefinite can be helpful in preconditioning. Here a semidefinite extension of that algorithm is developed with new features which guarantee convergence, typically even linear convergence, in broader territory than previously foreseen. Moreover, inexact minimization is allowed in the subproblems in a variable-metric implementation which might open up quasi-Newton-like approaches.
  The key to the extension is applying an advanced form of the positive definite proximal point algorithm to a projected monotone mapping derived from the given mapping, and combining that with a completion step. For this to work, the maximality of the projected mapping is essential, but maximality is not automatic. The circumstances that guarantee maximality are definitively analyzed and convenient criteria for maximality are thereby identified. Consequences of the extended algorithm are worked out for application to convex inf-projection, augmented Lagrangian steps, and bracketed progressive decoupling based on Spingarn's partial inverse as a scheme for problem decomposition.

</details>


### [289] [Optimal High-Order Methods for Solving Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23557)
*Xinliang Zhang,Lesi Chen,Linxuan Pan,Chengchang Liu,Junchi Yang,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出新的二阶方法，达到单调变分不等式的最优收敛率O(T^{-2.5})，并将其推广到任意p阶方法，收敛率O(T^{-(3p-1)/2})，匹配已知下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解光滑单调变分不等式（MVI）。已有二阶方法NPE收敛率为O(T^{-1.5})，针对凸凹极小极大优化这一MVI子类，Chen等（COLT 2025）改进至Õ(T^{-1.75})，但与Chen等（2026）给出的下界Ω(T^{-2.5})仍有明显差距。本文旨在弥合该差距，实现最优收敛率。

Method: 提出一种新的二阶方法，达到最优收敛率O(T^{-2.5})；并将其推广至任意整数p≥1的p阶方法，收敛率为O(T^{-(3p-1)/2})。

Result: 二阶方法达到O(T^{-2.5})的最优收敛率；p阶方法达到O(T^{-(3p-1)/2})，匹配已知下界。

Conclusion: 所提方法在二阶情形达到最优速率，并在所有阶数上建立最优收敛率，弥合了先前结果与下界之间的显著差距。

Abstract: We study second- and higher-order methods for solving smooth monotone variational inequalities (MVI). Monteiro and Svaiter (SIAM J. Optim., 2012) showed that a second-order method, NPE, converges at a rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1.5})$. For convex-concave minimax optimization, a subclass of MVI problems, Chen, Liu, Luo, and Zhang (COLT 2025) recently improved this rate to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}( T^{-1.75})$. However, the result has a substantial gap compared to the lower bound of $Ω(T^{-2.5})$ established by Chen et al. (2026). In this paper, we propose a novel second-order method that achieves the optimal rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-2.5})$. Our algorithm also extends to higher-order methods: for any integer $ p \ge 1$, we obtain a $p$th-order method with a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-(3p-1)/2})$, matching the known lower bounds and therefore establishing optimal rates across all orders.

</details>


### [290] [Contextual Distributionally Robust Chance-Constrained Programs: Exact Reformulations and Valid Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23739)
*Hanbin Yang,Nan Jiang,Guodong Lyu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Wasserstein距离的上下文分布鲁棒机会约束规划(C-DRCCP)，针对仿射安全系统给出精确MIP重构，揭示了条件风险由运输预算的双重耦合使用决定，并据此导出强有效不等式与秩不等式，显著缩小线性松弛间隙并提升求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机会约束与分布鲁棒机会约束多针对无条件(边缘)违约概率，难以控制在特定上下文(共变量)邻域内的条件违约概率。将上下文信息纳入分布鲁棒框架会带来建模与求解上的困难，即精确重构与可扩展求解方法尚不完善。

Method: 在上下文与结果的联合分布上以Wasserstein距离构造模糊集，提出C-DRCCP模型以控制给定目标上下文邻域内的条件违约概率。针对带右端不确定性的仿射安全系统，推导精确的混合整数规划(MIP)重构；将最坏情况条件风险刻画为运输预算的两类耦合使用，并等价表述为到达条件风险边界所需最小运输成本不低于可用预算。通过校准模糊集获得真实条件违约概率的有限样本保证，进而构造利用上下文运输的强有效不等式：在常规分位数割(仅用诱导权重)基础上计入剩余预算，得到严格占优的强化分位数割；并基于上下文运输成本的交换性质导出秩不等式。

Result: 强化分位数割严格优于标准分位数界并给出更紧的MIP系数；秩不等式将不同上下文分配下的可行失效模式联系起来。大量数值实验表明，这些不等式大幅缩小线性松弛间隙、加速求解过程，并使基线重构无法处理的大样本实例变得可解。

Conclusion: 通过对条件风险的双重运输预算刻画，可系统性地导出强有效不等式与秩不等式，为C-DRCCP提供精确且可扩展的求解途径，在理论与计算上均优于仅使用诱导权重的常规方法。

Abstract: We study a contextual distributionally robust chance-constrained programming (C-DRCCP) model that controls violation probabilities conditional on a neighborhood of a target context. The ambiguity set is defined over the joint distribution of contexts and outcomes using the Wasserstein distance. For affine safety systems with right-hand-side uncertainty, we derive an exact mixed-integer programming (MIP) reformulation. The reformulation reveals that worst-case conditional risk is determined by two coupled uses of the transportation budget: moving empirical mass into the conditioning neighborhood and moving conditioned mass into the failure region. Equivalently, robust feasibility holds when the minimum transportation cost needed to reach the conditional risk boundary is at least the available budget. Calibrating the ambiguity set gives a finite-sample guarantee for the true conditional violation probability. This characterization leads to strong valid inequalities that exploit the contextual transport. Each contextual mass allocation determines both the conditional scenario weights and the budget available to move outcomes into failure, whereas conventional quantile cuts utilize only the induced weights. Accounting for the remaining budget generates strengthened quantile cuts that strictly dominate standard quantile bounds and yield tighter MIP coefficients. We also derive rank inequalities from an exchange property of contextual transportation costs, linking feasible failure patterns across contextual allocations. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that these inequalities close much of the linear-relaxation gap, speed up the solution process, and enable large-sample instances that are intractable for the baseline formulation.

</details>


### [291] [Duality in Optimization: A Visual Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23751)
*Mohini Bariya*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该教程以高度可视化的方式讲解拉格朗日对偶及其含义，面向广泛的学生与实践者群体，涵盖拉格朗日量的动机、两人博弈解释及与Fenchel对偶的联系，全程强调原始与对偶两侧解的几何意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日对偶在优化中具有基础地位（从求解器技术发展到解的解释），但其几何直觉仅靠数学推导难以把握，因此需要一个高度可视化、面向广泛读者的教程。

Method: 采用高度可视化的教学/综述方式，推导拉格朗日量的动机，引入两人博弈（two-player game）解释，并建立其与Fenchel对偶的联系；始终从原始和对偶两个角度强调解的几何意义。

Result: 提供了关于对偶及其含义的可视化解释，串联了拉格朗日量、两人博弈视角与Fenchel对偶三部分内容（摘要未给出具体数值或实验性结果）。

Conclusion: 该工作是对拉格朗日对偶的直观教学性阐述，旨在帮助读者通过几何视角理解对偶原理及原始-对偶解的含义。

Abstract: Lagrangian duality is fundamental to optimization, from the development of solver techniques to the interpretation of solutions; yet its geometric intuition can be difficult to grasp through mathematical derivation alone. This tutorial explains duality and its implications in a highly visual way for a broad audience of students and practitioners. It develops the motivation for the Lagrangian, the two-player game interpretation, and the connection to Fenchel duality. Throughout, it emphasizes the geometric meaning of the solution from both the primal and dual

</details>


### [292] [The Exponential Price of Determinism in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23837)
*Guy Kornowski*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了确定性算法求解非光滑非凸Lipschitz函数的(δ,ε)-Goldstein稳定点的预言机复杂度下界为(1/ε)^{Ω(d)}，从而否定了确定性算法能以d的多项式复杂度求解该问题的可能，闭合了此前的指数级差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果表明随机一阶算法可达到与维度无关的预言机复杂度，而确定性算法的复杂度至少线性依赖于维度d。但确定性算法能否以d的多项式复杂度求解该问题此前仍未解决，这一开放问题由Jordan et al. [2023]提出。

Method: 通过证明确定性算法复杂度下界，构造性地给出(1/ε)^{Ω(d)}量级的下界证明。

Result: 证明确定性算法在求解(δ,ε)-Goldstein稳定点时，预言机复杂度下界为(1/ε)^{Ω(d)}，闭合了此前已知上下界之间的指数级差距。

Conclusion: 确定性算法无法以维度多项式复杂度求解该问题。该结果还推广到更弱的稳定点概念、寻找下降方向及确定性平滑等情形，整体表明随机化在非光滑非凸优化中带来了指数级的计算优势。

Abstract: We study the complexity of finding $(δ,ε)$-Goldstein stationary points of nonsmooth nonconvex Lipschitz functions. By now, it is known that randomized first-order algorithms can solve this task with a dimension-free oracle complexity [Zhang et al., 2020], whereas deterministic algorithms cannot, as their complexity must scale at least linearly with the dimension $d$ [Jordan et al., 2023, Tian and So, 2024]. This leaves open whether deterministic algorithms can nevertheless solve the problem with oracle complexity polynomial in $d$. We answer this question negatively by proving a lower bound of order $(1/ε)^{Ω(d)}$ for deterministic algorithm, closing the exponential gap between the previously known lower and upper bounds and resolving an open problem posed by Jordan et al. [2023]. We further discuss several extensions and implications of this result to weaker stationarity notions, finding a descent direction and deterministic smoothing. Overall, our results establish an exponential computational advantage in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization offered by randomization.

</details>


### [293] [Accelerated Plug-and-Play Davis-Yin Splitting for Nonconvex Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23840)
*Kuntal Roy,Pankaj Gautam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究成像应用中的结构化非凸非光滑优化问题（三函数和形式），提出基于Davis-Yin分裂的统一收敛分析，并给出加速、拟牛顿线搜索与PnP扩展的算法变体，在图像恢复与低秩矩阵补全上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像与低秩矩阵补全等应用中的优化问题通常同时具有非凸性和非光滑性，且目标函数可分解为三个函数之和。现有方法在统一收敛理论与高效算法设计方面仍有不足，需要建立更通用的分析框架并提升收敛速度与重建质量。

Method: 以Davis-Yin分裂方法为基础，研究其FISTA型加速变体、拟牛顿线搜索方法以及plug-and-play（PnP）扩展；在温和假设（含Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质）下，基于Davis-Yin包络构建统一收敛分析。

Result: 在该框架下证明了迭代点列的次序列收敛与全局收敛到稳定点，并给出残差收敛速率。实验在图像恢复（含图像去模糊）与低秩矩阵补全任务上展开：矩阵补全使用合成数据与公开数据集评估恢复精度与效率；图像任务采用PSNR比较各算法变体的收敛行为与重建质量。结果表明FISTA加速与线搜索方法改善收敛性，PnP去噪器提升图像质量，所提方法在矩阵补全任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一收敛分析框架可覆盖多种Davis-Yin分裂算法变体，理论与实验共同表明加速策略、线搜索与PnP扩展能有效改善收敛性能与重建质量，方法在成像与低秩矩阵补全问题上具有实用性与竞争力。

Abstract: In this work, we study a class of structured non-convex and non-smooth optimization problems arising in imaging applications, where the objective is the sum of three functions. We consider the Davis-Yin splitting method, a FISTA-type accelerated variant, along with a quasi-Newton line-search method and a plug-and-play (PnP) extension to solve the problem. We develop a unified convergence analysis based on the Davis-Yin envelope under mild assumptions, including the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property. Within this framework, we establish subsequential and global convergence of the iterates to stationary points, together with residual convergence rates. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed methods on image restoration and low-rank matrix completion problems. For low-rank matrix completion, we perform experiments on both synthetic data and public datasets to evaluate recovery accuracy and efficiency. In imaging tasks, including image deblurring, we compare the convergence behaviour and reconstruction quality of several algorithmic variants using peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR). The results show that FISTA-acceleration and line-search methods improve convergence, while PnP denoisers enhance image quality, and the proposed methods remain competitive on matrix completion tasks.

</details>


### [294] [Anytime-Feasible Gradient Descent for Constrained Optimization Under Gradient Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23848)
*Sina Sharifi,Jiarui Wang,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在目标与约束梯度存在范数有界误差时仍保持"任意时刻可行"的一阶非线性约束优化方法，通过求解二阶锥规划获得鲁棒搜索方向并配合保护性回溯线搜索，理论上保证严格可行性、充分目标下降、步长正下界、O(1/K)收敛率及搜索方向趋于零，并在多智能体避障导航中验证了抗噪的无碰撞轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多工程系统需在实时、有限算力预算下满足严格安全与运行约束，优化算法常未完全收敛即被终止，因此"任意时刻可行性（anytime feasibility）"对安全部署至关重要。现有保证每次迭代都可行的方 法通常依赖精确梯度信息，而实际中因测量噪声、随机近似或模型失配，这一假设常被违背。作者因此希望在梯度有范数有界误差的情形下仍能保持可行性与下降保证。

Method: 针对目标与约束梯度存在范数有界误差的非线性约束优化问题，提出一种任意时刻可行的一阶方法：通过求解二阶锥规划（SOCP）计算鲁棒搜索方向，并采用带保护的（safeguarded）回溯线搜索确定步长。方法假设函数值可精确计算且初始点严格可行。

Result: 在精确函数评估与严格可行初始点假设下，方法保持严格可行性；只要搜索方向非零即可保证目标充分下降。理论结果包括：被接受步长的均匀正下界、平均平方搜索方向范数的 O(1/K) 界、搜索方向收敛到零；并证明严格可行点处搜索方向为零可证明近似一阶驻点性。在多智能体杂乱环境导航任务中验证，方法在梯度含噪情况下仍维持无碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: 该方法在不精确梯度下兼顾了任意时刻可行性、目标下降与收敛保证，为实时安全关键场景提供了理论支撑，并通过多智能体导航实验验证了其抗噪有效性。

Abstract: Constrained optimization is central to many engineering systems in which decisions must satisfy strict safety and operational requirements, especially in real-time settings with limited computational budgets. In such scenarios, optimization algorithms are often terminated before full convergence, making *anytime feasibility* essential for safe deployment. Existing methods that guarantee feasibility at every iterate typically rely on exact gradient information, an assumption that is often violated in practice due to measurement noise, stochastic approximations, or model mismatch. We develop an anytime-feasible first-order method for nonlinear constrained optimization under norm-bounded errors in the objective and constraint gradients. The method computes a robust search direction by solving a second-order cone program and selects a step size through safeguarded backtracking. Assuming exact function evaluations and a strictly feasible initialization, the method preserves strict feasibility and guarantees sufficient objective decrease whenever the computed search direction is nonzero. We establish a uniform positive lower bound on the accepted step sizes, an O(1/K) bound on the average squared search direction norm, and convergence of the search directions to zero. We also show that a zero search direction at a strictly feasible point certifies approximate first-order stationarity. We validate the proposed method on a multi-agent navigation task in cluttered environments and show that it maintains collision-free trajectories despite noisy gradient information.

</details>


### [295] [Modeling and Control of a Unicycle Robot on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23878)
*Adeel Akhtar,Mohamed Al Lawati*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入式流形上运动学独轮车的路径跟随控制，提出静态与动态反馈律，并在单位切丛上刻画路径跟随流形，证明其局部指数稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将路径跟随问题从平面/轨迹跟踪扩展到嵌入 R^3 的二维流形，避免对路径施加时间参数化。

Method: 在几何框架下，将路径提升至曲面的单位切丛，构建路径跟随流形并论证其控制不变性；设计两种反馈律：(1) 规定非零平移速度的静态控制器；(2) 基于平移速度动态扩展的动态控制器。随后在适当正则性条件下分析闭环稳定性。

Result: 所提控制器使路径跟随流形在适当正则性条件下局部指数稳定；数值仿真验证了控制器有效性，代码与动画已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作为流形上独轮车的无时间参数化路径跟随提供了几何控制方案，能够保证局部指数稳定，并可推广至嵌入式曲面场景。

Abstract: This paper studies path-following control for a kinematic unicycle evolving on a smooth manifold embedded in \(\mathbb{R}^3\). The objective is to stabilize a one-dimensional path on the manifold without imposing a temporal parameterization, thereby distinguishing the problem from trajectory tracking. Working in a geometric framework, we propose two feedback laws: a static controller for prescribed nonzero translational speed and a dynamic controller based on a dynamic extension of the translational speed. By lifting the path to the unit tangent bundle of the surface, we characterize the corresponding path-following manifold and establish its control invariance. We then show that the proposed controllers render the path-following manifold locally exponentially stable under suitable regularity conditions. A numerical simulation illustrates the proposed controller. Code and animations are publicly available at https://gradslab.github.io/UnicycleOnManifolds/.

</details>


### [296] [The Implementation Cost of Fairness in Service Policy Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24072)
*Junjie Liu,Mingjie Hu,Kejia Hu,Siyang Gao,Jianqiang Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究公平性的"实施成本"（验证公平并可靠选出最优公平策略所需的采样量），指出其与"运营成本"同等重要。作者在固定预算下分析基于均值绩效、服务不足风险、分位数和上尾结果的公平策略选择，证明不同公平性度量可产生相同公平策略集与相同运营成本，却因估计量局部验证难度不同而需要显著不同的证据量；并在临界公平容忍度附近揭示所需采样预算与到边界距离的平方成反比，形成"有限预算实施鸿沟"。据此提出公平引导的自适应分配算法，在合成、呼叫中心和急诊科实验中验证机制并显著提升预算使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务组织用仿真、试点和历史数据选择兼顾总体绩效与公平性的服务策略。既有研究主要评估公平性的运营成本（限制可行策略集导致的绩效损失），但忽略了另一维度：验证公平性并可靠选出最优公平策略所需的采样努力。当公平要求是总体层面（population-level）时，它可能运营上很有吸引力，但在有限采样预算下统计上无法实施，因此需要识别并刻画这一被忽视的实施成本。

Method: 在固定预算策略选择框架下，考虑基于均值绩效、服务不足风险（under-service risk）、分位数和上尾结果四类公平性要求的策略选择问题。通过分析各公平性度量对应估计量的局部验证难度，比较产生相同公平策略集与相同运营成本的度量所需证据量的差异；进一步分析临界公平容忍度附近的采样预算随到边界距离的缩放规律。在此基础上设计公平引导的自适应分配算法，将样本导向决定错误选择的排序与公平性验证比较。

Result: 不同公平性度量可产生相同的公平策略集和相同的运营成本，但因估计量局部验证难度不同而需要差异显著的采样量。在临界公平容忍度附近，所需采样预算与到边界距离的平方成反比，导致有限预算下的实施鸿沟。合成、呼叫中心和急诊科实验证实了上述实施成本机制，并表明所提出的分配算法能更有效地利用采样预算。

Conclusion: 公平性度量、公平容忍度与可用采样预算应联合设计，因为总体层面的公平要求在运营上有吸引力但可能在给定预算下不可统计实施。提出的公平引导自适应分配算法通过聚焦主导错误选择的排序与公平性验证比较，显著提高预算使用效率，为实践中兼顾绩效与公平的策略选择提供了可操作的工具。

Abstract: Service organizations use simulation, pilot studies, and historical data to select service policies that balance aggregate performance and fairness. Existing work primarily evaluates the operational cost of fairness, defined as the performance loss caused by restricting the feasible policy set. In this research, we identify and study implementation cost as a distinct and equally important dimension of fairness, defined as the sampling effort required to verify fairness and reliably select the best fair policy. Specifically, we consider fixed-budget policy selection under fairness requirements based on mean performance, under-service risk, quantiles, and upper tail outcomes, and show that different fairness metrics can induce the same fair policy set and the same operational cost while requiring substantially different amounts of evidence because their estimators have different local verification difficulty. We further show that, near a critical fairness tolerance, the required sampling budget for implementation scales inversely with the square of the distance to the boundary, creating a finite-budget implementation gap. These results imply that the fairness metric, the fairness tolerance, and the available sampling budget should be designed jointly, because a population-level fairness requirement may be operationally attractive but not statistically implementable at the available budget. To translate this perspective into practice, we develop a fairness-guided adaptive allocation algorithm that directs samples toward the ranking and fairness verification comparisons governing false selection. Experiments in synthetic, call center, and emergency department settings demonstrate the implementation cost mechanisms identified in this work and show that the proposed allocation uses the sampling budget much more effectively.

</details>


### [297] [General Leader-Follower Linear-Quadratic Stochastic Graphon Games with Indefinite Control Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24185)
*Weijia Chen,Jingtao Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有不定控制权重的主从型线性二次随机图极限博弈，利用随机最大值原理刻画ollower的Nash响应与leader的最优控制，在Riccati可解性与凸性条件下构造Stackelberg-Nash均衡，并为有限网络博弈设计分散策略证明其构成近似均衡，进而得到混沌传播结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主从型图极限博弈模型的状态方程扩散项较为受限，且控制权重通常要求正定。本文旨在将模型推广至更具一般性的扩散项（依赖状态、控制、leader变量及图极限聚合项）和不定控制权重情形，从而涵盖更广泛的特殊模型。

Method: 构建含一个leader与连续统follower的主从型线性二次随机图极限博弈，follower间通过图极限聚合项交互，并与leader在动力学与代价泛函上耦合。采用随机最大值原理，将follower的Nash响应与leader的最优控制刻画为由带图极限聚合项的耦合正倒向随机系统。在适当的Riccati可解性与凸性条件下构造极限博弈的Stackelberg-Nash均衡，并针对有限玩家网络博弈设计分散策略。

Result: 在Riccati可解性与凸性条件下成功构造了极限图极限博弈的Stackelberg-Nash均衡；所设计的分散策略构成有限玩家网络博弈的近似Stackelberg-Nash均衡，并由此导出混沌传播结果。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了不定线性二次随机图极限博弈、Stackelberg平均场博弈以及带随机效应的随机图极限博弈等特例，为一般主从型随机图极限博弈提供了完整的均衡分析与分散策略设计。

Abstract: This paper studies general leader-follower linear-quadratic stochastic graphon games with indefinite control weights. The model consists of one leader and a continuum of followers, where the followers interact through graphon aggregate terms and are coupled with the leader through their dynamics and cost functionals. Compared with existing related models, the state equations considered here allow more general diffusion terms, including diffusion coefficients depending on the states, controls, leader variables, and graphon aggregate terms. Using the stochastic maximum principle, we characterize the followers' Nash response and the leader's optimal control through coupled forward-backward stochastic systems with graphon aggregate terms. Under suitable Riccati solvability and convexity conditions, we construct a Stackelberg-Nash equilibrium for the limiting graphon game. We further design decentralized strategies for the associated finite-player network game and prove that they form an approximate Stackelberg-Nash equilibrium, which yields a propagation of chaos result. The framework covers, as special cases, indefinite linear-quadratic stochastic graphon games, Stackelberg mean field games, and stochastic graphon games with random effects.

</details>


### [298] [Global Exponential Stabilization of a 3D Nonholonomic Vehicle in Spherical Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24192)
*Kwang Hak Kim,Velimir Todorovski,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用球坐标的几何奇异性，为仅靠前向速度、俯仰角速率和偏航角速率驱动的3D非完整车辆设计了一种连续时不变的反步反馈律，实现指数镇定，并证明其吸引域达到最大可能范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多空间（3D）车辆（如AUV和固定翼飞行器）本质上是非完整的且驱动受限，连续时不变镇定被Brockett必要条件从根本上阻断，因此需要寻找突破该障碍的反馈设计方法。

Method: 利用球坐标的几何奇异性，设计连续、时不变的反步反馈控制律；构造严格控制Lyapunov函数证明原点在给定域上的全局指数稳定性，并指定衰减率，同时防止系统趋近奇异集。

Result: 闭环吸引域仅排除坐标奇异性，即车辆位于过目标且垂直于目标平面的直线上这一余维二、测度零的初始条件集合，从而覆盖最大可能区域；系统在笛卡尔坐标下对原点指数吸引，球坐标与笛卡尔坐标的数值仿真验证了控制律的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在Brockett条件阻碍连续时不变镇定的情形下，通过巧用球坐标奇异性实现了3D非完整车辆的最大吸引域指数镇定。

Abstract: Many spatial (3D) vehicles, including AUVs and fixed-wing aircraft, are effectively nonholonomic and subject to limited actuation, such that continuous time-invariant stabilization is fundamentally obstructed by Brockett's necessary condition. To overcome this obstruction, we exploit the geometric singularity of spherical coordinates to design a backstepping continuous, time-invariant feedback law that exponentially stabilizes a 3D nonholonomic vehicle actuated solely by forward surge velocity, pitch rate, and yaw rate. The resulting closed-loop region of attraction excludes only the coordinate singularity, codimension-two, measure-zero set of initial conditions in which the vehicle lies on the line through the target orthogonal to the target plane, thereby covering the largest possible domain. We further construct a strict control Lyapunov function to prove global exponential stability of the origin on this domain with a user-specified decay rate, while simultaneously preventing the system from approaching the singular set. Finally, we show that the closed-loop system is exponentially attractive to the origin in Cartesian coordinates. Numerical simulation examples in both spherical and Cartesian coordinates illustrate the effectiveness of the control law.

</details>


### [299] [The Price of Covertness: Dual-Control Navigation in Uncertain Flows under Adversarial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24225)
*Ruimeng Hu,Botao Jin,Xu Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究不确定流场中的隐蔽导航问题，将运动规划与信息获取耦合建模，通过增广估计误差方差、推导HJB方程与零和博弈，揭示隐蔽时间前沿、信息获取时序效应及随机化感知分配的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆在不确定流场中导航时必须估计局部流场，但估计误差引发的修正机动会增加统计可区分性（泄漏），从而提高被探测风险。运动规划与信息获取之间存在耦合，需要统一建模以刻画隐蔽性与信息获取之间的权衡。

Method: 将位置状态增广估计误差方差，其演化依赖路径上的局部信息速率；通过小误差展开得到估计不确定性对期望可探测率的一阶修正；将路径规划问题刻画为一阶Hamilton–Jacobi–Bellman（HJB）方程；推导关于感知质量的精确灵敏度公式；构建零和感知分配博弈，证明泄漏率关于感知分配为凸，并证明防御方可将注意力限制在极端单点分配的随机化上；用列生成求解均衡，车辆最优响应由HJB方程给出。

Result: 截止时间索引的值函数确定隐蔽时间前沿；灵敏度公式表明路径上较早获取的信息可减少后续泄漏，而较晚获取的信息无法减少已发生的泄漏；泄漏率关于感知分配为凸，防御方可限制在极端单点分配的随机化；列生成结合HJB最优响应可计算均衡；数值实验展示了学习性绕行、截止时间—泄漏权衡、空间排序效应及随机化感知分配的收益。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了不确定流场中隐蔽导航的运动规划与信息获取耦合的统一框架，揭示了信息获取时序的不对称效应，并通过零和博弈与凸性分析给出了随机化感知分配的最优性及可计算求解方法，数值实验验证了理论发现。

Abstract: Covert navigation in an uncertain flow couples motion planning with information acquisition. A vehicle must estimate the local flow to navigate, while estimation error induces corrective maneuvers that increase statistical distinguishability, or leakage, and hence the vehicle's detectability. We model this coupling by augmenting position with the estimation-error variance, whose evolution depends on the route through the local information rate. A small-error expansion shows that estimation uncertainty contributes a leading-order correction to the expected detectability rate. The resulting route-planning problem is characterized by a first-order Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation. The deadline-indexed value determines the covert-time frontier, while an exact sensitivity formula with respect to sensing quality shows that information acquired earlier along the route can reduce leakage incurred later, whereas information acquired later cannot reduce leakage already incurred. We then formulate a zero-sum sensing-allocation game, show that the leakage rate is convex in the sensing allocation, and prove that the defender may restrict attention to randomization over extreme single-site allocations. The resulting equilibrium is computed by column generation, with vehicle best responses obtained from the HJB equation. Numerical experiments illustrate the learning detour, the deadline--leakage tradeoff, the spatial-ordering effect, and the benefit of randomized sensing allocations.

</details>


### [300] [Benign Geometry and Distributional Robustness of $\mathcal{H}_2$ Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24421)
*Arman Sharifi Kolarijani,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani,Tamás Keviczky,Mohamad Amin Sharifi Kolarijani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间线性时不变系统镇定状态反馈控制器的标准与分布鲁棒H2综合问题，建立了平稳性与全局最优性的等价关系及精确的Pythagorean恒等式，并将其推广到分布鲁棒情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准H2综合通常需要非奇异扰动可控性Gramian或正定控制惩罚等假设，本文旨在放松这些限制，并进一步考虑扰动分布不完全已知时的鲁棒H2综合问题。

Method: 不假设扰动可控性Gramian非奇异或控制惩罚正定，证明镇定增益的平稳性与全局最优性等价，推导性能差的精确Pythagorean恒等式；随后考虑零均值、二阶矩一致有界的独立同分布扰动，推广平稳性-最优性等价到分布鲁棒H2综合；最后针对Frobenius、KL和Wasserstein-2模糊集进行专门化分析。

Result: 证明了平稳性与全局最优性的等价性及精确Pythagorean恒等式；表明每个标准H2最优增益同时对每类分布鲁棒问题最优；给出三种模糊集下最坏情况协方差的显式刻画，从而为共同最优控制器提供性能证书。

Conclusion: 该框架在更弱假设下统一了标准与分布鲁棒H2综合，揭示了标准最优控制的分布鲁棒普适性，并为多种模糊集提供了显式的最坏情况分析和性能保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we study standard and distributionally robust $\mathcal{H}_2$ synthesis problem of a stabilizing state-feedback controller for discrete-time linear time-invariant systems. Without requiring a nonsingular disturbance controllability Gramian or a positive-definite control penalty, we establish that stationarity of a stabilizing gain in standard $\mathcal{H}_2$ synthesis is equivalent to global optimality and derive an exact Pythagorean identity for the performance difference. We then generalize the $\mathcal{H}_2$ synthesis problem by considering i.i.d. disturbances with zero mean and uniformly bounded second moments, and extend the stationarity-optimality equivalence to the corresponding distributionally robust $\mathcal{H}_2$ synthesis. Moreover, we show that every standard $\mathcal{H}_2$-optimal gain is simultaneously optimal for every such distributionally robust problem. Finally, we specialize the framework to Frobenius, Kullback--Leibler, and Wasserstein-2 ambiguity sets and obtain explicit characterizations of their worst-case covariances, which, in turn, provide performance certificates for a common optimal controller.

</details>


### [301] [Complexities of Weak Proximal Oracle Methods for Composite Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24423)
*Dan Garber*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文否定了基于弱近端预言机(WPO)的方法可被加速的可能性：对确定性与随机方法均建立了下界，表明WPO在降低单次预言机调用成本的同时必然带来预言机复杂度的固有损失，并给出了几乎匹配近端次梯度法的新的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WPO放松了标准近端算子的强最优性条件，当最优解具有稀疏结构时可显著提升单次调用效率，且已有算法在预言机复杂度上可媲美依赖精确近端操作的近端(次)梯度法。但一个悬而未决的问题是：这类WPO方法能否实现Nesterov意义下的加速。作者旨在回答该问题。

Method: 研究二次增长条件下的标准凸复合优化问题（目标函数平滑或非平滑），从理论层面分别针对确定性方法与随机方法建立下界；同时构造并分析新的WPO上界算法。

Result: 建立了确定性方法与随机方法的下界，给出否定性答案——WPO方法无法实现加速；同时给出非平滑凸复合优化情形下新的上界，几乎匹配近端次梯度法。

Conclusion: WPO虽能大幅降低单次预言机调用的开销，但这一优势伴随着预言机复杂度上的固有损失，即无法通过加速来弥补；并提供了接近最优上界的算法刻画。

Abstract: We consider a standard convex composite optimization problem with either smooth or nonsmooth objective function, and under quadratic growth. In recent years, several works gave algorithms based on a \textit{weak proximal oracle} (WPO) that essentially match in oracle complexities proximal (sub)gradient methods relying on exact prox operations. Importantly, such WPOs, which relax the strong optimality condition of the standard prox operator, may admit much more efficient implementation in terms of runtime when optimal solutions have some sparse structure. A question remained if such WPO-based methods can be accelerated (in the sense of Nesterov's accelerated gradient). In this work we provide a negative answer by establishing lower bounds against both deterministic and randomized methods. Thus, while WPOs can substantially reduce the cost of individual oracle calls, this comes with an inherent loss in oracle complexity. We also provide a new upper-bound for WPO-based nonsmooth convex composite optimization, nearly matching the proximal subgradient method.

</details>


### [302] [Numerical approximation of distributed optimal control problems governed by linear thermo-poroelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24448)
*Jeonghun J. Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性热孔隙弹性最优控制问题提出并分析了一种新的三场对称格式，证明了适定性和最优控制对的存在唯一性，推导了一阶最优性条件与三场伴随系统，并给出全离散先验误差估计与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性热孔隙弹性耦合问题涉及固体位移、流体压力与温度三个物理场，且可能同时存在分布流体源与热源两个控制变量，现有对称格式与最优控制理论尚不完善，需要建立适定且便于数值分析的三场对称表述。

Method: 提出三场对称表述：状态变量为固体位移 u、流体压力 p、温度 θ，控制变量为分布流体源 m_p 与分布热源 m_θ；证明对称微分格式的适定性，建立最优控制对 (m̄_p, m̄_θ) 的存在唯一性，并通过耦合三场伴随系统推导一阶必要最优性条件；全离散采用 dG(0) 时间离散与变分离散处理两个控制。

Result: 证明了对称微分格式适定性、最优控制对存在唯一性及一阶必要最优性条件；对基于 dG(0) 的全离散格式，得到状态、伴随与控制误差的 O(h^s + Δt) 先验误差估计；制造解实验验证了全离散最优性系统，并展示了分析覆盖的存储退化区域。

Conclusion: 该三场对称表述为线性热孔隙弹性最优控制问题提供了统一的理论与离散分析框架，兼具适定性、最优性条件与收敛性保证，并适用于存储退化情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and analyze a novel three-field symmetric formulation for optimal control problems governed by linear thermo-poroelasticity. The state variables are the solid displacement $\boldsymbol{u}$, the fluid pressure $p$, and the temperature $θ$, and both the distributed fluid source $m_p$ and the distributed heat source $m_θ$ serve as control variables. We prove well-posedness of the symmetric differentiated formulation, establish the existence and uniqueness of an optimal control pair $(\bar{m}_p,\bar{m}_θ)$, and derive the first-order necessary optimality conditions via a coupled three-field adjoint system. For the fully discrete scheme based on dG(0) time discretization, we employ a variational discretization approach for both controls and derive a priori error estimates of order $\mathcal{O}(h^s + Δt)$ for the state, adjoint, and control errors. We also present manufactured-solution experiments that validate the fully discrete optimality system and illustrate the storage-degenerate regime covered by our analysis.

</details>


### [303] [Parallel Optimization: Theory, Algorithms, and Applications,](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24505)
*Yair Censor,Stavros A. Zenios*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 这是一本30年前出版的学术专著，现由牛津大学出版社免费开放下载，纸质版仍可购买。该书曾获1999年INFORMS计算学会奖，并有János D. Pintér的书评（Journal of Global Optimization, 16: 107-108, 2000）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为让未来年轻科学家能继续获取该书内容，牛津大学出版社授权免费开放下载，兼顾学术传承与出版方商业发行。

Method: 该摘要为出版与获取信息的说明，未涉及论文的研究方法。

Result: 获得1999年INFORMS计算学会"运筹学与计算机科学交叉领域研究卓越奖"；书评载于Journal of Global Optimization, 16: 107-108, 2000。

Conclusion: 这是一部在运筹学与计算机科学交叉领域获得权威奖项认可、并被学术期刊评论的经典著作，现可免费获取。

Abstract: This 30 years old book is posted for free download by the generosity of Oxford University Press that granted permission to allow access to future generations of young scientists. A printed copy can be purchased from the publisher. The authors received for this book "The 1999 INFORMS Computing Society Prize for Research Excellence in the Interface Between Operations Research and Computer Science" from the Institute for Operations Research and the Management Sciences (INFORMS). For a book review by Janos D. Pinter go to: Journal of Global Optimization, 16: 107-108, 2000.

</details>


### [304] [Convergence of Rotation-based Matrix Optimizers: A Unified Analysis of SOAP, Conda, and SPlus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24522)
*Yiwen Sun,Huan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架，用于分析基于旋转的矩阵优化器的收敛性，首次给出了SOAP的收敛速率（具有尖锐的维度依赖）以及用核范数度量的Adam收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于旋转的矩阵优化器（如SOAP、Conda、truncated SPlus及矩阵参数化的Adam）在实际中表现优异，但缺乏统一的理论收敛性分析，尤其是其收敛速率对维度的依赖关系尚不清晰。作者希望建立一个统一框架来填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一的理论框架：基于旋转的优化器先用正交变换将动量映射到旋转空间，在该空间进行逐坐标或归一化更新，再旋转回原空间。框架涵盖SOAP、Conda、truncated SPlus及矩阵参数化Adam，并推广到允许任意正交旋转矩阵（可依赖每轮迭代的随机梯度）的变体。技术核心是"行列迹控制（row-column trace-control）"论证，将逐元素界转化为两个对角控制矩阵上的界，从而改进结果对维度的依赖。

Result: 首次建立了SOAP的收敛速率，且具有尖锐的维度依赖；同时给出了用核范数度量的Adam收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该统一框架不仅统一并推广了多种主流矩阵优化器的收敛分析，还通过行列迹控制技术改善了维度依赖，为基于旋转的矩阵优化器提供了新的理论工具与更紧的收敛保证。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a unified theoretical framework for analyzing the convergence of rotation-based matrix optimizers, which apply orthogonal transformations to map the momentum into a rotated space, perform coordinate-wise or normalized updates there, and then rotate the updates back. Our framework encompasses prominent matrix optimizers, including SOAP, Conda, and truncated SPlus, as well as matrix-parameterized Adam as a special case. As our main result, we establish, for the first time, the convergence rate of SOAP with sharp dimensional dependence, as well as the convergence rate of Adam measured by the nuclear norm. Our framework also covers a more general variant of rotation-based optimizers that allows arbitrary orthogonal rotation matrices, allowing these matrices to depend on the current stochastic gradient at each iteration. Technically, our framework leverages a row-column trace-control argument that converts elementwise bounds into bounds on two diagonal control matrices, thereby improving the dimension dependence of the resulting bound.

</details>


### [305] [Scalable Dynamic Pricing of Substitutable Products through Structure-Guided Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24605)
*Yue Su,Antoine Désir,Axel Parmentier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究有限产品库存下可替代产品的动态定价问题，提出两种基于MNL的政策学习方法，用小规模最优差距低于0.4%、大规模优于基准的效果验证了模型结构的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户替代行为使各产品定价决策相互耦合，而库存状态导致精确动态规划在现实规模下不可解，因此需要可扩展的近似定价方法。

Method: 提出两种MNL引导的政策学习方法，用从库存状态到定价决策的统计映射替代动态规划：第一种直接学习价格，第二种学习库存机会成本并借助最优MNL定价规则转化为价格；两者均采用决策导向学习训练，并提出高效生成前瞻性客户选择目标的方法。

Result: 在可计算最优动态规划的小规模实例上，学习策略平均最优性差距低于0.4%；在规模较大的航空类实例上，持续优于所测试的收益管理基准。

Conclusion: 两种架构的对比凸显模型结构的作用：当需求可由MNL良好刻画时，利用MNL定价刻画尤为有效；而在异质混合MNL需求下，直接学习价格具有更强灵活性。

Abstract: Problem definition: We study dynamic pricing of substitutable products with finite, product-specific inventories. Customer substitution couples pricing decisions across products, while the inventory state makes exact dynamic programming intractable at realistic scale. Methodology / results: We develop two MNL-guided policy-learning approaches that replace the dynamic program with a statistical mapping from inventory states to pricing decisions. The first learns prices directly, while the second learns inventory opportunity costs and converts them into prices using the optimal MNL pricing rule. Both policies are trained using decision-focused learning. We also propose an efficient method to generate anticipative customer-choice targets to train our policies. Managerial implications: We conduct an extensive numerical evaluation of our approaches. On small instances for which the optimal dynamic program can be computed, the learned policies achieve average optimality gaps below 0.4%. On larger airline-motivated instances, they consistently improve on the tested revenue-management benchmarks. The comparison between the two architectures also highlights the role of model structure: using the MNL pricing characterization is particularly effective when demand is well described by MNL, while directly learning prices provides greater flexibility under heterogeneous mixed-MNL demand.

</details>


### [306] [Conditional Infimum, Hidden Convexity and S-Procedure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24661)
*Jean-Philippe Chancelier,Michel de Lara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文引入"条件下确界"概念并建立其塔性质理论，用以揭示非凸优化问题中的隐藏凸性，进而给出非凸二次极小化问题隐藏凸性的新充分条件，并适配S-过程处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸极小化问题通常难以求得全局极小点。隐藏凸性是应对此类问题的工具之一，但现有揭示隐藏凸性的手段有限，尤其在非凸二次极小化和S-过程等经典问题上仍需更一般的理论条件。

Method: 引入条件下确界（conditional infimum）概念并发展相应理论，建立与极小化问题相关的塔性质（tower property）；用条件下确界揭示隐藏凸性，据此提出非凸二次极小化问题隐藏凸性的新充分条件，引入块符号对矩阵-向量（block-signed pair matrix-vector）概念；并将该方法用于处理S-过程。

Result: 所得充分条件涵盖并超出已知结果；条件下确界被证明特别适合处理S-过程。

Conclusion: 条件下确界为揭示隐藏凸性提供了一个统一而有效的框架，可推广非凸二次极小化的全局最优性条件并应用于S-过程。

Abstract: Detecting hidden convexity is one of the tools to address nonconvex minimization problems, and find global minimizers. We introduce the notion of conditional infimum, develop the theory, and establish a tower property, relevant for minimization problems. Then, we illustrate how the conditional infimum is instrumental in revealing hidden convexity. Thus equipped, we provide a new sufficient condition for hidden convexity in nonconvex quadratic minimization problems, that encompasses and goes beyond known results (with the notion of block-signed pair matrix-vector). We also show how the conditional infimum is especially adapted to tackle the so-called S-procedure.

</details>


### [307] [Reinforcement Learning in Operational Research: A Technical Review and Practical Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24750)
*Yahan Lu,Dongyang Xia,Nursen Aydin,Shadi Sharif Azadeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了强化学习（RL）赋能运筹学（OR）的三种关键角色，并展望未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂动态系统中实时、数据驱动决策的需求日益增长，给传统运筹学方法带来压力。强化学习凭借强大的学习与计算能力，成为处理动态不确定环境下序贯决策的补充手段。近年来RL与OR融合的研究兴趣上升，但整合方式与应用场景多样，缺乏系统且技术细节充分的综述。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，围绕RL赋能OR的三种关键角色展开：(i) 求解动态环境中的序贯决策问题；(ii) 作为端到端求解方法，或作为启发式与精确OR方法中的组件以求解组合优化问题；(iii) 通过与数字孪生系统集成支持扩展现实分析。对上述角色的最新进展进行批判性综合。

Result: 系统梳理了三种角色下RL赋能OR的最新进展，归纳了各角色的优势、实现前提、局限性与挑战，并据此提出未来研究路线图。

Conclusion: 通过系统整理RL赋能OR的整合方式与技术要求，为推进RL与OR在方法论与实践层面的深度融合提供了路线图。

Abstract: The growing demand for real-time, data-driven decision-making in complex and dynamic systems is placing increasing pressure on traditional Operational Research (OR) methodologies. Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a complementary approach, offering strong learning and computational capabilities for sequential decision-making in dynamic and uncertain environments. Recent research shows an increasing interest in integrating RL with OR to address dynamic decision-making problems, enhance heuristic and exact methods for combinatorial optimization, and support the development of digital replicas of operational systems. The overarching goal across these efforts is to leverage the learning capabilities of RL to strengthen traditional OR algorithms, improving solution quality, computational efficiency, and robustness. Given the diversity of integration approaches and application settings, there is a clear need for a systematic and technically detailed review of how RL empowers OR methods. To address this gap, this paper presents a structured review of three key roles that RL plays in empowering OR: (i) solving sequential decision-making problems in dynamic environments, (ii) serving as an end-to-end solution method or as a component integrated within heuristic and exact OR methods for combinatorial optimization problems, and (iii) facilitating extended reality analysis through integration with digital twin systems. We critically synthesize recent advances across these roles, highlighting their advantages, implementation requirements, limitations, and challenges. Finally, based on these insights, we outline a roadmap for future research to further advance the methodological and practical integration of RL and OR.

</details>


### [308] [Quantitative finite-population approximation of heterogeneous linear--quadratic mean-field control with common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24849)
*Aqib Ahmed*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带共同噪声与随机系数的异质线性二次平均场控制问题的有限总体逼近，推导N个非可交换主体的中心化社会计划者问题的精确有限维随机Riccati系统，并与连续体模型的Hilbert空间Riccati系统进行定量比较，给出完整后向系统的收敛速率及分散策略的渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限N主体系统与平均场连续体模型之间的定量误差缺乏系统刻画，尤其是共同噪声、异质性（非可交换性）和随机系数带来的对角、非对角、仿射与标量分量的缩放关系不清，需建立可分解的误差估计以支撑分散策略的最优性分析。

Method: 从N个非可交换主体的集中式社会计划者问题出发，推导有限维随机Riccati系统并识别对角、非对角、仿射及标量分量的缩放；将该系统与连续体模型的Hilbert空间值Riccati系统对比；估计完整后向系统（含共同噪声鞅被积函数）的收敛性；误差分解为系数与核一致性、共同噪声逼近、界面效应、精确对角修正及极限交互核在离散对角带上的质量；将估计传播至反馈增益，并通过逐胞耦合传播至最优状态、控制和初始社会价值；用逐胞投影构造分散有限总体策略。

Result: 主要估计给出完整后向系统（包括共同噪声鞅被积函数）的定量收敛；误差可分解为系数与核一致性、共同噪声逼近、界面效应、精确对角修正及极限交互核在离散对角带上的质量；估计传播至反馈增益，并经逐胞耦合传播至最优状态、控制和初始社会价值；极限代表主体反馈的逐胞投影给出分散有限总体策略，其最优性差距趋于零；在给定正则性假设下误差界由显式逼近与集中模量表示，定量界给出代数速率，有限型机制下后向系统达N^{-1/2}速率，最优轨迹速率还反映可用的条件矩界。

Conclusion: 该工作为异质线性二次平均场控制问题提供了有限总体逼近的完整定量框架，通过显式误差分解与逐胞耦合，建立了后向系统至N^{-1/2}的收敛速率，并证明逐胞投影分散策略的渐近最优性。

Abstract: We study the finite-population approximation of a heterogeneous linear--quadratic mean-field control problem with common noise and random coefficients. Starting from the centralized social planner problem for $N$ non-exchangeable agents, we derive its exact finite-dimensional stochastic Riccati system and identify the scaling of its diagonal, off-diagonal, affine, and scalar components. We then compare this system with the Hilbert-space-valued Riccati system of the continuum model. Our main estimates give quantitative convergence of the complete backward system, including its common-noise martingale integrands. The error separates coefficient and kernel consistency, common-noise approximation, interface effects, exact diagonal corrections, and the mass of the limiting interaction kernel on the discrete diagonal band. We propagate these estimates to the feedback gains and, by a cellwise coupling, to the optimal states, controls, and initial social values. Finally, a cellwise projection of the limiting representative-agent feedback yields a decentralized finite-population strategy whose optimality gap vanishes. Under the stated regularity assumptions, the error bounds are expressed through explicit approximation and concentration moduli. Quantitative bounds on these moduli yield algebraic rates, including an $N^{-1/2}$ rate for the backward system in finite-type regimes; the rates for optimal trajectories additionally reflect the available conditional moment bounds.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [309] [County-Level Heterogeneity in Opioid Harm Reduction and Treatment Effects: A Simulation Modeling Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22419)
*Abdulrahman A. Ahmed,M. Amin Rahimian,Qiushi Chen,Praveen Kumar*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究构建了阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）仿真模型，分别校准至宾夕法尼亚州六个县（大城市、中等城市、农村），预测2025-2029年在纳洛酮分发与丁丙诺啡治疗各提升10%/20%/30%情形下的过量死亡轨迹。结果显示同等比例干预在各县带来的死亡率下降差异巨大，且与各县历史纳洛酮分发波动性密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国阿片类药物过量致死仍是严重公共卫生危机，各县在流行轨迹、基线资源和本地情境上存在异质性。纳洛酮分发与丁丙诺啡治疗均为循证减害策略，但缺乏县级效果信息，妨碍决策者跨县配置资源。

Method: 开发OUD仿真模型，分别校准至宾夕法尼亚州六个县（大城市：Allegheny、Philadelphia；中等规模：Erie、Dauphin；农村：Clearfield、Columbia）。在2025-2029五年期内，模拟纳洛酮分发与丁丙诺啡处方量在基线之上提升10%、20%、30%三种水平下的县级过量死亡轨迹。

Result: 纳洛酮分发较县基线提升30%时，预计2029年过量死亡下降：Allegheny约70%（95% UI: 55-81%）、Erie 11%（95% UI: 4-17%）、Clearfield 28%（95% UI: 2-64%）。丁丙诺啡带来的降幅普遍更小（10%-23%），仅Erie例外（丁丙诺啡20% vs 纳洛酮11%）。纳洛酮响应异质性与各县历史纳洛酮分发波动性高度相关。

Conclusion: 同等比例的纳洛酮分发提升在不同县带来显著不同的死亡率降幅，取决于各县基线分发历史，这一模式仅凭死亡率统计无法察觉。县级情境对减害与治疗资源的县级优先级配置至关重要。

Abstract: Opioid overdose deaths remain a severe public health crisis in the US, with heterogeneous burden across counties that differ in epidemic trajectory, baseline resources, and local context. While harm reduction through naloxone distribution and buprenorphine treatment are both evidence-based strategies, limited information on county-level effects hinders the ability of policymakers to prioritize resources across counties. We developed a simulation model of opioid use disorder (OUD), calibrated separately to six Pennsylvania counties spanning large urban (Allegheny, Philadelphia), intermediate-sized (Erie, Dauphin), and rural (Clearfield, Columbia) settings. We projected county-specific overdose mortality trajectories under three levels of increase in buprenorphine dispensing and naloxone distribution (10%, 20%, and 30% above each county's baseline), over a five-year horizon from 2025 to 2029. A 30% increase in naloxone distribution above observed county baseline levels was projected to reduce 2029 overdose deaths by approximately 70% (95% uncertainty interval, UI: 55-81%) in Allegheny County, 11% (95% UI:4-17%) in Erie County, and 28% (95% UI:2-64%) in Clearfield County. Projected reductions in overdose deaths from increasing buprenorphine were consistently smaller (10%-23%), except that in Erie buprenorphine produced larger projected reduction by 20% vs 11% for naloxone. Heterogeneity in naloxone responsiveness was strongly associated with each county's historical naloxone dispensing variability. The same proportional increase in naloxone distribution yields substantially different projected mortality reductions across counties depending on each county's baseline distribution history, a pattern invisible from mortality statistics alone. County-level context is important for informing harm reduction and treatment prioritization at the county level.

</details>


### [310] [Adapting Pairs Trading to Gambling Markets A Case Study of the U.S. Presidential Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22639)
*Haoyu Liu,Len Thomas,Benjamin Baer,Carl Donovan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 将配对交易思想迁移至政治博彩市场：以时变均值回复的隐式Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程建模两大党候选人合计隐含概率，用状态空间似然估计参数，并以参数自助上预测界生成信号。2024年样本外检验得130个信号，单步经验覆盖率95.1%，平均合成赔率价格收益1.86%，每笔交易Sharpe型比率1.12（未年化、无摩擦）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对交易依赖相关资产关系的均值回复特性。作者希望把这一思路引入政治博彩市场，利用两大党候选人合计隐含概率的均值回复行为来识别交易信号，从而检验该整合框架在双候选人选举市场中的可行性。

Method: 以隐式Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程建模两大党候选人合计隐含概率，其均值回复水平随时间变化，观测含加性噪声。用状态空间似然从规则采样的赔率数据估计参数；连续相同取值仅保留单个观测，并在连续时间转移中保留经过的采样间隔数。用参数自助法上预测界识别合计隐含概率可能下降的信号时点；用无截距Bradley-Terry型模型选择具体候选人的赔率报价。候选选择模型在2020年美国总统选举数据上训练，在2024年数据上样本外评估。

Result: 2024年分析产生130个信号，一步经验覆盖率为95.1%，平均合成赔率价格收益1.86%，每笔交易未年化Sharpe型比率为1.12。作者强调这些收益是无摩擦的描述性数量，而非可执行的博彩交易所利润。

Conclusion: 结果支持该整合框架可作为双候选人选举市场的概念验证，表明配对交易式的均值回复建模结合状态空间估计与候选选择模型在政治博彩市场中具有初步可行性。

Abstract: Pairs trading exploits mean reversion in the relationship between related assets. We adapt this idea to political betting markets by modelling the combined implied probability of the two major-party nominees with a latent Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process whose mean-reversion level varies over time and whose observations contain additive noise. Model parameters are estimated from regularly sampled odds data using a state-space likelihood, with consecutive repeated values represented by a single retained observation and the elapsed number of sampling intervals preserved in the continuous-time transition. Parametric-bootstrap upper prediction bounds identify signal times at which the combined implied probability is likely to decline, and a no-intercept Bradley-Terry-type model selects the candidate-specific odds quote. The candidate-selection model is trained on 2020 U.S. presidential-election data and evaluated out of sample on 2024 data. The 2024 analysis produced 130 signals, empirical one-step coverage of 95.1%, a mean synthetic odds-price return of 1.86%, and an unannualized per-trade Sharpe-type ratio of 1.12. These returns are frictionless descriptive quantities rather than executable betting-exchange profits. The results support the integrated framework as a proof of concept for two-candidate electoral markets.

</details>


### [311] [Criminal Justice Risk Assessments with Unreliable Class Assignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22718)
*Richard Berk*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Mondrian保形预测集提升刑事司法风险评估中分类器在类别得分接近时的可靠性，并提供有效的预测不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量风险评估长期用于刑事司法决策，但当最大与次大风险得分相近时，结果近乎随机分配，分类不可靠，预测误差和公平性担忧随之上升。

Method: 采用Mondrian保形预测集（Mondrian conformal prediction sets），在分类器判别不确定时输出预测集合而非单一标签。

Result: 利用大规模缓刑犯风险预测数据集进行实证，展示了分类不确定性问题，并表明该方法能提升分类可靠性并提供有效的预测不确定性估计。

Conclusion: Mondrian保形预测集可缓解分类器在得分接近时的不可靠问题，并为刑事司法风险评估提供有统计保证的不确定性量化。

Abstract: Quantitative risk assessments have been used for decades to help inform criminal justice decisions. A set of predictors and a categorical response variable are used to train a statistical classifier. The trained classifier can be employed to compute risk scores for a new, unlabeled case for which a forecast is needed, one risk score for each response variable class. In the spirit of Bayes classifiers, the class with the largest risk score conventionally becomes the forecasted outcome label for that case. The largest and next largest risk scores can be very similar or very different. However, when they are much the same, the outcome class is essentially being assigned by a coin flip; the classification is unreliable. Forecasting error becomes more likely, and concerns about fairness can arise. This paper shows how Mondrian conformal prediction sets can increase classification reliability when the classifier is indecisive and provide valid estimates of forecast uncertainty. Risk forecasts for a large dataset of offenders on probation are used to illustrate the issues.

</details>


### [312] [Modeling Transition Dynamics and Network Structure in Cross-National Process Data: A Hierarchical Multi-State Survival Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22735)
*Doungjun Kim,Daeun Hwangbo,Minjeong Jeon,Ick Hoon Jin*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分层框架，将贝叶斯多状态生存模型与基于网络的转移结构表示相结合，用于跨国家建模计算机化测评的过程数据，并在14个国家的PIAAC两个问题解决题目上揭示了跨国异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机化测评的过程数据记录了答题者解题的动作序列与时序，蕴含解题节奏与结构信息。但跨国建模面临挑战：国家×作答组单元格往往样本量小且不平衡，且各国可观测的转移支持（transition supports）差异较大，传统方法难以在保持国家特异性的同时实现稳健估计。

Method: 提出分层框架：1）贝叶斯多状态生存模型，通过跨国部分池化（partial pooling）得到国家特异的协变量效应、关键动作效应、转移速度及国家间异质性估计；2）将后验转移概率网络嵌入共同潜空间，使用适配异质支持的有向图自编码器（directed graph auto-encoder）；3）在后验抽样间计算节点角色分布间的1-Wasserstein距离，以刻画全局网络结构并传播估计不确定性。

Result: 应用于PIAAC中14个国家的两个问题解决题目，结果发现：转移速度存在跨国异质性；全局网络组织存在系统性作答组差异；组内离散度在各国间的变化依赖于具体题目；以及围绕共享中间动作的路径选择存在局部差异。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在小样本、不平衡单元格与异质转移支持的条件下，同时估计国家特异的过程参数并刻画全局网络结构，为跨国过程数据的比较分析提供了兼顾灵活性与不确定性的工具。

Abstract: Process data from computer-based assessments record the sequence and timing of actions through which respondents solve a task, providing information about both the pace and structure of problem-solving behavior. Modeling such processes across countries is challenging because country-by-response-group cells are often small and unbalanced and the observed transition supports can differ substantially across countries. We propose a hierarchical framework that integrates a Bayesian multi-state survival model with a network-based representation of transition structure. Partial pooling across countries yields country-specific covariate and key-action effects, transition speed, and estimates of between-country heterogeneity. Posterior transition probability networks are embedded in a common latent space using a directed graph auto-encoder adapted to heterogeneous supports, and 1-Wasserstein distances between node-role distributions are evaluated across posterior draws to characterize global network structure while propagating estimation uncertainty. We apply the framework to two problem-solving items from the Programme for the International Assessment of Adult Competencies across 14 countries. The results reveal cross-country heterogeneity in transition speed, systematic response-group differences in global network organization, item-dependent variation in within-group dispersion across countries, and local differences in routing around shared intermediate actions.

</details>


### [313] [Auditing Bayesian Graph Alignment: Diagnostic Comparisons and Reference Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23232)
*Melika Gorgi,Kourosh Mirsohi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文审计贝叶斯图对齐中“对齐分数轨迹收敛”与“对应边缘概率准确”之间的鸿沟，并在多组精确图对上系统比较诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCMC收敛诊断（如R-hat）常被用于判断贝叶斯图对齐是否收敛，但对齐分数的收敛并不必然意味着对应概率（边缘分布）准确。作者希望检验这一差距，评估不同诊断指标能否真正反映后验误差，并检验参考解一致性、粒子数增加等常规做法是否可靠。

Method: 在显式边翻转似然下，使用240个新的精确图对（来自四个数据源家族）、240个20-100顶点的大型图对，以及60个精确实现检查案例，比较三种采样器，并评估分数型、边缘型、指示型、类别型和分类器型诊断。补充进行SMC粒子扩增、重焕新、长程informed运行、独立窗口与留出链检验，并推导一个基础可行对齐界。

Result: 对精确informed采样器，边缘不一致度比分数R-hat更能区分错误，但对原始局部采样改进不确定；基于分配的R*与短指示因子面板具有竞争力，但没有诊断在所有采样器和端点下占优。大尺寸下诊断预测的是后续边缘变化而非后验误差，分类表现依赖漂移阈值。独立窗口与留出链检验削弱但仍保留正相关。240个原始参考集中仅22个通过一致性筛查。在40个失败选取案例中，八倍SMC粒子扩增未解决不一致，而额外重焕新有帮助；更长informed运行仍不稳定。可行对齐界显示，在集中式100顶点情形下SMC与informed链分数严重不具代表性，且与近似参考一致性无关；另给出共起点链在精确边缘误差约0.967时不一致度接近零的反例。

Conclusion: 常规收敛诊断和参考一致性不足以证明贝叶斯图对齐的准确性。应发展对分配敏感（assignment-sensitive）的审计方法，同时正视有限预算、诊断排序与参考一致性作为准确性证据的局限。

Abstract: Bayesian graph alignment estimates correspondence probabilities, but convergence of an alignment-score trace need not imply accurate correspondence marginals. We audit this gap on 240 new exact graph pairs from four source families, 240 larger pairs with 20-100 vertices, and a separate 60-case exact implementation check. Under an explicit edge-flip likelihood, we compare three samplers and score, marginal, indicator, categorical, and classifier-based diagnostics. Marginal disagreement improves error discrimination over score R-hat for the exact informed sampler, but its improvement for vanilla local sampling is uncertain. Assignment-based R* and short indicator panels are competitive; no diagnostic dominates across samplers and endpoints. At larger sizes, diagnostics predict subsequent marginal changes, not posterior error, and classification performance depends on the drift threshold. Disjoint-window and held-out-chain checks attenuate but preserve positive associations. Only 22 of 240 original reference sets pass an agreement screen. On forty failure-selected cases, eightfold SMC particle escalation does not resolve disagreement, whereas additional rejuvenation helps. Longer informed runs remain unstable. An elementary feasible-alignment bound demonstrates severely unrepresentative SMC and informed-chain scores in concentrated 100-vertex cases, independently of approximate reference consensus. We also exhibit common-start chains with near-zero disagreement despite exact marginal error near .967. These results support assignment-sensitive auditing while identifying limits of finite budgets, diagnostic rankings, and reference agreement as evidence of accuracy.

</details>


### [314] [Fear Moves Markets: Sentiment-Augmented POMP for Volatility Modeling of Bitcoin Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23250)
*Suvrorup Mukherjee,Abeyankar Giridharan,Chang Li,Xinhe Wu*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出将情绪指标（恐惧与贪婪指数，FGI）作为外生回归变量引入潜在波动率动态，并用Student's t分布替代高斯测量噪声，构建部分观测马尔可夫过程（POMP）模型以刻画加密货币市场的波动率与状态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场存在极端价格波动和情绪驱动的状态转换，传统波动率模型（如Breto框架）难以刻画这些特征，因此需要引入情绪信号与重尾噪声来提升建模能力。

Method: 在Breto框架基础上扩展为部分观测马尔可夫过程（POMP）模型：一是将恐惧与贪婪指数（FGI）作为外生回归变量纳入潜在波动率动态；二是以Student's t分布替换高斯测量噪声；基于模拟的推断方法，使用2020年1月至2025年4月的比特币日度收益率与FGI数据进行拟合。

Result: 该模型在对数似然和滤波稳定性方面显著优于三个基准模型，且估计出的参数具有可解释性，与已知的金融波动率特征相一致。

Conclusion: 引入情绪信号与重尾噪声能够改进对加密货币市场波动率和状态转换的建模，验证了情绪因子与重尾分布在加密资产波动率建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets exhibit extreme price swings and sentiment-driven regime shifts, which traditional volatility models often fail to capture. To address this, we develop a partially observed Markov processes (POMP) model augmented with sentiment and heavy-tailed distributions. Specifically, we extend Breto's framework by including the Fear and Greed Index (FGI) as an exogenous regressor in the latent volatility dynamics and replacing Gaussian measurement noise with a Student's t distribution. We fit the model via simulation-based inference using daily Bitcoin returns and FGI data from January 2020 to April 2025. Our approach significantly outperforms three benchmark models in log-likelihood and filter stability. Moreover, our model yields interpretable parameters consistent with known features of financial volatility. These results demonstrate that incorporating sentiment signals and heavy-tailed noise improves the modeling of volatility and regime shifts in cryptocurrency markets.

</details>


### [315] [Quantifying Portfolio Demutualization: A Benchmark-Relative Pooling--Profiling Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23294)
*Arthur Charpentier,Laurence Barry*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出"基准相对汇集-画像尺度"（benchmark-relative pooling–profiling scale），用耦合L^p坐标与边际Wasserstein坐标刻画保险费率在汇集与差异化之间的定位，并以L^1坐标给出精算解释，最后通过合成与车险数据及重心组平权干预验证其意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险定价同时包含风险汇集（pooling）与风险区分（differentiation）：费率既可能将基准期望损失的差异部分互助化，也可能将其转化为保单层面的保费差异。现有做法缺乏一个统一、可比的尺度来同时度量"保单层面的分配对齐"与"整体保费分布的边际差异化"，因而难以厘清各类精算工具（大类、代理变量、收缩、尾部封顶）究竟改变了哪一维度。

Method: 构建基准相对汇集-画像尺度，包含两个互补坐标：(1) 耦合L^p坐标，度量被评估费率与给定基准纯保费之间的保单层面一致性；(2) 边际Wasserstein坐标，比较两者的风险暴露加权保费分布。以二者残差p-成本的差值定义"分配错配"（allocation mismatch）。在组合平衡（portfolio balance）条件下，耦合L^1坐标具有精确精算解释：等于完全汇集所引致的转移量中被该费率消除的比例。随后在合成数据与车险数据上应用，并实施重心（barycentric）组平权干预。

Result: 合成与车险应用表明：大类（broad classes）、代理变量（proxies）、收缩（shrinkage）与尾部封顶（tail caps）对边际差异化、保单层面分配及转移量的影响各不相同。重心组平权干预显示，条件保费差异的下降主要来自再分配与恢复的基准相对转移，而边际差异化变化很小。

Conclusion: 该尺度能够将汇集与差异化分解为可独立观测与解释的维度，并为费率公平性干预提供诊断工具：保费差距的缩小不一定来自边际差异化降低，而可能主要源于分配与转移的调整。

Abstract: Insurance pricing combines pooling with differentiation: a tariff may leave benchmark differences in expected loss partly mutualized or translate them into policy-level premium differences. We propose a benchmark-relative pooling--profiling scale with two complementary coordinates. The coupled $L^p$ coordinate measures policy-level alignment between an evaluated tariff and a stated benchmark pure premium, whereas the marginal Wasserstein coordinate compares their exposure-weighted premium distributions. The difference between their residual $p$-costs defines an allocation mismatch. Under portfolio balance, the coupled $L^1$ coordinate has an exact actuarial interpretation: it is the fraction of the transfer volume induced by full pooling that the tariff removes. Synthetic and motor-insurance applications show that broad classes, proxies, shrinkage and tail caps can affect marginal differentiation, policy-level allocation and transfers differently. A barycentric group-parity intervention further shows that conditional premium disparities can fall mainly through reallocation and restored benchmark-relative transfers, with little change in marginal differentiation.

</details>


### [316] [Timescales in Time-to-Event Analyses in Pregnancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23862)
*Chase D Latour,Shalini S. Ramachandra,Daniel A. Xu,Elizabeth Diemer,Viktor H. Ahlqvist,Kristin Palmsten,Stefanie Hinkle,Sunni L. Mumford,Enrique Schisterman,Jessie K. Edwards,Ellen C. Caniglia*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 在妊娠期因果推断中，结局定义所用"孕龄"时间尺度与结局测量所用"入组后时间"时间尺度并不一致；本文用目标试验框架证明两者各自对应不同的因果估计量，并给出各自的估计挑战与解决思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在妊娠期开展与因果估计量对齐的生存分析十分困难，因为结局常按"孕龄"定义，而结局实际按"入组后时间"测量。既往研究往往默认其中某一时间尺度"最优"，但缺乏对两者对应因果估计量的清晰界定。作者旨在说明两个时间尺度并无优劣之分，而是各自对应不同的因果估计量，从而为分析设计提供明确依据。

Method: 采用目标试验（target trial）框架，为每个时间尺度定义相应的因果估计量。以一项假想目标试验为例：在孕16–20周随机分配17-α羟孕酮己酸酯（17-OHPC）与安慰剂，以预防37周前分娩（包括流产、死产或活产）。分别推导"入组后时间"尺度与"孕龄"尺度下生存分析的因果估计量，并梳理各自的估计挑战与可能的解决方案。

Result: "入组后时间"尺度下，估计量为孕16–20周随机分配至17-OHPC相对于安慰剂对37周前分娩的影响；"孕龄"尺度下，因果估计量则对应一种反事实情形——即假设所有参与者均在某一特定孕龄（如16周）被随机化时的效应。两种尺度对应不同的因果问题，并各自面临特定的估计困难（如入组时间异质性、左截断/竞争风险等），文中给出了相应的可能解决办法。

Conclusion: 妊娠期生存分析中不存在普适的"最佳"时间尺度；"入组后时间"与"孕龄"分别对应不同且均具临床意义的因果估计量。研究者应依据所关注的因果问题选择时间尺度，并采用与目标试验框架相匹配的估计方法。

Abstract: The target trial framework is increasingly used to define the causal estimand for an observational analysis. However, designing time-to-event analyses that align with the causal estimand is challenging in pregnancy because the timescale on which outcomes are often defined, "gestational age", differs from the timescale on which outcomes are measured, "time since study entry". We argue that neither timescale is "best" but that each aligns with a different estimand. In this article, we use the target trial framework to define the causal estimands corresponding to each timescale. We consider a (hypothetical) target trial comparing randomization to 17-alpha hydroxyprogesterone caproate (17-OHPC) versus placebo between 16-20 weeks of gestation to prevent delivery before 37 weeks' gestation, whether by miscarriage, stillbirth, or live birth. We define the estimand of time-to-event analyses on the "time since study entry" timescale as the effect of randomization to 17-OHPC versus placebo between 16-20 weeks of gestation on delivery before 37 weeks'. In contrast, the causal estimand of time-to-event analyses using the "gestational age" timescale corresponds to the effect had, counter to fact, all participants been randomized at one gestational age (e.g., 16 weeks). We review specific challenges to estimation and possible solutions for each timescale.

</details>


### [317] [Transporting summary measures of relative effects from randomised trials to the treated patient population: an application to breast cancer endocrine therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24305)
*Bonnie E. Shook-Sa,Paul N. Zivich,Carolyn Taylor,David Dodwell,Jake Probert,Sarah C Darby,Paul McGale*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出结合常规照护队列与随机试验汇总效应量来估计目标人群绝对治疗效应（ATT）的方法，在可迁移性假设下证明可识别性并给出插入估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机试验报告的相对效应（风险比、风险比HR）针对试验人群，而临床决策更需目标人群的绝对效应。试验参与者未必代表目标人群，且个体数据获取常受限；常规照护数据具有代表性但存在未控制混杂，因此需要一种融合两类数据来源的估计策略。

Method: 将一份有代表性的、接受治疗的常规照护患者样本，与来自单个随机试验或试验荟萃分析的汇总效应量（风险比或HR）相结合。在边际或条件可迁移性假设下证明平均处理效应（ATT）的可识别性，讨论效应量可压缩性对可迁移性的影响，并给出ATT的插入（plug-in）估计量；通过模拟研究评估有限样本表现。

Result: 在多种设定下通过模拟考察了所提估计量的有限样本性能；并将方法应用于估计内分泌治疗对15年乳腺癌死亡率的ATT，数据来自随机试验荟萃分析结果与英格兰国家疾病登记服务数据。

Conclusion: 所提框架可在仅有汇总相对效应与代表性常规照护数据时识别并估计目标人群的绝对治疗效应ATT，为临床决策提供更贴近目标人群的效应估计。

Abstract: Randomised trials often report relative treatment effects, such as risk ratios and hazard ratios, for trial populations. Clinical decision-making, however, often benefits from estimates of absolute treatment effects in the population eligible for treatment. Trial participants may not represent this target population well, and restrictions on access to individual participant trial data can further complicate absolute effect estimation. Routine care data are often representative of the target population but may be subject to uncontrolled confounding. We consider estimation of the average treatment effect on the treated (ATT), an absolute measure, by combining a representative sample of treated routine care patients with summary measures (i.e., estimated risk or hazard ratios) from either a randomised trial or a meta-analysis of trials. Under marginal or conditional transportability assumptions, the ATT is shown to be identifiable. The implications of collapsibility of the effect measure on transportability are discussed, and plug-in estimators of the ATT are presented. Simulation studies are used to assess finite sample performance of the estimators in a range of settings. The proposed methods are applied to estimate the ATT of endocrine therapy on 15-year breast cancer mortality using results from a meta-analysis of randomised trials and England's National Disease Registration Service.

</details>


### [318] [Overcoming Model Misspecification in Bayesian Inference of Molecular Signalling Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24590)
*Yinghan Li,Heishiro Kanagawa,Francesca Romana Crucinio,Matthew A. Fisher,Chris. J. Oates*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种后贝叶斯（post-Bayesian）方法用于分子信号网络推断，将预测导向（PrO）后验扩展到潜变量模型，以应对模型误设定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯网络推断依赖边际似然可处理性，常用带独立误差和共轭先验的线性模型。但分子信号动力学是非线性的，且相关混杂因素常不可观测；忽略这些复杂性会导致标准贝叶斯框架下过度自信的推断。

Method: 基于"即使统计模型被误设定、即使数据趋于无限，不确定性也不应消失"的原则，作者开发了预测导向（PrO）的后贝叶斯推断方法，将由 McLatchie 等（2025）提出的 PrO 后验扩展到潜变量模型设定，并在具有挑战性的网络推断情境中实证研究其性质。

Result: 论文在潜变量模型下经验性地考察了 PrO 后验在分子信号网络推断中的表现与性质（摘要未报告具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 作者主张在存在非线性与未观测混杂时，应采用不随数据量增大而消失不确定性的后贝叶斯方法，为分子信号网络推断提供更稳健的推断框架。

Abstract: Bayesian inference of molecular signalling networks usually relies on tractability of the marginal likelihood, enabling the set of possible networks to be efficiently explored. As such, linear models with independent errors and conjugate priors are routinely used. However, the dynamics of molecular signalling are nonlinear, and relevant confounders are often unobserved; failure to account for these complexities will almost certainly lead to over-confident inferences in the standard Bayesian framework.
