Method: 重访 Dayan–Ganguly–Weiss 的概率论证，给出简化且自洽的证明，并借助 Hochman–Shmerkin 定理推广至全体进制同时成立；加性情形采用稀疏随机扰动论证（sparse random perturbation argument），将 Rauzy 对固定进制确定性数的刻画推广至绝对正态数；对映射正则性，用局部 $C^2$ 分析证明仿射线性，并构造 $C^{1,1}$ 非仿射微分同胚反例。

Result: 固定整数进制下，非零有理数恰好是乘法保正态数；该结论对所有进制同时成立（即绝对正态意义下亦然）。平移保持绝对正态当且仅当其参数在每个整数进制下都是确定性数。所有局部 $C^2$ 的保正态映射必为仿射线性；正则性不可弱化到 $C^{1,1}$，存在保持每个进制正态性（从而保持绝对正态性）的非仿射 $C^{1,1}$ 微分同胚。

Conclusion: 本文统一并推广了乘法与加性保正态数的特征：乘法情形为“非零有理数”，加性情形为“所有进制下均为确定性数”；同时确定了保正态映射的正则性临界点——$C^2$ 强制仿射线性，而 $C^{1,1}$ 不足以排除非仿射情形，$C^{1,1}$ 与 $C^2$ 之间存在本质正则性间隙。

Abstract: We study arithmetic operations and maps which preserve normality. Revisiting a recent argument of Dayan, Ganguly and Weiss, we give a streamlined, self-contained presentation of the probabilistic proof that the non-zero rationals are exactly the multiplicative normality preserving numbers in a fixed integer base. We also include the extension to all bases simultaneously, using the theorem of Hochman and Shmerkin. In the additive case, we extend Rauzy's characterization of deterministic numbers in a fixed integer base to absolutely normal numbers. Namely, a translation preserves absolute normality if and only if its parameter is deterministic in every integer base. We prove this extension by a sparse random perturbation argument. Finally, we show that all locally $C^2$ normality preserving maps need to be affine-linear. The regularity assumption cannot be weakened to $C^{1,1}$: we construct a non-affine $C^{1,1}$ diffeomorphism which preserves normality in every integer base, and hence absolute normality.

</details>


### [369] [Exceptional maps and abelian points in backward orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24701)
*Zhuchao Ji,Jiarui Song,Junyi Xie*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究极化自同态后向轨道生成的扩域，证明其几乎阿贝尔当且仅当映射为具CM的例外映射且点为预周期点，从而回答并推广了Andrews–Petsche猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答并由一维情形推广Andrews与Petsche提出的猜想：刻画数域上极化自同态后向轨道所生成扩域K(f^{-∞}(α))/K为几乎阿贝尔（virtually abelian）的充要条件。

Method: 设K为数域，f:X→X为正规射影簇上的极化自同态，α为非例外点；发展C上正规射影簇的例外映射理论，并证明度多重独立（multiplicatively independent）的可交换极化自同态必为例外映射。

Result: 证明K(f^{-∞}(α))/K几乎阿贝尔，当且仅当f为具有CM的例外映射且α为f-预周期点；同时给出多重独立度可交换极化自同态均为例外映射的结论。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了后向轨道生成扩域的伽罗瓦性质，建立高维例外映射理论并解决Andrews–Petsche猜想的高维推广。

Abstract: Consider a number field $K$, a polarized endomorphism $f:X\to X$ on a normal projective variety, and a non-exceptional point $α$ for $f$. We show that the extension $K(f^{-\infty}(α))/K$ generated by the backward orbit of $α$ is virtually abelian if and only if $f$ is an exceptional map with CM and $α$ is $f$-preperiodic. This answers a conjecture of Andrews and Petsche and extends it to higher dimensions. To this end, we develop the theory of exceptional maps on normal projective varieties over $\mathbb{C}$ and prove that commuting polarized endomorphisms of multiplicatively independent degrees are exceptional.

</details>


### [370] [A polynomial basis for the multizeta algebra in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24773)
*Li Lai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明 Thakur 多重 zeta 值生成的 $K$-代数 $\mathcal{Z}$ 是多项式代数，给出显式多项式基，并确定有限权重截断的超越次数，推广了 Ngo Dac–Nguyen Chu–Pham 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thakur 多重 zeta 值代数 $\mathcal{Z}$ 的结构（是否为多项式代数、其基与超越次数）是函数域上多重 zeta 值理论的核心问题。已有结果仅覆盖有限权重截断的部分情形（Ngo Dac–Nguyen Chu–Pham），缺少对整体代数结构及任意权重超越次数的统一刻画，本文旨在补全这一空白。

Method: 设 $K=\mathbb{F}_q(\theta)$，$\mathcal{Z}$ 为由 Thakur 多重 zeta 值 $\zeta_A(\mathfrak{s})$ 生成的 $K$-代数。证明综合使用三项工具：Chang 的分次定理、由 Chang–Chen–Mishiba 与 Im–Kim–Le–Ngo Dac–Pham 独立证明的线性基定理，以及 Im–Kim–Ngo Dac 的进位关系。关键步骤是构造由去拼接（deconcatenation）与线性泛函得到的适当导子，并证明这些导子可穿过进位关系下降到 $\mathcal{Z}$ 上。

Result: 证明 $\mathcal{Z}$ 是多项式代数，并显式构造其在 $K$ 上的多项式基；对每个整数 $w\geqslant 1$，确定 $K[\zeta_A(\mathfrak{s}):\operatorname{wt}(\mathfrak{s})\leqslant w]$ 在 $K$ 上的超越次数，从而推广 Ngo Dac–Nguyen Chu–Pham 的结论。

Conclusion: Thakur 多重 zeta 值代数 $\mathcal{Z}$ 具有多项式代数结构且基可显式给出，其有限权重截断的超越次数可被完全确定；去拼接导子经进位关系下降的技术为研究该代数提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Let $K=\mathbb{F}_q(θ)$, and let $\mathcal{Z}$ be the $K$-algebra generated by Thakur's multiple zeta values $ζ_A(\mathfrak{s})$. We prove that $\mathcal{Z}$ is a polynomial algebra and construct an explicit polynomial basis of $\mathcal{Z}$ over $K$. We also determine the transcendence degree of $K[ζ_A(\mathfrak{s}): \operatorname{wt}(\mathfrak{s}) \leqslant w]$ over $K$ for every integer $w \geqslant 1$, generalizing a result of Ngo Dac--Nguyen Chu--Pham.
  The proof uses Chang's grading theorem, the linear basis theorem proved independently by Chang--Chen--Mishiba and Im--Kim--Le--Ngo Dac--Pham, and the carry relations of Im--Kim--Ngo Dac. The key step is to show that suitable derivations obtained from deconcatenation and linear functionals descend through the carry relations to $\mathcal{Z}$.

</details>


### [371] [Abelian dynamical Galois groups over global function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24920)
*Andrea Ferraguti,Patrick Ingram,Carlo Pagano*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立 Andrews–Petsche 猜想在函数域上的类比，证明当多项式次数小于域特征 p 时，阿贝尔动力 Galois 群恰为等平凡（isotrivial）群；并给出四个独立步骤的证明链条，指出该结论在次数达到 p 时失效（出现来自 Drinfeld 模的新反例），进而提出全次数的猜想性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 Andrews–Petsche 提出了关于动力 Galois 群的猜想。本文旨在函数域情形下给出该猜想的类比与推进，刻画阿贝尔动力 Galois 群何时等价于等平凡群，并解释次数门槛 p 的关键作用。

Method: 证明通过四个相互独立的蕴含步骤完成：Abelian ⟹ Finite ramification ⟹ PCF map ⟹ Isotrivial map ⟹ Isotrivial pair；该论证对带有超吸引不动点的映射更一般地成立。同时对次数进行临界性分析，考察次数达到 p 时由 Drinfeld 模产生的非等平凡构造。

Result: 当 deg f < p 时，阿贝尔动力 Galois 群当且仅当对应映射为等平凡时成立；次数达到 p 后出现新的非等平凡例子，源自 Drinfeld 模及其相关 Lattès 映射。

Conclusion: 该蕴含链在次数意义上是尖锐的（sharp in the degree）。作者据此提出一个包含 Drinfeld 模及 Lattès 映射等新型例子在内的全次数猜想性分类。

Abstract: We establish a function field analogue of a recent conjecture of Andrews--Petsche. Our main result characterizes abelian dynamical Galois groups to be precisely the isotrivial ones, whenever the degree of the polynomial is smaller than $p$, the characteristic of the field.
  The proof of this characterization is achieved in four independent steps as follows: $$\text{Abelian} \implies \text{Finite ramification} \implies \text{PCF map} \implies \text{Isotrivial map} \implies \text{Isotrivial pair}, $$ and works more generally for maps with a superattracting fixed point.
  We observe that this chain of implications is sharp in the degree: as soon as one reaches $p$ there are new non-isotrivial examples coming from Drinfeld modules. We propose a full conjectural classification in all degrees, taking into account all of the new exotic examples coming from Drinfeld modules and their associated Lattès maps.

</details>


### [372] [$\Kab$ has the Bogomolov property for canonical heights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24938)
*Andrea Ferraguti,Carlo Pagano*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了：对任意数域 $K$ 及任意非共轭于幂映射、(带符号)Chebyshev 映射或 Lattès 映射的有理映射 $f\in K(x)$，$K^{\mathrm{ab}}$ 关于 $f$ 的典范高度具有强 Bogomolov 性质；并分类了后向轨道含无穷多 abelian 点的对 $(f,\alpha)$，从而在数域上的有理映射及后向轨道的无穷 abelian 子集情形下解决了 Andrews–Petsche 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Andrews–Petsche 猜想在数域上有理映射及后向轨道无穷 abelian 子集的情形，并建立 $K^{\mathrm{ab}}$ 对典范高度的强 Bogomolov 性质。

Method: 证明核心分三步：先应用等分布结果 \cite{Yua08}；其次利用 $(f,f)$-预周期曲线的分类 \cite{Pak23,Pak20,Bea25}；最后在参数化的预周期曲线上再次应用等分布。主要思路在与 Astra 的讨论中形成。

Result: 对任意数域 $K$ 与任意非共轭于幂、Chebyshev 或 Lattès 映射的有理映射 $f\in K(x)$，$K^{\mathrm{ab}}$ 关于 $f$ 的典范高度具有强 Bogomolov 性质；同时完成了后向轨道含无穷多 abelian 点的 $(f,\alpha)$ 对的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Andrews–Petsche 猜想推广并证实于数域上的有理映射以及后向轨道的无穷 abelian 子集情形。

Abstract: We show that for any number field $K$ and any rational map $f$ in $K(x)$ that is not conjugate to a power, (signed) Chebyshev or Lattès map, then $K^{\mathrm{ab}}$ has the strong Bogomolov property for the canonical height of $f$. We also classify the pairs $(f,α)$ whose backward orbit contains infinitely many abelian points. This settles the Andrews--Petsche conjecture to rational maps over a number field and to infinite abelian subsets of backward orbits.
  The authors were led to the main idea of the proof in conversation with \emph{Astra}. The key insight consists of applying the equidistribution results \cite{Yua08}, followed by a classification of $(f,f)$-preperiodic curves \cite{Pak23,Pak20, Bea25} followed by an additional application of equidistribution on a parametrized preperiodic curve.

</details>

### [373] [Geometrically Thin Sub-Eddington AGN Accretion Disks show a suppressed Lyman Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22421)
*Ish Kaul,Yan-Fei Jiang,Omer Blaes,Lizhong Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 磁压主导的低密度吸积盘可自然抹平AGN紫外连续谱中理论预期的HI莱曼跃变，为长期存在的光薄/光厚矛盾提供了一个可能解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典光学厚吸积盘大气模型预言AGN紫外连续谱在13.6 eV处存在强内禀HI莱曼跃变，但观测普遍缺乏该特征，这一长期矛盾亟待解释。

Method: 基于此前对10^8 M_sun黑洞、亚爱丁顿（L/L_Edd~0.03）几何薄3D吸积盘的灰近似、角度依赖辐射磁流体力学模拟，重启计算并引入多频率组，直接由频率依赖辐射通量计算逃逸连续谱。

Result: 该盘由磁压主导，表面密度比对应的辐射压支撑α盘预言低两个数量级以上；出射连续谱在13.6 eV处无锐利HI莱曼跃变，主因是低密度使莱曼不透明度跳变远弱于标准盘模型。

Conclusion: 磁压支撑可能是调和光学厚AGN盘与莱曼跃变附近平滑观测连续谱的可行途径；但13.6 eV附近相当部分辐射预期来自模拟域之外更靠近黑洞的内区，需扩展至内盘、引入非LTE辐射转移、提高频率分辨率并覆盖更广吸积率范围方可给出更精细光谱预言。

Abstract: The observed ultraviolet continua of active galactic nuclei (AGN) generally lack a strong intrinsic HI Lyman edge predicted by classical optically thick accretion disk atmosphere models. We revisit this long-standing problem using our previous sub-Eddington ($L/L_{\rm Edd}\sim 0.03$) geometrically thin 3D accretion disk simulation evolved with gray angle-dependent radiation magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) around a $10^8M_{\odot}$ black hole. This disk is magnetic pressure dominated, and has surface densities more than two orders of magnitude below the corresponding radiation-pressure-supported $α$-disk prediction. By restarting the simulation with multiple frequency groups, we compute the emergent continuum directly from the frequency-dependent radiation fluxes and find no sharp HI Lyman edge at 13.6 eV. This suppressed Lyman edge is mainly caused by the low densities in a magnetic pressure supported disk where the Lyman opacity jump is far weaker than in standard disk models. These results indicate that magnetic pressure support may provide a possible solution to reconciling optically thick AGN disks with the smooth observed continuum emission around the Lyman edge. However, in this simulation, a substantial fraction of the emission near 13.6 eV is expected to originate at smaller radii closer to the central black hole, which is outside our simulation domain. More detailed predictions of the spectrum will require an extension to the inner disk, non-LTE radiation transfer, higher frequency resolution and a survey across a broader range of accretion rates.

</details>


### [374] [GRB 220706A: a gamma-ray burst with a month-long engine and a luminous supernova](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22426)
*Benjamin P. Gompertz,Nusrin Habeeb,Dheeraj R. Pasham,Antonio de Ugarte Postigo,Daniele B. Malesani,Phil A. Evans,Kim L. Page,Ben Rayson,J. Feliciano Agui Fernandez,Michael Bremer,Andrew J. Levan,Genevieve Schroeder,Nial R. Tanvir,Christina C. Thone,Eric Burns,Valerio D'Elia,Massimiliano De Pasquale,Dimple,Dieter Hartmann,Pall Jakobsson,Sylvio Klose,Antonio Martin-Carrillo,Matt Nicholl,Ana M. Nicuesa Guelbenzu,David O'Neill,Giovanna Pugliese,Arne Rau,Andrea Rossi,Jesper Sollerman,Rhaana Starling,Darach Watson,Isabelle Worssam,Makenzie E. Wortley*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了GRB 220706A的多波段观测，其X射线耀发持续至触发后约51天（静止系27天），刷新了伽马暴中心引擎活动时长纪录，并发现一颗可能的超亮超新星伴随事件，对超长伽马暴的起源模型提出新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长伽马暴的前身星通常被认为是大质量、包层剥离、快速自转恒星的核心坍缩，但时长分布极端的一端（数千秒以上的"超长"伽马暴）难以用典型Wolf-Rayet星的引擎时标解释。本文旨在通过GRB 220706A的观测，检验超长伽马暴是否为独立族群，并探讨其前身星模型与超长时标中心引擎活动的供能机制。

Method: 对GRB 220706A开展X射线后续观测，探测其耀发活动并测量持续时间；结合红移z=0.8577将观测时标换算至静止系；识别并分析可能的伴随超新星，通过间接论证约束光学消光范围（0.9 ≤ A_V ≤ 3.6 mag），估算其峰值绝对星等，并与已知超亮超新星（如SN 2011kl）进行对比。

Result: X射线耀发持续至触发后约51天，在z=0.8577对应静止系27天，是迄今观测到的伽马暴中心引擎活动最晚案例，比此前纪录延后约21个静止系天。识别出可能的伴随超新星，在考虑消光后其峰值绝对星等M_r ≤ -20.25，与伴随超长伽马暴111209A的高能超新星SN 2011kl相似，符合超亮超新星特征。

Conclusion: GRB 220706A的极晚中心引擎活动与超亮超新星伴随特征，对超长伽马暴前身星模型构成挑战，暗示其可能源自与标准长伽马暴不同的前身星或供能机制（如磁星、持续吸积等）。该事件为理解超长伽马暴族群及其极端时标中心引擎活动提供了关键观测约束。

Abstract: While the progenitors of many long gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) are well established as the core collapse of very massive, envelope-stripped, rapidly-rotating stars, it has been suggested that bursts at the extremely long end of the duration distribution may be a separate population of `ultra-long' GRBs. With durations of thousands of seconds or more, these bursts are difficult to reconcile with the engine timescales expected from the compact Wolf-Rayet stars that are typically assumed to produce more `standard' long GRBs. Here, we present observations of GRB~220706A, where X-ray follow-up reveals flaring episodes that last until $\approx 51$ days after trigger. At the measured redshift of $z = 0.8577$, this corresponds to 27 days in the GRB rest frame, and represents the latest central engine activity ever observed in a GRB by a margin of $\approx 21$ rest-frame days. We also identify a likely supernova (SN) which, when accounting for the inferred optical extinction of $0.9 \leq A_V \leq 3.6$ mag (constrained by indirect arguments), has a peak absolute magnitude of $M_r \leq -20.25$, resembling the energetic SN\,2011kl found accompanying ultra-long GRB\,111209A, and consistent with super-luminous SNe. We discuss the implications GRB~220706A has for ultra-long GRB progenitor models and possible powering mechanisms for the extremely late central engine activity.

</details>


### [375] [Importance of the Resonant Cosmic-Ray Streaming Instability Upstream of Collisionless Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22480)
*Bricker Ostler,Benedikt Schroer,Damiano Caprioli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文通过求解冷束和有限展宽宇宙线（CR）分布下的色散关系，证明对于最高能量的逃逸CR群体，共振模式可以超过非共振（Bell）模式；慢激波下共振主导更显著，足够的俯仰角展宽甚至可稳定非共振模式。相对论混合PIC模拟验证了冷束线性理论，并给出非线性饱和水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逃逸无碰撞激波的宇宙线通过电流放大上游磁场，这一过程通常被归因于非共振（Bell）不稳定性。然而，若忽略共振不稳定性，可能低估约束最高能量逃逸CR所需尺度上的磁场增长，因此需要重新评估共振与非共振模式的相对重要性。

Method: 求解冷束和有限速度展宽CR分布下的色散关系，比较共振模式与非共振模式的增长率；并使用相对论混合粒子-网格（hybrid PIC）模拟验证线性理论并研究非线性演化。

Result: 对于最高能量逃逸CR群体，共振模式可超过非共振模式；在较慢激波下，这种主导对更宽的CR分布依然成立；足够强的俯仰角展宽可稳定非共振模式而共振模式仍保持不稳定。相对论混合PIC模拟证实了冷束线性理论预测，并在非线性阶段饱和于δB/B0 ~ 1，伴随显著的俯仰角再分布。

Conclusion: 共振不稳定性在磁场放大中不可忽略；忽略它会低估约束最高能量逃逸CR所需尺度上的磁场增长。

Abstract: Cosmic rays (CRs) escaping collisionless shocks form a dilute, relativistic population whose current amplifies the upstream magnetic field, a process widely attributed to the non-resonant (Bell) instability. Solving the dispersion relation for both cold and finite-spread CR distributions, we show that for the maximum-energy escaping population, the resonant mode can outgrow the non-resonant mode. At slower shocks, this dominance persists for broader CR distributions, and sufficient pitch-angle broadening can even stabilize the non-resonant mode while leaving the resonant mode unstable. Relativistic hybrid particle-in-cell simulations confirm the cold-beam linear theory predictions and, in the nonlinear regime, saturate at $δB/B_0 \sim 1$ with significant pitch-angle redistribution. Neglecting the resonant instability thus underestimates magnetic field growth at the very scale needed to confine the highest-energy escaping CRs.

</details>


### [376] [The chirping of Lense-Thirring precession in tidal disruption event accretion flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22559)
*Andrew Mummery,Eliot Quataert*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 潮汐撕裂事件（TDE）吸积盘因Lense-Thirring进动产生的X射线周期性调制会被"剧烈啁啾"（chirping）效应抹除，这源于厚盘（H/R~O(1)）刚性进动所必然伴随的快速周期变化，对TDE计时特征及薄盘相变初期性质的理解具有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDE的X射线光变曲线并未显示清晰的周期性调制，这与理论预期存在矛盾：被撕裂恒星起源于星系大尺度，无法感知黑洞自旋轴方向，本应形成错位吸积盘，并通过Lense-Thirring力矩进动，从而调制内盘X射线辐射。作者旨在解释这一观测与理论的表观不一致。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了实现刚性体进动（solid-body precession）所需的条件——即厚盘（H/R~O(1)）。此类厚盘在向外扩展以守恒角动量时，进动周期会发生快速变化，每次进动周期的相对增幅可达ΔT_prec/T_prec~O(1-10)，即进动被"剧烈啁啾"。

Result: 厚TDE盘的进动被强烈啁啾，除极窄参数空间外，任何观测到多个进动周期的可能性都被抹除。全局盘对齐时间尺度（global disk alignment timescale）受同一机制同样强烈啁啾，导致TDE盘在超Eddington阶段通常不会与黑洞自旋轴对齐，且在薄盘相初期普遍表现出全局准稳态翘曲（warp）轮廓。

Conclusion: 该结果对诠释TDE盘相关计时特征具有重要含义，包括准周期X射线爆发（QPE）计时现象学模型，以及TDE盘向薄盘相初始转变的观测性质。

Abstract: Tidal disruption event X-ray light curves do not appear to show clear signs of periodic modulation. This is naively somewhat surprising since the stars that are disrupted originate at large scales in the galaxy where they cannot know about the orientation of the black hole spin axis. This should lead to the formation of a misaligned disk that precesses due to Lense-Thirring torques, modulating X-ray emission from the inner disk regions. We argue that in fact the properties which are required for solid-body precession, namely a thick $(H/R\sim {\mathcal O}(1))$ disk, naturally lead to rapid precession period change, with a per-period increase of $ΔT_{\rm prec}/T_{\rm prec} \sim {\mathcal O}(1-10)$, as the disk spreads to larger radii to conserve angular momentum. In other words the precession of thick TDE disks is aggressively chirped, washing out any possibility of observing multiple cycles other than for fine tuned regions of parameter space. The global disk alignment timescale is equally strongly chirped by the exact same mechanism, meaning that TDE disks will not in general align with the black hole spin axis during a super-Eddington phase, and should generically show global quasi-steady warp profiles at the beginning of any thin disk phase. These results have important implications for interpreting timing features associated with TDE disks, including models for quasi-periodic X-ray eruption timing phenomenology, and the observational properties of TDE disks in the initial transition to the thin disk phase.

</details>


### [377] [Resolving the Galactic Center Region's Gamma-Ray Emission and Searching for its Neutrino Counterpart with HAWC and IceCube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22577)
*S. Yun-Carcamo,R. Babu,for the HAWC,IceCube collaborations*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用HAWC约2860天银河系中心伽马射线数据构建含点源与银盘弥散的多成分模型，并据此推测强子起源中微子模板，在12年IceCube缪子径迹数据中搜索，未发现显著中微子超出，给出首个关联该多成分模型的高能中微子通量上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中心是拍电子伏宇宙线加速的重要候选天体，HAWC已观测到能量超过100 TeV的伽马射线；需通过中微子观测检验其强子起源（光学薄强子场景），并给出对多成分空间模型的中微子约束。

Method: 分析约2860天HAWC数据，拟合包含点源HAWC J1745-2857与银河弥散成分的伽马射线发射模型；在假定强子起源下构建对应中微子信号模板，并在12年IceCube缪子径迹数据中进行搜索。

Result: 未发现显著中微子超出；给出首个基于HAWC数据银河系中心多成分空间模型的高能中微子通量上限，该上限约为强子解释下HAWC伽马射线模型预期标称中微子通量的12倍。

Conclusion: 当前中微子通量上限尚不足以检验光学薄强子场景，即未能对强子起源给出有效约束，需要更高灵敏度或更长曝光的中微子观测。

Abstract: The Galactic Center (GC) is a prime candidate for petaelectronvolt cosmic-ray acceleration, with gamma-ray emission observed by the High Altitude Water Cherenkov (HAWC) Observatory extending beyond 100 TeV. We present an analysis of the GC using ~2860 days of HAWC data, with increased exposure and improved sensitivity relative to previous results. The best-fit gamma-ray emission model includes a point source (HAWC J1745-2857) and a Galactic diffuse emission component. Assuming a hadronic origin, we construct the corresponding neutrino signal template and search for it in 12 years of IceCube muon-track data. No significant neutrino excess is found, and we report the first upper limit on the high-energy neutrino flux associated with the multi-component spatial model of the GC derived from HAWC data. The limit is approximately 12 times the nominal neutrino flux predicted from the HAWC gamma-ray model under a hadronic interpretation, and therefore it does not yet test the optically thin hadronic scenario.

</details>


### [378] [Stereoscopic gravitational-wave probe: Gravitational lensing of a binary merger in active galactic nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22660)
*Paul Martens,Samson H. W. Leong,Ryan Zhang,Otto A. Hannuksela*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 若大量双星并合发生在活动星系核（AGN）吸积盘内，AGN-CBC系统可产生引力透镜化的多"像"；本文揭示其独特的"多视角透镜"与多普勒效应，并建立波形变换框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGN吸积盘中发生的致密双星并合（CBC）系统因源绕中心超大质量黑洞近距离轨道运动，其透镜"像"的波形与常规点质量透镜系统不同，需要专门的理论框架来描述并生成一致的透镜化波形。

Method: 建立一套变换规则框架，统一描述两个观测视角的视角变换（有效视角差异），以及源绕透镜轨道运动引起的相对论性多普勒频移（因靠近透镜而被放大）；并给出从任意现有GW波形生成一致的AGN透镜化波形的方法。参数化上，若将AGN角动量与双星轨道角动量关联，则仅比标准点质量透镜系统多一个自由参数，否则多三个。

Result: 证明此类系统可对同一天体源产生来自两个不同视角的2个"快照"，视角差被编码于引力波中，使探测器能从两个视角分析波形；同时存在多普勒频移差异。

Conclusion: AGN-CBC系统提供对双星并合的两个独特观测视角，为研究并合性质开辟了其他透镜系统所不具备的新窗口，所建立的框架具有通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that, if a large fraction of the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA compact binary coalescences (CBCs) occur in accretion disks around active galactic nuclei (AGNs), many such AGN-CBC systems will likely experience lensing and produce multiple gravitational-wave (GW) "images" of the CBCs that will become detectable with current or future GW detectors. However, the waveforms produced by such lensed "images" will differ from those produced by other lensing systems because the binary source is orbiting the AGN's central supermassive black hole at a close distance. Here, we show that these systems produce 2 snapshots of the same binary source from 2 different viewing angles. These differences in the effective viewing angle between the images are encoded in the GWs they emit, such that GW detectors can effectively analyse the waveforms from 2 viewpoints. Beyond such "multi-view lensing", the GW images may also differ by their Doppler shift due to the expected relativistic orbital motion of the source around the AGN, an effect which is further amplified by the proximity to the lens. If the AGN's own angular momentum can be related to the binary's orbital angular momentum, we can describe the entire AGN-CBC system using only one additional free parameter compared with a typical point-mass lens system, or with 3 additional parameters when we do not make such an assumption. In this work, we establish a framework of transformation rules that describe both the angles in the 2 points of view, and the Doppler effect that comes from the source's motion around the lens. We also provide the framework to produce consistent AGN-lensed waveforms from any currently available GW waveform. These AGN-CBC systems give 2 unique points of view on a binary merger thus opening up a new window into the merger's properties unlike any other lens system.

</details>


### [379] [Dark matter self-annihilation as a hidden energy source in white dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22873)
*Alvin Cheuk-Nam Chu,Cheuk-Man Yiu,Ching-Hui Lam,Peter Siu-Hei Cheung,Fong-Ching Ho,Ming-chung Chu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出白矮星可作为暗物质探测窗口，通过双流体模型证明少量自湮灭暗物质掺入即可解释观测到的白矮星质径关系偏差及额外光度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白矮星结构理论成熟，但理论与观测的质径关系存在显著差异，尤其热Q-branch白矮星存在无法解释的加热源。作者希望利用这一矛盾，探索白矮星作为暗物质物理探针的可能性。

Method: 在自洽求解双流体静力学平衡方程与热扩散方程的基础上，研究掺入自湮灭暗物质（SADM）对白矮星的影响；推导光度L和有效温度Teff关于暗物质质量分数f_d、湮灭截面⟨σv⟩、粒子质量m_χ及总质量M_t的经验公式，并对费米子与玻色子两种统计下的质径关系与观测数据进行比较。

Result: 观测到的白矮星质径关系偏差大部分可由掺入质量分数f_d ≤ 10⁻⁵、湮灭截面⟨σv⟩远小于热遗迹截面3×10⁻²⁶ cm³ s⁻¹的自湮灭暗物质解释。

Conclusion: 白矮星可作为灵敏的暗物质探测器，暗物质自湮灭可为白矮星提供额外加热，从而解释其观测到的额外光度。

Abstract: White dwarfs (WDs) are compact remnants of stars, supported by electron degeneracy pressure, and their structures are generally considered to be well understood. Significant discrepancies exist between the theoretical and observed white dwarf mass-radius (MR) relations, especially for hot Q-branch white dwarfs with unexplained heating sources. In this study, we explore the potential of WDs as a portal to dark matter (DM) physics by investigating the effect of self-annihilating DM (SADM) admixed in WDs, solving the set of two-fluid hydrostatic equilibrium and heat diffusion equations self-consistently. We obtain empirical formulae for the WD luminosity $L$ and effective temperature $T_\text{eff}$ as functions of the DM mass fraction $f_d$, annihilation cross section $\langleσv\rangle$, and particle mass $m_χ$, as well as the WD's total mass $M_t$. We also compare the WD MR relations for various $L$, $f_d$, and both Fermionic and Bosonic DM particle statistics, with the observational data. We show that the majority of observed deviations in the WD MR relation can be accounted for by having SADM admixed with $f_d \le 10^{-5}$ and $\langleσv\rangle \ll 3\times10^{-26}$ $\text{cm}^3$ $\text{s}^{-1}$, the thermal relic cross section. Therefore, our results suggest that WDs can be sensitive DM detectors, and DM self-annihilation could provide additional heating in WDs, accounting for their observed extra luminosities.

</details>


### [380] [A dynamic magneto-ionic environment around a long-period radio transient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23259)
*Botao Li,Yongjie Jin,Shike Qu,Chaohua Gao,Wei Wang,Pei Wang,Shumei Jia,Jifeng Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对421秒长周期射电暂现源CHIME J0630+25进行全斯托克斯观测，首次发现其RM在约-400至-1630 rad m^-2间剧烈变化，且变化时间尺度极短，表明源附近存在紧凑、结构化、快速演化的磁离子环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期射电暂现源（LPTs）是近年新发现的一类相干射电源，周期长达数百至数千秒，但其物理本质仍不清楚。法拉第旋转是探测磁化等离子体的直接手段，RM随时间的变化可追踪介质密度、磁场强度或几何结构的变化，因此对LPT开展RM监测有助于揭示其物理起源。

Method: 对421秒周期的LPT源CHIME J0630+25开展全斯托克斯（full-Stokes）偏振观测，测量其旋转测度（RM）随时间的演化，并将其DM-RM分布与其他射电源（星际介质、脉冲星等）进行对比分析。

Result: 首次观测到极端RM变化，范围约从-400到-1630 rad m^-2。RM变化迅速：两个相邻旋转周期之间相差约50 rad m^-2，约2500秒内突变量约536 rad m^-2。该源DM仅约22 pc cm^-3，但|RM|超过1.6×10^3 rad m^-2，远超星际介质和脉冲星的预期，在DM-RM平面上占据异常区域。

Conclusion: 快速且大幅度的RM变化应源于源附近紧凑、结构化且快速演化的磁离子环境，可能与紧凑双星系统有关。该结果揭示了LPT源CHIME J0630+25具有独特的局域磁化环境，为理解LPT物理本质提供了关键线索。

Abstract: Faraday rotation provides one of the most direct probes of magnetized plasma along the line of sight, from the large-scale Galactic magnetic field to compact plasma environments local to radio sources. In particular, temporal variations in rotation measure can trace changes in the density, magnetic-field strength, or geometry of the Faraday-rotating medium, as observed in radio pulsars and fast radio bursts. Long-period radio transients (LPTs), a recently identified class of coherent radio sources with periods of hundreds to thousands of seconds, remain poorly explored because their physical nature is still uncertain. Here we present full-Stokes observations of the 421-s LPT source CHIME J0630+25 and report, for the first time, extreme rotation measure (RM) variability from approximately -400 to -1630 rad m^-2. The rapid RM changes occur on short timescales, including a difference of approximately 50 rad m^-2 between two consecutive rotation periods and a sudden change of approximately 536 rad m^-2 within approximately 2500 s. Compared with other radio-emitting sources, CHIME J0630+25 occupies an unusual region of the DM-RM plane: despite its very small DM of approximately 22 pc cm^-3, |RM| reaches over 1.6 x 10^3 rad m^-2, far exceeding that expected from the interstellar medium and pulsars. The fast and large-amplitude RM variations should arise from a compact, structured, and rapidly evolving magneto-ionic environment local to the source, possibly associated with a compact binary system.

</details>


### [381] [Observational Evidence of an Internal Shock Collision in the Relativistic Jet of the Nearby Radio Galaxy 3C 111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23410)
*Kenzo Kawamura,Motoki Kino,Mareki Honma,Kazuhiro Hada,Paul Tiede,Evgeniya Kravchenko,Marcello Giroletti,Rocco Lico,Kazunori Akiyama,Mieko Takamura,Filippo D'Ammando,Monica Orienti,Gabriele Giovannini,Fumie Tazaki,Bong Won Sohn,Salvatore Buttaccio,Lang Cui,Giuseppe Maccaferri,Andrea Melis,Alexey Melnikov,Carlo Migoni,Matteo Stagni,Kiyoaki Wajima,Takeshi Sakai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对近邻射电星系3C 111开展多频高分辨VLBI观测，发现喷流中后发节点追上先发节点并发生内激波，为AGN喷流内激波提供直接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核喷流的辐射变化机制尚不明确，内激波与驻激波在相对论喷流中的作用及相互关系缺乏直接观测证据。

Method: 利用2020—2023年EATING VLBI的10个22 GHz观测历元，结合VLBA 15与43 GHz数据，构建64历元多频高分辨图像序列；采用高分辨贝叶斯Stokes I成像与高斯成分建模，提取光变曲线与节点运动学，并结合档案偏振图像分析。

Result: 发现一个后发射的较快节点在距中心引擎约10 pc的去投影距离处追上较早发射的较慢节点；该相互作用节点以相对论速度传播，呈现约6个月的上升-衰减演化；Stokes I流量演化支持内激波解释，相互作用区域在流量极大附近出现偏振度暂时升高；2021年GeV耀发在时序上更可能关联核区附近活动。

Conclusion: 结果提供了AGN喷流中内激波的直接证据，支持内激波与驻激波在相对论喷流中共存并在变化性中扮演不同角色的混合图景。

Abstract: We report 10 epochs of 22 GHz East Asia To Italy: Nearly Global VLBI (EATING VLBI) observations of the nearby radio galaxy 3C~111 obtained from 2020 to 2023. Combined with VLBA data at 15 and 43 GHz, these observations yield a 64-epoch, multi-frequency sequence of high-resolution radio images. We used high-resolution Bayesian Stokes~I imaging and Gaussian component modeling to derive light curves and knot kinematics. We find evidence that a faster, later-emitted knot catches up with a slower, earlier knot at a deprojected distance of about 10 pc from the central engine. The knot component responsible for this interaction propagated at relativistic speed and exhibited a rise-and-decay evolution over approximately six months. The Stokes~I flux evolution supports the internal shock interpretation, while archival polarization images reveal a temporary increase in fractional linear polarization in the interaction region near the Stokes~I flux maximum. The timing of the 2021 GeV flare suggests that this earlier high-energy event is more naturally associated with activity near the core. These results provide direct evidence for an internal shock in an active galactic nucleus jet and support a hybrid picture in which internal and standing shocks coexist in relativistic jets and may play different roles in their variability.

</details>


### [382] [The role of photon fields of local sources in the formation of the cosmic rays energy spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23480)
*S. E. Pyatovsky,S. K. Machavariani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于局部源（LS）光子场的宇宙线（CR）能谱形成模型，认为CR能谱是初级宇宙辐射穿过局部源光子场时形成的各分谱之和，并指出E0≈100 PeV处的"bump"由Mira型变星光子场形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察局部源光子场对宇宙线能谱（E0）形成的影响，并解释CR能谱中不规则结构（如~100 PeV处的"bump"）的成因。

Method: 提出基于局部源模型的CR能谱不规则性形成模型：将CR能谱视为初级宇宙辐射（PCR）在穿过不同局部CR源（各类最活跃变星，从矮星到超巨星）光子场过程中形成的分谱之和，并对Mira型变星（红巨星+白矮星双星系统）的光子场进行具体分析。

Result: 研究表明，E0≈100 PeV附近的"bump"是在Mira型变星（通常为红巨星与白矮星组成的多重恒星系统）的光子场中形成的。

Conclusion: CR能谱可由局部源光子场中形成的分谱叠加构成，局部源（从矮星到超巨星的活跃变星）在其中起关键作用，Mira型变星的光子场可解释~100 PeV处的能谱凸起。

Abstract: The influence of photon fields of local sources (LS) on the formation of the cosmic ray (CR) energy spectrum (E0) is considered. A model for the formation of irregularities in the CR spectrum, based on the local source model, is proposed. It is shown that the CR spectrum can be formed as a sum of partial spectra formed near local CR sources during the passage of primary cosmic radiation (PCR) through the photon fields of the sources. Local sources can be the most active stars of various types of variability in the stellar population, - from dwarfs to hypergiants. Studies have shown that the so-called "bump" near E0 = 100 PeV is formed in the photon fields of Mira-type variable stars, which are typically multiple stellar systems, consisting of a red giant and a white dwarf.

</details>


### [383] [Spectral Analysis of the Egress and Ingress Phases of Cen X-3 with XRISM/Resolve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23748)
*J. Planelles-Villalva,L. Gu,J. J. Rodes-Roca,G. Sanjurjo-Ferrín,J. de Plaa,J. M. Torrejón,F. S. Porter,Y. Mochizuki,M. Loewenstein,E. Costantini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用XRISM/Resolve对Cen X-3食入食出过程进行相位分辨X射线光谱分析，揭示局部稠密吸收结构与Fe K发射区的复杂特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在约束Cen X-3中的吸收物质与Fe K发射区，借助高分辨率光谱探测食双星吸积环境的几何与运动学特征。

Method: 用SPEX建模光谱，包含星际与本地星风吸收、幂律与黑体连续谱、盘反射及Fe XXV/Fe XXVI高斯成分；将吸收柱密度的轨道变化与光滑CAK星风模型对比，并分析Fe K alpha线宽变化。

Result: 本地吸收柱密度变化近两个数量级，从食入前4.25×10^22 cm^-2增至食入时5.9×10^23 cm^-2，超出光滑星风模型预期，暗示视线方向存在局部稠密结构。Fe K alpha线宽从食入前约510 km/s变至食出时约1400 km/s，食入与食出后约900 km/s。整个轨道均探测到高度电离Fe XXV与Fe XXVI发射，但食入期分辨成分较少。

Conclusion: 结果表明Cen X-3周围存在含局部稠密物质与多个再处理区的复杂星周环境，验证了XRISM/Resolve探测食双星吸积环境几何与运动学的能力。

Abstract: We investigate the phase-resolved X-ray spectral evolution of the high-mass X-ray binary Cen X-3 across eclipse ingress and egress, using two out-of-eclipse intervals as references, to constrain the absorbing material and Fe K emitting regions with high-resolution XRISM/Resolve spectroscopy. We model the spectra with SPEX, including interstellar and local wind absorption, a power-law and blackbody continuum, disk reflection, and Gaussian components for Fe XXV and Fe XXVI. We compare the orbital variation of the absorbing column with a smooth CAK wind model and investigate the variability of the Fe K alpha line width.
  The local absorbing column varies by nearly two orders of magnitude, from N_H^local = 4.25 x 10^22 cm^-2 during pre-ingress to 5.9 x 10^23 cm^-2 at ingress. The excess ingress absorption cannot be reproduced by a smooth wind model alone, suggesting a localized overdense structure along the line of sight. The Fe K alpha width varies from approximately 510 km s^-1 during pre-ingress to 1400 km s^-1 during egress, with intermediate, statistically indistinguishable values of approximately 900 km s^-1 during ingress and post-egress. Highly ionized Fe XXV and Fe XXVI emission is detected throughout the orbit, although fewer components are resolved during ingress. These diagnostics reveal a complex circumstellar environment with localized dense material and multiple reprocessing regions. The results demonstrate the capability of XRISM/Resolve to probe the geometry and kinematics of accretion environments in eclipsing high-mass X-ray binaries.

</details>


### [384] [A Linear Instability and Damping in the Acoustic Dispersion Relation of Fluids Subject to Inverse Compton Drag](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23824)
*Yiting Wang,Sebastian Heinz,Vladimir Zhdankin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 外部辐射场逆康普顿散射产生的辐射阻力改变相对论流体的声学色散关系，可致声模阻尼或失稳，并产生色散，且与背景辐射加速率同量级，对相对论喷流等天体物理场景可能普遍重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射阻力对相对论背景流的加速与动力学已受关注，但其对流体微扰（声学模）的影响尚未系统刻画。作者希望明确辐射阻力如何改变声波的色散关系，并判断在不稳定、阻尼与色散方面的可观测/动力学意义。

Method: 推导含外部辐射场逆康普顿散射辐射阻力的相对论流体线性色散关系，分析不同电子分布与声速下各声模的增长率与阻尼率；用狭义相对论流体力学数值模拟验证所得速率。最后将该色散关系应用于伽马射线暴、宽线区耀变体以及穿越宇宙微波背景与银河辐射场的类星体喷流等相对论外流条件。

Result: 辐射阻力使两个声模或同时被阻尼，或一个被阻尼而另一个被驱动失稳；短波长上失稳增长稳健，长波长上增长缓慢且过阻尼，此时线性分析受背景加速限制。增长/阻尼率约为体辐射加速率量级。存在参数范围较广的纯阻尼区。声波变为色散波：长波长相速度偏离普通声速，短波长趋近声速。数值模拟支持解析速率。应用于上述天体物理喷流后发现失稳、阻尼与色散在一系列相对论外流条件下可能相关。

Conclusion: 辐射阻力不仅影响相对论背景流，也同等重要地影响其声学微扰，导致短波长失稳、宽参数范围的纯阻尼以及波长相关的色散；该机制在伽马射线暴、宽线区耀变体和穿越强辐射场的类星体喷流等相对论外流中可能广泛适用。

Abstract: Radiation drag from inverse-Compton scattering of an external radiation field changes the acoustic dispersion relation of a relativistic fluid. We derive the linear dispersion relation and show that, depending on the electron distribution and fluid sound speed, radiation drag either damps both modes or damps one while driving the other unstable. The unstable growth is robust at short wavelengths but slow and overdamped at long wavelengths, where the linear analysis is limited by the background acceleration. The resulting growth and damping rates are of order the bulk radiative acceleration rate, so whenever radiation drag is important for the background flow, its effects on fluid perturbations are also important. A purely damped region exists for a wide range of parameters.
  Radiation drag also makes acoustic waves dispersive: The phase speed deviates from the ordinary sound speed at longer wavelengths, and approaches the sound speed at short wavelengths. We validate the derived rates using special-relativistic hydrodynamic simulations. We apply the derived relation to astrophysical jets such as gamma-ray bursts, blazars in the broad-line region of the host active galactic nucleus, and quasar jets traveling through the CMB and galactic radiation field. We find that the derived instability, damping and dispersion may be relevant across a range of relativistic outflow conditions.

</details>


### [385] [AT2019aalc: An obscured tidal disruption event candidate in an active galactic nucleus revealed by its first flare?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24005)
*Ying Gu,Xiao Li,Xue-Guang Zhang,En-Wei Liang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文将含时遮蔽TDE模型应用于重复TDE候选体AT2019aalc，成功再现其光变曲线，并解释其耀发前短暂变暗与耀发后光度偏低的现象，认为它是AGN宿主中被遮蔽TDE候选体的第二个成员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AT2019aalc被视为AGN中重复TDE候选体，但其耀发前短暂光学变暗及耀发后光度显著低于耀发前水平等特征此前未被关注。作者希望解释这些异常特征并厘清其物理本质。

Method: 采用含时遮蔽（time-dependent obscured）TDE模型拟合AT2019aalc的光变曲线，推导被瓦解恒星与SMBH的质量参数；并结合g-r颜色演化与E(B-V)预言进行对比分析。

Result: 拟合得到一颗1.122_{-0.176}^{+0.246} M⊙主序星被1.862_{-0.259}^{+0.255}×10^7 M⊙ SMBH潮汐瓦解；事件总能量1.24×10^51 erg，约0.2 M⊙碎片被吸积。耀发前变暗与耀发后光度降低均由AGN本征辐射被遮蔽所致；首个耀发期间净耀发g-r颜色呈持续变红趋势，与遮蔽TDE模型预言的E(B-V)逐渐增加一致。

Conclusion: AT2019aalc可视为近期提出的AGN宿主中被遮蔽TDE候选体类别的第二个成员。

Abstract: AT2019aalc is considered a repeating tidal disruption event (TDE) candidate occurring in an active galactic nucleus (AGN). In this paper, we highlight previously unnoticed but intriguing features that include a brief optical dimming prior to the rise of the main flare and a significantly lower post-flare luminosity compared to the pre-flare level. By applying a time-dependent obscured TDE model to AT2019aalc, we successfully reproduce its light curves with the tidal disruption of a $1.122_{-0.176}^{+0.246}\rm M_\odot$ main-sequence star by a $1.862_{-0.259}^{+0.255} \times 10^7{\rm M_\odot}$ supermassive black hole (SMBH). Both the pre-flare dimming and the reduced post-flare luminosity are attributed to obscuration of the intrinsic AGN emission. The total energy of the event derived from our fit is $1.24\times10^{51}$ ergs and about 0.2 ${\rm M_\odot}$ of debris mass is accreted by the SMBH. In addition, the $g-r$ color of the net flare in AT2019aalc exhibits a continuous reddening trend during the first flare, consistent with a gradual increase in $E(B-V)$ predicted by the obscured TDE model. These results suggest that AT2019aalc can be considered a second member of the recently proposed class of obscured TDE candidates hosted in AGNs. } \keywords{galaxies: active-galaxies: nuclei-quasars: supermassive black holes-quasars: individual (AT2019aalc)-transients: tidal disruption events.

</details>


### [386] [Constraints on Axion-like Dark Matter from Cosmic Birefringence with a Polarization Array of Repeating Fast Radio Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24085)
*Xiaohui Liu,Zi-Yan Yuwen,Yun-Long Zhang,Zerui Liu,Shiqian Zhao,Shuai Feng,Wei-Yang Wang,Xuelei Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文针对轴子类暗物质（ALDM）通过Chern-Simons耦合光子产生宇宙双折射的效应，建立贝叶斯框架，利用三颗高活跃重复快速射电暴（FRB）的偏振角数据进行单源及联合（FRBPA）搜寻，未发现显著振荡信号，并在10^-23至10^-18 eV质量范围内给出耦合强度的95%可信上限（10^-12.6至10^-7.9 GeV^-1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALDM是动机充分的暗物质候选者，其与光子的Chern-Simons耦合可诱导宇宙双折射，在偏振角信号中产生时间振荡与空间关联。高线偏振度的高活跃重复FRB是探测此类信号的理想探针，但需要发展合适的统计框架以充分利用多源数据。

Method: 建立贝叶斯框架，搜寻单个重复FRB中由ALDM引起的偏振角振荡；利用共同地球项产生的相关性，将多个源组合为FRB偏振阵列（FRBPA）。对三颗高活跃重复FRB的偏振角数据集开展分析，通过模拟校准的晕轮廓估计各FRB宿主处的暗物质密度，并采用完整的两分量双折射模型（源项与地球项）进行约束。

Result: 推得的暗物质密度与本地基准密度ρ0=0.4 GeV/cm^3量级相当。单源分析与联合FRBPA分析均未发现ALDM诱导偏振角振荡的显著证据。对Chern-Simons耦合强度给出95%可信上限：在轴子质量10^-23至10^-18 eV范围内，FRBPA的上限介于10^-12.6与10^-7.9 GeV^-1之间。

Conclusion: 对高活跃重复FRB的长期偏振监测是探测ALDM的独立且具前景的手段，其灵敏度有望随观测基线的延长和样本源的增多而提升。

Abstract: Axion-like dark matter (ALDM) is a well-motivated dark matter candidate whose Chern-Simons coupling to photons can induce cosmic birefringence, producing temporal oscillations and spatial correlation in polarization angle (PA) signals from different sources. Hyperactive repeating fast radio bursts (FRBs) with high linear polarization are a particularly promising probe of such signatures. We develop a Bayesian framework to search for PA oscillations caused by ALDM in individual repeating FRBs, and combine multiple sources in an FRB polarization array (FRBPA) by exploiting correlations arising from the common Earth term. We apply this framework to PA datasets from three well-studied hyperactive repeating FRBs, and constrain the Chern-Simons coupling over a broad range of ALDM masses. For each FRB, we estimate the dark matter density at its host location using a simulation-calibrated halo profile. The inferred dark matter densities are comparable to the fiducial local density $ρ_0 = 0.4~\mathrm{GeV/cm^3}$. The comparable source and Earth dark matter densities, together with the redshift-induced difference between the frequencies of the source and Earth terms, motivate our adoption of the full two-component birefringence model. We find no compelling evidence for ALDM-induced PA oscillations in either the individual source analyses or the joint FRBPA analysis. We place 95\% credible upper limits on the Chern-Simons coupling strength as a function of ALDM mass. Across the axion mass from $10^{-23}$ to $10^{-18}~\mathrm{eV}$, the resulting upper limits of the coupling strength of FRBPA lie between $10^{-12.6}$ and $10^{-7.9}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. Our results establish long-term polarimetric monitoring of hyperactive repeating FRBs as a promising and independent probe of ALDM, with sensitivity expected to improve with longer observational baselines and larger source samples.

</details>


### [387] [Fast Radio Bursts Associated with Persistent Radio Sources Prefer Hydrogen-poor Superluminous Supernova Host Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24240)
*Fujia Li,Fei Liu,Di Li,Dongzi Li,Ziheng Ding,Ran Gao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 重复FRB中与致密持续射电源（PRS）成协的子群，其寄主星系位于矮星系、高比恒星形成率、低金属丰度的极端端，性质最接近I型超亮超新星（SLSNe-I）寄主，暗示其起源为低金属丰度大质量恒星通道形成的年轻磁星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分重复快速射电暴（FRB）与致密持续射电源（PRS）成协，但其寄主星系性质与一般FRB寄主群体差异显著，尚不清楚FRB-PRS究竟追踪恒星形成活动、一般FRB寄主群体，还是特定类型的极端恒星形成环境。作者希望通过对标已知的瞬变源寄主样本，厘清FRB-PRS的环境偏好并约束其前身星通道。

Method: 将FRB-PRS寄主与定义明确的三类样本（SLSNe-I、SLSNe-II、II型核心坍缩超新星CCSNe）进行对比；采用累积分布函数（CDF）及统计检验，在恒星质量、比恒星形成率（sSFR）、气相金属丰度等寄主星系参数空间中进行比较，并注意控制恒星质量以检验sSFR与金属丰度的相对偏移。

Result: FRB-PRS寄主既不符合已定域非PRS FRB寄主的分布，也不符合一般恒星形成星系按SFR加权或质量加权的模拟寄主分布；它们占据寄主参数空间的极端低质量、高sSFR、低金属丰度端，即便相对同质量星系仍偏高sSFR、偏低金属丰度。统计上其寄主与SLSNe-I最为相似，与CCSNe寄主显著不同，SLSNe-II寄主介于两者之间。

Conclusion: FRB-PRS并非简单追踪恒星形成活动或一般FRB寄主群体，而是偏好SLSN-I式环境。该偏好与低金属丰度大质量恒星通道形成的年轻磁星一致；SLSN-I式抛射物对应在600 MHz处约29年的特征自由-自由透明时间尺度，可作为该起源情景的可检验预言。

Abstract: A subset of repeating fast radio bursts (FRBs) is associated with compact persistent radio sources (PRSs). The FRB-PRS hosts differ dramatically from the general FRB host population: they do not follow the distribution of localized non-PRS FRB hosts, nor the SFR-weighted or mass-weighted mock host distributions expected for the general star-forming galaxy population, instead occupying the extreme low-mass, high-sSFR, and low-metallicity end of host-galaxy parameter space. We compare these hosts with well-defined samples of hydrogen-poor superluminous supernovae (SLSNe-I), hydrogen-rich superluminous supernovae (SLSNe-II), and Type II core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe). Using cumulative distribution functions and statistical tests, we find that FRB-PRSs tend to reside in dwarf galaxies with high specific star formation rates and low gas-phase metallicities, even relative to galaxies of similar stellar mass. Their hosts are most similar to those of SLSNe-I, but differ significantly from CCSN hosts, with SLSN-II hosts lying between these populations. These results suggest that FRB-PRSs do not simply trace star formation activity or the general FRB host population, but instead favor SLSN-I-like environments. This preference is consistent with young magnetars formed through low-metallicity massive-star channels, for which SLSN-I-like ejecta imply a characteristic free--free transparency time of ~29 yr at 600 MHz.

</details>


### [388] [Rotating Radio Transient Phases of Isolated Neutron Star Systems and Descendants of Central Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24286)
*Ş. Demirok,Ü. Ertan,A. A. Gençali*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在回落盘模型框架下，计算了孤立中子星各族沿演化轨迹可产生RRAT（旋转射电暂现源）的阶段，界定了P-Ṗ图上的RRAT区域，并据此探讨了CCO（中心致密天体）的后代演化归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回落盘模型曾提出多数孤立中子星演化过程中会经历RRAT阶段，其机制可能是内盘穿入光柱导致死亡脉冲星极区开放磁场线流量增强。作者希望检验这一设想，并借此推断CCO的演化归宿。

Method: 在回落盘模型下，沿孤立中子星各族在周期—周期导数（P-Ṗ）图上的演化曲线，确定该机制可产生RRAT的阶段，从而在P-Ṗ图上划定RRAT区域，并与已知RRAT分布比对；同时用该结果研究CCO的后代。

Result: 计算得到的RRAT区域与已知RRAT分布相符。类似已测得P和Ṗ的三颗CCO源，其演化后不太可能成为射电脉冲星（RPs）；而新近确认的CCO——Calvera情况不同，Calvera类源在模型中会成为寿命超过10^8 yr的RPs，且P-Ṗ图上Calvera类CCO后代演化所处区域确有RP聚集。作者还估计其诞生率极低，因而其直接后代目前可能并不存在于银河系中。

Conclusion: 该RRAT产生机制在P-Ṗ图上的预言与观测RRAT分布一致，支持回落盘模型；多数CCO不会演化为射电脉冲星，但Calvera类源可演化为长寿命射电脉冲星，只是诞生率极低，其直接后代或在银河系中尚未出现。

Abstract: Rotating radio transients (RRATs) form an isolated neutron star (INS) population characterized by their sporadic and short radio bursts. In the fallback disc model, it was suggested that most INS populations pass through an RRAT phase during their evolution, and the mechanism producing these bursts could be the enhancement of the flux of open magnetic field lines through the poles of dead pulsars due to penetration of the inner disc into the light cylinder. To test this idea, we determine the RRAT phases that could be produced by this mechanism along the evolutionary curves of the INS families on the period-period derivative ($P$--$\dot{P}$ diagram). These RRAT phases form an RRAT region in the $P$--$\dot{P}$ diagram, which is in agreement with the known RRAT distribution. Using our results, we also investigate the descendants of central compact objects (CCOs). We estimate that the CCOs similar to the three CCOs with measured $P$ and $\dot{P}$ are not likely to become radio pulsars (RPs). The situation is rather different for Calvera, a recently identified CCO. In the model, Calvera-like sources become RPs with lifetimes $ > 10^{8}$ yr. Indeed, there is a cluster of RPs in the region of \PPdot~diagram where descendants of Calvera-like CCOs evolve in our model. We also estimate that they have a very low birth rate, and therefore their immediate descendants may not currently exist in our Galaxy.

</details>


### [389] [Multi-wavelength Behaviour and Lepto-hadronic Modeling of PKS 0215+015 Around the Epoch of IceCube-220225A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24633)
*Athira M Bharathan,Markus Böttcher*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究PKS~0215+015与IceCube-220225A中微子事件的关联，通过多波段SED建模表明轻子过程主导，中微子通量远低于单事件预期，高红移FSRQ作为中微子源存在能量学挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能天体物理中微子的探测为寻找宇宙粒子加速器开辟新途径。耀变体（喷流朝向观测者的活动星系核）是产生PeV级强子相互作用的潜在场所。作者旨在检验与IceCube-220225A事件位置一致的平谱射电类星体PKS~0215+015是否为该中微子的来源。

Method: 结合Fermi-LAT γ射线数据与Swift-XRT、Swift-UVOT观测，刻画中微子事件前后源的多波段行为；构建耀发期宽波段SED，并用轻子模型与轻子-强子模型分别拟合；同时对短时标γ射线光变进行独立分析以约束辐射区尺寸。

Result: PKS~0215+015在2022年初表现出显著的γ射线、X射线和光学活动，符合喷流扰动传播图像。SED可由同步辐射、同步自康普顿和外康普顿组成的轻子模型良好再现，电子过程主导。共加速质子成分辐射贡献次要，但所需质子动能光度约为磁场均分值的两个数量级以上。预测中微子通量远低于单个IceCube-220225A类事件所要求的值。短时标γ射线光变给出的辐射区尺寸与SED拟合一致。

Conclusion: PKS~0215+015与IceCube-220225A的关联在时间和能量学上存在挑战，该源的轻子主导特性使得单事件中微子通量难以解释。研究对高红移FSRQ的未来多信使研究具有启示意义。

Abstract: The detection of high-energy astrophysical neutrinos has opened a new avenue for identifying cosmic particle accelerators. Blazars, active galactic nuclei (AGN) with relativistic jets oriented close to the observer's line of sight, are promising sites of hadronic interactions capable of producing PeV-scale neutrinos. We investigate the flat-spectrum radio quasar (FSRQ) PKS~0215+015 ($z \simeq 1.715$), positionally consistent with the \textit{IceCube-220225A} neutrino event of estimated energy $\sim$154~TeV. We combine \textit{Fermi}-LAT $γ$-ray data with Swift-XRT and Swift-UVOT observations to characterise the source around the neutrino epoch. PKS~0215+015 exhibited pronounced $γ$-ray, X-ray and optical activity in early 2022, consistent with a disturbance propagating along the jet. We construct broadband spectral energy distributions (SEDs) for the flaring interval and model them using leptonic and lepto-hadronic frameworks. The SED is well reproduced by a leptonic model comprising synchrotron, synchrotron self-Compton and external Compton emission, indicating that electron processes dominate. A co-accelerated proton population remains radiatively subdominant but requires a proton kinetic luminosity roughly two orders of magnitude above equipartition with the magnetic field. The predicted neutrino flux is well below that implied by a single \textit{IceCube-220225A}-like detection. Independent analysis of short-term $γ$-ray variability yields an emission-region size consistent with the SED fit, supporting the model's physical self-consistency. We discuss the temporal and energetic plausibility of the association between PKS~0215+015 and \textit{IceCube-220225A} and implications for future multimessenger studies of high-redshift FSRQs.

</details>


### [390] [Particle Acceleration in Stratified 2D MRI Turbulence: From Reconnection to First- and Second-Order Fermi and Shear Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24735)
*Astor Sandoval,Mario Riquelme,Jorge Cuadra*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析二维PIC模拟，识别了MRI驱动的电流片中粒子加速的三个阶段：非理想电场注入、一阶Fermi加速（与瞬时回旋频率成正比）和二阶Fermi/剪切加速，最终得出最大能量与$\omega_{c,0}/\Omega_0$成正比的标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光度黑洞吸积盘弱碰撞环境可能支持非热粒子加速。S24的局域二维PIC模拟发现非热粒子最大能量与尺度分离比$\omega_{c,0}/\Omega_0$成正比，但其加速机制及标度依赖尚不明确，因此需要识别具体加速机制并量化其尺度分离依赖性。

Method: 基于Sandoval et al. (2024)的二维分层磁旋转不稳定性(MRI)对等离子体PIC模拟，追踪粒子在MRI发电机相关大尺度电流片中的能量演化，分阶段识别加速机制：重联驱动分裂引发的非理想电场注入、新形成分离等离子体团间汇聚流中的一阶Fermi加速、以及反复与等离子体团作用的二阶Fermi加速和亚主导剪切加速。

Result: 粒子初始由等离子体团分裂驱动的重联中的非理想电场注入，能量与尺度分离无关；随后在汇聚流中经历快速一阶Fermi型加速，能量增长率与瞬时非相对论回旋频率成正比，与$\omega_{c,0}/\Omega_0$无关，由此得到最大能量正比于$\omega_{c,0}/\Omega_0$；之后反复与等离子体团作用，以较慢的二阶Fermi加速为主，剪切加速为辅，数个轨道周期内能量仅增加数倍，与$\omega_{c,0}/\Omega_0$无关。

Conclusion: 三阶段加速的组合解释了S24发现的最大能量正比于$\omega_{c,0}/\Omega_0$的标度关系，并表明MRI可在低光度吸积盘中将粒子加速至超相对论能量。三维验证是下一步重要工作。

Abstract: Low-luminosity accretion disks around black holes are weakly collisional, potentially enabling nonthermal particle acceleration. Local two-dimensional particle-in-cell simulations of the stratified magnetorotational instability (MRI) in pair plasmas by Sandoval et al. (2024; hereafter S24) revealed nonthermal populations with maximum energies proportional to the scale-separation ratio $ω_{c,0}/Ω_0$, where $ω_{c,0}$ and $Ω_0$ are the initial particle cyclotron and Keplerian frequencies, respectively. We identify the acceleration mechanisms involved and quantify their scale-separation dependence. Acceleration occurs primarily within large-scale current sheets associated with an MRI dynamo. Particles are initially injected by non-ideal electric fields during reconnection driven by plasmoid splitting, attaining energies independently of scale separation. They subsequently undergo rapid first-order Fermi-like acceleration occurring within converging flows between newly formed, separating plasmoids. During this stage, energies grow at a rate proportional to the instantaneous non-relativistic cyclotron frequency, independently of $ω_{c,0}/Ω_0$. This fast acceleration rate implies a maximum energy proportional to $ω_{c,0}/Ω_0$. Particles then continue interacting repeatedly with plasmoids, undergoing slower, predominantly second-order Fermi acceleration, with a subdominant contribution from shear acceleration. This stage increases particle energies by only a factor of {\it a few} over several orbits, regardless of $ω_{c,0}/Ω_0$. The combination of these three stages implies a maximum energy proportional to $ω_{c,0}/Ω_0$, explaining the scaling found by S24 and suggesting that the MRI may accelerate particles to ultrarelativistic energies in low-luminosity disks. Testing these results in three dimensions remains an important next step.

</details>


### [391] [Jet variability in small mass ratio supermassive black hole binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24837)
*Jay V. Kalinani,Manuela Campanelli,Michail Chabanov,Maria Chiara de Simone,Carlos O. Lousto,Helvi Witek,Yosef Zlochower*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过三维广义相对论磁流体模拟，研究质量比q=1/7的进动超大质量黑洞双星嵌入环双星盘时，双星腔内动力学如何驱动并塑造相对论喷流，并给出可用于证认小质量比双星的关联喷流特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双星腔体动力学与千倍引力半径尺度喷流变率之间的联系，并寻找比单一特征更可靠的小质量比超大质量黑洞双星观测证据。

Method: 对嵌入环双星盘、质量比q=1/7的进动超大质量黑洞双星开展三维广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟，追踪腔内动力学、吸积流、磁场及喷流的长期演化。

Result: 主黑洞主导吸积与喷流功率；次黑洞每轨道两次穿越并激扰内吸积流，交替将磁化气体驱入上、下喷流漏斗，产生准周期外传扰动。每一喷流瓣每轨道出现一次主导外流。更长时标上喷流进动、磁手性反转并经历两个弱相位：首个源于主黑洞自旋重新取向，喷流恢复后投影磁场环向反转；第二个出现在次黑洞轨道与环双星盘近乎共面、抵达主黑洞的气体与磁通减少时。

Conclusion: 结果将腔体动力学与大尺度喷流变率联系起来，并预言一组具有特定相对时序与因果顺序的关联喷流信号，其联合特征可能比任何单一特征更能证明小质量比超大质量黑洞双星的存在。

Abstract: We present three-dimensional general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations of a precessing supermassive black hole binary with mass ratio q=1/7, embedded in a circumbinary disk. We investigate how dynamics within the binary cavity launch and shape a relativistic jet to thousands of gravitational radii. The primary black hole dominates both accretion and jet power, while the smaller black hole repeatedly crosses and shocks the inner accretion flow. These passages of the secondary drive magnetized gas alternately into the upper and lower jet funnels, producing disturbances that propagate outward as quasi-periodic outflows. Although the inner flow is perturbed twice per orbit, each jet lobe shows one dominant outflow per orbit. On longer timescales, the jet precesses, reverses its magnetic handedness, and undergoes two weak phases. The first results from a reorientation of the primary spin and is followed, after the jet recovers, by a reversal in the circulation of the projected magnetic field. The second weak phase occurs as the secondary's orbit becomes nearly coplanar with the circumbinary disk, reducing the gas and magnetic flux reaching the primary. Together, these results connect cavity dynamics to large-scale jet variability and predict a set of correlated jet signatures whose relative timing and causal ordering may provide stronger evidence for a small-mass-ratio supermassive black hole binary than any one feature alone.

</details>


### [392] [Luminosity Signatures of Dark Sector Particles from Black Hole Evaporation in Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24914)
*Ioannis Dalianis,Anastasios Irakleous*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 中子星内部暗物质塌缩可形成微观黑洞，其霍金辐射产物中弱相互作用长寿命媒介子可逃逸并衰变为高能中微子/光子/带电粒子，成为霍金辐射的间接探针。本文研究这些次级粒子的联合能量-角分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型粒子被致密星体介质高效吸收，需借助能逃逸的弱相互作用长寿命粒子间接探测中子星内微观黑洞的霍金辐射，并区分其与暗物质湮灭等其它产生机制的信号。

Method: 计算逃逸媒介子衰变产物的联合能量-角分布，纳入媒介子传播、能量依赖衰变及相对论衰变运动学；以引力耦合标量、暗光子、暗Z玻色子、重中性轻子为例展示现象学，并与暗物质湮灭的单能媒介子产生情形对比。

Result: 对相对论媒介子，归一化能量积分角分布近似与黑洞温度无关，其特征角尺度主要由cτ_S/D控制（寿命与源距之比），因衰变长度的boost增强被相对论束流效应补偿。与暗物质湮灭相比，能量积分角分布可近乎简并，但能谱与能量分辨角分布仍可区分。

Conclusion: 该机制可行，其互补的能谱与角分布特征为高能中微子与伽马射线搜寻提供了探测目标。

Abstract: Microscopic black holes may form at the centers of neutron stars through the accumulation and collapse of dark matter. While Standard Model particles produced by Hawking evaporation are efficiently absorbed by the dense stellar medium, sufficiently weakly interacting and long-lived particles can escape and subsequently decay into high-energy neutrinos, photons, or charged particles, providing an indirect probe of Hawking radiation. We examine the joint energy--angular distribution of these secondary particles, incorporating mediator propagation, energy-dependent decay, and relativistic decay kinematics. For relativistic mediators, the normalized energy-integrated angular profile becomes approximately independent of the black hole temperature, with its characteristic extent controlled primarily by the combination $cτ_S/D$ of the mediator lifetime and source distance, as the boost enhancement of the decay length is compensated by relativistic beaming. We demonstrate the viability of this mechanism and illustrate its phenomenology with gravitationally coupled scalars, dark photons, dark-$Z$ bosons, and heavy neutral leptons. Comparing with monochromatic mediator production from dark matter annihilation, we find that the energy-integrated angular profiles can be nearly degenerate, while the energy spectra and energy-resolved angular distributions remain distinct. These complementary spectral and angular signatures provide targets for high-energy neutrino and gamma-ray searches.

</details>


### [393] [Magnetar magnetothermal evolution with Landau-quantized electrons: enhanced Joule heating due to free electron diamagnetism and the magnetar heating problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24918)
*Peter B. Rau*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用QMFM码对真实中子星壳层进行2.5D电子磁流体+热演化模拟，发现Landau量子化效应可增强焦耳加热，但不足以解释磁星加热问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星系统性比其它中子星更亮，维持如此高的光度需要超出标准欧姆耗散的额外热源，即"磁星加热问题"。此前周期盒模拟表明，Landau量子化诱导磁化强度的de Haas–van Alphen（dHvA）振荡可反复产生大梯度磁场分量并快速欧姆耗散，需在真实中子星壳层中定量评估这一增强效应。

Method: 使用为此开发的QMFM有限体积代码，对真实中子星壳层开展轴对称2.5D电子磁流体力学加温度演化的模拟，纳入全部Landau量子化效应，量化其对磁场耗散与星体加热的增强作用。

Result: Landau量子化效应产生强的小尺度磁场结构，其耗散可使赤道磁场约束热斑温度高于无Landau量子化时的温度。增强加热在较弱初始磁场B~10^14 G时最显著，磁能耗散快30%，此时壳层冷却足够使dHvA振荡达到大振幅。对于驱动最亮磁星所需的较强磁场（≳5×10^14 G），增强有限，因为耗散加热壳层足以热抑制dHvA振荡。在加热显著增强的情形下，大部分热量进入中微子光度，表面光子光度仅略微增加。

Conclusion: 对于所模拟的简单壳层约束磁场，Landau量子化增强的焦耳加热不足以解释磁星加热问题。

Abstract: Magnetars are systematically more luminous than other neutron stars. Previous studies suggest that supporting such high luminosities requires a heat source beyond standard Ohmic dissipation-- the ``magnetar heating problem''. We previously demonstrated using periodic box simulations that de Haas--van Alphen (dHvA) oscillations of the Landau quantization-induced magnetization in a neutron star could repeatedly generate large-gradient field components that undergo rapid Ohmic dissipation. Using the QMFM finite volume code developed for this purpose, we perform axisymmetric 2.5D electron magnetohydrodynamics plus thermal evolution simulations of a realistic neutron star crust, including all Landau quantization effects, to quantify this enhancement to field dissipation and heating of the star. We show that Landau quantization effects generate strong, small-scale magnetic field structures whose dissipation can heat the field-confined equatorial hot spot beyond its temperature in the absence of Landau quantization. Enhanced heating is greatest for weaker initial fields $B\sim10^{14}$ G, for which magnetic energy is dissipated 30% faster: in this case, the crust cools sufficiently for the dHvA oscillations to reach large amplitudes. For the stronger fields $\gtrsim B\sim5\times10^{14}$ G required to power the most luminous magnetars, the enhancement is modest, as dissipation of these fields heats the crust sufficiently to thermally suppress dHvA oscillations. In cases where heating is significantly enhanced, most of the heat goes into increasing the neutrino luminosity, and surface photon luminosity is only slightly increased. For the simple crust-confined fields that we simulated, Landau quantization-enhanced Joule heating is thus insufficient to explain the magnetar heating problem.

</details>


### [394] [PEARLS: NuSTAR and XMM-Newton Extragalactic Survey of the JWST North Ecliptic Pole Time-Domain Field IV: X-Ray Variability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24963)
*Ross Silver,Francesca Civano,Xiurui Zhao,Samantha Creech,S. P. Willner,Christopher N. A. Willmer,Rogier A. Windhorst,Haojing Yan,Anton M. Koekemoer,Rosalia O'Brien,Rafael Ortiz,Rolf A. Jansen,Gibson B. Bowling,Payaswini Saikia,W. Peter Maksym,Nico Cappelluti,Francesca Fornasini,Massimo Ricotti,Timothy Carleton,Seth H. Cohen,Rachel Honor,Jake Summers,Jordan C. J. D'Silva,Sibasish Laha,Dan Coe,Christopher J. Conselice,Jose M. Diego,Simon P. Driver,Brenda Frye,Norman A. Grogin,Madeline A. Marshall,Nor Pirzkal,Aaron Robotham,Russell E. Ryan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于NuSTAR与XMM-Newton对JWST北黄极时域场近五年的准同时X射线观测，本文对112个NuSTAR源和453个XMM-Newton源开展X射线光度变异性分析，发现少数源存在显著变化，且变幅与光度、计数及时间尺度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用JWST北黄极时域场（NEP TDF）长期、准同时的X射线观测数据，系统研究该天区X射线源的变异性特征，以补充X射线选源与多波段时域研究的基础信息。

Method: 自2019年起，NuSTAR与XMM-Newton对该场进行了近五年连续观测，累积分别获得3.5 Ms与228 ks的准同时数据。本文仅基于X射线测光数据，对探测到的112个NuSTAR源和453个XMM-Newton源进行变异性分析。

Result: 4个NuSTAR源和11个XMM-Newton源在至少一个能带上以≥99%置信度表现出变异。具有红移测量的源显示光度与变异性之间存在关系：74%的变异源亮度高于巡天灵敏度极限的5倍。这一结论得到支持——约三分之一的计数超过400的源被探测到变异，而计数较少的源中仅4个显示变异性。变异时标难以确定，但变幅倾向于在较长时间尺度上更大。

Conclusion: 该NEP X射线巡天的长期数据表明，X射线源变异性整体较弱且仅部分源显著变化，变异性与源亮度及计数密切相关，变幅可能随观测时间尺度增加而增大；变异时标仍需更长时间基线或更高时间采样来约束。

Abstract: NuSTAR and XMM-Newton have observed the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) North Ecliptic Pole (NEP) Time-Domain Field (TDF) for almost five contiguous years starting in 2019. In that time, the NEP X-ray survey has accumulated 3.5 Ms and 228 ks of quasi-simultaneous NuSTAR and XMM-Newton observations, respectively. This paper presents variability results for the 112 NuSTAR and 453 XMM-Newton sources detected in this field, based solely on the X-ray photometric data. Four NuSTAR sources and 11 XMM-Newton sources varied in at least one band at >=99% confidence. The sources with redshift measurements show a relationship between luminosity and variability with 74% of variable sources brighter than 5x the sensitivity limit of the survey. This is supported by about 1/3 of sources with more than 400 counts detected being variable and only 4 sources with fewer counts showing variability. Variability timescales are not well determined, but variability amplitude tends to be larger on longer timescales.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [395] [Bootstrap certification of string order in quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22420)
*Sagnik Banerjee,Haoyu Guo,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出"弦算符作为主对象"的多体自举框架，利用弦算符与局域词构成的闭代数，将弦关联函数的计算成本从指数降为关于弦长的多项式，从而无波函数地严格认证拓扑相中的非局域弦序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多体自举仅靠正定性约束关联函数并最小化能量，无需变分波函数即可认证基态性质，但其代价随所涉算符尺寸指数增长，导致用于诊断拓扑相的长程弦关联无法处理。作者希望突破这一指数壁垒，实现对非局域弦序的严格认证。

Method: 将弦算符视为基本对象：裸弦与端点修饰弦彼此以及与局域词之间满足封闭算符代数，由此构造半定规划，其成本仅随弦长多项式增长；一次计算即可得到所有长度的弦关联估计。当基态能量被限定在一个窗口内时，对每个目标弦给出严格的双侧界。方法在cluster Ising链与spin-1 Heisenberg链上与DMRG基准对比。

Result: 单次计算即可给出所有长度的弦关联估计，并对每个目标弦得到严格双侧界；在cluster Ising与spin-1 Heisenberg链上与DMRG吻合，直接从哈密顿量认证了cluster相与Haldane相的非局域弦序。发现界的锐度由进入计算的算符决定，而非仅由弦长决定。

Conclusion: 通过利用弦与局域词的代数封闭性，该框架将空间延展的可观测量转化为多项式可解的自举变量，开辟了量子多体系统中非局域序的无波函数认证途径。

Abstract: The many-body bootstrap certifies ground-state properties by minimizing energy over correlators constrained only by positivity, without any variational wavefunction. Its cost, however, grows exponentially with the size of the operators involved, placing the long-range string correlators that diagnose topological phases out of reach. We overcome this by treating string operators as primary objects: bare and endpoint-dressed strings satisfy a closed operator algebra with one another and with local words, yielding a semidefinite program whose cost grows only polynomially with string length. A single computation then yields estimates of string correlators of all lengths. Rigorous two-sided bounds are obtained for each target string once the ground-state energy is pinned within a window. When benchmarked against density-matrix renormalization group computations on the cluster Ising and spin$-1$ Heisenberg chains, the method certifies the nonlocal string order of the cluster and Haldane phases directly from the Hamiltonian. Our rigorous certification of string order parameters reveal that the sharpness of the bounds is set by which operators enter the calculation, not by their length alone. By exploiting algebraic closure of strings and local words, our framework lifts spatially extended observables into polynomially tractable bootstrap variables, opening up a route to wavefunction-free certification of nonlocal order in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [396] [Approaching the Inverse Neutron Scattering Problem with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22535)
*Jingyi Luo,Fletcher Williams,Ang-Kun Wu,David Dahlbom,Cristian D. Batista,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明神经网络可高效求解从非弹性中子散射谱反推量子磁体微观哈密顿量的逆问题，在八参数蜂窝格子Heisenberg模型的合成数据集上，FCNN、CNN1D、CNN2D三种架构均实现高精度参数还原，并在达信号强度10%的噪声下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非弹性中子散射测量中确定量子磁体的微观哈密顿量是凝聚态物理的核心难题。动力学自旋结构因子原则上包含相互作用信息，但由于参数空间大且实验噪声存在，从实验谱中提取哈密顿量参数构成高度非平凡的逆问题，缺乏高效可靠的求解手段。

Method: 以完全极化相下的八参数蜂窝格子Heisenberg自旋模型为基准，在线性自旋波理论框架内精确计算动力学自旋结构因子；构建大规模中子散射谱合成数据集，训练神经网络由动力学响应直接推断哈密顿量参数；对比全连接网络（FCNN）、一维卷积网络（CNN1D）与二维卷积网络（CNN2D）三种架构的性能，并测试不同噪声水平下的鲁棒性。

Result: 三种网络架构均达到高预测精度；训练后的模型对实验不确定性具有显著鲁棒性，即使输入谱被幅度达信号强度10%的随机噪声污染，仍能可靠恢复哈密顿量参数。

Conclusion: 研究确立机器学习作为从中子散射数据进行定量哈密顿量重构的有力框架，并为量子磁性材料的自动化表征提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Determining the microscopic Hamiltonian of a quantum magnet from inelastic neutron scattering measurements remains a central challenge in condensed matter physics. While the dynamical spin structure factor contains, in principle, sufficient information about the underlying interactions, extracting Hamiltonian parameters from experimental spectra constitutes a highly nontrivial inverse problem due to the large parameter space and the presence of experimental noise. Here we demonstrate that neural networks can efficiently solve this inverse problem for a broad class of quantum magnets. As a benchmark, we consider an eight-parameter family of honeycomb lattice Heisenberg spin models in the fully polarized phase, where the dynamical spin structure factor can be computed exactly within linear spin-wave theory. Using a large synthetic dataset of neutron scattering spectra, we train neural networks to infer the underlying Hamiltonian parameters directly from the dynamical response. We compare the performance of three different architectures---fully connected neural networks (FCNNs), one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (CNN1Ds), and two-dimensional convolutional neural networks (CNN2Ds)---and find that all achieve high predictive accuracy. Remarkably, the trained models remain robust in the presence of substantial experimental uncertainty, reliably recovering the Hamiltonian parameters even when the input spectra are contaminated by random noise with amplitudes reaching $10\%$ of the signal intensity. Our results establish machine learning as a powerful framework for quantitative Hamiltonian reconstruction from neutron scattering data and provide a practical route toward automated characterization of quantum magnetic materials.

</details>


### [397] [Structural and magnetic properties of TbCuAs$_2$ studied by X-ray and neutron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22589)
*M. G. Kim,T. Heitmann,S. Boney,C. Neupane,R. Acevedo-Esteves,A. Sapkota,D. Evans,P. C. Canfield,C. Nelson,E. D. Mun,J. -W. Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过单晶中子衍射、X射线共振磁散射和单晶X射线衍射，确定了反铁磁金属TbCuAs₂中Tb磁矩取向及反铁磁排列方式，并将其与同族化合物对比，提出低温电阻率异常的共性物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TbCuAs₂在低温下出现异常电阻率上翘，被认为源于其磁基态，但其反铁磁有序的具体性质此前尚未确定，需要实验予以澄清。

Method: 联合使用单晶中子衍射、X射线共振磁散射与单晶X射线衍射，测定Tb磁矩方向、沿高对称方向的反铁磁排布、结构与磁性的相互作用及其温度依赖性。

Result: 实验明确确定了Tb磁矩取向及反铁磁排列方式，并将结果与同族RcuAs₂（R = Sm、Gd）的先前研究进行对比。

Conclusion: 作者提出，具有低温电阻率异常的稀土铜砷化物其结构与磁性质受共同的底层物理机制支配。

Abstract: We investigated antiferromagnetic metal TbCuAs$_2$, which exhibits an anomalous resistivity upturn at low temperatures. This resistivity upturn has been proposed to originate from the material's magnetic ground state; however, the precise nature of the antiferromagnetic ordering in this compound has not been previously established. Here, we present a combined single crystal neutron diffraction, X-ray resonant magnetic scattering, and single crystal X-ray diffraction study of TbCuAs$_2$. These measurements unambiguously determine the Tb magnetic moment direction, the antiferromagnetic arrangement along high symmetry directions, the interplay between structure and magnetism, and their temperature dependence. Our observations are compared with previous studies on sister compounds in the rare-earth copper arsenide family ($R$CuAs$_2$, $R$ = Sm and Gd), which also displays resistivity anomalies. We suggest a common underlying physics governing the structural and magentic properties of rare-earth copper arsenides that exhibit low-temperature resistivity anomalies.

</details>


### [398] [Probing $d$-wave pairing in the $t$-$t'$-$U$ Hubbard model with tensor-backflow wave functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22618)
*Xiao Liang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于张量-背流方法结合Lanczos步骤，研究了二维t-t'-U Hubbard模型的d波配对，能量与最先进神经量子态相当，发现负t'增强d波配对涨落但未观察到长程有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究t-t'-U Hubbard模型中d波配对的增强机制，验证负次近邻跃迁t'对d波配对涨落的影响。

Method: 采用张量-背流方法；8×8格点用对称性投影加一步Lanczos改进波函数，12×12和16×16格点仅用一步Lanczos；参数为n=0.875、U=8、t'=-0.2t；分析实空间配对关联和配对密度矩阵谱权重。

Result: 16×16格点能量相对差为4.4×10⁻³，与对称性保持神经量子态相当；t'=-0.2t时横向与纵向键配对分量间出现特征相对符号，配对密度矩阵谱权重增加；8×8格点主特征值增强，大格点谱权重分布在多个近简并本征模中。

Conclusion: 负t'增强并拓宽了d波配对涨落，但无单一特征值表现出发散标度，因此未达到长程有序（ODLRO）。

Abstract: Building on the recently developed tensor-backflow method, which accurately describes energies and stripe states in the Fermi-Hubbard model, we investigate $d$-wave pairing in the two-dimensional $t$-$t'$-$U$ Hubbard model. For the $8\times8$ lattice, we improve the wave function using symmetry projections followed by one Lanczos step, whereas for the $12\times 12$ and $16\times 16$ lattices, we apply one Lanczos step without symmetry projection. We focus primarily on the parameter regime with filling $n=0.875$, on-site repulsion $U=8$ and next-nearest-neighbor hopping $t'=-0.2t$, a regime in which enhanced $d$-wave pairing has been reported. The resulting energies are competitive with those obtained using state-of-the-art neural quantum states. For example, on a $16\times 16$ lattice with periodic boundary conditions, the tensor-backflow wave function after one Lanczos step without explicit symmetry enforcement achieves a relative energy difference of $4.4\times 10^{-3}$ from the symmetry-preserving neural quantum state result. From the real space pair correlations, for $t'=-0.2t$, we observe stronger $d$-wave pair correlations together with the characteristic relative sign between horizontal and vertical bond-pair components. Compared with $t'=0$, finite negative $t'$ increases the spectral weight of the corresponding pair-density matrix, which indicates the enhanced overall $d$-wave pair fluctuations. The leading eigenvalue is enhanced on the $8\times8$ lattice for both with and without symmetry projections. For larger lattices such as $12\times 12$ and $16\times 16$, the leading pair-density matrix spectral weight is distributed among several nearly degenerate eigenmodes rather than being concentrated in a single dominant mode. This indicates enhanced and more broadly distributed $d$-wave pair fluctuations, but no single eigenvalue exhibits the extensive scaling required for ODLRO.

</details>


### [399] [On the precise boundary of the Néel antiferromagnetic phase in the Shastry-Sutherland model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22907)
*Rongyi Lv,Xiangjian Qian,Mingpu Qin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模DMRG计算，测定Shastry-Sutherland模型Néel反铁磁相的边界，发现交错磁化在争议区间0.78≲J1/J2≤0.81内仍为有限值，结合PVBS相边界约0.78的共识，支持PVBS与Néel AFM之间的直接相变，否定中间量子自旋液相的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shastry-Sutherland模型的基态相图仍存争议，尤其PVBS相与Néel AFM相之间相变的性质尚无定论：部分研究认为是直接相变，另一些则提出存在狭窄的中间量子自旋液相。该争议亟需通过高精度数值方法加以澄清。

Method: 采用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算，确定Shastry-Sutherland模型中Néel AFM相的边界。具体应用交错边界钉扎场（staggered boundary pinning fields）并直接测量体交错磁化（bulk staggered magnetization），同时对截断误差进行系统外推，并开展细致的有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 交错磁化在被认为可能存在量子自旋液相的争议区间0.78≲J1/J2≤0.81内始终保持有限值；结合既有共识给出的PVBS相边界J1/J2≈0.78，表明该区间内不存在中间量子自旋液相。

Conclusion: 研究结果否定了中间量子自旋液相的存在，支持Shastry-Sutherland模型中PVBS与Néel AFM之间的直接相变。

Abstract: The ground state phase diagram of the Shastry-Sutherland model remains controversial. In particular, the nature of the transition between the plaquette valence bond solid (PVBS) phase and the Néel antiferromagnetic (AFM) phase is still under debate. While some studies suggest a direct transition between the two phases, others propose the existence of a narrow intermediate quantum spin liquid regime. To address this issue, we perform large-scale Density Matrix Renormalization Group calculations to determine the Néel AFM phase boundary in the Shastry-Sutherland model. By applying staggered boundary pinning fields and directly measuring the bulk staggered magnetization, and through systematic extrapolations with respect to truncation error as well as careful finite-size scaling, we find that the staggered magnetization remains finite across the controversial region $0.78 \lesssim J_{1}/J_{2} \leq 0.81$, which was suggested to host a quantum spin liquid phase in some studies. Together with the previously established consensus placing the PVBS phase boundary at $J_{1}/J_{2} \approx 0.78$, these results provide evidence against the existence of an intermediate quantum spin liquid phase and support a direct PVBS--Néel AFM transition in the Shastry-Sutherland model.

</details>


### [400] [Correlation-assisted spin-selective metallicity in strained and bilayer altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23150)
*Marnin J. Di Nunzio,Frank Lechermann,Ilya M. Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在最小Hubbard型模型中研究电子关联、掺杂、单轴应变与层间耦合对单层和双层交替磁体（altermagnet）的调控，比较Hartree-Fock与RISB方法，揭示了交替磁序的鲁棒性、序起始与准粒子相干性丧失的分离，以及通过应变/掺杂/堆叠实现全自旋极化费米面的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体兼具零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂电子结构，可在无需铁磁性的条件下提供自旋极化载流子。作者希望厘清电子关联等相互作用因素如何影响交替磁序及其金属态，并寻找实现全自旋极化费米面的调控手段。

Method: 构建单层与双层系统的最小Hubbard型模型，同时考虑电子关联、掺杂、单轴应变和层间耦合；采用Hartree-Fock理论与旋转不变Slave-Boson（RISB）方法进行对比计算，分析磁序、准粒子权重与谱学性质。

Result: 交替磁序在准粒子重整化下保持鲁棒，磁序起始与强耦合下准粒子相干性丧失相分离；单层中掺杂产生显著粒子-空穴不对称，掺杂与单轴应变结合可在近半满处形成全自旋极化费米面；双层中有利层间铁性排列的堆叠方式使层交替磁序参量的动量依赖自旋劈裂相长叠加，弱非对称掺杂还诱导晶胞内电荷序并产生全自旋极化低能载流子；在四轨道填充五个电子的双层系统中，出现连续顺磁-交替磁转变，并在更强耦合下向准粒子权重受抑的类Mott区演化。

Conclusion: 电子关联、应变、掺杂与堆叠是调控交替磁金属及产生全自旋极化费米面的互补手段，为无净磁化自旋电子学提供理论依据。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine vanishing net magnetization with a momentum-dependent spin-split electronic structure, providing a route to spin-polarized carriers without ferromagnetism. Here, we investigate how electronic correlations, doping, uniaxial strain, and interlayer coupling control altermagnetism within a minimal Hubbard-type model for mono- and bilayer systems. Comparing Hartree--Fock theory with the rotationally invariant slave-boson (RISB) approach, we demonstrate the robustness of altermagnetic order against quasiparticle renormalization and reveal a separation between the onset of magnetic order and the loss of quasiparticle coherence at stronger coupling. In the monolayer, doping produces a pronounced particle--hole asymmetry, while its combination with uniaxial strain generates a fully spin-polarized Fermi surface close to half-filling. In the bilayer, stacking that favors ferroic alignment of the layer altermagnetic order parameters allows their momentum-dependent spin splittings to combine constructively. Weak asymmetric doping additionally induces intra-unit-cell charge order, resulting in fully spin-polarized low-energy carriers. At a bilayer filling of five electrons in four orbitals, we find a continuous paramagnet-to-altermagnet transition followed, at stronger coupling, by an evolution toward a Mott-like regime with strongly suppressed quasiparticle weight. Our results establish correlations, strain, doping, and stacking as complementary means of controlling altermagnetic metals and generating fully spin-polarized Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [401] [Hund-driven local-itinerant duality of Eu-4$f$ electrons in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23645)
*Yingying Cao,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合DFT+DMFT方法，对Eu掺杂无限层镍酸盐112结构中Eu-4f与Ni-3d电子同时显式处理库仑相互作用，揭示了Eu-4f构型随Hund耦合JH的三区间演化，阐明混合价与局域矩共存的微观起源，并预测可能与Ni-3d杂化的低能4f准粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eu替代无限层镍酸盐中被发现存在重入超导与Tc升高现象，这引出关键疑问：Eu-4f态的性质及其与Ni-3d电子的相互作用机制尚不清楚。已有实验推断出Eu离子呈现磁性兼混合价态，但缺乏对其电子结构起源的理论解释，因此需要理论计算来阐明4f电子构型、价态与磁性的微观机理。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论与动力学平均场理论相结合的DFT+DMFT方法，在镍酸盐112结构中同时显式处理Ni-3d与Eu-4f壳层的库仑排斥，系统研究Eu离子的价态与磁性随Hund规则耦合JH的变化规律。

Result: 发现Eu-4f构型随JH呈现三区间演化：(1)小JH时为非磁态；(2)中等JH时为磁性且混合价态，局域矩与强关联的j=7/2准粒子共存，与实验推断一致；(3)大JH时为完全自旋极化的二价态。该局域-巡游二重性源于4f内部j=5/2与7/2流形间电荷转移所诱导的有效空穴掺杂轨道选择Mott态。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解释了实验中观测到的Eu离子混合价与局域矩行为的起源，还预测存在可能与Ni-3d电子杂化的低能4f准粒子，并可能对Tc升高有贡献，为理解Eu替代无限层镍酸盐异常的铁磁与超导性质提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Recent discovery of reentrant superconductivity and elevated $T_c$ in Eu-substituted infinite-layer nickelates raises a critical question concerning the nature of Eu-4$f$ states and their potential interaction with Ni-$d$ electrons. Here we combine density functional theory with the dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT) calculations to investigate the valence and magnetic properties of Eu-ions in the nickelate 112 structure, explicitly treating the Coulomb repulsion in both Ni-3$d$ and Eu-4$f$ shells. We find a three-regime evolution of the Eu-4$f$ configuration driven by its Hund's rule coupling $J_{\rm H}$. The magnetic and mixed-valence Eu ionic state inferred by experiments only occurs for moderate $J_{\rm H}$, with coexisting local moments and strongly correlated $j=7/2$ quasiparticles, in contrast to the nonmagnetic state at small $J_{\rm H}$ and the fully spin-polarized divalent state at large $J_{\rm H}$. This local-itinerant duality arises from an effectively hole-doped orbital-selective Mott state induced by the intra-4$f$ charge transfer between $j=5/2$ and $7/2$ manifolds. It not only explains the origin of the mixed-valence and local moment behaviors of the Eu-ions observed experimentally, but also predicts low-energy 4$f$ quasiparticles that may hybridize with the Ni-3$d$ electrons and contribute to the elevated $T_c$. Our work provides a basis for understanding the unusual ferromagnetic and superconducting properties of Eu-substituted infinite-layer nickelates.

</details>


### [402] [Electronic structure and anisotropic magnetotransport in the topological kagome ferromagnet MgMn6Sn6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23699)
*Debasmita Swain,Mohit Mudgal Suman Nandi,Aradhana Kumari,Yogendra Kumar,Vivek Kumar Malik,Anup Pradhan Sakhya,Masahiro Sawada,Shin-ichiro Ideta,Kenya Shimada,Jayita Nayak*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文综合磁化、磁电阻、XMCD、ARPES与第一性原理计算，系统研究了kagome铁磁体MgMn6Sn6的磁性与电子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究kagome铁磁体中磁各向异性、各向异性磁输运与kagome衍生电子结构之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用磁化测量、角度依赖磁电阻、X射线磁圆二色（XMCD）、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）及第一性原理计算。

Result: MgMn6Sn6在TC≈295 K附近铁磁有序，具明显易面磁各向异性；低温低场下MR强烈依赖磁场取向，由面内负MR向面外更接近二次方行为演变，并呈显著二重各向异性叠加高阶贡献；XMCD给出有限轨道/自旋矩比，表明自旋轨道耦合作用显著；计算显示K点Dirac型能带交叉与M点van Hove奇异性；ARPES分辨出六重对称费米面及其随结合能的系统演化，与计算总体一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了MgMn6Sn6中磁各向异性、各向异性磁输运与kagome衍生电子结构之间的联系。

Abstract: We report the magnetic, magnetotransport, and electronic properties of the kagome ferromag net MgMn6Sn6 using magnetization, angle dependent magnetoresistance, x-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD), angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), and first-principles cal culations. MgMn6Sn6 exhibits ferromagnetic ordering near TC=295 K with pronounced easy plane magnetic anisotropy. At low temperatures and low magnetic fields, the magnetoresistance (MR) is strongly anisotropic with respect to the magnetic field orientation, evolving from a predominantly negative MR for in-plane fields to a more quadratic behavior for out-of-plane fields. Angle depen dent measurements further reveal a pronounced twofold MR anisotropy with additional higher order contributions. The finite orbital-to-spin moment ratio revealed by XMCD suggests a significant role of spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in MgMn6Sn6. The first-principles calculations show a Dirac-like band crossing at the K point and a van Hove singularity (VHS) at the M point, as expected for kagome materials. ARPES measurements resolve a sixfold symmetric Fermi surface and its systematic evo lution with binding energy, in overall agreement with first-principles calculations. The measured band dispersions are also broadly consistent with the calculated multiband electronic structure.
