These results establish the connection between magnetic anisotropy, anisotropic magnetotransport, and the kagome derived electronic structure of MgMn6Sn6.

</details>


### [403] [Decoupling the Magnetic Field Operator as an Independent Operator Class in Stochastic Series Expansion Quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23978)
*Shijie Jin,Lu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种更通用的随机级数展开（SSE）量子蒙特卡洛框架，将磁场算符作为独立算符类参与环更新，并在二维反铁磁海森堡模型磁场下验证其正确性与竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SSE方法通常把磁场项吸收进海森堡相互作用中，虽高效但限制了算符处理的灵活性；作者希望让磁场算符与其他算符平等参与更新，从而提升对相关可观测量测量及更广问题的适用性。

Method: 在SSE框架中把磁场算符设为独立算符类，使其在Monte Carlo更新中与所有其他算符同等对待，并允许磁场算符与相互作用算符在更新过程中相互转化；以二维反铁磁海森堡模型在外磁场下的模拟作为示例实现该算法。

Result: 新算法的模拟结果与常规算法一致，验证了该新表述的正确性；积分自关联时间的比较表明新算法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将磁场算符作为独立算符类参与更新的通用SSE框架可行且正确，不仅便于测量与算符相关的可观测量，还可能为更广泛的问题提供计算优势。

Abstract: The Stochastic Series Expansion (SSE) quantum Monte Carlo method with loop updates is among the most powerful approaches for quantum spin and boson systems. In the standard formulation, the magnetic field term is routinely absorbed into the Heisenberg interactions---a strategy that has proven highly efficient across a wide range of field strengths. In this work, we propose a more general SSE framework in which the magnetic field operator is treated as an independent operator class, enabling it to participate in Monte Carlo updates on an equal footing with all other operators. Importantly, the field operator and other interaction operators can transform into one another during the update process. We demonstrate this algorithm using the two-dimensional antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model in a magnetic field as a concrete example. The simulation results agree with those of the conventional algorithm, confirming the correctness of the new formulation. A comparison of the integrated autocorrelation time shows that the new algorithm is competitive. This flexibility not only facilitates measurements of observables associated with operators but also offers potential computational advantages for a broader class of problems.

</details>


### [404] [A Majorana Formulation of Time-Dependent Two-Particle Reduced-Density-Matrix Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23993)
*Haoran Wang,Alessandro Principi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对相互作用 Majorana 费米子体系的含时双粒子约化密度矩阵（TP-RDM）算法，通过在双粒子层次截断 BBGKY 层级并施加保能量等守恒量的正定性投影来稳定演化，在 Hubbard 模型淬火与 Kitaev 蜂窝模型通量动力学上验证了其相对 Hartree-Fock 的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时相互作用量子多体体系的精确模拟代价高昂，单粒子（Hartree-Fock）近似又会遗漏重要的双粒子关联。作者希望在可承受的计算成本下，既能捕捉强关联动力学，又能保持密度矩阵的物理性质（正定性）与守恒量，从而稳定长时间演化。

Method: 构建双粒子约化密度矩阵的含时演化算法：在双粒子层次关闭 BBGKY 层级，通过由低阶关联重构三粒子约化密度矩阵实现截断；为保证数值稳定，对双粒子密度矩阵施加正定性投影，同时保持能量等选定守恒量不变；在一维 Hubbard 模型淬火与 Kitaev 蜂窝模型通量动力学上进行基准测试。

Result: 在 Hubbard 淬火中，TP 捕捉到 Hartree-Fock 遗漏的强耦合动力学；在 Kitaev 淬火中，TP 在多个参数区间准确复现早期通量动力学，并在双粒子关联重要时显著优于 Hartree-Fock。作者进一步表明规范相干性对通量动力学是必需的。此外，投影过程引入了有效不可逆性，影响长时间动力学。

Conclusion: 含时双粒子约化密度矩阵方法在兼顾计算效率与关联精度方面具有优势，适用于相互作用 Majorana 费米子体系，并能改进单粒子近似。其局限在于正定性投影带来有效不可逆性，制约了长时间动力学的可靠性，未来需在保持物理约束与长时间精度之间进一步权衡。

Abstract: We introduce a time-dependent two-particle (TP) reduced-density-matrix algorithm for systems of interacting Majorana fermions. We close the BBGKY hierarchy at the two-particle level by reconstructing the three-particle reduced density matrix from lower-order correlations. To stabilize the evolution, we impose a positivity projection on the two-particle density matrix while preserving chosen conserved quantities such as the energy. We benchmark the approach on quenches in the one-dimensional Hubbard model and on flux dynamics in the Kitaev honeycomb model. For the Hubbard quench, TP captures strong-coupling dynamics missed by Hartree--Fock (HF). For the Kitaev quenches, TP accurately reproduces the early-time flux dynamics in several regimes and provides a substantial improvement over HF when two-particle correlations are important. We further show that gauge coherence is essential for flux dynamics. The projection procedure however introduces an effective irreversibility, which affects the long-time dynamics.

</details>


### [405] [Carrier duality from the convergence of Dirac fermions and high-order van Hove singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24044)
*Meng Lyu,Kaiyi Zhai,Nikolai Peshcherenko,Junyan Liu,Jinying Yang,Subir Sen,Binbin Wang,Langsheng Ling,Zhaosheng Wang,Gang Li,Jieyi Liu,Yang Xu,Xiyang Li,Claudia Felser,Yang Zhang,Wujun Shi,Lexian Yang,Enke Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Kagome金属Co3In2S2中观察到高迁移率电子与重电子共存的双载流子区域，源于拓扑Dirac费米子与平带高阶Van Hove奇点（HOVHS）在费米面共存，并表现为非费米液体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高迁移率Dirac费米子与平带重电子的结合是涌现量子现象的理想平台，但此类状态在实验上一直难以实现。

Method: 采用磁扭矩量子振荡（magnetic-torque quantum oscillation）与角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）探测Co3In2S2的费米面结构，并结合输运与热力学测量，用最小双口袋模型（two-pocket model）分析Dirac费米子与HOVHS载流子的相互作用。

Result: Co3In2S2呈现双载流子区域：电荷输运由高迁移率电子主导，而热力学响应显示重电子行为；费米面处拓扑Dirac费米子与4阶Van Hove奇点共存；相互作用表现为约100 K以上的亚线性电阻率和低温下磁场诱导的非费米液体行为。

Conclusion: Co3In2S2可作为探索拓扑与关联效应交叉领域多体物理的模型平台，并为设计承载多样涌现态的量子材料提供基础框架。

Abstract: The convergence of highly mobile Dirac fermions and flat-band heavy electrons offers a paradigm for emergent quantum phenomena. However, experimental realization of such intriguing state remains elusive. In this study, we report a dual carrier regime in the kagome metal Co3In2S2, wherein the charge transport is governed by high-mobility electrons while the thermodynamic responses exhibit heavy-electron behavior. This exotic duality arises from the coexistence of topological Dirac fermions and flat-band high-order (4th-order) Van Hove singularities (HOVHSs) at the Fermi surface, as revealed by magnetic-torque quantum oscillation and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. The interaction between Dirac and HOVHS-derived carriers can be captured by a minimal two-pocket model, manifesting as sublinear resistivity above ~100 K and field-induced non-Fermi-liquid behavior at low temperatures. Our study establishes Co3In2S2 as a model platform for exploring many-body physics at the intersection of topology and correlation effects, and provides a foundational framework for designing quantum materials hosting diverse emergent states.

</details>


### [406] [Slave-Rotor and Auxiliary Chain Analysis of the Mott Transition in a Modified Periodic Anderson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24315)
*Ankur Majumder,Sudeshna Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用从属转子平均场理论研究三轨道周期Anderson模型的Mott-Hubbard转变，引入双格簇考虑短程空间关联，并构建辅助半无限链框架探讨该转变的拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mott-Hubbard转变是强电子关联驱动的典型现象，但短程空间关联对其转变点的影响以及该转变是否具有拓扑特征仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在三轨道修正周期Anderson模型中运用从属转子平均场理论，引入最小双格簇扩展以刻画短程空间关联；并进一步计算动力学自能，映射为非相互作用半无限辅助链的边界格林函数。

Result: 与单格解相比，短程空间关联导致转变点发生移动；金属相的跳跃模式对应拓扑平庸的广义Su–Schrieffer–Heeger链。

Conclusion: 该辅助链构造为探究该模型中Mott转变可能的拓扑特征提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The Mott-Hubbard transition is a paradigmatic phenomenon driven by strong electronic correlations. We investigate this transition in a three-orbital modified periodic Anderson model using slave-rotor mean-field theory. A minimal two-site cluster extension is introduced to understand the minimal effects of short-range spatial correlations. In comparison to the single-site solution, short-range spatial correlations reveal a shift of the transition point. Going beyond the slave-rotor analysis, we also evaluate the dynamical self-energy and map it onto the boundary Green's function of a non-interacting semi-infinite auxiliary chain. For the metallic phase, the resulting hopping pattern corresponds to a topologically trivial generalized Su--Schrieffer--Heeger chain. This construction provides a framework for exploring the possible topological character of the Mott transition in this model.

</details>


### [407] [Spin-Orbit Induced Confinement of Correlated Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24429)
*Kai Chen,Junyan Guan,Zhongming Gu,Jie Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文证明一维Fermi-Hubbard模型中的自旋轨道耦合可将排斥束缚的双占据态（doublon）驱动进入双粒子散射连续谱，其中大部分态与扩展通道杂化并衰变，但一部分保持解耦与空间束缚，形成相互作用诱导的连续谱束缚态（BIC）；作者通过耦合声学腔二维晶格映射并实验观测了辐射连续谱与BIC态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排斥束缚doublon通常与散射连续谱分离；引入自旋轨道耦合会改变能带结构，可作为调控手段将其推向连续谱，但进入该区域往往导致立即解离。因此，能否在保持束缚性质的同时将doublon驱动进入连续谱，是一个尚未解决的基础问题。

Method: 在一维Fermi-Hubbard模型中引入自旋轨道耦合，分析两粒子问题；将相互作用两粒子问题映射到耦合声学腔的二维晶格上，并在该平台上进行实验观测。

Result: 自旋轨道耦合可将doublon驱动到双粒子散射连续谱中；大多数此类态与扩展通道杂化并衰变，而一部分保持解耦且空间束缚，形成多体连续谱束缚态（BIC）。实验上观测到辐射型doublon连续谱和局域BIC态。

Conclusion: 自旋轨道耦合可将选定的doublon转化为相互作用诱导的BIC，深化了对相互作用束缚对连续谱物理的理解。

Abstract: Repulsively bound doublons are two-particle composites formed by strong interactions and are usually separated from the scattering continuum. Introducing spin-orbit coupling fundamentally alters the underlying band structure, providing a powerful tuning knob to shift these isolated pairs toward this continuum. However, because entering such a regime typically dictates immediate dissociation, whether this coupling can drive these pairs inside while preserving their bound nature constitutes a fundamental unresolved challenge. Here we show that spin-orbit coupling in the one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model can drive doublons into the two-particle scattering continuum. Most of these states hybridize with extended channels and decay, whereas a subset remains decoupled and spatially bound, forming many-body bound states in the continuum (BICs). We map the interacting two-particle problem onto a two-dimensional lattice of coupled acoustic cavities, and experimentally observe both the radiating doublon continuum and the confined BIC states. These results demonstrate that spin-orbit coupling can turn selected doublons into interaction-induced BICs, deepening the understanding of continuum physics for interaction-bound pairs.

</details>


### [408] [Charge-4e Superconducting Ground State without Pair Condensation: Exact Quartet Dynamics, Rigorous Order, and a Microscopic Route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24445)
*Jin-Tao Jin,Pengfei Li,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文围绕"直接电荷4e超导体"（四电子凝聚而所有2e配对通道不凝聚）建立三项互补结果：可解最小母模型及其精确性质、有限程费米子族的严格四体ODLRO证明、以及仅用电子跃迁与两体相互作用导出的强耦合实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷4e超导是一种反常的超导有序形态：四电子相干凝聚而电荷2e配对通道却完全不凝聚。既有研究缺乏一个既能精确求解又能刻画其普适临界性质的最小模型，也缺乏对相互作用型费米子模型中"有四体ODLRO但无2e ODLRO"的严格证明，以及从纯电子跳跃与两体相互作用出发的物理实现机制。

Method: 1) 在Yoshida–Katsura的η-聚类态与二分母模型基础上，构造任意连通图上两项最小母哈密顿量并精确求解；通过映射到经典硬核排除动力学得到固定粒子数扇区的完整能隙与四体密度模支。2) 构造具显式四体转移的有限程费米子模型族，在强在位惩罚下证明半四体填充时四体ODLRO存在而2e ODLRO不存在（超立方XY点d≥2及方格子XXZ有限区间）。3) 用电子跳迁与两体相互作用推导强耦合实现：局域能隙U0下，配对跳跃K在K²/U0阶产生四体运动，电子跳跃t首现于t⁴/U0³阶，而2e激发保持O(U0)能隙。

Result: 最小两项母模型：固定数基态具四体ODLRO、无2e ODLRO；非零四体刚度与消失的逆四体压缩率表明其为z=2的相分离边界。有限程费米子族：在半四体填充下严格证明四体ODLRO且无2e ODLRO。强耦合实现：四体运动主导（K²/U0≫t⁴/U0³），2e激发有O(U0)能隙；二分格子上正逆四体压缩率给出受控均匀z=1区间（短程对关联），负曲率导致相分离。

Conclusion: 四电子相干有序可以在缺少任何电荷2e凝聚的情况下独立存在，并被一个精确可解的最小母模型、一类可严格证明的费米子模型以及一个纯电子跳跃+两体相互作用的强耦合机制三方面一致刻画。四体刚度与四体压缩率的符号区分了均匀四体超导相与相分离行为。

Abstract: A direct charge-\(4e\) superconductor exhibits coherent four-electron order while every charge-\(2e\) pairing channel remains uncondensed. We establish three complementary results. First, building on the \(η\)-clustering states and bipartite parent of Yoshida and Katsura, we formulate and exactly solve a minimal two-term parent on any connected graph. Its fixed-number ground states have quartet off-diagonal long-range order (ODLRO) without charge-\(2e\) ODLRO, while an exact mapping to classical hard-core exclusion dynamics yields the full fixed-sector gap and a branch of quartet-density modes. Nonzero quartet stiffness and vanishing inverse quartet compressibility identify this \(z=2\) parent as a phase-separation boundary. Second, for a finite-range fermionic family with explicit quartet transfer and sufficiently large onsite penalty, we rigorously prove quartet ODLRO without charge-\(2e\) ODLRO at half quartet filling, both at the hypercubic XY point for \(d\geq2\) and throughout a finite XXZ interval on the square lattice. Third, we derive a strong-coupling realization using only electron hopping and two-body interactions. With local gap \(U_0\), pair hopping \(K\) generates quartet motion at order \(K^2/U_0\), whereas electron hopping \(t\) first contributes at order \(t^4/U_0^3\); charge-\(2e\) excitations remain gapped at \(O(U_0)\). On bipartite lattices, positive inverse quartet compressibility opens an asymptotically controlled homogeneous \(z=1\) regime with short-ranged pair correlations, while negative curvature drives phase separation.

</details>


### [409] [Carrier-mediated spin helices in low-dimensional systems: Josephson-interference features and correlation renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24502)
*Yung-Yeh Chang,Chen-Hsuan Hsu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合线滑移Luttinger液体中由载流子诱导的螺旋磁性，分析螺旋序参量、载流子能隙重构及其在约瑟夫森结中的输运特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维相互作用系统耦合局域磁矩可产生载流子诱导的螺旋磁性，但其在耦合线滑移Luttinger液体框架下（含/不含谷自由度）的集体行为及可输运探测手段尚不清晰。

Method: 以孤立Tomonaga-Luttinger液体为参考极限，研究含与不含谷自由度的耦合线滑移Luttinger液体；载流子自旋磁化率中介RKKY作用选择螺旋序波矢，通道间关联锁定相对螺旋相位形成准二维织构；用变分处理估算螺旋诱导的部分能隙并分析剩余无能隙载流子模重构；将螺旋纳入各向异性超导-正常金属-超导约瑟夫森结，利用界面通量聚焦使面内场成为自旋螺旋的可控探针。

Result: 相较无螺旋结，螺旋交换场改变中心瓣宽度和相对瓣权重；在横向通道几何中还产生依赖磁场的侧瓣不对称和中心峰位移，而在匹配参考参数下可忽略。重构后的载流子扇区呈现修正的局域态密度指数、自旋弛豫行为和竞争关联，在孤立与耦合线系统中具有不同特征。

Conclusion: 研究将耦合线系统中的集体螺旋序与载流子扇区重构联系起来，并确立场可调约瑟夫森干涉作为空间自旋织构的互补输运探针。

Abstract: Carrier-induced helical magnetism can arise in interacting one-dimensional systems coupled to localized moments. Here, we investigate the coupled-wire sliding-Luttinger-liquid counterparts with and without valley degrees of freedom, using isolated Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids as reference limits. The carrier spin susceptibility mediates a Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida interaction that selects the helical ordering wave vector, while interchannel correlations lock the relative helix phases and stabilize a collective quasi-two-dimensional texture. Using a variational treatment, we estimate the helix-induced partial gap and analyze the reconstruction of the remaining gapless carrier modes. To probe the spatial texture through transport, we incorporate the helix into an anisotropic superconductor-normal-metal-superconductor Josephson junction motivated by the coupled-channel structure of twisted bilayer WTe$_2$. We exploit flux focusing near the junction interfaces to make an applied in-plane field a controlled probe of the spin helix. Relative to junctions without the helix, the helical exchange field modifies the central-lobe width and relative lobe weights. In the transverse-channel geometry, it also produces field-dependent side-lobe asymmetry and central-peak displacement, which remain negligible in the matched references for the parameters considered. The reconstructed carrier sector also exhibits modified local-density-of-states exponents, spin-relaxation behavior, and competing correlations, with distinct signatures in isolated and coupled-wire systems. Our results connect collective helical order in coupled-wire systems to carrier-sector reconstruction and identify field-tunable Josephson interferometry as a complementary transport probe of the spatial spin texture.

</details>


### [410] [Raman Scattering Evidence for Fluctuating Kagome-Plane Moments in $\mathrm{B} \parallel [111]$ in Pr$_2$Zr$_2$O$_7$ pyrochlore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24696)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Huiyuan Man,Nan Tang,Li Xiang,Sami Muhammad,Komalavalli Thirunavukkuarasu,Dmitry Smirnov,Satoru Nakatsuji,Natalia Drichko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用磁场下的磁拉曼光谱探测Pr₂Zr₂O₇中Pr³⁺的晶体电场激发，发现沿[100]方向的行为可由常规Zeeman劈裂描述，而沿[111]方向kagome子晶格的激发不劈裂反而展宽，可用动致窄化（motional narrowing）描述，暗示kagome面内磁矩在meV时间尺度涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烧绿石Pr₂Zr₂O₇的奇异磁性由自旋、轨道和晶格自由度耦合决定，其磁性来自非Kramers离子Pr³⁺，现有讨论分别基于磁矩的偶极-四极性质与破坏非Kramers双重态的无序效应。作者希望借助磁场下的磁拉曼光谱直接探测Pr³⁺的晶体电场（CEF）激发，以厘清其磁场响应机制。

Method: 在2 K温度下，对Pr₂Zr₂O₇进行磁拉曼光谱测量，外加磁场最高达14 T，分别沿[100]和[111]晶向施加。通过追踪拉曼活性晶体电场模式的磁场演化，分析Pr³⁺基态双重态的Zeeman劈裂行为，并与理论模型（常规Zeeman劈裂及动致窄化模型）进行定量比较。

Result: 对于B∥[100]，晶体电场模式的磁场演化可由Pr³⁺基态双重态的常规Zeeman劈裂定量描述，包括升温时热激发的上Zeeman分支。对于B∥[111]，谱线分离为三角和kagome子晶格的响应：三角晶格上Pr³⁺的CEF激发表现出符合完全磁矩极化的Zeeman位移；而kagome晶格上的CEF激发不按预期劈裂，反而随磁场增强而强烈展宽。该行为与静态极化构型不符，可用kagome面内磁矩在Zeeman劈裂能级间以meV时间尺度涨落的动致窄化描述加以唯象解释。

Conclusion: kagome子晶格的动致窄化行为为B∥[111]下观测到的磁化强度降低提供了自然解释，并进一步支持磁矩具有偶极-四极性质。该工作表明拉曼散射是探测阻挫磁体中奇异自旋-轨道动力学的灵敏手段。

Abstract: The exotic magnetic properties of the pyrochlore Pr$_2$Zr$_2$O$_7$ are determined by the coupling of spin, orbital, and lattice degrees of freedom. Magnetism in this material originates from the non-Kramers Pr$^{3+}$ ion, and the exotic behavior has been discussed both in the framework of dipole-quadrupole properties of magnetic moments and disorder splitting the non-Kramers doublet. We use magneto-Raman spectroscopy to probe the crystal electric field (CEF) excitations of Pr$^{3+}$ in magnetic fields up to 14 T applied along the [100] and [111] crystallographic directions at 2 K. For $\mathrm{B}\parallel[100]$, the field evolution of the Raman active crystal field modes is quantitatively described by conventional Zeeman splitting of the Pr$^{3+}$ ground state doublet, including the thermally populated upper Zeeman branch at elevated temperature. For $\mathrm{}{B}\parallel[111]$, the spectra separate into responses from the triangular and kagome sublattices, and the CEF excitations of Pr$^{3+}$ on the triangular lattice exhibit the expected Zeeman shift consistent with full moments polarization. In contrast, the CEF excitations of Pr$^{3+}$ on the kagome lattice do not split as expected and instead broaden strongly with increasing field. This behavior is inconsistent with a static polarized configuration and is captured phenomenologically by a motional-narrowing description in which the kagome plane moments fluctuate between Zeeman-split levels on a meV timescale. This result offers a natural explanation for the reduced magnetization observed in $\mathrm{}{B}\parallel[111]$ and provides further evidence of the dipole-quadrupole properties of magnetic moments. It highlights Raman scattering as a sensitive probe of exotic spin-orbital dynamics in frustrated magnets.

</details>


### [411] [Infrared evidence for strong $C_{3}$ symmetry breaking in 1$T$-TiSe$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24705)
*Esther van Grondelle,Kai Rossnagel,Jasper van Wezel,Erik van Heumen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用红外光谱研究1T-TiSe₂的电荷密度波相变，发现E_u光学声子在T_CDW≈190 K发生劈裂，直接证明三重旋转对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料中晶格与电子自由度的相互作用导致竞争有序态与相继的对称性破缺相变，各相往往仅通过残余点群对称性区分，差异细微，因此需要灵敏的对称性探针来辨别相变路径与序参量。

Method: 采用红外光学光谱测量1T-TiSe₂在电荷密度波相变附近的对称性破缺，追踪双重简并E_u光学声子的劈裂行为及其线宽随温度的变化。

Result: 在T_CDW≈190 K观察到此前未发现的E_u简并声子劈裂，构成三重旋转对称性破缺的直接证据；两个原E_u伴峰的能量差只能由与键长变化同量级的大C₃破缺畸变解释；T_CDW以上单一声子模的线宽与三重对称性亦被破缺的涨落态相符。

Conclusion: 研究排除了CDW经保持三重对称的中间态向低温手性/向列相转变的设想，表明C₃对称性破缺在CDW相变中起主导作用。

Abstract: The interplay between lattice and electron degrees of freedom gives rise to competing ordered states in quantum materials, which can lead to a series of subsequent symmetry breaking transitions. While the differences are often subtle, these phases can be distinguished by their remaining point group symmetries. Here, we use infrared optical spectroscopy to probe symmetry breaking at the charge density wave transition of 1$T$-TiSe$_{2}$. We uncover a previously unobserved splitting of a doubly degenerate $E_u$ optical phonon at $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}\approx$ 190 K that constitutes direct evidence for the breaking of three-fold rotational symmetry. Our finding rules out proposals where the CDW transition to a low temperature chiral or nematic phase takes place through an intermediary state that preserves three-fold symmetry. The energy difference between the two former $E_{u}$ partners can only be explained by large $C_{3}$ breaking distortions of the same order of magnitude as previously observed changes in bond lengths, suggesting that $C_{3}$ symmetry breaking plays a dominant role in the CDW transition. The linewidth of the single phonon mode above $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$ could be consistent with a fluctuating state where three-fold symmetry is also broken.

</details>


### [412] [Rapidly prototyping kagome flat band physics with acoustic metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24721)
*Jiatong Yang,Benjamin H. November,Yiting Huang,S. Minhal Gardezi,Harris Pirie,Jennifer E. Hoffman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出基于声学超材料的平带系统快速原型工作流：用空气腔作为格点、通道控制跃迁以直接实现紧束缚模型，并在kagome晶格上验证理论与实验一致，进而设计出逐级抵消长程跃迁、使平带更平坦的扩展kagome晶格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带具有零群速度与淬灭动能，是关联态和非常规超导等奇异现象的温床；但在量子材料中工程化与表征平带耗时且昂贵，限制了对其广阔设计空间的探索。声学超材料易仿真、低成本、可快速制备与测量，为快速筛选平带候选体系提供了可行替代方案。

Method: 设计上用空气腔作为晶格格点、以连接通道调控跃迁，从而直接实现紧束缚模型，并可推广至不同晶格几何。以kagome晶格作为概念验证，将紧束缚理论、有限元仿真与实验测量进行交叉比对；随后设计一族扩展kagome晶格，通过新增格点逐级抵消更长程跃迁以压平平带。

Result: 紧束缚理论、有限元仿真与实验测量三者高度吻合；扩展kagome晶格中被逐级抵消更长程跃迁后，平带被显著压平。制备与测量以小时而非月计，总成本比量子材料低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该声学超材料平台支持对候选晶格进行快速迭代，可加速平带物理的发现，并为量子材料中新型平带体系的探索提供高效预筛途径。

Abstract: Flat bands with vanishing group velocity and quenched kinetic energy provide fertile ground for correlated states and exotic phenomena such as unconventional superconductivity. Yet, engineering and characterizing flat bands in quantum materials is time-consuming and costly, limiting the exploration of the vast design space of possible flat band systems. Acoustic metamaterials offer an accessible alternative: they can be easily simulated, cheaply fabricated, and quickly measured. Here we present a complete workflow to rapidly prototype flat band systems using acoustic metamaterials. Our design directly implements a tight-binding model using air cavities as lattice sites connected by channels that control hopping, allowing it to generalize to diverse lattice geometries. Using the kagome lattice as a proof-of-concept, we demonstrate excellent agreement between tight-binding theory, finite-element simulations, and experimental measurements. We then design a family of extended kagome lattices whose added sites cancel successively longer-range hopping, flattening the flat band. With fabrication and measurement requiring hours rather than months and a total cost orders of magnitude lower than quantum materials, our approach enables rapid iteration through candidate lattices, facilitating the discovery of new flat band physics in quantum materials.

</details>


### [413] [Anisotropic Surface State Band Splitting and Low Energy Flat Bands in 3d Correlated Topological Kondo Insulator Candidate FeSb$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24854)
*Ziling Cao,Jie Pang,Yu Xu,Taimin Miao,Bo Liang,Wenpei Zhu,Neng Cai,Mingkai Xu,Jumin Shi,Yingjie Shu,Yiwen Chen,Jiachen Wang,Shenjin Zhang,Fengfeng Zhang,Feng Yang,Zhimin Wang,Qinjun Peng,Zhihai Zhu,Xintong Li,Hanqing Mao,Guodong Liu,Zuyan Xu,Youguo Shi,Lin Zhao,X. J. Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用高分辨激光角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)系统研究了关联窄带半导体FeSb₂的电子结构，发现布里渊区中心附近表面态具有各向异性劈裂，进入电阻平台区(<6 K)后表面态依然稳健但谱峰更锐、谱重增强，并观察到两个平带式特征（~127 meV与~70 meV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FeSb₂是常被讨论为3d电子Kondo绝缘体候选者的关联窄带半导体，在低温下表现出电阻平台并可能存在表面主导的导电行为；其电子结构、电阻平台起源及拓扑性质尚不清晰，因此需要系统的高分辨ARPES研究加以澄清。

Method: 采用高分辨激光角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)对FeSb₂进行系统的变温测量，重点考察布里渊区中心附近表面态的各向异性劈裂行为，以及降温至电阻平台区(<6 K)时谱峰形状、谱重与低能平带特征的变化。

Result: 区中心附近的表面态表现出清晰的各向异性劈裂；温度降至电阻平台区(<6 K)时，表面态保持稳健，但光电子谱峰显著变锐并谱重增强。低能区观察到两个明显不同的平带式特征：一个位于费米能级下方约127 meV处，仅沿特定高对称方向存在；另一个位于费米能级下方约70 meV处，在所有测量的区中心附近动量切面上均出现。

Conclusion: 这些结果为进一步理解FeSb₂中的重整化效应、电阻平台行为及其拓扑本质提供了新的关键信息。

Abstract: FeSb$_2$ is a correlated narrow-gap semiconductor that has often been discussed as a $3d$-electron Kondo insulator candidate and exhibits a low-temperature resistance plateau with possible surface-dominated conduction. We carried out a systematic high-resolution laser-based angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) study of FeSb$_2$ to investigate its electronic structure. The surface states around the zone center show clear anisotropic splitting. When the temperature is lowered into the resistance plateau regime ($<6\,\mathrm{K}$), the surface states remain robust, but their photoemission peaks become much sharper and gain spectral weight. Two distinct flat-band-like features are observed at low energy. One is located at $\sim$127 meV below the Fermi level, which exists only along a specific high-symmetry direction, while the other is located at $\sim$70 meV below the Fermi level and is present along all the measured momentum cuts around the zone center. These results provide new information to understand the renormalization effects, the resistance plateau, and the topological nature of FeSb$_2$.

</details>


### [414] [Quantum-interference-driven orbital density wave and high-temperature superconductivity in trilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24857)
*Youichi Yamakawa,Hiroshi Kontani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一微观框架，用含顶点修正的密度波方程解释三层镍酸盐中交织的密度波序与高温超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温超导多层镍酸盐中电荷密度波（CDW）与自旋密度波（SDW）序交织，且 CDW 波矢满足 Qcdw ≈ 2Qsdw，其微观机制尚不清晰，需要统一理论解释层选择性电子重构与超导共存现象。

Method: 采用含顶点修正的密度波方程，分析外层 NiO2 层 q ≈ Qsdw 的短程 SDW 涨落之间的量子干涉效应。

Result: 发现量子干涉在外层间生成 Qcdw ≈ 2Qsdw 的键序，进而诱导以内层为中心的轨道序，表现为 Ni d3z2-r2 与 dx2-y2 占据的反相位调制；产生强轨道极化但总电荷调制很弱。该交织键-轨道序解释了 NMR/NQR 推断的层选择性电子重构，并与拉曼光谱和扫描隧道显微镜测量一致；轨道与自旋涨落通过 Mz 镜面宇称选择规则协同稳定 s± 波超导。

Conclusion: 研究为多层镍酸盐中交织密度波序与高温超导提供了统一的微观框架。

Abstract: Intertwined charge-density-wave (CDW) and spin-density-wave (SDW) orders are a hallmark of high-temperature superconducting multilayer nickelates. In trilayer La4Ni3O_{10}, charge correlations develop at temperatures above the onset of long-range spin order, and the characteristic ordering wavevectors satisfy $Q_{cdw} \approx 2Q_{sdw}$. Here, using a density-wave equation with vertex corrections, we show that quantum interference between short-range SDW fluctuations at $q \approx Q_{sdw}$ on the outer NiO2 layers generates an inter-outer-layer bond order at $Q_{cdw} \approx 2 Q_{sdw}$. This bond order induces a pronounced inner-layer-centered orbital order, with antiphase modulations of the Ni $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ and $d_{x^2-y^2}$ occupations, producing strong orbital polarization but only weak total charge modulation. This intertwined bond-and-orbital order accounts for the layer-selective electronic reconstruction inferred from NMR/NQR and is consistent with Raman spectroscopy and scanning tunnelling microscopy measurements. The same orbital and spin fluctuations also cooperate to stabilize $s_{\pm}$-wave superconductivity through $M_z$ mirror-parity selection rules. Our results provide a unified microscopic framework for intertwined density-wave order and high-Tc superconductivity in multilayer nickelates.

</details>


### [415] [Staged emergence of anomalous Hall transport in a correlated uranium Weyl semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24897)
*Sabin Regmi,Shuxiang Zhou,Chandan K. Singh,Alexei Fedorov,Jonathan Denlinger,Zeyu Ma,Yidi Wang,Jennifer E. Hoffman,Peter M. Oppeneer,Dariusz Kaczorowski,Tomasz Durakiewicz,Krzysztof Gofryk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合磁输运、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、热力学测量与第一性原理计算，研究了铀铁磁体UPS中电子关联对拓扑态的调控，揭示磁序、关联电子重构与反常霍尔输运在相互重叠但不同的温区中演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电子关联如何重塑拓扑量子态是量子材料研究的核心挑战之一；铀基5f电子的巡游/局域二重性为研究强关联与拓扑相互作用提供了理想平台，但其具体机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用磁输运测量、角分辨光电子能谱（含共振光电子能谱）、热力学测量与第一性原理计算相结合的多手段综合实验与理论方法。

Result: 共振光电子谱在费米能级处观察到窄的U-5f谱权重与宽的非相干态共存；反常霍尔电导率约为4.5×10² Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹，但不简单跟随有序磁矩；变温光电子谱显示在T_C=118 K附近变化较小，而在约90 K以下出现低能5f谱权重的显著重新分布；第一性原理计算识别出具有显著贝里曲率的对称性保护的Weyl交叉点，并给出约9.6×10² Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹的本征反常霍尔电导率。

Conclusion: 磁序、关联电子重构与反常霍尔输运在既相互区别又相互重叠的温区中发展，表明强关联显著重塑了铀基Weyl半金属的拓扑输运行为。

Abstract: Understanding how electronic correlations reshape topological states remains a central challenge in quantum materials. Here we investigate the uranium ferromagnet UPS using magnetotransport, angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, thermodynamic measurements, and first principles calculations. Resonant photoemission reveals narrow U-$5f$ spectral weight at the Fermi level coexisting with broad incoherent states, consistent with the itinerant/localized duality characteristic of uranium $5f$ electrons. The anomalous Hall conductivity reaches approximately $4.5\times10^{2}\,Ω^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$, yet does not simply follow the ordered magnetic moment. Temperature dependent photoemission reveals relatively little change across $T_C=118$~K, followed by a pronounced redistribution of low-energy $5f$ spectral weight below approximately 90~K. First-principles calculations identify a symmetry protected Weyl crossing with pronounced Berry curvature and yield an intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity of approximately $9.6\times10^{2}\,Ω^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$. These results demonstrate that magnetic order, correlated electronic reconstruction, and anomalous Hall transport develop over distinct but overlapping temperature ranges, revealing how strong correlations reshape topological transport in a uranium Weyl semimetal.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [416] [Moving Geometric Objects to Render Their Intersection Graph Connected or Locally Dense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22958)
*Tesshu Hanaka,Nicolás Honorato-Droguett,Hirotaka Ono,Samuel Wolf,Alexander Wolff*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究几何交图的编辑问题：通过移动几何对象使交图满足预定性质，并最小化总移动距离，给出了区间图在clique、连通性等性质下的算法与硬度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何交图上的图编辑问题具有实际意义，但现有工作多关注边编辑，缺少对"移动几何对象"这一自然编辑操作的系统研究。作者希望填补这一空白，针对局部稠密（含k-clique）与k-连通性等性质，设计高效精确算法并刻画问题复杂度。

Method: 将问题形式化为寻找移动向量D使交图G_{S+D}满足目标性质且总距离|D|（或加权|w·D|）最小。对含k-clique性质，针对加权区间用O(k^{1/3} n log^{1+ε} n)时间算法；对k-连通性，针对无权单位区间用O(n^2 log n)时间、k=1时O(n log n)时间。硬度方面归约证明：任意长度区间与含两种权重的加权单位圆盘上k=1为强NP-hard；加权区间（长度等于权重）上为弱NP-hard。

Result: 加权区间含k-clique性质：O(k^{1/3} n log^{1+ε} n)。无权单位区间k-连通：O(n^2 log n)，k=1：O(n log n)。k=1时：任意长度区间及仅两种权重的加权单位圆盘强NP-hard；加权区间（长度=权重）弱NP-hard。

Conclusion: 论文系统研究了交图上的几何移动编辑问题，明确了在区间与圆盘等结构下，局部稠密与连通性问题的可解性边界，揭示了移动编辑相对边编辑在复杂度上的显著差异。

Abstract: In this paper, we study graph editing problems on geometric intersection graphs. For a tuple $\mathcal{S}=(S_1,\dots,S_n)$ of geometric objects in some Euclidean space, let $G_\mathcal{S}$ be their intersection graph. We study the problem of finding a tuple $D=(d_1,\dots,d_n)$ of movement vectors such that the resulting intersection graph $G_{\mathcal{S}+D}$ (after moving, for every $i \in \{1,\dots,n\}$, object $S_i$ by $d_i$) has a predefined property and the total movement distance $|D|$ is minimum. In the weighted version, we are also given a weight vector $w=(w_1,\dots,w_n)$ with positive entries, and the objective is to minimise the total weighted movement distance $|w \cdot D|$.
  We first consider the property locally dense, which we define as containment of a $k$-clique. Given $n$ weighted intervals, we solve the problem with respect to this property in $O(k^{1/3} n \log^{1+\varepsilon} n)$ time for any $\varepsilon>0$. We then consider $k$-connectivity for $1\le k \le n-1$. Given $n$ unweighted unit intervals, we solve the problem in $O(n^2 \log n)$ time and, for $k=1$, in $O(n\log n)$ time. For $k=1$, we prove strong NP-hardness on intervals of arbitrary length and on weighted unit disks (with only two distinct weights), and weak NP-hardness on weighted intervals (even when lengths equal weights).

</details>


### [417] [Bichromatic Line-Centers for Point Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23569)
*Jaegun Lee,Youjung Bae,Taehoon Ahn,Sang Won Bae,Hee-Kap Ahn*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究平面上n对点的双色线中心问题，对线中心方向约束的多种变体给出了高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点对需分别分配到红集R和蓝集B，目标是最小化两色点集最小宽度条带宽度的最大值，即线中心问题，需在方向约束下求解。

Method: 按线中心的方向约束分类讨论：一个线中心（求包含每对至少一点的条带）；两个线中心分水平、平行、方向均给定、仅一个方向给定；以及无约束情形，分别设计算法。

Result: 单个线中心O(n²)；两线中心同为水平O(n)；两中心平行或方向均给定时Θ(n log n)；仅一个方向给定时O(n²)；无约束情形O(n³ log n)。

Conclusion: 针对双色线中心问题的各类方向约束变体，给出了相应时间复杂度的有效算法，其中部分达到紧确界。

Abstract: We study the \emph{bichromatic line-center problem} for $n$ pairs of points in the plane. A feasible solution assigns one point from each pair to the red set $R$ and the other to the blue set $B$. The goal is to minimize $\max\{w^\circ(R),\,w^\circ(B)\}$, where $w^\circ(X)$ denotes the minimum width of a strip enclosing $X$; the midlines of the corresponding optimal strips define the line-centers of $R$ and $B$.
  We consider several variants induced by orientational constraints on line-centers and provide efficient algorithms for each. For one line-center, which consists of computing a minimum-width strip that contains at least one point from each pair, we give an $O(n^2)$-time algorithm. For two line-centers, we obtain an $O(n)$-time algorithm when both are horizontal, and $Θ(n\log n)$-time algorithms when the two centers are parallel or when both orientations are prescribed. When exactly one orientation is prescribed, we give an $O(n^2)$-time algorithm. Finally, for the unrestricted case, we present an $O(n^3\log n)$-time algorithm.

</details>


### [418] [Witness Set in Weak Visibility Polygons is Polynomial-Time Solvable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24460)
*Udvas Das,Shouvik Mondal,Sasanka Roy*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文首次为弱可见多边形（WVP）中的见证集问题（WSP）给出精确多项式时间算法，涵盖离散与连续两种设定，并证明离散算法的下界最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典美术馆问题中守卫数的精确求解困难，其自然对偶问题——见证集问题（WSP）可作为守卫数的下界。此前WSP的精确多项式算法仅适用于单调山（monotone mountains），这是WVP的真子类，因此需要将结果推广到更一般的WVP。

Method: 利用WVP中可见性交集图的结构性质：该图为梯形图（两条平行线之间梯形的交集图），且该类图真包含区间图与置换图。离散设定（DiscWSP）基于此结构在O(n+m log(n+m))时间内求解最大见证子集；连续设定（ContWSP）设计精确算法，运行时间为O(n log n + ρ²(n+ρ²))，其中ρ为凹顶点数。

Result: DiscWSP：n顶点凸多边形和m个给定点，在O(n+m log(n+m))时间内求最大见证子集；并证明m=Θ(n)时在代数决策树模型下存在Ω(n log n)下界，算法最优。可见性交集图为梯形图，真包含区间图与置换图。ContWSP：给出运行时间为O(n log n+ρ²(n+ρ²))的精确算法。

Conclusion: 本文首次为WVP中的WSP提供精确多项式时间算法，覆盖离散与连续两种设定；离散算法达到最优，且揭示的梯形图结构在图论上具有独立价值。

Abstract: In the classical Art Gallery Problem (AGP), guards are placed in a polygon so that together they see every point. The Witness Set Problem (WSP), introduced by Amit, Mitchell, and Packer, is a natural dual to the AGP. In this paper, we study the WSP in weak visibility polygons (WVPs), the simple polygons in which every point is seen from some point of one fixed edge. A witness set is a set of points whose visibility regions are pairwise disjoint, so that no single guard sees two of them. A maximum witness set, therefore, lower-bounds the guard number. Exact polynomial-time algorithms for the WSP are known only for monotone mountains, a proper subclass of WVPs. We give the first exact polynomial-time algorithms for the WSP in WVPs, in two settings.
  In the Discrete Witness Set Problem (DiscWSP), the witnesses come from a given set of $m$ points, and we find a maximum witness subset in $O(n + m \log(n+m))$ time on an $n$-vertex polygon. The algorithm rests on a structural fact: the visibility intersection graph of a WVP, in which two points are adjacent if their visibility regions intersect, is a trapezoid graph, that is, an intersection graph of trapezoids between two parallel lines. Moreover, the class of these graphs properly contains the interval graphs and the permutation graphs, which may be of independent interest in graph theory. We also prove an $Ω(n \log n)$ lower bound in the algebraic decision-tree model for instances with $m = Θ(n)$, so our algorithm for DiscWSP is optimum.
  In the Continuous Witness Set Problem (ContWSP), a witness may be any point of the polygon, and we give an exact algorithm running in $O(n \log n + ρ^{2}(n + ρ^{2}))$ time, where $ρ$ is the number of reflex vertices.

</details>
